function copyList(t) local o = {}; for k, v in pairs(t) do o[k] = v end; return o end

CUR_MAP = "Eriador"
WATER = {}
function theatreMap(th) return (th == "Rohan" or th == "Gondor") and th or "Eriador" end
function useTheatreMap(th)
  local key = theatreMap(th)
  if CUR_MAP == key then return false end
  local m = THEATRE_MAPS[key]
  WATER = m.water or {}
  for _, t in ipairs({ { NAMES, m.names }, { ADJ, m.adj }, { START, m.start } }) do
    for k in pairs(t[1]) do t[1][k] = nil end
    for k, v in pairs(t[2]) do t[1][k] = v end
  end
  CUR_MAP = key
  return true
end
function terrByName(name) for n, nm in ipairs(NAMES) do if nm == name then return n end end end
-- Change the theatre. A different map is only possible before the campaign starts (or as it is started anew).
function setTheatre(th, starting)
  if not THEATRE_MAPS[theatreMap(th)] or not ALLIANCES[th] then return "There is no theatre called " .. tostring(th) .. "." end
  local same = theatreMap(th) == theatreMap(S.settings.theatre or "Eriador")
  if not same and S.started and not starting then return "The " .. th .. " theatre has its own map: start a new campaign there (Settings > Campaign) to change to it." end
  S.settings.theatre = th
  if not same then
    useTheatreMap(th)
    local m = THEATRE_MAPS[theatreMap(th)]
    S.settings.capGood, S.settings.capEvil = m.capGood, m.capEvil
    S.routes = {}; S.slotOv = {}
    if not S.started then for n = 1, #NAMES do S.terr[n] = { owner = START[n] or "Neutral", stock = 0, b = {}, q = {}, conq = nil, bslot = {} } end end
  end
  return true
end

-- Fortress Upgrade: raises a Fortifications' garrison a step (600/800/1000), one upgrade at a time; not a building of its own
FORT_UP = { key = "fortup", name = "Fortress Upgrade", short = "FUp", cost = 150, turns = 2,
  rules = "Raises this Fortifications' garrison by 200 points: 600, then 800, then 1000. Up to three upgrades, one at a time. Doesn't count as a building.",
  desc = "Garrison +200 pts (600/800/1000); one at a time." }
BUILDING_BY_KEY.fortup = FORT_UP
-- a finished construction: a building, or one more Fortress Upgrade
function finishBuild(n, item)
  local t = S.terr[n]
  if item.key == "fortup" then t.fortup = (t.fortup or 0) + 1; pieceHook("built", n, "fort"); return " (garrison now " .. garrisonSize(n) .. " pts)" end
  t.b[item.key] = true; pieceHook("built", n, item.key); return ""
end

function allianceFor(side)
  local th = ALLIANCES[(S and S.settings.theatre) or "Eriador"] or ALLIANCES.Eriador
  return th[side]
end

function alliedArmy(side)
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if a.side == side and a.allied then return a end end
end

S = nil -- campaign state (saved)

-- Physical pieces hook (defined in the pieces module). Never lets a piece error break the rules engine.
function pieceHook(...)
  if QUIET then return end   -- an action being run just to count its dice moves nothing
  if piecesOn then
    local ok, err = pcall(piecesOn, ...)
    if not ok then print("War Council pieces: " .. tostring(err)) end
  end
end

-- ===================================================================
-- helpers
-- ===================================================================
function d6() return math.random(1, 6) end   -- replaced by the physical-dice version in dice.lua

function tname(n) return n and (tostring(n) .. " " .. (NAMES[n] or "?")) or "-" end

function newState()
  local s = {
    version = 1, started = false, turn = 0, stage = 1, nextId = 1,
    prio = nil, first = nil, winner = nil,
    ready = { Good = false, Evil = false },
    settings = { sound = true, sounds = {}, soundGap = 4, goodColor = "Green", evilColor = "Red", hidden = false, override = false, readyCheck = true, dropMove = true, popups = true, lockSeats = true, wagonFace = true, driveSpeed = "Slow", theatre = "Eriador",
                 startRP = 400, capGood = 26, capEvil = 6, mapHints = "on", newsTiming = "now", panelSize = "Normal", backups = true },
    mapVer = 2, terr = {}, armies = {}, agents = {}, battles = {}, log = {}, routes = {}, news = {}, newsId = 0, newsSeen = {}, hist = {}, reports = {}, bpSeen = {},
  }
  for n = 1, #NAMES do
    s.terr[n] = { owner = START[n] or "Neutral", stock = 0, b = {}, q = {}, conq = nil, bslot = {} }
  end
  return s
end

function newId()
  local id = S.nextId
  S.nextId = S.nextId + 1
  return id
end

-- vis: nil = everyone, "Good"/"Evil" = only that side (and spectators)
-- cat: when set, the line is also a pop-up dispatch under that heading.
function clog(msg, vis, cat)
  table.insert(S.log, { t = S.turn, s = S.stage, m = msg, v = vis, c = cat })
  while #S.log > 400 do table.remove(S.log, 1) end
  if cat then
    S.newsId = (S.newsId or 0) + 1
    S.news = S.news or {}
    table.insert(S.news, { id = S.newsId, t = S.turn, s = S.stage, m = msg, v = vis, c = cat })
    while #S.news > 150 do table.remove(S.news, 1) end
  end
end

-- How `viewer` refers to army `a` (nil viewer = full name).
-- Every army has a public number per side: until its leader is revealed the enemy (and its model on the
-- table) knows it only as "Good Army 3". Numbers are handed out in the order armies are raised and never reused.
function numberArmy(a)
  if not a or a.no then return a and a.no end
  S.armyNo = S.armyNo or {}
  S.armyNo[a.side] = (S.armyNo[a.side] or 0) + 1
  a.no = S.armyNo[a.side]
  S.armyNoById = S.armyNoById or {}
  S.armyNoById[tostring(a.id)] = a.no
  return a.no
end
function publicName(a) return a.side .. " Army " .. tostring(a.no or numberArmy(a) or "?") end
-- Has the enemy learned who this army is? (Hidden information off: everyone knows everything.)
function armyRevealed(a)
  if not S.settings.hidden then return true end
  return (a.seenLeader and a.seenLeader[OTHER[a.side]]) and true or false
end

-- How everyone knows an army: by name once its leader has been revealed, by its number until then.
function pubName(a) return armyRevealed(a) and a.name or publicName(a) end
-- A message everyone will read: the army's real name swapped for the one the enemy knows.
function pubMsg(a, msg)
  if not a or not a.name or a.name == "" or armyRevealed(a) then return msg end
  local pat = string.gsub(a.name, "[%^%$%(%)%%%.%[%]%*%+%-%?]", "%%%0")
  return (string.gsub(msg, pat, function() return publicName(a) end))
end

function aname(a, viewer)
  if not a then return "the garrison" end
  if not viewer or viewer == a.side or not S.settings.hidden then return a.name end
  if a.seenLeader and a.seenLeader[viewer] then return a.name end
  return publicName(a)
end

function capSentences(s)
  s = string.gsub(s, "^%l", string.upper)
  s = string.gsub(s, "([%.%!%?]%s+)(%l)", function(p, c) return p .. string.upper(c) end)
  return s
end

-- Log a line that names armies: with hidden information on, each side gets its own wording.
-- tpl uses {1}, {2}, ... for the armies in refs.
function clogFor(tpl, refs, cat, onlySide)
  local function fill(viewer) return capSentences((string.gsub(tpl, "{(%d+)}", function(k) return aname(refs[tonumber(k)], viewer) end))) end
  if not S.settings.hidden then clog(fill(nil), onlySide, cat); return end
  for _, sd in ipairs(SIDES) do
    if not onlySide or onlySide == sd then clog(fill(sd), sd, cat) end
  end
end

function stageName() return STAGES[S.stage] end

-- Returns true when the action is allowed in the current stage; otherwise an error string.
function stageOk(needed)
  if S.settings.override then return true end
  if not S.started then return "Start the campaign first (Settings tab)." end
  if STAGES[S.stage] == needed then return true end
  return "That can only be done in the " .. needed .. " stage."
end

function armyById(id)
  id = tonumber(id)
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if a.id == id then return a end end
end

function agentById(id)
  id = tonumber(id)
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do if g.id == id then return g end end
end

function removeArmy(a)
  pieceHook("removed", "army", a)
  for i, x in ipairs(S.armies) do if x == a then table.remove(S.armies, i) break end end
  -- attached surgeons die with the army
  for i = #S.agents, 1, -1 do
    local g = S.agents[i]
    if g.army == a.id then
      clog(g.side .. " Surgeon attached to " .. a.name .. " was lost.", g.side, "agents")
      table.remove(S.agents, i)
    end
  end
end

function removeAgent(g)
  pieceHook("removed", "agent", g)
  for i, x in ipairs(S.agents) do if x == g then table.remove(S.agents, i) return end end
end

function armiesIn(n, side)
  local out = {}
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
    if a.loc == n and (side == nil or a.side == side) then table.insert(out, a) end
  end
  return out
end

-- Location of an agent (surgeons ride with their army)
function agentLoc(g)
  if g.army then
    local a = armyById(g.army)
    return a and a.loc or nil
  end
  return g.loc
end

function agentsIn(n, side, typ)
  local out = {}
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do
    if agentLoc(g) == n and (side == nil or g.side == side) and (typ == nil or g.type == typ) then
      table.insert(out, g)
    end
  end
  return out
end

function ownedBy(side)
  local out = {}
  for n, t in ipairs(S.terr) do if t.owner == side then table.insert(out, n) end end
  return out
end

-- Neutral territories produce nothing.
function income(n)
  if S.terr[n].owner == "Neutral" then return 0 end
  if (S.terr[n].noIncome or 0) > 0 then return 0 end   -- pillaged or scorched
  return BASE_INCOME + (S.terr[n].b.city and CITY_INCOME or 0) + industryBonus(n) + whiteCityBonus(n)
end

function countBuildings(side, key)
  local c = 0
  for _, t in ipairs(S.terr) do if t.owner == side and t.b[key] then c = c + 1 end end
  return c
end

function countAgents(side, typ)
  local c = 0
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do if g.side == side and g.type == typ then c = c + 1 end end
  return c
end

function mercCount(side)
  local c = 0
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if a.side == side and a.merc then c = c + 1 end end
  return c
end

function isAdjacent(a, b)
  for _, x in ipairs(ADJ[a] or {}) do if x == b then return true end end
  return false
end

-- Roads: leaving a territory with Roads costs no movement, a limited number of times a turn:
-- once for an army, up to three times for an agent or a Supply Wagon. Past that, a step costs the usual 1.
ROAD_FREE_ARMY, ROAD_FREE_OTHER = 1, 3

-- Land Mapping: in a mapped territory and every territory next to it, nothing hinders its owner's pieces
-- (road blockages, snow, the extra cost of Neutral land for wagons).
function mappedFor(n, side)
  if not side or not n or not S.terr[n] then return false end
  local t = S.terr[n]
  if t.b and t.b.mapping and t.owner == side then return true end
  for _, m in ipairs(ADJ[n] or {}) do local u = S.terr[m]; if u and u.b and u.b.mapping and u.owner == side then return true end end
  return false
end
function snowedFor(n, side) return isSnowed(n) and not mappedFor(n, side) end

-- What one step from u to v costs, and the free road crossings left after it. A Supply Wagon spends 2 whenever
-- it enters or leaves Neutral territory (roads don't help there), so wagons can't race through unclaimed land.
function stepCostFor(u, v, wagon, free, side)
  free = free or 0
  if wagon and ((S.terr[u].owner == "Neutral" and not mappedFor(u, side)) or (S.terr[v].owner == "Neutral" and not mappedFor(v, side))) then return 2, free end
  if S.terr[u].b.roads and free > 0 then return 0, free - 1 end
  return 1, free
end

-- The free road crossings a mover starts with: armies 1, agents and wagons 3 (less any used earlier this turn).
function roadFreeFor(wagon, free)
  if free ~= nil then return free end
  return wagon and ROAD_FREE_OTHER or ROAD_FREE_ARMY
end
function agentRoadFree(g) return math.max(0, ROAD_FREE_OTHER - (g.roadUsed or 0)) end

-- Every way of moving from start within budget, keeping each territory's cheapest cost for every number of free road
-- crossings left (more left is better). pen(u, v) adds a routing penalty that isn't movement (to go around enemies).
function moveSearch(start, budget, wagon, free, pen, side)
  free = roadFreeFor(wagon, free)
  local st = { [start] = { [free] = { p = 0, c = 0 } } }
  local done = {}
  while true do
    local bn, bf, bs
    for n, byF in pairs(st) do for f, x in pairs(byF) do
      if not (done[n] and done[n][f]) and (not bs or x.p < bs.p) then bn, bf, bs = n, f, x end
    end end
    if not bs then break end
    done[bn] = done[bn] or {}; done[bn][bf] = true
    for _, v in ipairs(ADJ[bn] or {}) do
      if not snowedFor(v, side) then
        local c, f2 = stepCostFor(bn, v, wagon, bf, side)
        local nc = bs.c + c
        if nc <= budget + 1e-6 then
          local np = bs.p + c + (pen and pen(bn, v) or 0)
          st[v] = st[v] or {}
          local cur = st[v][f2]
          -- a state no better than one already found (cheaper or as cheap with as many crossings left) is dropped
          local dominated = false
          for f3, y in pairs(st[v]) do if f3 >= f2 and y.p <= np and y.c <= nc and not (f3 == f2 and y == cur) then dominated = true; break end end
          if not dominated and (not cur or np < cur.p) then st[v][f2] = { p = np, c = nc, prev = bn, pf = bf } end
        end
      end
    end
  end
  return st
end

-- Movement cost from start to everywhere within budget, and the free road crossings left on the cheapest way there.
function reachDist(start, budget, wagon, free, side)
  local st = moveSearch(start, budget, wagon, free, nil, side)
  local dist, left = {}, {}
  for n, byF in pairs(st) do
    for f, x in pairs(byF) do
      if dist[n] == nil or x.c < dist[n] - 1e-9 or (math.abs(x.c - dist[n]) < 1e-9 and f > left[n]) then dist[n], left[n] = x.c, f end
    end
  end
  return dist, left
end

-- An agent spends its movement over as many moves as it likes in the Logistics stage.
function agentMoveLeft(g)
  if g.moved then return 0 end
  return math.max(0, (AGENTS[g.type] and AGENTS[g.type].move or 0) - (g.mvUsed or 0))
end

function reachable(start, budget, wagon, free, side)
  local dist = reachDist(start, budget, wagon, free, side)
  local out = {}
  for n, _ in pairs(dist) do if n ~= start then table.insert(out, n) end end
  table.sort(out)
  return out
end

-- The way army a goes from `from` to `dest` within budget: around enemy armies if it can, else the shortest way.
function armyPath(a, from, dest, budget)
  if from == dest then return {} end
  local st = moveSearch(from, budget or 99, false, ROAD_FREE_ARMY,
    function(u, v) return ((v ~= dest and #armiesIn(v, OTHER[a.side]) > 0) and 100 or 0) end, a.side)
  local at, bf
  for f, x in pairs(st[dest] or {}) do if not at or x.p < at.p then at, bf = x, f end end
  if not at then return shortestPath(from, dest) end
  local path, cur, cf = {}, dest, bf
  while cur ~= from do
    table.insert(path, 1, cur)
    local x = st[cur][cf]
    cur, cf = x.prev, x.pf
  end
  return path
end

-- Fewest-borders path from a to b (excluding a, including b). Used to animate tokens.
function shortestPath(a, b)
  if a == b then return {} end
  local prev, queue, head = { [a] = a }, { a }, 1
  while head <= #queue do
    local u = queue[head]; head = head + 1
    if u == b then break end
    for _, v in ipairs(ADJ[u] or {}) do
      if not prev[v] then prev[v] = u; table.insert(queue, v) end
    end
  end
  if not prev[b] then return { b } end
  local path, cur = {}, b
  while cur ~= a do table.insert(path, 1, cur); cur = prev[cur] end
  return path
end

-- Supplies: an army outside its own territory eats from the RP it carries each turn.
-- Running campaign totals for the Chronicle (upkeep paid, supplies delivered by wagons).
function addTotal(kind, side, v)
  if QUIET or not v or v == 0 then return end
  S.totals = S.totals or {}
  S.totals[kind] = S.totals[kind] or {}
  S.totals[kind][side] = (S.totals[kind][side] or 0) + v
end

function armyUpkeep(a) return ((a.size or 400) <= 600) and 25 or 50 end
function inFriendlyLand(a) return a.loc and S.terr[a.loc] and S.terr[a.loc].owner == a.side end
-- Out of supplies: outside its own territory with nothing left to eat. It can't move and fights at +2 Courage.
function outOfSupplies(a)
  return a and a.status ~= "building" and not a.fangorn and not inFriendlyLand(a) and (a.carried or 0) <= 0
end

function armyMove(a)
  return baseArmyMove(a) + ruleMoveBonus(a) + eventMoveBonus(a)
end

-- Cavalry-only armies. The Wolf Pack of Angmar list always counts as one, though it lacks the Cavalry keyword.
function isWolfPack(list)
  local l = string.gsub(string.lower(list or ""), "[^a-z]", "")
  return string.find(l, "wolfpackofangmar", 1, true) ~= nil
end
function isCavalry(a) return a and (a.cavalry or isWolfPack(a.list)) and true or false end

function baseArmyMove(a)
  local m
  if isCavalry(a) then m = (a.size == 400) and 3 or 2
  else m = (a.size == 400) and 2 or 1 end
  return m
end

function armyBuildCost(a) return ARMY_COST[a.size][1] end

-- Pay from an army's carried supplies first, then the territory's stockpile (if owned), then your wagons there.
function canPay(side, n, amount, army)
  local have = 0
  if army then have = have + (army.carried or 0) end
  if n and S.terr[n].owner == side then have = have + S.terr[n].stock end
  if n then for _, g in ipairs(wagonsIn(n, side)) do have = have + (g.carried or 0) end end
  return have >= amount, have
end

function pay(side, n, amount, army)
  local rest = amount
  if army and (army.carried or 0) > 0 then
    local take = math.min(army.carried, rest)
    army.carried = army.carried - take
    rest = rest - take
  end
  if rest > 0 and n and S.terr[n].owner == side then
    local take = math.min(S.terr[n].stock, rest)
    S.terr[n].stock = S.terr[n].stock - take
    rest = rest - take
  end
  if rest > 0 and n then
    for _, g in ipairs(wagonsIn(n, side)) do
      local take = math.min(g.carried or 0, rest)
      if take > 0 then
        g.carried = g.carried - take
        rest = rest - take
        clog(wagonLabel(g) .. " paid " .. take .. " RP.", side)
      end
      if rest <= 0 then break end
    end
  end
  if rest > 0 and S.settings.override and n then S.terr[n].stock = S.terr[n].stock - rest end
end

function payOrFail(side, n, amount, army)
  local ok, have = canPay(side, n, amount, army)
  if not ok and not S.settings.override then
    return "Not enough resources here: need " .. amount .. ", have " .. have .. "."
  end
  pay(side, n, amount, army)
  return true
end

-- ===================================================================
-- board sync
-- ===================================================================
function getBoard()
  local b = getObjectFromGUID(BOARD_GUID)
  if b then return b end
  for _, o in ipairs(getAllObjects()) do
    if o.getName() == "Eriador Campaign Map" then return o end
  end
end

function syncOwner(n)
  local b = getBoard()
  if b then b.call("setOwner", { id = n, owner = S.terr[n].owner }) end
end

function syncAllOwners()
  for n = 1, #S.terr do syncOwner(n) end
end

function setOwner(n, owner, why)
  local t = S.terr[n]
  if t.owner == owner then return end
  local old = t.owner
  t.owner = owner
  t.conq = nil
  if why == "conquered" then pcall(eventConquered, n, owner); pcall(theatreConquered, n, owner) end
  if t.sow and why == "conquered" then
    clog("The discontent in " .. tname(n) .. " has passed.", nil, "territories")
    t.sow = nil
  end
  syncOwner(n)
  clog(tname(n) .. ": " .. old .. " -> " .. owner .. (why and (" (" .. why .. ")") or ""), nil, "territories")
  pcall(checkEnts)
  checkVictory()
end

function checkVictory()
  if not S.started or S.winner then return end
  for _, side in ipairs(SIDES) do
    if #ownedBy(side) == 0 then
      S.winner = OTHER[side]
      S.winnerNote = side .. " has lost all of its territory. " .. S.winner .. " is victorious after " .. S.turn .. " turn" .. ((S.turn == 1) and "" or "s") .. "."
      clog("*** " .. side .. " has lost all territory. " .. S.winner .. " wins the campaign! ***", nil, "war")
      broadcastToAll("War Council: " .. S.winner .. " wins the campaign!", { 1, 0.85, 0.4 })
    end
  end
end

-- ===================================================================
-- campaign start and stage machine
-- ===================================================================
function startCampaign()
  pieceHook("reset")
  local keep = S.settings
  local keepSlots = S.slotOv or {}
  local keepRoutes = S.routes or {}
  local keepVer = S.mapVer
  S = newState()
  S.mapVer = keepVer or S.mapVer
  S.settings = keep
  S.slotOv = keepSlots
  S.routes = keepRoutes
  S.started = true
  S.turn = 1
  S.stage = 1
  S.terr[keep.capGood].owner = "Good"
  S.terr[keep.capEvil].owner = "Evil"
  S.terr[keep.capGood].stock = keep.startRP
  S.terr[keep.capEvil].stock = keep.startRP
  -- Each capital begins fortified.
  for _, cap in ipairs({ keep.capGood, keep.capEvil }) do
    S.terr[cap].b.fort = true
    pieceHook("built", cap, "fort")
  end
  pcall(rulesAtStart)
  clog("The campaign begins. Each side receives " .. keep.startRP .. " RP (Good at " .. tname(keep.capGood) ..
      ", Evil at " .. tname(keep.capEvil) .. "). Both capitals begin with Fortifications.")
  startOfTurn(true)
  syncAllOwners()
end

-- Small public picture of the map: owners, buildings, conquests and army positions (names filtered at export).
local BKEYS = { hospital = "H", mustering = "M", tavern = "T", medical = "C", slums = "S", demolition = "D", roads = "R", fort = "F", city = "Y", scouttower = "W", archery = "A", mapping = "P" }
function publicSnapshot()
  local o, b, c, a = {}, {}, {}, {}
  for n, t in ipairs(S.terr) do
    table.insert(o, string.sub(t.owner, 1, 1))
    local s = ""
    for _, bd in ipairs(BUILDINGS) do if t.b[bd.key] then s = s .. (BKEYS[bd.key] or "?") end end
    table.insert(b, s)
    if t.conq then table.insert(c, { n = n, by = t.conq.by, p = t.conq.prog, q = t.conq.need, h = t.conq.held }) end
  end
  for _, x in ipairs(S.armies) do
    table.insert(a, { id = x.id, s = x.side, l = x.loc, nm = x.name, sz = x.size, ld = x.leader, r = (x.status == "building") and 1 or nil,
                      no = x.no, rv = armyRevealed(x) and 1 or nil, fg = (x.figure and x.figure ~= "") and x.figure or nil,
                      c = x.carried or 0, oos = outOfSupplies(x) and 1 or nil, m = x.merc and 1 or nil,
                      up = (x.status ~= "building" and not inFriendlyLand(x)) and armyUpkeep(x) or nil,
                      d = ((x.damage or 0) > 0) and x.damage or nil })
  end
  local w = {}
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents or {}) do
    if g.type == "Supply Wagon" and g.loc then
      table.insert(w, { id = g.id, s = g.side, l = g.loc, c = ((g.carried or 0) > 0) and g.carried or nil,
                        sr = (g.route and g.route.mode == "army" and g.route.on) and g.route.army or nil })
    end
  end
  local x = {}
  pcall(function() x = actionSnapshot() end)
  -- construction under way: { n, k = building letter, l = turns left }
  local q = {}
  for n, t in ipairs(S.terr) do
    for _, item in ipairs(t.q or {}) do table.insert(q, { n = n, k = BKEYS[item.key] or "?", l = item.left }) end
  end
  return { t = S.turn, st = S.stage, o = table.concat(o), b = b, c = c, a = a, w = w, x = x, q = q }
end

-- Runs when a new turn begins: income and all countdowns. The campaign's first turn collects no income.
function startOfTurn(firstTurn)
  pcall(checkEnts)
  if not firstTurn then pcall(theatreTurnStart) end
  for n, t in ipairs(S.terr) do
    if not firstTurn then
      t.stock = t.stock + income(n)
      if (t.noIncome or 0) > 0 then
        t.noIncome = t.noIncome - 1
        if t.noIncome == 0 then t.noIncome = nil; t.noIncomeBy = nil; clog(tname(n) .. " produces supplies again.", nil, "territories") end
      end
    end
    -- construction
    for i = #t.q, 1, -1 do
      local item = t.q[i]
      if item.halt then
        item.halt = false
        clog(tname(n) .. ": construction of " .. BUILDING_BY_KEY[item.key].name .. " was halted this turn.", t.owner ~= "Neutral" and t.owner or nil)
      else
        item.left = item.left - 1
        if item.left <= 0 then
          local extra = finishBuild(n, item)
          table.remove(t.q, i)
          clog(t.owner .. ": " .. BUILDING_BY_KEY[item.key].name .. " completed at " .. tname(n) .. extra .. ".", nil, "construction")
        end
      end
    end
  end
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
    if not firstTurn and a.status ~= "building" and not inFriendlyLand(a) then
      local need = armyUpkeep(a)
      local paid = math.min(a.carried or 0, need)
      a.carried = (a.carried or 0) - paid
      addTotal("upkeep", a.side, paid)
      if paid > 0 then
        clog(a.name .. " used " .. paid .. " RP of supplies at " .. tname(a.loc) .. " (" .. a.carried .. " left).", a.side)
      end
      if a.carried <= 0 then
        clog(a.name .. " is out of supplies at " .. tname(a.loc) .. ": it can't move and fights at +2 Courage until it is resupplied.", a.side, "routes")
      end
    end
    a.moved = false
    a.march = false
    if a.status == "building" then
      a.buildLeft = a.buildLeft - 1
      if a.buildLeft <= 0 then
        a.status = "ready"
        clog(a.side .. " army " .. a.name .. " is ready at " .. tname(a.loc) .. ".", a.side, "armies")
        pieceHook("ready", "army", a)
      end
    end
    if a.upLeft then
      a.upLeft = a.upLeft - 1
      if a.upLeft <= 0 then
        a.size = a.upTo
        a.upLeft, a.upTo = nil, nil
        -- the bigger list the player gave when ordering the upgrade takes over now
        local nl = a.upList
        if nl then
          if nl.leader and nl.leader ~= "" then a.leader = nl.leader end
          if nl.list and nl.list ~= "" then a.list = nl.list end
          if nl.units then a.units = nl.units end
          if nl.roster then a.roster = nl.roster end
          a.seenUnits = {}
          a.upList = nil
        end
        clog(a.side .. " army " .. a.name .. " upgraded to " .. a.size .. " points" .. (nl and ", with its new list" or "") .. ".", a.side, "armies")
      end
    end
  end
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do g.moved = false; g.acted = false; g.mvUsed = 0; g.roadUsed = 0 end
  S.prio, S.first = nil, nil
  clog("=== Turn " .. S.turn .. " begins." .. (firstTurn and "" or " Owned territories collect income.") .. " ===")
  -- per-side turn summary and history
  S.hist = S.hist or {}
  local row = { t = S.turn }
  for _, side in ipairs(SIDES) do
    local terrs, inc, rp = 0, 0, 0
    for n, t in ipairs(S.terr) do
      if t.owner == side then terrs = terrs + 1; inc = inc + income(n); rp = rp + t.stock end
    end
    local armies = 0
    for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if a.side == side then armies = armies + 1; rp = rp + (a.carried or 0) end end
    for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do if g.side == side then rp = rp + (g.carried or 0) end end
    row[side] = { terr = terrs, rp = rp, armies = armies }
    if not firstTurn then
      clog("Turn " .. S.turn .. ": " .. side .. " collected " .. inc .. " RP from " .. terrs .. " territories. Total resources " .. rp .. " RP; " ..
           armies .. " arm" .. ((armies == 1) and "y" or "ies") .. ".", side, "turn")
    end
  end
  table.insert(S.hist, row)
  while #S.hist > 100 do table.remove(S.hist, 1) end
  S.snaps = S.snaps or {}
  table.insert(S.snaps, publicSnapshot())
  while #S.snaps > 200 do table.remove(S.snaps, 1) end
  if onNewTurn then pcall(onNewTurn) end
end

-- Runs on entering the End stage.
function endOfTurn()
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
    if a.replenishing then
      a.replenishing = false
      a.damage = 0
      a.result = nil
      clog(a.side .. " army " .. a.name .. " is fully replenished.", a.side, "armies")
    end
    if (a.absent or 0) > 0 then
      a.absent = a.absent - 1
      if a.absent == 0 then clog(a.side .. " army " .. a.name .. ": Leader has returned.", a.side, "armies") end
    end
    if (a.exh or 0) > 0 then a.exh = a.exh - 1 end
  end
  -- conquest (fortress protection is judged from the map as it stood at the start of this step)
  local shield = {}
  for n = 1, #S.terr do shield[n] = fortShield(n) end
  for n, t in ipairs(S.terr) do
    local present = {}
    for _, a in ipairs(armiesIn(n)) do present[a.side] = true end
    local attacker = nil
    for _, side in ipairs(SIDES) do
      if present[side] and t.owner ~= side and not present[OTHER[side]] then attacker = side end
    end
    if attacker and t.owner == "Good" and S.peasants == S.turn then
      clog(tname(n) .. " holds out this turn (Peasants' Pride)" .. (t.conq and t.conq.by == attacker and (": the conquest stays at " .. (t.conq.prog or 0) .. "/" .. (t.conq.need or "?")) or "") .. ".", nil, "territories")
    elseif attacker and shield[n] then
      if not t.conq or t.conq.prog > 0 or not t.conq.held then
        clog(tname(n) .. " can't be conquered while the fortress at " .. tname(shield[n]) .. " holds.", nil, "territories")
      end
      local cut = t.conq and t.conq.by == attacker and t.conq.cut or nil
      t.conq = { by = attacker, prog = 0, need = math.max(1, conquestNeed(n, attacker) - (cut or 0)), held = shield[n], cut = cut }
    elseif attacker then
      if not t.conq or t.conq.by ~= attacker then
        t.conq = { by = attacker, prog = 0, cut = insightCut(n, attacker) }
      end
      t.conq.held = nil
      t.conq.need = math.max(1, conquestNeed(n, attacker) - (t.conq.cut or 0))
      t.conq.prog = t.conq.prog + 1
      if t.conq.prog >= t.conq.need then
        setOwner(n, attacker, "conquered")
      else
        clog(tname(n) .. ": " .. attacker .. " conquest " .. t.conq.prog .. "/" .. t.conq.need .. ".", nil, "territories")
      end
    elseif t.conq then
      clog(tname(n) .. ": conquest by " .. t.conq.by .. " interrupted.", nil, "territories")
      t.conq = nil
    end
  end
  checkVictory()
end

-- A completed Fortifications protects the owner's adjacent territories: they can't be conquered while it holds.
-- Returns the protecting fort territory, or nil.
function fortShield(n)
  local o = S.terr[n].owner
  if o == "Neutral" then return nil end
  for _, m in ipairs(ADJ[n] or {}) do
    if S.terr[m].b.fort and S.terr[m].owner == o and not (m == S.settings.capGood and NAMES[n] == "West Osgiliath" and hasRule("Good", "whitecity")) then return m end
  end
  return nil
end

-- Neutral territories next to an enemy fortress take one extra turn to conquer.
function fortDelay(n, attacker)
  if S.terr[n].owner ~= "Neutral" then return 0, nil end
  for _, m in ipairs(ADJ[n] or {}) do
    local o = S.terr[m].owner
    if S.terr[m].b.fort and o ~= "Neutral" and o ~= attacker then return 1, m end
  end
  return 0, nil
end

-- End stages needed to conquer n. attacker may be nil (worst case is shown).
function conquestNeed(n, attacker)
  local t = S.terr[n]
  local need = 2 + ((t.owner ~= "Neutral") and 1 or 0) + (t.b.city and 1 or 0) + (t.sow and 1 or 0)
  if attacker then
    need = need + fortDelay(n, attacker)
  elseif t.owner == "Neutral" then
    for _, m in ipairs(ADJ[n] or {}) do
      if S.terr[m].b.fort and S.terr[m].owner ~= "Neutral" then need = need + 1; break end
    end
  end
  return need
end

function advanceStage()
  if not S.started then return "Start the campaign first (Settings tab)." end
  if STAGES[S.stage] == "Retreat" and not S.settings.override then
    for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
      if a.needsRetreat then return a.side .. " army " .. a.name .. " still has to retreat (Armies tab)." end
    end
  end
  S.ready = { Good = false, Evil = false }
  if S.stage == #STAGES then
    pcall(eventsTurnEnd)
    pcall(resolveOrders)
    pcall(noteSieges)   -- a siege begun this turn is dated this turn
    S.turn = S.turn + 1
    S.stage = 1
    startOfTurn()
  else
    S.stage = S.stage + 1
    clog("-- " .. STAGES[S.stage] .. " stage --")
    if STAGES[S.stage] == "Logistics" then
      runRoutes()
      for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
        if a.wounds then
          for i = #a.wounds, 1, -1 do
            if a.wounds[i].t < S.turn then table.remove(a.wounds, i) end
          end
        end
      end
    elseif STAGES[S.stage] == "Battle" then
      S.bpTurn, S.bpSeen = S.turn, {}
      pcall(revealBattles)
    elseif STAGES[S.stage] == "Retreat" then
      pcall(eventsRetreatStage)
    elseif STAGES[S.stage] == "End" then
      endOfTurn()
    end
  end
  return true
end

-- Ready check: the stage advances once both sides are ready.
function setReady(side, val)
  if not S.started then return "Start the campaign first (Settings tab)." end
  if side ~= "Good" and side ~= "Evil" then return "Pick a side first." end
  S.ready[side] = val and true or false
  if val then clog(side .. " is ready to leave the " .. stageName() .. " stage.") end
  if S.ready.Good and S.ready.Evil then
    local r = advanceStage()
    if r ~= true then
      S.ready = { Good = false, Evil = false }
      return r
    end
    return true, "advanced"
  end
  return true
end

function rollPriority()
  local ok = stageOk("Priority"); if ok ~= true then return ok end
  local g, e
  -- a tie rolls again; while the dice are still being thrown by the players, one pair is asked for at a time
  repeat g, e = d6("Good", "Priority"), d6("Evil", "Priority") until g ~= e or (DICE and DICE.short)
  S.prio = { Good = g, Evil = e, winner = (g > e) and "Good" or "Evil" }
  S.first = nil
  clog("Priority: Good " .. g .. ", Evil " .. e .. ". " .. S.prio.winner .. " chooses who acts first.")
  pcall(rollEvents)
  return true
end

function chooseFirst(side)
  if not S.prio then return "Roll priority first." end
  S.first = side
  clog(side .. " will act first this turn.")
  return true
end

-- ===================================================================
-- territories
-- ===================================================================
-- A territory holds at most 4 buildings, counting those still being built.
MAX_BUILDINGS = 4
CITY_PER_TERR = 4   -- one City for every 4 territories a side owns
function buildingCount(n)
  local t, c = S.terr[n], 0
  for _, has in pairs(t.b or {}) do if has then c = c + 1 end end
  for _, item in ipairs(t.q or {}) do if item.key ~= "fortup" then c = c + 1 end end
  return c
end

function construct(side, n, key)
  local ok = stageOk("Building"); if ok ~= true then return ok end
  local t, b = S.terr[n], BUILDING_BY_KEY[key]
  if t.owner ~= side and not S.settings.override then return "You don't own " .. tname(n) .. "." end
  if key == "fortup" then return fortUpgrade(side, n) end
  if t.b[key] then return tname(n) .. " already has a " .. b.name .. "." end
  for _, item in ipairs(t.q) do if item.key == key then return b.name .. " is already being built there." end end
  if key == "city" and not S.settings.override then
    local owned, cities = 0, 0
    for _, x in ipairs(S.terr) do
      if x.owner == side then owned = owned + 1 end
      if x.owner == side and x.b.city then cities = cities + 1 end
      for _, item in ipairs(x.q or {}) do if item.key == "city" and (item.side or x.owner) == side then cities = cities + 1 end end
    end
    local allowed = math.floor(owned / CITY_PER_TERR)
    if cities + 1 > allowed then
      return side .. " may have 1 City for every " .. CITY_PER_TERR .. " territories it owns: " .. owned .. " territories allow " .. allowed ..
             " Cit" .. (allowed == 1 and "y" or "ies") .. ", and it already has " .. cities .. " (built or being built)."
    end
  end
  if buildingCount(n) >= MAX_BUILDINGS and not S.settings.override then
    return tname(n) .. " already has " .. MAX_BUILDINGS .. " buildings (finished or being built). A territory can hold at most " .. MAX_BUILDINGS .. "."
  end
  local r = payOrFail(side, n, b.cost); if r ~= true then return r end
  local turns = buildTurns(side, key, n)
  table.insert(t.q, { key = key, left = turns, total = turns, side = side })
  clog(side .. " began building " .. b.name .. " at " .. tname(n) .. " (" .. turns .. " turns" .. ((turns < b.turns) and (", " .. ((side == "Good") and "Hobbit Laborers" or "Tear Them All Down")) or "") .. ").", side)
  return true
end

-- the upgrades a fortress may still take, and starting one
function fortUpRoom(n)
  local t = S.terr[n]
  if not t.b.fort then return 0, "needs finished Fortifications" end
  for _, item in ipairs(t.q) do if item.key == "fortup" then return 0, "an upgrade is already under way" end end
  local left = math.floor((1000 - garrisonSize(n)) / 200)
  if left <= 0 then return 0, "the garrison is already 1000 pts" end
  return left
end
function fortUpgrade(side, n)
  local t = S.terr[n]
  if t.owner ~= side and not S.settings.override then return "You don't own " .. tname(n) .. "." end
  local room, why = fortUpRoom(n)
  if room <= 0 then return "Can't upgrade the fortress at " .. tname(n) .. ": " .. why .. "." end
  local r = payOrFail(side, n, FORT_UP.cost); if r ~= true then return r end
  table.insert(t.q, { key = "fortup", left = FORT_UP.turns, total = FORT_UP.turns, side = side })
  clog(side .. " began a Fortress Upgrade at " .. tname(n) .. " (" .. FORT_UP.turns .. " turns; garrison " .. garrisonSize(n) .. " to " .. (garrisonSize(n) + 200) .. " pts).", side)
  return true
end

function adjustStock(n, delta, who)
  S.terr[n].stock = math.max(0, S.terr[n].stock + delta)
  clog(tname(n) .. ": stockpile adjusted by " .. delta .. " (" .. who .. ").")
  return true
end

-- ===================================================================
-- armies
-- ===================================================================
-- Point Cap: each side may field armies totalling up to 2000 points, +600 for each City it has built.
-- Any number of armies is allowed while their points (counting armies still being raised, and the size an
-- army is upgrading to) stay within the cap.
POINT_CAP_BASE, POINT_CAP_CITY = 2000, 600
function armyCap(side) return POINT_CAP_BASE + POINT_CAP_CITY * countBuildings(side, "city") end
function armyPoints(side, except)
  local p = 0
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if a.side == side and a ~= except and not a.fangorn then p = p + (a.upTo or a.size or 0) end end
  return p
end
function armyCount(side)
  local c = 0
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if a.side == side then c = c + 1 end end
  return c
end

function createArmy(side, f)
  local ok = stageOk("Building"); if ok ~= true then return ok end
  local n = tonumber(f.terr)
  local size = tonumber(f.size) or 400
  if armyPoints(side) + size > armyCap(side) and not S.settings.override then
    return side .. " fields " .. armyPoints(side) .. " of its " .. armyCap(side) .. " pt Point Cap (2000, +600 for each City): a " .. size ..
           " pt army would go over. Build a City or raise a smaller army."
  end
  if not n then return "Choose a territory to raise the army in." end
  if S.terr[n].owner ~= side and not S.settings.override then return "You must raise armies in your own territory." end
  if not ARMY_COST[size] then return "Invalid army size." end
  local name = (f.name and f.name ~= "") and f.name or (side .. " Army " .. S.nextId)
  local leader = f.leader or ""
  if S.noDenethor and (string.find(string.lower(leader .. " " .. (f.units or "")), "denethor", 1, true)) and not S.settings.override then
    return "The King has returned: no army may contain Denethor."
  end
  if f.named and leader ~= "" then
    for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
      if a.side == side and a.named and string.lower(a.leader) == string.lower(leader) then
        return leader .. " already leads " .. a.name .. ". A named hero can only lead one army."
      end
    end
  end
  local cost = ARMY_COST[size][1]
  if f.allied then
    local al = allianceFor(side)
    local cur = alliedArmy(side)
    if cur then return al.name .. ": you already field " .. cur.name .. " as your allied army." end
    f.merc = false
    if not f.list or f.list == "" then f.list = al.list end
  end
  if f.merc then
    local mc = mercCount(side)
    if mc >= 2 then return "You can only have 2 Mercenary armies at a time." end
    for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
      if a.side == side and a.merc and string.lower(a.list or "") == string.lower(f.list or "") then
        return "You already have a Mercenary army from " .. (f.list or "that list") .. "."
      end
    end
    cost = cost + MERC[mc + 1].cost
  end
  local r = payOrFail(side, n, cost); if r ~= true then return r end
  local turns = ARMY_COST[size][2] - (S.terr[n].b.mustering and 1 or 0)
  if turns < 1 then turns = 1 end
  local a = {
    id = newId(), side = side, name = name, size = size, leader = leader, named = f.named and true or false,
    list = f.list or "", merc = f.merc and true or false, cavalry = f.cavalry and true or false, canMarch = f.canMarch and true or false, allied = f.allied and true or false,
    units = f.units or "", roster = f.roster, loc = n, status = "building", buildLeft = turns, damage = 0, carried = 0, paid = cost, raisedAt = n,
    seenLeader = {}, seenUnits = {}, wounds = {},
  }
  table.insert(S.armies, a)
  numberArmy(a)
  clog(side .. " began raising " .. name .. " (" .. size .. " pts" .. (a.merc and ", Mercenary" or "") ..
      ") at " .. tname(n) .. ": " .. cost .. " RP, ready in " .. turns .. " turns.", side)
  pieceHook("created", "army", a)
  return true, a
end

-- Call off an army that is still being raised: it is removed and everything paid for it comes back to the stockpile
-- it was raised in (or the capital, if that territory has been lost since).
function raiseCost(a)
  if a.paid then return a.paid end
  local c = ARMY_COST[a.size] and ARMY_COST[a.size][1] or 0
  if a.merc and MERC[1] then c = c + MERC[1].cost end
  return c
end
function cancelRaise(a)
  if not a then return "Choose an army." end
  if a.status ~= "building" then return a.name .. " has already been raised: disband it instead." end
  local ok = stageOk("Building"); if ok ~= true and not S.settings.override then return ok end
  local refund = raiseCost(a)
  local n = a.raisedAt or a.loc
  if not S.terr[n] or S.terr[n].owner ~= a.side then n = ((a.side == "Good") and S.settings.capGood or S.settings.capEvil) or n end
  if n and S.terr[n] then S.terr[n].stock = (S.terr[n].stock or 0) + refund end
  clog(a.side .. " called off raising " .. a.name .. ": " .. refund .. " RP returned to " .. (n and tname(n) or "the treasury") .. ".", a.side)
  removeArmy(a)
  return true, refund
end

function moveArmy(a, dest, march)
  local ok = stageOk("Logistics"); if ok ~= true then return ok end
  dest = tonumber(dest)
  if a.status == "building" and not S.settings.override then return a.name .. " is still being raised." end
  if a.moved and not S.settings.override then return a.name .. " has already moved this turn." end
  if outOfSupplies(a) and not S.settings.override then
    return a.name .. " is out of supplies and can't move. Send it supplies with a wagon or a neighbouring army."
  end
  local held = eventNoMove(a)
  if held and not S.settings.override then return a.name .. " can't move this turn: " .. held .. "." end
  local budget = armyMove(a) + (march and marchBonus(a) or 0) - (a.slow or 0) - sailCost(a)
  local lifting = besieging(a) and dest ~= a.loc
  if lifting and not S.settings.override then
    if march then return a.name .. " is lifting the siege of " .. tname(a.loc) .. ": it can move only 1 territory this turn, so no Heroic March." end
    if not isAdjacent(a.loc, dest) then return a.name .. " is lifting the siege of " .. tname(a.loc) .. ": it can move only 1 territory this turn, whatever its special rules." end
    budget = 1
  end
  if fangornBlocked(a, tonumber(dest)) and not S.settings.override then return "The Ents will only march into the Ent lands (Wellinghall, Treegarth, Limsworth and Entmoot)." end
  if not S.settings.override then
    local mad = madnessBlocks(a, dest); if mad then return mad end
    local par = paranoiaAllowed(a, reachable(a.loc, budget, false, nil, a.side))
    if par and not par[tonumber(dest)] then return "Sauron's Paranoia: " .. a.name .. " must move as close as it can to the Good army it is hunting." end
  end
  local okDest = false
  for _, x in ipairs(reachable(a.loc, budget, false, nil, a.side)) do if x == dest then okDest = true end end
  if not okDest and not S.settings.override then return tname(dest) .. " is out of range." end
  local from = a.loc
  if lifting then clog(a.side .. " lifts the siege of " .. tname(from) .. ": " .. a.name .. " withdraws, 1 territory at most this turn.", nil, "territories") end
  local leave = leaveBlock(from, a, true)
  if leave == "slow" and not isAdjacent(from, dest) and not S.settings.override then
    return "The roads out of " .. tname(from) .. " are blocked: leaving takes " .. a.name .. "'s whole move, so it can only go to a neighbouring territory."
  end
  local path = armyPath(a, from, dest, budget)
  local stopAt = blockOnPath(path, a.side)
  local engaged = nil
  if not passesEnemies(a) then
    for i, m in ipairs(path) do
      if i < #path and (not stopAt or i < (select(2, blockOnPath(path, a.side)) or 99)) and #armiesIn(m, OTHER[a.side]) > 0 then engaged = m; break end
    end
  end
  if engaged then stopAt = nil; dest = engaged
  elseif stopAt then dest = stopAt end
  if engaged then clog(a.name .. " ran into an enemy army at " .. tname(engaged) .. " and had to stop.", nil, "armies") end
  a.loc = dest
  pcall(eyeAfterMove, a, from, path, dest)
  pcall(eventAfterMove, a)
  a.moved = true
  a.slow = nil
  if leave then afterLeave(from, leave) end
  if stopAt or (S.terr[dest].block and not mappedFor(dest, a.side)) then hitBlock(dest, a.side, a.name) end
  armyLeft(a, from)
  if march then a.exh = 3; clog(a.name .. " made a Heroic March and is exhausted for 2 turns.", a.side) end
  clog(a.side .. " army " .. a.name .. " moved " .. tname(from) .. " -> " .. tname(dest) .. ".", a.side)
  pieceHook("moved", "army", a, armyPath(a, from, dest, budget))
  armyArrivedCaptures(a)
  if S.terr[dest].owner ~= a.side then
    clog("An army has entered " .. tname(dest) .. ".")
  end
  return true
end

function replenishCost(a)
  local tier = math.floor((a.damage or 0) / 25)
  if tier < 1 then return 0 end
  local c = REPLENISH[a.size][tier]
  if S.terr[a.loc].b.hospital and S.terr[a.loc].owner == a.side then c = c - HOSPITAL_DISCOUNT[a.size] end
  if a.merc then c = c + MERC[math.max(1, math.min(2, mercCount(a.side)))].rep end
  return math.max(0, c)
end

function replenish(a)
  local ok = stageOk("Building"); if ok ~= true then return ok end
  if (a.damage or 0) <= 0 then return a.name .. " has no losses to replenish." end
  if a.replenishing then return a.name .. " is already replenishing." end
  local cost = replenishCost(a)
  local r = payOrFail(a.side, a.loc, cost, a); if r ~= true then return r end
  a.replenishing = true
  clog(a.side .. " army " .. a.name .. " is replenishing (" .. cost .. " RP). Vulnerable until the end of the turn.", a.side)
  return true
end

function editArmy(a, f)
  local ok = stageOk("Building"); if ok ~= true then return ok end
  local r = payOrFail(a.side, a.loc, EDIT_COST, a); if r ~= true then return r end
  if f.leader and f.leader ~= "" then a.leader = f.leader end
  if f.list and f.list ~= "" then a.list = f.list end
  if f.units then a.units = f.units end
  if f.named ~= nil then a.named = f.named end
  if f.roster then a.roster = f.roster end
  a.seenUnits = {}
  clog(a.side .. " edited " .. a.name .. " (" .. EDIT_COST .. " RP).", a.side)
  return true
end

-- The new list for an upgrade (from a pasted roster): kept until the upgrade finishes, then it replaces the army's list.
function setUpgradeList(a, rr)
  if not a then return "Choose an army." end
  if not rr then return "Paste the army's new list first." end
  local to = a.upTo
  if not to then for i, s in ipairs(SIZES) do if s == a.size then to = SIZES[i + 1] end end end
  if rr.points and to and rr.points > to then return "That list is " .. rr.points .. " pts: an upgraded " .. a.name .. " can field " .. to .. " pts." end
  a.upList = { leader = rr.general, list = rr.list, units = string.sub(rr.summary or "", 1, 600), roster = rosterForArmy(rr) }
  return true
end

function upgradeArmy(a, rr)
  local ok = stageOk("Building"); if ok ~= true then return ok end
  if a.upLeft then return a.name .. " is already upgrading." end
  local nextSize
  for i, s in ipairs(SIZES) do if s == a.size then nextSize = SIZES[i + 1] end end
  if not nextSize then return a.name .. " is already at the largest size." end
  if armyPoints(a.side, a) + nextSize > armyCap(a.side) and not S.settings.override then
    return "Upgrading " .. a.name .. " to " .. nextSize .. " pts would take " .. a.side .. " over its " .. armyCap(a.side) .. " pt Point Cap (" .. armyPoints(a.side) .. " pts fielded)."
  end
  if rr and rr.points and rr.points > nextSize then return "That list is " .. rr.points .. " pts: an upgraded " .. a.name .. " can field " .. nextSize .. " pts." end
  local r = payOrFail(a.side, a.loc, UPGRADE_COST, a); if r ~= true then return r end
  a.upLeft, a.upTo = UPGRADE_TURNS, nextSize
  if rr then setUpgradeList(a, rr) end
  clog(a.side .. " is upgrading " .. a.name .. " to " .. nextSize .. " pts (" .. UPGRADE_COST .. " RP, " .. UPGRADE_TURNS .. " turn).", a.side)
  return true
end

function disbandRefund(a)
  local base = armyBuildCost(a)
  if a.result == "Quartered" then return 0 end
  if a.result == "Broken" then return math.floor(base * 0.25) end
  return math.floor(base * 0.5)
end

function disbandArmy(a)
  local ok = stageOk("Building"); if ok ~= true then return ok end
  local refund = disbandRefund(a) + (a.carried or 0)
  local t = S.terr[a.loc]
  if t.owner == a.side then
    t.stock = t.stock + refund
    clog(a.side .. " disbanded " .. a.name .. "; " .. refund .. " RP returned to " .. tname(a.loc) .. ".", a.side)
  else
    clog(a.side .. " disbanded " .. a.name .. " in hostile land; resources were lost.", a.side)
  end
  removeArmy(a)
  return true
end

function retreatArmy(a, dest)
  local ok = stageOk("Retreat"); if ok ~= true then return ok end
  dest = tonumber(dest)
  if not a.needsRetreat and not S.settings.override then return a.name .. " doesn't need to retreat." end
  if not dest or not S.terr[dest] then return "Choose where to retreat to." end
  local paths, far = retreatPaths(a)
  if not paths[dest] and not S.settings.override then
    if far then return "Retreat at least 2 territories (up to " .. math.max(2, armyMove(a)) .. ") through friendly or neutral land." end
    return "Retreat must be to an adjacent friendly or neutral territory."
  end
  local from = a.loc
  local route = paths[dest] or { from, dest }
  local leave = leaveBlock(from, a, true)
  a.loc = dest
  a.needsRetreat = false
  if leave then afterLeave(from, leave) end
  if S.terr[dest].block and not mappedFor(dest, a.side) then hitBlock(dest, a.side, a.name) end
  armyLeft(a, from)
  clog(a.side .. " army " .. a.name .. " retreated " .. tname(from) .. " -> " .. tname(dest) .. ((#route > 2) and (" (" .. (#route - 1) .. " territories)") or "") .. ".", a.side)
  local steps = {}; for i = 2, #route do table.insert(steps, route[i]) end   -- the path the token drives, without its start
  pieceHook("moved", "army", a, steps)
  armyArrivedCaptures(a)
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do
    if g.army == a.id and g.type == "Surgeon" then
      local r = d6(a.side, "Surgeon withdrawal")
      if r == 1 then
        clog("The Surgeon with " .. a.name .. " failed to withdraw and was killed (rolled 1).", a.side, "agents")
        removeAgent(g)
      else
        clog("The Surgeon with " .. a.name .. " withdrew safely (rolled " .. r .. ").", a.side)
      end
      break
    end
  end
  return true
end

-- Where a beaten army may retreat: at least 2 territories away (further, up to its full movement, if it is faster),
-- through and into friendly or neutral land, never through enemy armies. If nowhere 2 away can be reached, 1.
-- Returns { [dest] = path }, and whether the retreat is the full (2+) kind.
function retreatPaths(a)
  local function safe(n) local o = S.terr[n].owner; return o == a.side or o == "Neutral" end
  local function clear(n) return #armiesIn(n, OTHER[a.side]) == 0 end
  local maxD = math.max(2, armyMove(a))
  local prev, dist, queue = { [a.loc] = false }, { [a.loc] = 0 }, { a.loc }
  local qi = 1
  while qi <= #queue do
    local n = queue[qi]; qi = qi + 1
    if dist[n] < maxD and (n == a.loc or clear(n)) then
      for _, m in ipairs(ADJ[n] or {}) do
        if dist[m] == nil and safe(m) then dist[m] = dist[n] + 1; prev[m] = n; table.insert(queue, m) end
      end
    end
  end
  local function pathTo(n) local p = {}; while n do table.insert(p, 1, n); n = prev[n] end; return p end
  local out, any = {}, false
  for n, d in pairs(dist) do if d >= 2 and clear(n) then out[n] = pathTo(n); any = true end end
  if any then return out, true end
  for _, m in ipairs(ADJ[a.loc] or {}) do if safe(m) and clear(m) then out[m] = { a.loc, m }; any = true end end
  if not any then for _, m in ipairs(ADJ[a.loc] or {}) do if safe(m) then out[m] = { a.loc, m } end end end
  return out, false
end

function retreatOptions(a)
  local out = {}
  for n in pairs((retreatPaths(a))) do table.insert(out, n) end
  table.sort(out)
  return out
end

function destroyArmy(a, why)
  clogFor("{1} was destroyed" .. (why and (" (" .. why .. ")") or "") .. ".", { a }, "armies")
  removeArmy(a)
  return true
end

-- Can `side` see full details of army `a`?
function towerSees(side, n)
  for _, m in ipairs(ADJ[n] or {}) do
    if S.terr[m].owner == side and S.terr[m].b.scouttower then return true end
  end
  return S.terr[n].owner == side and S.terr[n].b.scouttower
end

-- ===================================================================
-- agents
-- ===================================================================
function recruitAgent(side, typ, n, armyId, name)
  local ok = stageOk("Building"); if ok ~= true then return ok end
  local def = AGENTS[typ]
  if not def then return "Choose an agent type." end
  n = tonumber(n)
  local army = nil
  if typ == "Surgeon" then
    army = armyById(armyId)
    if not army or army.side ~= side then return "Choose one of your armies for the Surgeon to join." end
    for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do if g.army == army.id then return army.name .. " already has a Surgeon." end end
    n = army.loc
  end
  if not n then return "Choose a territory." end
  if S.terr[n].owner ~= side and not S.settings.override then return "Agents must be recruited in your own territory." end
  if def.building then
    local cap, have = countBuildings(side, def.building), countAgents(side, typ)
    if have >= cap and not S.settings.override then
      return "You can have " .. cap .. " " .. typ .. "(s) - build another " .. BUILDING_BY_KEY[def.building].name .. "."
    end
  end
  local r = payOrFail(side, n, def.cost); if r ~= true then return r end
  if typ == "Supply Wagon" then name = wagonName(n) end
  if name and name ~= "" then
    for _, x in ipairs(S.agents) do if x.name and string.lower(x.name) == string.lower(name) then return "Another agent is already called " .. name .. "." end end
  else name = nil end
  local g = { id = newId(), side = side, type = typ, name = name, loc = (typ ~= "Surgeon") and n or nil,
              army = army and army.id or nil, carried = 0 }
  table.insert(S.agents, g)
  clog(side .. " recruited a " .. typ .. " at " .. tname(n) .. (army and (" attached to " .. army.name) or "") ..
      " (" .. def.cost .. " RP).", side)
  pieceHook("created", "agent", g)
  return true, g
end

function scoutArmies(g)
  local found = 0
  for _, a in ipairs(armiesIn(g.loc, OTHER[g.side])) do
    found = found + 1
    a.seenLeader[g.side] = true
    if a.seenUnits[g.side] then
      -- already scouted: nothing more to learn, so no roll
      clog("Scout at " .. tname(g.loc) .. ": " .. a.name .. ", led by " .. (a.leader ~= "" and a.leader or "an unnamed leader") .. ", is already known.", g.side, "agents")
      goto continue
    end
    local r = d6(g.side, "Scout")
    if r >= scoutNeed(g.side) then
      a.seenUnits[g.side] = true
      clog("Scout at " .. tname(g.loc) .. ": found " .. a.name .. ", led by " .. (a.leader ~= "" and a.leader or "an unnamed leader") ..
          ". Rolled " .. r .. " - its units are revealed.", g.side, "agents")
    else
      clog("Scout at " .. tname(g.loc) .. ": found an army led by " .. (a.leader ~= "" and a.leader or "an unnamed leader") ..
          ". Rolled " .. r .. " - units not revealed.", g.side, "agents")
    end
    ::continue::
  end
  return found
end

function moveAgent(g, dest)
  local ok = stageOk("Logistics"); if ok ~= true then return ok end
  dest = tonumber(dest)
  if g.army then return "Surgeons move with their army." end
  local left = agentMoveLeft(g)
  if left <= 0 and not S.settings.override then return "This agent has used all its movement this turn." end
  if dest == g.loc then return "It's already there." end
  local free = agentRoadFree(g)
  local dist, roadsLeft = reachDist(g.loc, left, g.type == "Supply Wagon", free, g.side)
  if not dist[dest] and not S.settings.override then
    return tname(dest) .. " is out of range (" .. (left == math.floor(left) and left or string.format("%.1f", left)) .. " movement left)."
  end
  local cost = dist[dest] or left
  local from = g.loc
  local leave = (g.type == "Supply Wagon") and leaveBlock(from, g, false) or nil
  if leave == "slow" and not isAdjacent(from, dest) and not S.settings.override then
    return "The roads out of " .. tname(from) .. " are blocked: leaving takes the wagon's whole move, so it can only go to a neighbouring territory."
  end
  local stopAt = (g.type == "Supply Wagon") and blockOnPath(shortestPath(from, dest), g.side) or nil
  if stopAt then dest = stopAt end
  g.loc = dest
  g.mvUsed = (g.mvUsed or 0) + cost
  g.roadUsed = (g.roadUsed or 0) + math.max(0, free - (roadsLeft[dest] or free))
  -- leaving blocked roads or running into a blockage ends its move; so does running out of movement
  if leave == "slow" or stopAt or agentMoveLeft(g) < 0.5 then g.moved = true end
  if leave then afterLeave(from, leave) end
  if g.type == "Supply Wagon" and (stopAt or (S.terr[dest].block and not mappedFor(dest, g.side))) then hitBlock(dest, g.side, wagonLabel(g)) end
  clog(g.side .. " " .. g.type .. " moved " .. tname(from) .. " -> " .. tname(dest) .. ".", g.side)
  if g.type == "Supply Wagon" then
    pieceHook("routed", "agent", g, shortestPath(from, dest))
  else
    pieceHook("moved", "agent", g, shortestPath(from, dest))
  end
  if g.type == "Scout" then scoutArmies(g) end
  if g.type == "Supply Wagon" then wagonArrive(g) end
  return true
end

-- target strings: "agent:<id>", "leader:<armyId>", "hero:<armyId>", "build:<key>", "army:<armyId>"
function agentAction(g, target)
  local ok = stageOk("Logistics"); if ok ~= true then return ok end
  if g.acted and not S.settings.override then return "This agent has already acted this turn." end
  local kind, id = string.match(target or "", "^(%a+):(.+)$")
  if not kind then return "Choose a target." end
  local here = agentLoc(g)
  if g.type == "Assassin" then
    if kind == "agent" then
      local v = agentById(id)
      if not v or agentLoc(v) ~= here or v.side == g.side then return "That agent isn't here." end
      local r = d6(g.side, "Assassin")
      g.acted = true
      if r == 6 then
        clog(g.side .. " Assassin killed an enemy " .. v.type .. " at " .. tname(here) .. " (rolled 6).", nil, "agents")
        removeAgent(v)
      elseif r == 1 then
        clog(g.side .. " Assassin was killed attempting to strike at " .. tname(here) .. " (rolled 1).", nil, "agents")
        removeAgent(g)
      else
        clog(g.side .. " Assassin failed to kill the " .. v.type .. " (rolled " .. r .. ").", g.side)
      end
      return true
    elseif kind == "leader" or kind == "hero" then
      local a = armyById(id)
      if not a or a.loc ~= here or a.side == g.side then return "That army isn't here." end
      g.acted = true
      local r1 = d6(g.side, "Assassin")
      if r1 == 1 then
        clog(g.side .. " Assassin was caught and killed at " .. tname(here) .. " (rolled 1).", nil, "agents")
        removeAgent(g)
        return true
      end
      if r1 ~= 6 then
        clog(g.side .. " Assassin found no opening against " .. aname(a, g.side) .. " (rolled " .. r1 .. ").", g.side)
        return true
      end
      local need = (kind == "leader") and 5 or 4
      local r2 = d6(g.side, "Assassin")
      if r2 >= need then
        local who = (kind == "leader") and ("the Leader" .. (a.leader ~= "" and (" (" .. a.leader .. ")") or "")) or "a Hero"
        table.insert(a.wounds, { who = who, t = S.turn })
        clogFor("An Assassin wounded " .. who .. " of {1} (rolled 6, then " .. r2 .. "). The wound lasts until the next Logistics stage.", { a }, "agents")
      else
        clog(g.side .. " Assassin struck at " .. aname(a, g.side) .. " but failed to wound (rolled 6, then " .. r2 .. ", needed " .. need .. "+).", g.side)
      end
      return true
    end
  elseif g.type == "Saboteur" then
    if kind == "build" then
      local t = S.terr[here]
      if t.owner == g.side then return "That's your own construction." end
      local item
      for _, q in ipairs(t.q) do if q.key == id then item = q end end
      if not item then return "Nothing like that is being built here." end
      g.acted = true
      local r = d6(g.side, "Saboteur")
      if r == 6 then
        item.left = item.left + 1
        item.halt = true
        clog(g.side .. " Saboteur set back the " .. BUILDING_BY_KEY[item.key].name .. " at " .. tname(here) .. " by a turn (rolled 6).", nil, "agents")
      elseif r >= 4 then
        item.halt = true
        clog(g.side .. " Saboteur halted the " .. BUILDING_BY_KEY[item.key].name .. " at " .. tname(here) .. " this turn (rolled " .. r .. ").", nil, "agents")
      else
        clog(g.side .. " Saboteur failed to disrupt construction (rolled " .. r .. ").", g.side)
      end
      return true
    elseif kind == "army" then
      local a = armyById(id)
      if not a or a.loc ~= here or a.side == g.side then return "That army isn't here." end
      g.acted = true
      local r = d6(g.side, "Saboteur")
      if r == 6 then
        a.slow = 1
        clogFor(g.side .. " Saboteur slowed {1}: -1 region on its next move (rolled 6).", { a }, "agents")
      elseif r == 1 then
        clogFor(g.side .. " Saboteur was caught by {1} and killed (rolled 1).", { a }, "agents")
        removeAgent(g)
      else
        clog(g.side .. " Saboteur failed to slow " .. aname(a, g.side) .. " (rolled " .. r .. ").", g.side)
      end
      return true
    end
  elseif g.type == "Supply Wagon" then
    if kind == "army" then
      local a = armyById(id)
      if not a or a.side == g.side then return "Choose an enemy army." end
      local gain = math.floor((g.carried or 0) / 2)
      local room = a.size - (a.carried or 0)
      a.carried = (a.carried or 0) + math.min(gain, room)
      clogFor(g.side .. " Supply Wagon was captured by {1}: " .. (g.carried or 0) .. " RP lost, " .. math.min(gain, room) .. " taken.", { a }, "captures")
      g.carried = 0
      g.acted = true
      return true
    end
  end
  return "That agent can't do that."
end

function agentTargets(g)
  local out = {}
  local here = agentLoc(g)
  if not here then return out end
  if g.type == "Assassin" then
    for _, v in ipairs(agentsIn(here, OTHER[g.side])) do table.insert(out, { "agent:" .. v.id, "Kill enemy " .. v.type }) end
    for _, a in ipairs(armiesIn(here, OTHER[g.side])) do
      table.insert(out, { "leader:" .. a.id, "Wound Leader of enemy army" .. (a.seenLeader[g.side] and (" (" .. a.leader .. ")") or "") })
      table.insert(out, { "hero:" .. a.id, "Wound a Hero of enemy army" })
    end
  elseif g.type == "Saboteur" then
    if S.terr[here].owner ~= g.side then
      for _, q in ipairs(S.terr[here].q) do table.insert(out, { "build:" .. q.key, "Sabotage " .. BUILDING_BY_KEY[q.key].name }) end
    end
    for _, a in ipairs(armiesIn(here, OTHER[g.side])) do table.insert(out, { "army:" .. a.id, "Slow enemy army" }) end

  end
  return out
end

-- ===================================================================
-- supplies
-- ===================================================================
-- endpoints: "stock:<n>", "army:<id>", "wagon:<id>"
function endpoint(e)
  local kind, id = string.match(e or "", "^(%a+):(%d+)$")
  if kind == "stock" then
    local n = tonumber(id)
    return { kind = kind, loc = n, get = function() return S.terr[n].stock end,
             set = function(v) S.terr[n].stock = v end, cap = math.huge, label = "Stockpile of " .. tname(n) }
  elseif kind == "army" then
    local a = armyById(id); if not a then return nil end
    return { kind = kind, loc = a.loc, side = a.side, get = function() return a.carried or 0 end,
             set = function(v) a.carried = v end, cap = a.size, label = a.name }
  elseif kind == "wagon" then
    local g = agentById(id); if not g then return nil end
    return { kind = kind, loc = g.loc, side = g.side, get = function() return g.carried or 0 end,
             set = function(v) g.carried = v end, cap = WAGON_CAP, label = "Supply Wagon #" .. g.id }
  end
end

-- An army takes RP from (or leaves RP in) the stockpile of the friendly territory it stands in.
-- amount nil: as much as it can.
function armySupplies(a, dir, amount)
  if not a then return "Choose an army." end
  local t = S.terr[a.loc]
  if t.owner ~= a.side and not S.settings.override then return a.name .. " isn't in one of your territories, so there is no stockpile to use here." end
  local n = tonumber(amount)
  if dir == "pick" then
    local room = math.max(0, a.size - (a.carried or 0))
    if room <= 0 then return a.name .. " is already carrying all it can (" .. a.size .. " RP)." end
    n = math.min(n or room, room, t.stock or 0)
    if n <= 0 then return "The stockpile at " .. tname(a.loc) .. " is empty." end
    return transfer(a.side, "stock:" .. a.loc, "army:" .. a.id, n)
  else
    n = math.min(n or (a.carried or 0), a.carried or 0)
    if n <= 0 then return a.name .. " isn't carrying anything." end
    return transfer(a.side, "army:" .. a.id, "stock:" .. a.loc, n)
  end
end

-- A new name for an army. Its model's named side follows on its own.
function renameArmy(a, name)
  if not a then return "Choose an army." end
  name = tostring(name or "")
  name = string.gsub(name, "^%s+", "")
  name = string.gsub(name, "%s+$", "")
  if name == "" then return "Type the new name first." end
  if #name > 48 then return "That name is too long (48 letters at most)." end
  if name == a.name then return "That's already its name." end
  for _, b in ipairs(S.armies) do
    if b ~= a and string.lower(b.name or "") == string.lower(name) then return "Another army is already called " .. name .. "." end
  end
  local old = a.name
  a.name = name
  -- the enemy only hears about it if they already know the army by name
  clog(old .. " is now called " .. name .. ".", (S.settings.hidden and not armyRevealed(a)) and a.side or nil, "armies")
  return true
end

function transfer(side, fromE, toE, amount)
  local ok = stageOk("Logistics"); if ok ~= true then return ok end
  local a, b = endpoint(fromE), endpoint(toE)
  if not a or not b then return "Choose where to move resources from and to." end
  -- no amount: as much as the source has and the target can take
  if amount == nil or tostring(amount) == "" then amount = math.min(a.get(), b.cap - b.get()) end
  amount = math.floor(tonumber(amount) or 0)
  if amount <= 0 then
    if a.get() <= 0 then return a.label .. " has nothing to give." end
    if b.cap - b.get() <= 0 then return b.label .. " is full." end
    return "Enter an amount."
  end
  if fromE == toE then return "Choose two different places." end
  if a.kind == "stock" and S.terr[a.loc].owner ~= side and not S.settings.override then return "You don't own that stockpile." end
  if b.kind == "stock" and S.terr[b.loc].owner ~= side and not S.settings.override then return "You don't own that stockpile." end
  local bothArmies = a.kind == "army" and b.kind == "army"
  if a.loc ~= b.loc and not (bothArmies and isAdjacent(a.loc, b.loc)) and not S.settings.override then
    return "They must be in the same territory (armies may also trade with adjacent armies)."
  end
  if a.kind == "stock" and b.kind ~= "stock" and droughtAt(a.loc) and not S.settings.override then
    return "Great Drought: " .. tname(a.loc) .. " won't give up its stores this turn."
  end
  if a.get() < amount then return a.label .. " only has " .. a.get() .. " RP." end
  local room = b.cap - b.get()
  if room < amount then return b.label .. " can only take " .. room .. " more RP." end
  a.set(a.get() - amount)
  b.set(b.get() + amount)
  clog(side .. " moved " .. amount .. " RP: " .. a.label .. " -> " .. b.label .. ".", side)
  return true
end

-- ===================================================================
-- battles
-- ===================================================================
-- Sieges: an enemy army at a fortress starts a siege, and the siege battle is fought from the next turn on, never the turn it begins.
function noteSieges()
  if not S.started then return end
  for n, t in ipairs(S.terr) do
    local besieged = t.b.fort and t.owner ~= "Neutral" and #armiesIn(n, OTHER[t.owner]) > 0
    if besieged and not t.siegeT then
      t.siegeT = S.turn
      t.siegeDef = {}   -- the owner's armies already inside when the siege began
      for _, a in ipairs(armiesIn(n, t.owner)) do t.siegeDef[tostring(a.id)] = true end
      clog(OTHER[t.owner] .. " lays siege to " .. tname(n) .. ": the assault comes on turn " .. (S.turn + 1) .. ".", nil, "territories")
    elseif not besieged and t.siegeT then
      t.siegeT = nil; t.siegeDef = nil
    end
  end
end
-- true when territory n's siege may be fought this turn (or it isn't a siege at all)
function siegeReady(n)
  local t = S.terr[n]
  if not (t.b.fort and t.owner ~= "Neutral") then return true end
  if reliefBattle(n) then return true end
  return t.siegeT ~= nil and t.siegeT < S.turn
end
-- An army of the owner's that marches on the besiegers the turn the siege begins fights them in an ordinary battle, then and there.
function reliefBattle(n)
  local t = S.terr[n]
  if not (t.siegeT and t.siegeT == S.turn and t.owner ~= "Neutral") then return false end
  for _, a in ipairs(armiesIn(n, t.owner)) do if not (t.siegeDef or {})[tostring(a.id)] then return true end end
  return false
end
-- Is a battle at n a Siege? (a fortress held by a side, and not a relieving army's battle)
function siegeBattle(n)
  local t = S.terr[n]
  return (t.b.fort and t.owner ~= "Neutral" and not reliefBattle(n)) and true or false
end
-- An army besieging a fortress: it may lift the siege, but then moves at most 1 territory that turn.
function besieging(a)
  local t = a and a.loc and S.terr[a.loc]
  return (t and t.b.fort and t.owner == OTHER[a.side] and t.siegeT) and true or false
end

function battleTerritories()
  pcall(noteSieges)
  local out = {}
  for n, t in ipairs(S.terr) do
    local g, e = #armiesIn(n, "Good") > 0, #armiesIn(n, "Evil") > 0
    local garrison = t.b.fort and t.owner ~= "Neutral" and #armiesIn(n, OTHER[t.owner]) > 0
    if ((g and e) or garrison) and siegeReady(n) then table.insert(out, n) end
  end
  return out
end

function battleModifiers(n, atk, def)
  local t = S.terr[n]
  local lines = {}
  local prio = { Good = 0, Evil = 0 }
  if reliefBattle(n) then
    table.insert(lines, t.owner .. " marches on the besiegers the turn the siege began: an ordinary battle, not a Siege (no garrison). If " .. OTHER[t.owner] .. " loses, the siege is lifted and it retreats as normal.")
  end
  if siegeBattle(n) then
    table.insert(lines, "Fortifications: this is a SIEGE. " .. t.owner .. " gains a " .. garrisonSize(n) .. " pt garrison" .. (((t.fortup or 0) > 0) and (" (" .. t.fortup .. " Fortress Upgrade" .. (t.fortup == 1 and "" or "s") .. ")") or ((garrisonSize(n) > 400) and " (The Hornburg)" or "")) .. ".")
    -- siege supplies: RP for siege equipment only, this battle
    local ints = function(sz) return math.max(1, math.floor(((sz or 400) - 200) / 200)) end
    local foe, atkRP, parts = OTHER[t.owner], 0, {}
    for _, x in ipairs(armiesIn(n, foe)) do local v = 20 * ints(x.size); atkRP = atkRP + v; table.insert(parts, x.name .. " " .. v) end
    local defRP = 30 * ints(garrisonSize(n))
    table.insert(lines, "Siege supplies (points for siege equipment only): " .. foe .. " " .. atkRP .. " pts" .. ((#parts > 1) and (" (" .. table.concat(parts, ", ") .. ")") or "") ..
         "; " .. t.owner .. " " .. defRP .. " pts from its " .. garrisonSize(n) .. " pt garrison.")
  end
  if t.b.scouttower and t.owner ~= "Neutral" then
    prio[t.owner] = prio[t.owner] + 1
    table.insert(lines, "Scout Tower: " .. t.owner .. " +1 to all priority rolls.")
  end
  if t.b.archery and t.owner ~= "Neutral" then table.insert(lines, "Ballista Tower: " .. t.owner .. " places a Ballista Tower in their deployment zone (Shoot 4+, Strength 8, 48\", normal Ballista scatter; up to 3 shots a game; destroyed by an unengaged enemy model in base contact in a Fight phase).") end
  -- Scout Towers here or next door: that side chooses who deploys first
  local towers = {}
  local near = { n }; for _, m in ipairs(ADJ[n] or {}) do near[#near + 1] = m end
  for _, m in ipairs(near) do local u = S.terr[m]; if u and u.b.scouttower and u.owner ~= "Neutral" then towers[u.owner] = true end end
  if towers.Good and towers.Evil then table.insert(lines, "Scout Towers: both sides have one here or next door, so deployment follows the scenario.")
  elseif towers.Good or towers.Evil then local w = towers.Good and "Good" or "Evil"; table.insert(lines, "Scout Tower: " .. w .. " chooses who deploys first, whatever the scenario rules or warband totals say.") end
  for _, a in ipairs({ atk, def }) do
    if a then
      if a.replenishing then
        prio[a.side] = prio[a.side] - 1
        table.insert(lines, a.name .. " is replenishing: -1 to all priority rolls; losses not yet restored.")
      end
      if (a.damage or 0) > 0 and not a.replenishing then
        table.insert(lines, a.name .. " is at -" .. a.damage .. "%: field it as if its points limit were " .. math.floor(a.size * (1 - a.damage / 100)) .. ".")
      end
      for _, w in ipairs(a.wounds or {}) do table.insert(lines, a.name .. ": " .. w.who .. " starts with a Wound.") end
      if (a.absent or 0) > 0 then table.insert(lines, a.name .. ": its Leader is absent (lost a Heroic Duel).") end
      if (a.exh or 0) > 0 then table.insert(lines, a.name .. ": exhausted from a Heroic March, -1\" movement.") end
      if outOfSupplies(a) then table.insert(lines, a.name .. " is out of supplies: +2 Courage for all its models this battle.") end
      if a.dug == n then table.insert(lines, a.name .. " is dug in: this battle must use the Dig-In Defense scenario (rulebook p.28).") end
      pcall(eventBattleLines, n, a, lines)
      if osgFatigue(a) > 0 then table.insert(lines, a.name .. ": Osgiliath Fatigue, +" .. osgFatigue(a) .. " Courage this battle.") end
    end
  end
  pcall(reinforcementLines, n, lines)
  if eyeOn(n) then table.insert(lines, "The Eye of Sauron is on " .. NAMES[n] .. ": the Good army has +1 Courage and the Evil army -1 Courage this battle.") end
  if hasRule("Good", "dunedain") and #agentsIn(n, "Good", "Scout") > 0 then
    table.insert(lines, "Dúnedain Scouts: a Good Scout is here, so Good's army gains a free Ranger of the North model for this battle.")
  end
  if #lines == 0 then table.insert(lines, "No special conditions.") end
  return lines, prio
end

-- Armies that are about to fight see each other: leader, name and units are revealed to the enemy (their
-- models on the table turn to their named side).
function revealArmyTo(a, foe, where)
  if not a or not foe then return end
  a.seenLeader = a.seenLeader or {}
  a.seenUnits = a.seenUnits or {}
  local before = armyRevealed(a)
  local pub = publicName(a)
  a.seenLeader[foe] = true
  a.seenUnits[foe] = true
  if not before and armyRevealed(a) then
    clog("Battle at " .. tname(where or a.loc) .. ": " .. pub .. " is revealed as " .. a.name ..
         ((a.leader and a.leader ~= "") and (", led by " .. a.leader) or "") .. ".", nil, "armies")
  end
end
function revealBattles(only)
  for _, n in ipairs(battleTerritories()) do
    if not only or only == n then
      local t = S.terr[n]
      for _, a in ipairs(armiesIn(n)) do
        local foe = OTHER[a.side]
        if #armiesIn(n, foe) > 0 or (t.b.fort and t.owner == foe) then revealArmyTo(a, foe, n) end
      end
    end
  end
end

function rollBattlePriority(n, atk, def)
  pcall(revealBattles, tonumber(n))
  local _, prio = battleModifiers(n, atk, def)
  local g, e
  repeat
    g, e = d6("Good", "Battle priority") + prio.Good, d6("Evil", "Battle priority") + prio.Evil
  until g ~= e or (DICE and DICE.short)
  local w = g > e and "Good" or "Evil"
  clog("Battle priority at " .. tname(n) .. ": Good " .. g .. ", Evil " .. e .. " (with modifiers). " .. w .. " has priority.")
  return true
end

-- Calling the retreat: it must be called the moment the army breaks, and once used it is locked for 2 turns.
RETREAT_LOCK = 2
function retreatLocked(a) return a and a.retreatTurn and S.turn <= a.retreatTurn + RETREAT_LOCK end

local function tierOf(p)
  if p >= 75 then return 75 elseif p >= 50 then return 50 elseif p >= 25 then return 25 end
  return 0
end

local function surgeonFor(a)
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do if g.army == a.id and g.type == "Surgeon" then return g end end
end

-- p: terr, atk, def (army id or "garrison"), winner ("atk"/"def"), outcome ("Broken"/"Quartered"/"Destroyed"),
--    casA, casD (percent), duel ("none"/"atk"/"def")
-- A battle may have several armies a side (reinforcements, sieges). p.atk / p.def are the lead armies; p.atkAll and
-- p.defAll list every army that fought ("12,15"); p.cas gives each army's casualty % ("12:20,15:5"). The losing side
-- shares one outcome; each army takes its own casualties, loses its own supplies and retreats on its own.
function applyBattle(p)
  local ok = stageOk("Battle"); if ok ~= true then return ok end
  local n = tonumber(p.terr)
  if not n then return "Choose the territory where the battle was fought." end
  local A = armyById(p.atk)
  local D = (p.def ~= "garrison") and armyById(p.def) or nil
  if not A then return "Choose the attacking army." end
  if p.def ~= "garrison" and not D then return "Choose the defending army (or the garrison)." end
  if D and D.side == A.side then return "The two armies must be on different sides." end
  if p.winner ~= "atk" and p.winner ~= "def" then return "Choose who won." end
  -- every army on each side, lead first
  local function listOf(lead, ids, side)
    local out, seen = {}, {}
    if lead then out[1] = lead; seen[lead.id] = true end
    for id in string.gmatch(tostring(ids or ""), "%d+") do
      local x = armyById(tonumber(id))
      if x and not seen[x.id] and x.side == side then out[#out + 1] = x; seen[x.id] = true end
    end
    return out
  end
  local AS = listOf(A, p.atkAll, A.side)
  local DS = listOf(D, p.defAll, D and D.side or OTHER[A.side])
  local cas = {}
  for id, v in string.gmatch(tostring(p.cas or ""), "(%d+):(%d+)") do cas[tonumber(id)] = tonumber(v) end
  if cas[A.id] == nil then cas[A.id] = tonumber(p.casA) or 0 end
  if D and cas[D.id] == nil then cas[D.id] = tonumber(p.casD) or 0 end
  local atkWon = p.winner == "atk"
  local WS_ = atkWon and AS or DS          -- the winning armies (empty when the garrison alone won)
  local LS_ = atkWon and DS or AS          -- the losing armies (empty when the garrison alone lost)
  local W, Lz = WS_[1], LS_[1]
