  -- each losing army has its own outcome, and calls the retreat (or not) on its own.
  -- p.outs "12:Destroyed,15:Broken" and p.rets "15"; p.outcome / p.retreat are the lead loser's (and the default)
  local outs, rets = {}, {}
  for id, o in string.gmatch(tostring(p.outs or ""), "(%d+):(%a+)") do outs[tonumber(id)] = o end
  local anyRets = false
  for id in string.gmatch(tostring(p.rets or ""), "%d+") do rets[tonumber(id)] = true; anyRets = true end
  local function outOf(x)
    local o = outs[x.id] or p.outcome or "Broken"
    if o ~= "Broken" and o ~= "Quartered" and o ~= "Destroyed" then o = "Broken" end
    return o
  end
  local function retOf(x)
    if outOf(x) == "Destroyed" then return false end
    if anyRets or p.outs then return rets[x.id] == true end
    return p.retreat and x == Lz or false
  end
  for _, x in ipairs(LS_) do
    if retOf(x) and not S.settings.override and retreatLocked(x) then
      return x.name .. " called the retreat on turn " .. x.retreatTurn .. " and can't do so again until turn " .. (x.retreatTurn + RETREAT_LOCK + 1) .. "."
    end
  end
  local outcome = Lz and outOf(Lz) or (p.outcome or "Broken")     -- the lead loser's, for the battle record
  if Lz and retOf(Lz) then outcome = "Broken" end
  for _, x in ipairs(AS) do pcall(battleCancels, x) end
  for _, x in ipairs(DS) do pcall(battleCancels, x) end
  pcall(function() local up = table.unpack or unpack; eventAfterBattle(up(AS)); eventAfterBattle(up(DS)) end)
  for _, x in ipairs(LS_) do if retOf(x) then x.retreatTurn = S.turn end end
  -- both sides saw each other's armies on the table
  for _, x in ipairs(AS) do for _, y in ipairs(DS) do
    x.seenLeader[y.side] = true; x.seenUnits[y.side] = true; y.seenLeader[x.side] = true; y.seenUnits[x.side] = true
  end end
  -- names in the report: {k} is replaced by each viewer's name for that army
  local refs, idx = {}, {}
  for _, x in ipairs(AS) do refs[#refs + 1] = x; idx[x] = #refs end
  for _, x in ipairs(DS) do refs[#refs + 1] = x; idx[x] = #refs end
  local function R(a) return a and idx[a] and ("{" .. idx[a] .. "}") or "the garrison" end
  local function group(list, fallback)
    if #list == 0 then return fallback end
    local parts = {}
    for _, x in ipairs(list) do parts[#parts + 1] = R(x) end
    if #parts == 1 then return parts[1] end
    return table.concat(parts, ", ", 1, #parts - 1) .. " and " .. parts[#parts]
  end
  local aNames, dNames = group(AS, "the attackers"), group(DS, "the garrison")
  local winNames = atkWon and aNames or dNames
  local function fate(x) return retOf(x) and "called the retreat and counts as Broken" or ("was " .. outOf(x)) end
  local summary = { "Battle at " .. tname(n) .. ": " .. aNames .. " vs " .. dNames .. ". " .. winNames .. " won." }
  if #LS_ == 1 then summary[1] = summary[1] .. " The loser " .. fate(LS_[1]) .. "."
  else for _, x in ipairs(LS_) do table.insert(summary, R(x) .. " " .. fate(x) .. ".") end end
  -- casualties, army by army
  local leadersLost = {}
  for id in string.gmatch(tostring(p.leaders or ""), "(%d+)") do leadersLost[tonumber(id)] = true end
  for _, x in ipairs(refs) do
    local c = cas[x.id] or 0
    -- a winning army that lost every warband leader takes 25% more casualties (it can't fall below 25% strength)
    local won = false
    for _, w in ipairs(WS_) do if w == x then won = true end end
    if won and leadersLost[x.id] then
      c = c + 25
      table.insert(summary, R(x) .. " won but lost every warband leader: casualties +25% (now " .. c .. "%).")
    end
    local sg = surgeonFor(x)
    if sg then
      local r = d6(x.side, "Surgeon")
      local red = (r >= 5) and 10 or ((r >= 3) and 5 or 0)
      c = math.max(0, c - red)
      table.insert(summary, { side = x.side, text = R(x) .. "'s Surgeon rolled " .. r .. ": casualties -" .. red .. "% (now " .. c .. "%)." })
    end
    cas[x.id] = c
    local tier = tierOf(c)
    x.damage = math.min(75, (x.damage or 0) + tier)
    if tier > 0 then table.insert(summary, R(x) .. " is now at -" .. x.damage .. "%.") end
  end
  -- what the battle does to a conquest under way here (judged as it stood when the battle was fought)
  local terr = S.terr[n]
  local cq = terr.conq
  local WS = W and W.side or terr.owner                 -- the garrison wins for the territory's owner
  local LS = Lz and Lz.side or nil
  local doubleDrop = cq and LS and cq.by == LS and terr.owner == WS
  -- the winners share what they capture, the lead army first
  local function capture(amount)
    local got = 0
    for _, x in ipairs(WS_) do
      if amount - got <= 0 then break end
      local room = math.max(0, x.size - (x.carried or 0))
      local take = math.min(room, amount - got)
      if take > 0 then x.carried = (x.carried or 0) + take; got = got + take; table.insert(summary, R(x) .. " captured " .. take .. " RP.") end
    end
    return got
  end
  -- loot and retreat, loser by loser
  for _, x in ipairs(LS_) do
    local outcome = retOf(x) and "Broken" or outOf(x)
    local carried = x.carried or 0
    local lost
    if outcome == "Destroyed" then lost = carried
    elseif outcome == "Quartered" then lost = math.floor(carried * 0.75)
    else lost = math.floor(carried * 0.5) end
    if doubleDrop and outcome ~= "Destroyed" then
      lost = math.min(carried, lost * 2)
      table.insert(summary, R(x) .. " was beaten while conquering " .. WS .. " land and drops twice the supplies.")
    end
    local gain = math.floor((outcome == "Destroyed" and carried or lost) / 2)
    x.carried = carried - lost
    if lost > 0 then table.insert(summary, R(x) .. " lost " .. lost .. " RP of supplies.") end
    if gain > 0 then capture(gain) end
    x.result = outcome
  end
  for _, x in ipairs(LS_) do
    local outcome = retOf(x) and "Broken" or outOf(x)
    if outcome == "Destroyed" then
      table.insert(summary, R(x) .. " was destroyed.")
      removeArmy(x)
    else
      x.needsRetreat = true
      table.insert(summary, R(x) .. " must retreat in the Retreat stage.")
    end
  end
  for _, x in ipairs(WS_) do x.result = nil end
  -- a win in enemy territory that the winner isn't conquering: it pillages the territory at once, of all its RP
  if W and terr.owner == OTHER[W.side] and not (cq and cq.by == W.side) then
    local take = terr.stock or 0
    terr.stock = 0
    local got = 0
    for _, x in ipairs(WS_) do
      local room = math.max(0, x.size - (x.carried or 0))
      local t = math.min(room, take - got)
      if t > 0 then x.carried = (x.carried or 0) + t; got = got + t end
    end
    terr.noIncome = math.max(terr.noIncome or 0, 1)
    terr.noIncomeBy = { k = "pillage", s = W.side }
    table.insert(summary, winNames .. " pillaged " .. tname(n) .. " after the battle: " .. take .. " RP taken from its stores" ..
                          ((got < take) and (" (" .. got .. " carried off, the rest destroyed)") or "") .. ". It produces no supplies next turn.")
    pcall(function()   -- shown on the Chronicle map like any other pillage
      if backfillActEvents then backfillActEvents() end
      S.actEvents = S.actEvents or {}
      table.insert(S.actEvents, { t = S.turn, n = n, k = "pillage", s = W.side, d = pubName(W) .. " took " .. got .. " RP" })
    end)
  end
  -- the winner presses on
  if cq and terr.conq == cq and LS then
    if terr.owner == "Neutral" and W then
      table.insert(summary, winNames .. " won the contest for " .. tname(n) .. " and take" .. ((#WS_ > 1) and "" or "s") .. " it at once.")
      setOwner(n, W.side, "conquered")
    elseif cq.by == WS and terr.owner == LS then
      cq.cut = (cq.cut or 0) + 1
      cq.need = math.max(1, (cq.need or conquestNeed(n, WS)) - 1)
      table.insert(summary, WS .. "'s conquest of " .. tname(n) .. " now takes 1 turn less (" .. (cq.prog or 0) .. "/" .. cq.need .. ").")
    end
  end
  -- duel: the lead armies' Leaders
  if p.duel == "atk" or p.duel == "def" then
    local loser = (p.duel == "atk") and D or A
    if loser and armyById(loser.id) then
      loser.absent = 2
      table.insert(summary, "Heroic Duel: " .. R(loser) .. "'s Leader lost and is absent until the end of next turn.")
    end
  end
  for _, s2 in ipairs(summary) do
    if type(s2) == "table" then clogFor(s2.text, refs, nil, s2.side) else clogFor(s2, refs) end
  end
  local function fill(text, viewer)
    return (string.gsub(text, "{(%d+)}", function(k) return aname(refs[tonumber(k)], viewer) end))
  end
  local function fillFor(viewer)
    local parts = {}
    for _, s2 in ipairs(summary) do
      if type(s2) ~= "table" then table.insert(parts, s2)
      elseif not viewer or viewer == s2.side then table.insert(parts, s2.text) end
    end
    return capSentences(fill(table.concat(parts, " "), viewer))
  end
  local function avg(list, fb)
    if #list == 0 then return fb end
    local t, w = 0, 0
    for _, x in ipairs(list) do t = t + (cas[x.id] or 0) * x.size; w = w + x.size end
    return math.floor(t / w + 0.5)
  end
  local function ids(list) local o = {} for _, x in ipairs(list) do o[#o + 1] = x.id end return o end
  local function sizeOf(list, fb) local t = 0 for _, x in ipairs(list) do t = t + x.size end return (#list > 0) and t or fb end
  local casMap = {} for _, x in ipairs(refs) do casMap[tostring(x.id)] = cas[x.id] or 0 end
  local fought = {}
  for _, x in ipairs(refs) do
    local won = false
    for _, y in ipairs(WS_) do if y == x then won = true end end
    local fate = won and "won" or (retOf(x) and "Retreated" or outOf(x))
    fought[#fought + 1] = { id = x.id, s = x.side, nm = x.name, sz = x.size, cas = cas[x.id] or 0, fate = fate,
                            atk = idx[x] <= #AS and true or false, fg = (x.figure and x.figure ~= "") and x.figure or nil }
  end
  table.insert(S.battles, { t = S.turn, n = n, text = fillFor(nil), tGood = fillFor("Good"), tEvil = fillFor("Evil"),
                            win = W and W.side or (#DS == 0 and S.terr[n].owner) or WS,
                            atkSide = A.side, defSide = D and D.side or S.terr[n].owner, outcome = outcome,
                            atkId = A.id, defId = D and D.id or nil, atkIds = ids(AS), defIds = ids(DS), cas = casMap, armies = fought, garrison = (D == nil or siegeBattle(n)) and true or nil,
                            atkSize = sizeOf(AS, A.size), defSize = sizeOf(DS, 400),
                            casA = avg(AS, 0), casD = avg(DS, 0),
                            names = { All = { fill(aNames, nil), fill(dNames, nil) },
                                      Good = { fill(aNames, "Good"), fill(dNames, "Good") },
                                      Evil = { fill(aNames, "Evil"), fill(dNames, "Evil") } } })
  while #S.battles > 200 do table.remove(S.battles, 1) end
  return true
end

-- ===================================================================
-- persistence
-- ===================================================================
function exportState() return JSON.encode(S) end

-- A quick copy of the campaign for Undo and for dice rolls. Encoding the whole campaign every click is slow (it
-- grows with every turn), so the long history lists (log, turn snapshots, news, battles...) aren't copied: they
-- only ever grow at the end, so restoring just cuts them back to where they were.
SNAP_LISTS = { "log", "snaps", "news", "hist", "battles", "diceLog", "actEvents", "eventLog", "ruleLog" }
-- Battle reports and the saved-route library only change through their own few actions: everything else leaves
-- them out of its copy (and Undo leaves them as they are).
SNAP_LIGHT = { "reports", "routes" }
-- The copy is made in Lua, table by table: turning the campaign into text and back (JSON) is much slower in
-- Tabletop Simulator, especially reading it back.
function deepCopy(t, seen)
  if type(t) ~= "table" then return t end
  seen = seen or {}
  local c = seen[t]
  if c then return c end
  c = {}
  seen[t] = c
  for k, v in pairs(t) do
    if type(v) == "table" then c[k] = deepCopy(v, seen) else c[k] = v end
  end
  return c
end
function stateSnap(full)
  local skip, marks, light = {}, {}, {}
  for _, k in ipairs(SNAP_LISTS) do
    local l = S[k]
    if type(l) == "table" then
      skip[k] = true
      local last = l[#l]
      marks[k] = { n = #l, last = last, key = (last ~= nil) and JSON.encode(last) or nil }
    end
  end
  if not full then
    for _, k in ipairs(SNAP_LIGHT) do
      if S[k] ~= nil then skip[k] = true; table.insert(light, k) end
    end
  end
  local tbl, seen = {}, {}
  for k, v in pairs(S) do
    if not skip[k] then tbl[k] = deepCopy(v, seen) end
  end
  return { tbl = tbl, marks = marks, light = light }
end
function stateRestore(snap)
  if type(snap) == "string" then return JSON.decode(snap) end
  local data
  if snap.tbl then data = deepCopy(snap.tbl)   -- a fresh copy, so the snapshot can be restored again
  else data = JSON.decode(snap.js) end         -- an older copy, made before this version
  if type(data) ~= "table" then return data end
  for k, m in pairs(snap.marks or {}) do
    local cur = (S and S[k]) or {}
    local keep = {}
    if m.n > 0 then
      local found
      for i = #cur, math.max(1, #cur - 400), -1 do
        if cur[i] == m.last or (m.key and JSON.encode(cur[i]) == m.key) then found = i; break end
      end
      if found then for i = 1, found do keep[i] = cur[i] end else keep = cur end
    end
    data[k] = keep
  end
  for _, k in ipairs(snap.light or {}) do data[k] = S and S[k] end
  return data
end

function importState(txt)
  local ok, data = pcall(JSON.decode, txt)
  if not ok or type(data) ~= "table" or not data.terr then return "That doesn't look like a War Council export." end
  S = data
  fixState()
  syncAllOwners()
  clog("Campaign imported.")
  return true
end

-- JSON round-trips can turn numeric keys into strings; normalise here.
function fixState()
  useTheatreMap(S.settings and S.settings.theatre or "Eriador")
  local def = newState()
  if S.mapVer == nil then S.mapVer = 1 end   -- saved before the cropped map
  for k, v in pairs(def) do if S[k] == nil then S[k] = v end end
  for k, v in pairs(def.settings) do if S.settings[k] == nil then S.settings[k] = v end end
  -- map highlights now default to On; switch existing campaigns over once
  if not S.settings.hintsV2 then
    if S.settings.mapHints == "auto" then S.settings.mapHints = "on" end
    S.settings.hintsV2 = true
  end
  local terr = {}
  for k, t in pairs(S.terr) do terr[tonumber(k)] = t end
  S.terr = terr
  for n = 1, #NAMES do
    S.terr[n] = S.terr[n] or def.terr[n]
    S.terr[n].b = S.terr[n].b or {}
    S.terr[n].q = S.terr[n].q or {}
    S.terr[n].bslot = S.terr[n].bslot or {}
  end
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
    a.seenLeader = a.seenLeader or {}
    a.seenUnits = a.seenUnits or {}
    a.wounds = a.wounds or {}
  end
  -- campaigns from before public army numbers: number every army ever seen, in the order raised
  if not S.armyNo then
    S.armyNo, S.armyNoById = { Good = 0, Evil = 0 }, {}
    local seen, all = {}, {}
    local function add(id, side) id = tonumber(id); if id and side and not seen[id] then seen[id] = true; table.insert(all, { id = id, side = side }) end end
    for _, sn in ipairs(S.snaps or {}) do for _, x in ipairs(sn.a or {}) do add(x.id, x.s) end end
    for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do add(a.id, a.side) end
    table.sort(all, function(p, q) return p.id < q.id end)
    for _, e in ipairs(all) do
      S.armyNo[e.side] = (S.armyNo[e.side] or 0) + 1
      S.armyNoById[tostring(e.id)] = S.armyNo[e.side]
    end
    for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do a.no = S.armyNoById[tostring(a.id)] end
  end
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if not a.no then numberArmy(a) end end
  S.slotOv = S.slotOv or {}
  local ov = {}
  for k, v in pairs(S.slotOv) do ov[tonumber(k)] = v end
  S.slotOv = ov
end

-- ===================================================================
-- battles with more than two armies: who deploys, and when the rest arrive
-- ===================================================================
REINFORCE_EVERY, REINFORCE_ROLL, REINFORCE_MIN = 4, 4, 2
function reinforcementPlan(n)
  local t = S.terr[n]
  local siege = siegeBattle(n)
  local count = { Good = #armiesIn(n, "Good"), Evil = #armiesIn(n, "Evil") }
  local total = count.Good + count.Evil
  if total <= 2 then return nil end
  local plan = { siege = siege, count = count, sides = {} }
  local cap = siege and 2 or 99
  for _, sd in ipairs(SIDES) do
    plan.sides[sd] = { field = math.min(count[sd], cap), extra = math.max(0, math.min(count[sd], cap) - math.min(count[OTHER[sd]], cap)) }
  end
  plan.defender = siege and t.owner or nil
  return plan
end
function reinforcementLines(n, lines)
  local P = reinforcementPlan(n)
  if not P then return end
  local c = P.count
  if P.siege then
    table.insert(lines, "Siege with several armies: at most 2 armies a side take part (Good " .. math.min(c.Good, 2) .. ", Evil " .. math.min(c.Evil, 2) .. ")." ..
      ((c.Good > 2 or c.Evil > 2) and " Any others stay out of this battle." or ""))
  end
  local g, e = P.sides.Good, P.sides.Evil
  if g.extra == 0 and e.extra == 0 then
    table.insert(lines, "Several armies: both sides have the same number here, so every army deploys at the start.")
    return
  end
  local sd = (g.extra > 0) and "Good" or "Evil"
  local x = P.sides[sd]
  local turns = {}
  for i = 1, x.extra do turns[#turns + 1] = REINFORCE_EVERY * i end
  local defender = P.siege and sd == P.defender
  if defender then
    table.insert(lines, sd .. " defends the siege with a reinforcing army. Choose one: deploy half of it inside the fort with the rest arriving on turn 6, " ..
      "or have it arrive on turn " .. REINFORCE_EVERY .. " from the left or right board edge. Arriving warbands roll a D6 each: " .. REINFORCE_ROLL ..
      "+ to arrive, the roll needed drops by 1 after every failure (to a minimum of " .. REINFORCE_MIN .. "+).")
  else
    table.insert(lines, sd .. " has " .. x.extra .. " more arm" .. (x.extra == 1 and "y" or "ies") .. " here: it deploys as many as " .. OTHER[sd] ..
      " and the rest reinforce" .. (x.extra > 1 and " in an order " .. sd .. " chooses" or "") .. ", from turn " .. table.concat(turns, ", turn ") ..
      ". From that turn, roll a D6 for each warband: " .. REINFORCE_ROLL .. "+ arrives at " .. sd .. "'s deployment edge; every failure lowers the roll needed by 1 (minimum " .. REINFORCE_MIN .. "+).")
  end
end

--[[ ===================================================================
     WAR COUNCIL - Supply Wagons: naming, cargo, capture and supply routes
     =================================================================== ]]

local WAGON = "Supply Wagon"
local ROMAN = { "", "II", "III", "IV", "V", "VI", "VII", "VIII", "IX", "X", "XI", "XII" }

local function deepcopy(t)
  if type(t) ~= "table" then return t end
  local o = {}
  for k, v in pairs(t) do o[k] = deepcopy(v) end
  return o
end

-- "Fornost Supply Wagon", then "II Fornost Supply Wagon", "III ..." (lowest free numeral).
function wagonName(n)
  local base = NAMES[n] .. " Supply Wagon"
  local used = {}
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do if g.name then used[string.lower(g.name)] = true end end
  for i = 1, #ROMAN do
    local nm = (ROMAN[i] == "") and base or (ROMAN[i] .. " " .. base)
    if not used[string.lower(nm)] then return nm end
  end
  return base .. " " .. S.nextId
end

function isWagon(g) return g and g.type == WAGON end

function wagonsIn(n, side)
  local out = {}
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do
    if isWagon(g) and g.loc == n and (side == nil or g.side == side) then table.insert(out, g) end
  end
  return out
end

function wagonLabel(g) return g.name or (g.side .. " Supply Wagon #" .. g.id) end

-- ===================================================================
-- capture
-- ===================================================================
-- A captured wagon changes hands with everything it carries. Its old supply route goes with its old owners,
-- and it can't move again this turn.
local function captureWagon(g, a)
  local carried = g.carried or 0
  local was = g.side
  local label = wagonLabel(g)
  g.side = a.side
  g.route = nil
  g.moved = true
  g.mvUsed = (AGENTS[g.type] and AGENTS[g.type].move) or 0
  g.acted = true
  clogFor(label .. " was captured by {1} at " .. tname(g.loc) .. ((carried > 0) and (" with the " .. carried .. " RP it carried") or "") ..
          ". It now serves " .. a.side .. ".", { a }, "captures")
  pieceHook("captured", "agent", g, was)
  return true
end

-- A wagon in the same territory as an enemy army is captured.
-- A wagon is safe while one of its own side's armies is with it.
function wagonProtected(g) return g.loc and #armiesIn(g.loc, g.side) > 0 end
function wagonCaptureCheck(g)
  if not isWagon(g) or not g.loc then return false end
  if wagonProtected(g) then return false end
  local best
  for _, a in ipairs(armiesIn(g.loc, OTHER[g.side])) do
    if not best or (a.size - (a.carried or 0)) > (best.size - (best.carried or 0)) then best = a end
  end
  if best then return captureWagon(g, best) end
  return false
end

-- An army arriving somewhere captures every enemy wagon there.
function armyArrivedCaptures(a)
  for _, g in ipairs(wagonsIn(a.loc, OTHER[a.side])) do if not wagonProtected(g) then captureWagon(g, a) end end
end

-- ===================================================================
-- cargo
-- ===================================================================
-- Floating "+100" / "-100" over the wagon token: changes are queued per wagon with the territory they happened in,
-- and shown when the token is (or arrives) there.
CARGO_FX = CARGO_FX or {}
function cargoFx(g, delta)
  if QUIET or not g or not delta or delta == 0 then return end
  CARGO_FX[g.id] = CARGO_FX[g.id] or {}
  table.insert(CARGO_FX[g.id], { n = g.loc, d = delta })
  if delta < 0 and soundEvent then soundEvent("wagon") end
end
function wagonAutoUnload(g)
  if g.autoUnload and S.terr[g.loc].owner == g.side and (g.carried or 0) > 0 then
    S.terr[g.loc].stock = S.terr[g.loc].stock + g.carried
    clog(wagonLabel(g) .. " unloaded " .. g.carried .. " RP at " .. tname(g.loc) .. ".", g.side)
    cargoFx(g, -g.carried)
    g.carried = 0
  end
end

-- Called after any wagon move (manual, dropped token or route).
function wagonArrive(g)
  if wagonCaptureCheck(g) then return end
  wagonAutoUnload(g)
end

-- amount: nil = as much as possible
local function doLoad(g, amount)
  local t = S.terr[g.loc]
  if t.owner ~= g.side then return "You can only load from your own stockpile." end
  if droughtAt and droughtAt(g.loc) and not S.settings.override then return "Great Drought: " .. tname(g.loc) .. " won't give up its stores this turn." end
  local room = WAGON_CAP - (g.carried or 0)
  local amt = math.min(room, t.stock)
  if amount then amt = math.min(amt, amount) end
  if amt <= 0 then return (room <= 0) and "The wagon is full." or "The stockpile here is empty." end
  t.stock = t.stock - amt
  g.carried = (g.carried or 0) + amt
  clog(wagonLabel(g) .. " loaded " .. amt .. " RP at " .. tname(g.loc) .. ".", g.side)
  cargoFx(g, amt)
  return true
end

local function doUnload(g, amount)
  local t = S.terr[g.loc]
  if t.owner ~= g.side then return "You can only unload into your own stockpile." end
  local amt = g.carried or 0
  if amount then amt = math.min(amt, amount) end
  if amt <= 0 then return "The wagon is empty." end
  t.stock = t.stock + amt
  g.carried = (g.carried or 0) - amt
  addTotal("delivered", g.side, amt)
  clog(wagonLabel(g) .. " unloaded " .. amt .. " RP at " .. tname(g.loc) .. ".", g.side)
  cargoFx(g, -amt)
  return true
end

function wagonLoadAll(g, amount)
  local ok = stageOk("Logistics"); if ok ~= true then return ok end
  if amount and amount <= 0 then amount = nil end
  local r = doLoad(g, amount)
  pieceHook("cargo", "agent", g)
  return r
end

function wagonUnloadAll(g, amount)
  local ok = stageOk("Logistics"); if ok ~= true then return ok end
  if amount and amount <= 0 then amount = nil end
  local r = doUnload(g, amount)
  pieceHook("cargo", "agent", g)
  return r
end

-- ===================================================================
-- routes
--   g.route = { start = n, on = false, idx = 1,
--     legs = { { depart = { k, a }, steps = { n, ... }, acts = { { k = "none"|"load"|"unload", a = amount or nil }, ... } }, ... } }
--   depart happens at the turn's starting territory before the wagon leaves (or while it stays put); acts[i] happens at stop i.
--   Each leg is one turn's movement. Leaving a territory costs 1 (none with Roads, up to 3 times a turn); a leg may spend up to 3.
-- ===================================================================
local function stepCost(from, to, free, side) return stepCostFor(from, to, true, free == nil and ROAD_FREE_OTHER or free, side) end
-- The side whose wagon follows this route (Land Mapping helps only its owner).
function routeSide(route)
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents or {}) do if g.route == route then return g.side end end
  return nil
end

local function noAct() return { k = "none" } end

-- Upgrade older routes (one action per turn) to per-stop actions.
function fixLeg(leg)
  leg.steps = leg.steps or {}
  if not leg.acts then
    leg.acts = {}
    for i = 1, #leg.steps do leg.acts[i] = noAct() end
    if leg.act and leg.act ~= "none" then
      if #leg.steps > 0 then leg.acts[#leg.steps] = { k = leg.act } else leg.hold = { k = leg.act } end
    end
  end
  for i = 1, #leg.steps do leg.acts[i] = leg.acts[i] or noAct() end
  for i = #leg.acts, #leg.steps + 1, -1 do leg.acts[i] = nil end
  leg.depart = leg.depart or leg.hold or noAct()
  leg.hold = nil
  leg.act = nil
  return leg
end

function isArmyRoute(r) return r and r.mode == "army" end

function fixRoute(r)
  if not r or isArmyRoute(r) then return end
  for _, leg in ipairs(r.legs) do fixLeg(leg) end
end

function actText(a)
  if not a or a.k == "none" then return "" end
  return (a.k == "load" and "load " or "unload ") .. (a.a and tostring(a.a) or "all")
end

function legSummary(r, k)
  local leg = r.legs[k]
  local parts = { NAMES[legStart(r, k)] }
  fixLeg(leg)
  local dt = actText(leg.depart)
  if #leg.steps == 0 then
    return parts[1] .. "  (stay" .. (dt ~= "" and (", " .. dt) or "") .. ")"
  end
  if dt ~= "" then parts[1] = parts[1] .. " (" .. dt .. ")" end
  for i, n in ipairs(leg.steps) do
    local t = actText(leg.acts[i])
    table.insert(parts, NAMES[n] .. (t ~= "" and (" (" .. t .. ")") or ""))
  end
  return table.concat(parts, " > ")
end

function legStart(route, k)
  if k <= 1 then return route.start end
  local prev = route.legs[k - 1]
  local s = legStart(route, k - 1)
  if prev and #prev.steps > 0 then return prev.steps[#prev.steps] end
  return s
end

function legEnd(route, k)
  local leg = route.legs[k]
  if leg and #leg.steps > 0 then return leg.steps[#leg.steps] end
  return legStart(route, k)
end

-- Checks a leg against the map as it is now. Returns true or an error string.
function validateLeg(route, k)
  local here = legStart(route, k)
  local spent, free, side = 0, ROAD_FREE_OTHER, routeSide(route)
  for i, n in ipairs(route.legs[k].steps) do
    if not isAdjacent(here, n) then return "Turn " .. k .. ", move " .. i .. ": " .. NAMES[n] .. " doesn't border " .. NAMES[here] .. "." end
    local c; c, free = stepCost(here, n, free, side)
    spent = spent + c
    if spent > AGENTS[WAGON].move + 1e-6 then return "Turn " .. k .. " moves too far (" .. AGENTS[WAGON].move .. " movement a turn; leaving Roads is free up to 3 times, entering or leaving Neutral land costs 2)." end
    here = n
  end
  return true
end

function routeClosed(route)
  return #route.legs > 0 and legEnd(route, #route.legs) == route.start
end

function routeProblems(route)
  local out = {}
  if isArmyRoute(route) then return armyRouteProblems(route) end
  for k = 1, #route.legs do
    local r = validateLeg(route, k)
    if r ~= true then table.insert(out, r) end
  end
  if #route.legs == 0 then table.insert(out, "Add at least one turn.")
  elseif not routeClosed(route) then
    table.insert(out, "The route ends at " .. NAMES[legEnd(route, #route.legs)] .. " but starts at " .. NAMES[route.start] ..
                      ". It must end where it starts to repeat.")
  end
  return out
end

-- Territories offered in box s of leg k (empty if the wagon has no movement left).
function stepOptions(route, k, s)
  local leg = route.legs[k]
  local here = legStart(route, k)
  local spent, free, side = 0, ROAD_FREE_OTHER, routeSide(route)
  for i = 1, s - 1 do
    local n = leg.steps[i]
    if not n then return {} end
    local c; c, free = stepCost(here, n, free, side)
    spent = spent + c
    here = n
  end
  local out = {}
  for _, v in ipairs(ADJ[here] or {}) do if spent + stepCost(here, v, free, side) <= AGENTS[WAGON].move + 1e-6 then out[#out + 1] = v end end
  return out
end

local function newLeg() return { steps = {}, acts = {}, depart = noAct() } end

function newRoute(g)
  g.route = { start = g.loc, legs = { newLeg() }, on = false, idx = 1 }
  return true
end

-- Changing (or clearing) stop s removes every later stop of that turn; later turns are re-checked.
function setRouteStep(g, k, s, n)
  local r = g.route
  if not r or not r.legs[k] then return "No such turn in the route." end
  local leg = fixLeg(r.legs[k])
  if n then
    local okN = false
    for _, x in ipairs(stepOptions(r, k, s)) do if x == n then okN = true end end
    if not okN then return NAMES[n] .. " can't be reached at that point." end
  end
  local keepAct = leg.acts[s]
  for i = #leg.steps, s, -1 do table.remove(leg.steps, i); table.remove(leg.acts, i) end
  if n then
    table.insert(leg.steps, n)
    table.insert(leg.acts, keepAct or noAct())
  end
  r.on = false
  return true
end

function addRouteStop(g, k, n)
  local r = g.route
  if not r or not r.legs[k] then return "No such turn in the route." end
  return setRouteStep(g, k, #fixLeg(r.legs[k]).steps + 1, n)
end

-- i = 0 sets the action at the turn's starting territory (before leaving, or while staying put).
function setStopAct(g, k, i, kind, amount)
  local r = g.route
  if not r or not r.legs[k] then return "No such turn in the route." end
  local leg = fixLeg(r.legs[k])
  local a = { k = kind or "none", a = amount }
  if a.k == "none" then a.a = nil end
  if i == 0 then leg.depart = a else
    if not leg.steps[i] then return "No such stop." end
    leg.acts[i] = a
  end
  return true
end

function setStopAmount(g, k, i, amount)
  local r = g.route
  if not r or not r.legs[k] then return "No such turn in the route." end
  local leg = fixLeg(r.legs[k])
  local a = (i == 0) and leg.depart or leg.acts[i]
  if not a then return "No such stop." end
  if amount and amount <= 0 then amount = nil end
  a.a = amount
  return true
end

function addRouteLeg(g)
  if not g.route then newRoute(g) end
  table.insert(g.route.legs, newLeg())
  g.route.on = false
  return true
end

function removeRouteLeg(g)
  local r = g.route
  if not r or #r.legs == 0 then return "Nothing to remove." end
  table.remove(r.legs)
  if r.idx > #r.legs then r.idx = 1 end
  r.on = false
  return true
end


-- Which turn of its route the wagon is on, judged by where it stands.
local function syncIdx(g)
  local r = g.route
  if isArmyRoute(r) then return true end
  if legStart(r, r.idx) == g.loc then return true end
  for k = 1, #r.legs do
    if legStart(r, k) == g.loc then r.idx = k; return true end
  end
  return false
end

function toggleRoute(g)
  local r = g.route
  if not r then return "This wagon has no route." end
  fixRoute(r)
  if r.on then r.on = false; clog(wagonLabel(g) .. " route paused.", g.side); return true end
  local probs = routeProblems(r)
  if #probs > 0 then return probs[1] end
  if not syncIdx(g) then return "The wagon is at " .. NAMES[g.loc] .. ", which isn't the start of any turn on this route." end
  r.on = true
  if isArmyRoute(r) then
    clog(wagonLabel(g) .. " is now supplying " .. armyById(r.army).name .. " from " .. tname(r.depot) .. ".", g.side)
  else
    clog(wagonLabel(g) .. " route active (next: turn " .. r.idx .. " of " .. #r.legs .. ").", g.side)
  end
  return true
end

function setAllRoutes(side, on)
  local n = 0
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do
    if isWagon(g) and g.side == side and g.route then
      if on then
        if not g.route.on and toggleRoute(g) == true then n = n + 1 end
      elseif g.route.on then
        g.route.on = false; n = n + 1
      end
    end
  end
  return true, n
end

-- Runs when the Logistics stage begins.
function runRoutes()
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do
    local r = isWagon(g) and g.route
    if r and r.on and not g.moved and isArmyRoute(r) then
      runArmySupply(g, r)
    elseif r and r.on and not g.moved then
      local ok = #routeProblems(r) == 0 and syncIdx(g)
      if not ok then
        r.on = false
        clog(wagonLabel(g) .. " couldn't follow its route from " .. tname(g.loc) .. " - route paused.", g.side, "routes")
      else
        local k = r.idx
        local leg = r.legs[k]
        local v = validateLeg(r, k)
        if v ~= true then
          r.on = false
          clog(wagonLabel(g) .. ": " .. v .. " Route paused.", g.side, "routes")
        else
          fixLeg(leg)
          local from = g.loc
          local caught = false
          local lastAct = leg.depart
          local function perform(a)
            if not a or a.k == "none" then return end
            local res = (a.k == "load") and doLoad(g, a.a) or doUnload(g, a.a)
            if res ~= true then
              clog(wagonLabel(g) .. " couldn't " .. a.k .. " at " .. tname(g.loc) .. ": " .. res, g.side, "routes")
            end
          end
          perform(leg.depart)
          local visited = {}
          local held = false
          if #leg.steps > 0 then
            local leave = leaveBlock(g.loc, g, false)
            for i, n in ipairs(leg.steps) do
              if i > 1 and leave == "slow" then held = true; break end   -- leaving blocked roads took the whole move
              if snowedFor(n, g.side) then held = true; break end       -- snowed in: nobody gets through (but Land Mapping)
              local was = g.loc
              g.loc = n
              table.insert(visited, n)
              if i == 1 and leave then afterLeave(was, leave) end
              if wagonCaptureCheck(g) then caught = true; break end
              perform(leg.acts[i])
              lastAct = leg.acts[i]
              if S.terr[n].block and i < #leg.steps and not mappedFor(n, g.side) then hitBlock(n, g.side, wagonLabel(g)); held = true; break end
            end
          end
          if held then
            r.on = false
            clog(wagonLabel(g) .. " was held up at " .. tname(g.loc) .. " and couldn't finish its route - route paused.", g.side, "routes")
          end
          g.moved = true
          if not caught then
            if not lastAct or lastAct.k == "none" then wagonAutoUnload(g) end
          end
          if g.loc ~= from then
            clog(wagonLabel(g) .. " followed its route " .. tname(from) .. " -> " .. tname(g.loc) .. ".", g.side)
            pieceHook("routed", "agent", g, visited)
          else
            pieceHook("cargo", "agent", g)
          end
          if not caught then
            r.idx = (k % #r.legs) + 1
          end
        end
      end
    end
  end
end

-- ===================================================================
-- army supply runs
--   g.route = { mode = "army", army = id, depot = n, on = false }
--   Each Logistics stage the wagon spends its 3 moves shuttling: empty -> to the depot to load,
--   loaded -> to the army to hand over as much as it can carry. Then it repeats, turn after turn.
-- ===================================================================
function newArmyRoute(g, armyId, depot)
  local a = armyById(armyId)
  if not a or a.side ~= g.side then return "Choose one of your armies." end
  if not depot or not S.terr[depot] or S.terr[depot].owner ~= g.side then return "Choose one of your territories to load supplies from." end
  local was = g.route and g.route.on
  g.route = { mode = "army", army = a.id, depot = depot, on = false }
  if was then return toggleRoute(g) end
  return true
end

function armyRouteProblems(r)
  local out = {}
  local a = armyById(r.army)
  if not a then table.insert(out, "The army it was supplying is gone.") end
  if not r.depot or not S.terr[r.depot] then table.insert(out, "Choose a depot to load from.")
  else
    local g
    for _, x in ipairs(S.agents) do if x.route == r then g = x end end
    if g and S.terr[r.depot].owner ~= g.side then table.insert(out, NAMES[r.depot] .. " is no longer yours, so the wagon can't load there.") end
  end
  return out
end

-- Cheapest way from a to b for side: Roads make leaving free; enemy land costs more and enemy armies are avoided.
function supplyPath(a, b, side)
  if a == b then return {} end
  local dist, prev, done = { [a] = 0 }, {}, {}
  while true do
    local u, best = nil, nil
    for n, d in pairs(dist) do if not done[n] and (not best or d < best) then u, best = n, d end end
    if not u or u == b then break end
    done[u] = true
    for _, v in ipairs(ADJ[u] or {}) do
      local c = stepCost(u, v, nil, side) + (snowedFor(v, side) and 10000 or 0)
      local o = S.terr[v].owner
      if o ~= side and o ~= "Neutral" then c = c + 4 end
      if v ~= b and #armiesIn(v, OTHER[side]) > 0 then c = c + 100 end
      if not dist[v] or best + c < dist[v] then dist[v] = best + c; prev[v] = u end
    end
  end
  if not prev[b] then return nil end
  local path, cur = {}, b
  while cur ~= a do table.insert(path, 1, cur); cur = prev[cur] end
  return path
end

-- What the wagon will do next, for the panel.
function armyRouteStatus(g)
  local r = g.route
  local a = armyById(r.army)
  if not a then return "The army is gone." end
  if (g.carried or 0) > 0 then
    if g.loc == a.loc then return "With " .. a.name .. ", waiting until it has room for more." end
    return "Taking " .. g.carried .. " RP to " .. a.name .. " at " .. NAMES[a.loc] .. "."
  end
  if g.loc == r.depot then return "Loading at " .. NAMES[r.depot] .. " (stockpile " .. (S.terr[r.depot].stock or 0) .. " RP)." end
  return "Heading back to " .. NAMES[r.depot] .. " to load."
end

function runArmySupply(g, r)
  local probs = armyRouteProblems(r)
  if #probs > 0 then
    r.on = false
    clog(wagonLabel(g) .. ": " .. probs[1] .. " Supply run paused.", g.side, "routes")
    return
  end
  local a = armyById(r.army)
  local from = g.loc
  local visited, caught = {}, false
  local function act()
    if (g.carried or 0) > 0 and g.loc == a.loc then
      local room = math.max(0, a.size - (a.carried or 0))
      local amt = math.min(room, g.carried)
      if amt > 0 then
        a.carried = (a.carried or 0) + amt
        g.carried = g.carried - amt
        addTotal("delivered", g.side, amt)
        cargoFx(g, -amt)
        clog(wagonLabel(g) .. " delivered " .. amt .. " RP of supplies to " .. a.name .. " at " .. tname(g.loc) .. ".", g.side, "routes")
      end
    end
    if (g.carried or 0) <= 0 and g.loc == r.depot and (S.terr[r.depot].stock or 0) > 0 then doLoad(g) end
  end
  act()
  local spent, budget, free = 0, AGENTS[WAGON].move, ROAD_FREE_OTHER
  local leave = leaveBlock(g.loc, g, false)
  for _ = 1, 12 do
    local target = ((g.carried or 0) > 0) and a.loc or r.depot
    if g.loc == target then break end
    local path = supplyPath(g.loc, target, g.side)
    if not path or #path == 0 or snowedFor(path[1], g.side) then break end
    local c, f2 = stepCost(g.loc, path[1], free, g.side)
    if leave == "slow" then c = budget end   -- blocked roads: leaving takes the whole move
    if spent + c > budget + 1e-6 then break end
    spent, free = spent + c, f2
    local was = g.loc
    g.loc = path[1]
    table.insert(visited, g.loc)
    if leave then afterLeave(was, leave); leave = nil end
    if wagonCaptureCheck(g) then caught = true; break end
    act()
    if S.terr[g.loc].block and not mappedFor(g.loc, g.side) then hitBlock(g.loc, g.side, wagonLabel(g)); break end   -- blocked roads end its move
  end
  g.moved = true
  if #visited > 0 then
    clog(wagonLabel(g) .. " travelled " .. tname(from) .. " -> " .. tname(g.loc) .. " on its supply run for " .. a.name .. ".", g.side)
    pieceHook("routed", "agent", g, visited)
  else
    pieceHook("cargo", "agent", g)
  end
end

-- ===================================================================
-- route library
-- ===================================================================
function saveRoute(g, name)
  if not g.route then return "This wagon has no route." end
  if isArmyRoute(g.route) then return "Army supply runs can't be saved to the library." end
  name = (name and name ~= "") and name or (wagonLabel(g) .. " route")
  for i, rt in ipairs(S.routes) do
    if string.lower(rt.name) == string.lower(name) then table.remove(S.routes, i) break end
  end
  table.insert(S.routes, { name = name, start = g.route.start, legs = deepcopy(g.route.legs) })
  return true
end

function applyRoute(g, idx)
  local rt = S.routes[idx]
  if not rt then return "Choose a saved route." end
  g.route = { start = rt.start, legs = deepcopy(rt.legs), on = false, idx = 1 }
  fixRoute(g.route)
  if not syncIdx(g) then
    return true, "Route copied, but the wagon is at " .. NAMES[g.loc] .. " - move it to " .. NAMES[rt.start] .. " (or another stop) before switching it on."
  end
  return true, "Route copied. Switch it on when ready."
end

function deleteRoute(idx)
  if not S.routes[idx] then return "Choose a saved route." end
  table.remove(S.routes, idx)
  return true
end

--[[ ===================================================================
     WAR COUNCIL - territory actions taken by armies in the Logistics stage
       Pillage, Sabotage, Scorched Earth (public, shown on the map)
       Road Blockage, Dig In, Sow Discontent (secret until revealed)
     Ordering one ends the army's movement. Orders complete at the end of the turn;
     a battle involving the army first cancels it.
     =================================================================== ]]

ACTIONS = {
  pillage  = { name = "Pillage", verb = "pillaging", secret = false,
               rules = "Take half the supplies stored here and stop the territory producing supplies for 1 turn. Enemy territory only." },
  sabotage = { name = "Sabotage", verb = "sabotaging", secret = false,
               rules = "Destroy a building that is being constructed here. Enemy territory only." },
  scorch   = { name = "Scorched Earth", verb = "scorching", secret = false,
               rules = "Destroy every building here and stop the territory producing supplies for 4 turns. Your own territory only." },
  block    = { name = "Road Blockage", verb = "blocking the roads", secret = true,
               rules = "Armies and wagons that travel into this territory stop there, and leaving takes their entire next move; the blockage then clears. Your own army that built it leaves freely the first time." },
  dig      = { name = "Dig In", verb = "digging in", secret = true,
               rules = "Until this army leaves the territory, any battle here must use the Dig-In Defense scenario (rulebook p.28)." },
  sow      = { name = "Sow Discontent", verb = "sowing discontent", secret = true,
               rules = "This Neutral territory takes 1 extra turn to conquer (for anyone). It goes away after the first conquest." },
}
PUBLIC_ACTIONS = { "pillage", "sabotage", "scorch" }
SECRET_ACTIONS = { "block", "dig", "sow" }
SCORCH_TURNS = 4

do  -- keep helpers out of the main chunk (Lua allows 200 locals there)

local function seatColor(side)
  return (side == "Good" and S.settings.goodColor) or (side == "Evil" and S.settings.evilColor) or nil
end

-- A private note to one side: their seat hears it, and it goes in their Secret orders panel.
function secretNote(side, msg)
  S.secretLog = S.secretLog or {}
  S.secretLog[side] = S.secretLog[side] or {}
  table.insert(S.secretLog[side], { t = S.turn, m = msg })
  while #S.secretLog[side] > 30 do table.remove(S.secretLog[side], 1) end
  local c = seatColor(side)
  if c then pcall(function() broadcastToColor("Secret: " .. msg, c, { 0.75, 0.7, 0.95 }) end) end
end

local function enemyHere(a)
  return #armiesIn(a.loc, OTHER[a.side]) > 0
end

-- Can army a start action k here now? true or a reason.
function actionAllowed(a, k)
  local def = ACTIONS[k]
  if not def then return "Unknown action." end
  if not S.started then return "Start the campaign first." end
  if STAGES[S.stage] ~= "Logistics" and not S.settings.override then return "Actions are taken in the Logistics stage." end
  if a.status == "building" then return a.name .. " is still being raised." end
  if a.needsRetreat then return a.name .. " must retreat first." end
  if a.order then return a.name .. " is already " .. ACTIONS[a.order.k].verb .. " this turn." end
  if enemyHere(a) and not S.settings.override then return "An enemy army is here: there will be a battle first." end
  local t = S.terr[a.loc]
  if k == "pillage" then
    if t.owner ~= OTHER[a.side] then return "Only enemy territory can be pillaged." end
  elseif k == "sabotage" then
    if t.owner == a.side then return "You can't sabotage your own construction." end
    if #(t.q or {}) == 0 then return "Nothing is being built here." end
  elseif k == "scorch" then
    if t.owner ~= a.side then return "Scorched Earth can only be done in your own territory." end
  elseif k == "block" then
    if t.block then return "The roads here are already blocked." end
  elseif k == "dig" then
    if a.dug == a.loc then return a.name .. " is already dug in here." end
  elseif k == "sow" then
    if t.owner ~= "Neutral" then return "Discontent can only be sown in Neutral territory." end
    if t.sow then return "The people here are already discontent." end
  end
  return true
end

function orderAction(a, k, target)
  local ok = actionAllowed(a, k)
  if ok ~= true then return ok end
  local def = ACTIONS[k]
  a.order = { k = k, n = a.loc, t = S.turn, target = target, wasMoved = a.moved and true or false, secret = def.secret, revealed = false }
  a.moved = true
  if def.secret then
    secretNote(a.side, a.name .. " is " .. def.verb .. " at " .. tname(a.loc) .. ". It completes at the end of the turn.")
  else
    local extra = ""
    if k == "sabotage" and target and BUILDING_BY_KEY[target] then extra = " (the " .. BUILDING_BY_KEY[target].name .. " under construction)" end
    clog(pubName(a) .. " began " .. def.verb .. " " .. tname(a.loc) .. extra .. ". It completes at the end of the turn.", nil, "territories")
  end
  return true
end

local function orderLabel(a)
  local o = a.order
  return a.name .. "'s " .. ACTIONS[o.k].name .. " at " .. tname(o.n)
end

-- why: shown in the log; restore: give the army its movement back (cancelled by its own side)
function cancelOrder(a, why, restore)
  local o = a.order
  if not o then return "Nothing to cancel." end
  a.order = nil
  if restore and not o.wasMoved then a.moved = false end
  local msg = orderLabel({ name = a.name, order = o }) .. " was cancelled" .. (why and (" - " .. why) or "") .. "."
  if o.secret and not o.revealed then secretNote(a.side, msg) else clog(pubMsg(a, msg), nil, "territories") end
  return true
end

-- Hidden things in territory n: { side, label, ref = table holding .revealed }
function hiddenAt(n)
  local out = {}
  local t = S.terr[n]
  if t.block and not t.block.revealed then table.insert(out, { side = t.block.side, label = "Road Blockage", ref = t.block }) end
  if t.sow and not t.sow.revealed then table.insert(out, { side = t.sow.side, label = "Sow Discontent", ref = t.sow }) end
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
    if a.dug == n and not a.dugRevealed then
      table.insert(out, { side = a.side, label = pubName(a) .. " dug in", ref = a, flag = "dugRevealed" })
    end
    if a.order and a.order.secret and a.order.n == n and not a.order.revealed then
      table.insert(out, { side = a.side, label = pubName(a) .. " " .. ACTIONS[a.order.k].verb, ref = a.order })
    end
  end
  return out
end

-- Reveal everything the other side hid in n to `side` (and so to the table).
function revealAt(n, side, why)
  local any = false
  for _, h in ipairs(hiddenAt(n)) do
    if h.side ~= side then
      h.ref[h.flag or "revealed"] = true
      any = true
      clog("Revealed (" .. why .. "): " .. h.side .. " - " .. h.label .. " at " .. tname(n) .. ".", nil, "territories")
      pcall(function() broadcastToAll("Revealed at " .. tname(n) .. ": " .. h.side .. " " .. h.label .. " (" .. why .. ").", { 0.95, 0.8, 0.45 }) end)
    end
  end
  return any
end

local function towerSees(side, n)
  local t = S.terr[n]
  if t.b.scouttower and t.owner == side then return true end
  for _, m in ipairs(ADJ[n] or {}) do
    local u = S.terr[m]
    if u.b.scouttower and u.owner == side then return true end
  end
  return false
end

-- Scouts, Scout Towers and armies on the spot uncover the other side's secrets.
function revealSweep()
  if not S or not S.started then return end
  for n = 1, #S.terr do
    local h = hiddenAt(n)
    if #h > 0 then
      for _, side in ipairs(SIDES) do
        if #armiesIn(n, side) > 0 then revealAt(n, side, side .. " army arrived")
        elseif #agentsIn(n, side, "Scout") > 0 then revealAt(n, side, side .. " Scout")
        elseif towerSees(side, n) then revealAt(n, side, side .. " Scout Tower") end
      end
    end
  end
end

-- Is something blocking the roads out of n for this mover? "free" (the builder's first exit), "slow" or nil.
function leaveBlock(n, mover, isArmy)
  local b = n and S.terr[n] and S.terr[n].block
  if not b then return nil end
  if mover and mappedFor(n, mover.side) then return nil end   -- Land Mapping: blockages don't hold up its owner
  if isArmy and b.by == mover.id and b.free then return "free" end
  return "slow"
end

-- After leaving n: the builder uses up its free exit; anyone else paying the full move clears it.
function afterLeave(n, kind)
  local b = S.terr[n].block
  if not b then return end
  if kind == "free" then b.free = false; return end
  S.terr[n].block = nil
  if b.revealed then clog("The road blockage at " .. tname(n) .. " has been cleared.", nil, "territories")
  else secretNote(b.side, "The road blockage at " .. tname(n) .. " has been cleared.") end
end

-- First blocked territory a mover would run into along path (not counting its destination), or nil.
function blockOnPath(path, side)
  for i, n in ipairs(path or {}) do
    if i < #path and S.terr[n].block and not mappedFor(n, side) then return n, i end
  end
  return nil
end

-- A mover of `side` was stopped at n by a blockage: they now know it's there.
function hitBlock(n, side, who)
  local b = S.terr[n].block
  if not b then return end
  if b.side ~= side and not b.revealed then
    b.revealed = true
    clog("Revealed: " .. who .. " ran into a road blockage at " .. tname(n) .. ".", nil, "territories")
  elseif b.side == side and not b.revealed then
    secretNote(side, who .. " was held up by your own road blockage at " .. tname(n) .. ".")
  else
    clog(who .. " was held up by the road blockage at " .. tname(n) .. ".", nil, "territories")
  end
end

-- An army that leaves its territory stops being dug in.
function armyLeft(a, from)
  if a.dug and a.dug ~= a.loc then
    if a.dugRevealed then clog(pubName(a) .. " left its dug-in position at " .. tname(a.dug) .. ".", nil, "territories")
    else secretNote(a.side, a.name .. " left its dug-in position at " .. tname(a.dug) .. ".") end
    a.dug, a.dugRevealed = nil, nil
  end
end

-- Finished actions, kept for the Chronicle (secret ones only once discovered).
local function actEvent(a, o, detail)
  backfillActEvents()
  table.insert(S.actEvents, { t = o.t, n = o.n, k = o.k, s = a.side, d = detail, secret = o.secret, rev = o.revealed })
  while #S.actEvents > 300 do table.remove(S.actEvents, 1) end
end

local function applyOrder(a, o)
  local n, t = o.n, S.terr[o.n]
  local say = function(msg)
    if o.secret and not o.revealed then secretNote(a.side, msg) else clog(pubMsg(a, msg), nil, "territories") end
  end
  if o.k == "pillage" then
    if t.owner ~= OTHER[a.side] then return say(orderLabel(a) .. " found nothing to take (the territory changed hands).") end
    local take = math.floor((t.stock or 0) / 2)
    local room = math.max(0, a.size - (a.carried or 0))
    local bonus = pillageBonus(a)   -- Rhudaur Raiders: extra loot that isn't taken from the territory
    local got = math.min(take + bonus, room)
    t.stock = (t.stock or 0) - take
    a.carried = (a.carried or 0) + got
    t.noIncome = math.max(t.noIncome or 0, 1)
    t.noIncomeBy = { k = "pillage", s = a.side }
    a.pillagedT = S.turn
    say(a.name .. " pillaged " .. tname(n) .. ": " .. take .. " RP taken from its stores" .. ((bonus > 0) and (", plus " .. bonus .. " from " .. pillageBonusWhy(a)) or "") ..
        ((got < take + bonus) and (" (" .. got .. " carried off, the rest destroyed)") or "") .. ". It produces no supplies next turn.")
    actEvent(a, o, pubName(a) .. " took " .. got .. " RP")
  elseif o.k == "sabotage" then
    local idx
    for i, item in ipairs(t.q or {}) do if item.key == o.target then idx = i end end
    if not idx and #(t.q or {}) > 0 then idx = 1 end
    if not idx then return say(orderLabel(a) .. " found nothing left to destroy.") end
    local item = table.remove(t.q, idx)
    say(a.name .. " sabotaged the " .. BUILDING_BY_KEY[item.key].name .. " being built at " .. tname(n) .. ".")
    actEvent(a, o, pubName(a) .. " destroyed the unfinished " .. BUILDING_BY_KEY[item.key].name)
  elseif o.k == "scorch" then
    local lost = {}
    for key, has in pairs(t.b) do if has then table.insert(lost, BUILDING_BY_KEY[key].name) end end
    for _, item in ipairs(t.q or {}) do table.insert(lost, BUILDING_BY_KEY[item.key].name .. " (unfinished)") end
    t.b, t.q, t.bslot = {}, {}, {}
    t.noIncome = math.max(t.noIncome or 0, SCORCH_TURNS)
    t.noIncomeBy = { k = "scorch", s = a.side }
    actEvent(a, o, (#lost > 0) and (table.concat(lost, ", ") .. " destroyed") or "nothing left standing")
    say(a.side .. " burned " .. tname(n) .. " (Scorched Earth)" .. ((#lost > 0) and (": " .. table.concat(lost, ", ") .. " destroyed") or "") ..
        ". It produces no supplies for " .. SCORCH_TURNS .. " turns.")
    pcall(function() if reconcileBuildings then reconcileBuildings() end end)
  elseif o.k == "block" then
    t.block = { side = a.side, by = a.id, free = true, revealed = o.revealed }
    actEvent(a, o, pubName(a) .. " blocked the roads")
    say(a.name .. " blocked the roads at " .. tname(n) .. ".")
  elseif o.k == "dig" then
    a.dug, a.dugRevealed = n, o.revealed
    actEvent(a, o, pubName(a) .. " dug in")
    say(a.name .. " is dug in at " .. tname(n) .. ": battles here must use the Dig-In Defense scenario (rulebook p.28).")
  elseif o.k == "sow" then
    if t.owner ~= "Neutral" then return say(orderLabel(a) .. " came to nothing (the territory is no longer Neutral).") end
    t.sow = { side = a.side, revealed = o.revealed }
    actEvent(a, o, pubName(a) .. " stirred up the people")
    say(a.name .. " sowed discontent in " .. tname(n) .. ": it takes 1 extra turn to conquer.")
  end
end

-- End of the turn: every order still standing completes.
function resolveOrders()
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
    local o = a.order
    if o then
      if a.loc ~= o.n then cancelOrder(a, "it left the territory")
      elseif enemyHere(a) then cancelOrder(a, "engaged by the enemy")
      else
        a.order = nil
        local ok, err = pcall(applyOrder, a, o)
        if not ok then print("War Council action: " .. tostring(err)) end
      end
    end
  end
  revealSweep()
end

-- A battle involving these armies cancels what they were doing.
function battleCancels(...)
  for _, a in ipairs({ ... }) do
    if a and a.order then cancelOrder(a, "engaged in battle") end
  end
end

-- Where a unit can move to: leaving blocked roads takes the whole move, so only neighbours.
function moveRangeFor(unit, isArmy, budget)
  if leaveBlock(unit.loc, unit, isArmy) == "slow" then
    local out = {}
    for _, m in ipairs(ADJ[unit.loc] or {}) do table.insert(out, m) end
    return out
  end
  local out = reachable(unit.loc, budget, (not isArmy) and unit.type == "Supply Wagon", (not isArmy) and agentRoadFree(unit) or nil, unit.side)
  if isArmy then
    local keep = {}
    local par = paranoiaAllowed(unit, out)
    for _, m in ipairs(out) do if not madnessBlocks(unit, m) and (not par or par[m]) then table.insert(keep, m) end end
    out = keep
  end
  if isArmy and unit.fangorn then
    local keep = {}
    for _, m in ipairs(out) do if not fangornBlocked(unit, m) then table.insert(keep, m) end end
    out = keep
  end
  return out
end

-- Campaigns from before the Chronicle kept action records: rebuild them from the log once.
function backfillActEvents()
  if S.actBackfilled then return end
  S.actBackfilled = true
  local have = S.actEvents or {}
  S.actEvents = {}
  local function sideOfArmy(name, n, fallback)
    for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if a.name == name then return a.side end end
    return fallback
  end
  for _, e in ipairs(S.log or {}) do
    local m = e.m or ""
    local who, n = string.match(m, "^(.-) pillaged (%d+) ")
    if who then
      n = tonumber(n)
      local take = string.match(m, ": (%d+) RP taken")
      table.insert(S.actEvents, { t = e.t, n = n, k = "pillage", s = sideOfArmy(who, n, OTHER[S.terr[n].owner] or "Good"), d = who .. (take and (" took " .. take .. " RP") or "") })
    else
      local who2, bname, n2 = string.match(m, "^(.-) sabotaged the (.-) being built at (%d+) ")
      if who2 then
        n2 = tonumber(n2)
        table.insert(S.actEvents, { t = e.t, n = n2, k = "sabotage", s = sideOfArmy(who2, n2, OTHER[S.terr[n2].owner] or "Good"), d = who2 .. " destroyed the unfinished " .. bname })
      else
        local side, n3 = string.match(m, "^(%a+) burned (%d+) .-%(Scorched Earth%)")
        if side then
          table.insert(S.actEvents, { t = e.t, n = tonumber(n3), k = "scorch", s = side, d = string.match(m, "%): (.-) destroyed") and (string.match(m, "%): (.-) destroyed") .. " destroyed") or nil })
        end
      end
    end
  end
  for _, ev in ipairs(have) do table.insert(S.actEvents, ev) end   -- anything recorded since the update
  -- territories still without supplies: say who caused it
  for n, t in ipairs(S.terr) do
    if (t.noIncome or 0) > 0 and not t.noIncomeBy then
      for i = #S.actEvents, 1, -1 do
        local ev = S.actEvents[i]
        if ev.n == n and (ev.k == "pillage" or ev.k == "scorch") then t.noIncomeBy = { k = ev.k, s = ev.s }; break end
      end
    end
  end
end

-- Finished actions as a snapshot taken at the start of turn t would have shown them (for older turns).
function pastActs(t)
  backfillActEvents()
  local out = {}
  for _, ev in ipairs(S.actEvents) do
    if ev.t == t - 1 and (not ev.secret or ev.rev) then table.insert(out, { n = ev.n, k = ev.k, s = ev.s, st = "d", d = ev.d }) end
  end
  return out
end

-- Territory actions for the Chronicle snapshot: { n, k, s, st = "p" (under way) | "d" (finished last turn) | "a" (in effect), r = turns left, d = detail }
function actionSnapshot()
  backfillActEvents()
  local out = {}
  local seen = {}
  local function add(e) local key = e.n .. ":" .. e.k; if seen[key] then return end; seen[key] = true; table.insert(out, e) end
  for _, ev in ipairs(S.actEvents or {}) do
    if ev.t == S.turn - 1 and (not ev.secret or ev.rev or (ev.k == "block" and S.terr[ev.n].block and S.terr[ev.n].block.revealed)
                                 or (ev.k == "sow" and S.terr[ev.n].sow and S.terr[ev.n].sow.revealed)) then
      local r = ((ev.k == "scorch" or ev.k == "pillage") and S.terr[ev.n].noIncome) or nil
      add({ n = ev.n, k = ev.k, s = ev.s, st = "d", d = ev.d, r = r })
    end
  end
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
    local o = a.order
    if o and (not o.secret or o.revealed) then add({ n = o.n, k = o.k, s = a.side, st = "p", d = pubName(a) }) end
    if a.dug and a.dugRevealed then add({ n = a.dug, k = "dig", s = a.side, st = "a", d = pubName(a) }) end
  end
  for n, t in ipairs(S.terr) do
    if t.block and t.block.revealed then add({ n = n, k = "block", s = t.block.side, st = "a" }) end
    if t.sow and t.sow.revealed then add({ n = n, k = "sow", s = t.sow.side, st = "a" }) end
    if (t.noIncome or 0) > 0 and t.noIncomeBy then add({ n = n, k = t.noIncomeBy.k, s = t.noIncomeBy.s, st = "a", r = t.noIncome }) end
  end
  return out
end

-- Public lines about territory n (for the panel, the map and hover).
function actionLines(n)
  local t = S.terr[n]
  local out = {}
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
    local o = a.order
    if o and o.n == n and (not o.secret or o.revealed) then table.insert(out, { a.side, pubName(a) .. ": " .. ACTIONS[o.k].verb }) end
    if a.dug == n and a.dugRevealed then table.insert(out, { a.side, pubName(a) .. " is dug in" }) end
  end
  if t.block and t.block.revealed then table.insert(out, { t.block.side, "Roads blocked" }) end
  if t.sow and t.sow.revealed then table.insert(out, { t.sow.side, "Discontent: +1 turn to conquer" }) end
  if (t.noIncome or 0) > 0 then table.insert(out, { nil, "No supplies for " .. t.noIncome .. " turn" .. ((t.noIncome == 1) and "" or "s") }) end
  return out
end

-- Things only `side` knows about (their own secrets), for the Secret orders panel.
function secretLines(side)
  local out = {}
  for n, t in ipairs(S.terr) do
    if t.block and t.block.side == side then table.insert(out, "Roads blocked at " .. NAMES[n] .. (t.block.revealed and " (discovered)" or " (secret)")) end
    if t.sow and t.sow.side == side then table.insert(out, "Discontent in " .. NAMES[n] .. (t.sow.revealed and " (discovered)" or " (secret)")) end
  end
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
    if a.side == side and a.dug then table.insert(out, a.name .. " dug in at " .. NAMES[a.dug] .. (a.dugRevealed and " (discovered)" or " (secret)")) end
  end
  return out
end

end

--[[ ===================================================================
     WAR COUNCIL - each campaign's special rules for Good and Evil
     auto = the War Council applies it; declare = a button the side presses (with its cooldown)
     =================================================================== ]]

SPECIAL_RULES = {
  Eriador = {
    Good = {
      { key = "hobbits", name = "Hobbit Laborers", auto = true,
        text = "The Hobbits have pledged themselves to the cause and help build the roads: Roads take Good 1 less turn to build." },
      { key = "dunedain", name = "Dúnedain Scouts", auto = true,
        text = "Good Scouts learn what an enemy army contains on a 2+ instead of a 3+. In a battle where a Good Scout is in the territory, Good's army gains a free Ranger of the North model for that battle." },
      { key = "palantir", name = "Círdan's Palantír", declare = "Priority", cooldown = 5,
        text = "In the Priority stage, before any dice are rolled, Good may use the Palantír to win priority automatically. 5 turn cooldown." },
    },
    Evil = {
      { key = "raiders", name = "Rhudaur Raiders", auto = true,
        text = "An army of 400 points or less that pillages a territory gains an extra 50 RP. The territory only loses what was taken from it." },
      { key = "goblins", name = "Goblin Cunning", auto = true,
        text = "Moria armies can move through territories with enemy armies without stopping. If one ends the Logistics stage in the same territory as an enemy army, it is engaged as normal." },
      { key = "ironfist", name = "Iron Fist of Angmar", declare = "Logistics", cooldown = 5,
        text = "At the start of the Logistics stage, before either player has done anything, Evil may declare it: every Evil army moves 1 extra region this turn, and an army led by the Witch-king of Angmar moves first regardless of priority. 5 turn cooldown." },
    },
  },
  Rohan = {
    Good = {
      { key = "swiftness", name = "Swiftness of the Riddermark", auto = true, text = "A Heroic March gives +2 regions instead of +1." },
      { key = "hornburg", name = "The Hornburg", auto = true, text = "Good starts with Fortifications in the Hornburg (Helm's Deep); its garrison is 600 points instead of 400 (Fortress Upgrades take it to 800, then 1000)." },
      { key = "eorlingas", name = "Forth Eorlingas!", text = "Once per campaign, after a friendly territory is lost, Good may raise a free 600 point Kingdom of Rohan army in an adjacent friendly territory, ready at once. It can take no Heroes other than a Captain of Rohan." },
    },
    Evil = {
      { key = "whitehand", name = "An Army Worthy of Mordor", auto = true, text = "Evil starts the campaign with a free 400 point Army of the White Hand in its capital." },
      { key = "teardown", name = "Tear Them All Down", auto = true, text = "Evil's capital builds everything 1 turn quicker." },
      { key = "wormtongue", name = "Tongue of a Worm", text = "In the Logistics stage, Evil may target a Good army led by a Hero of Legend: on a 3+ it cannot move this turn unless forced to retreat. Usable every turn." },
    },
  },
  Gondor = {
    Good = {
      { key = "whitecity", name = "Splendor of the White City", auto = true, text = "Good's capital starts with Fortifications and a City, and its City gives +50 RP instead of +25. The capital's Fortifications don't protect West Osgiliath, but while West Osgiliath stands no enemy army or model can pass through the capital, whatever their special rules." },
      { key = "stewards", name = "Stubbornness of Stewards", text = "Denethor and Aragorn can't both lead armies unless Boromir also leads one. Aragorn leading without Denethor: -1 Courage for his army. Denethor leading without Aragorn: once per Logistics stage he may scout any Evil army, revealing it on a 2+. All three leading: Good's capital produces double resources." },
      { key = "beacons", name = "The Beacons Are Lit", text = "Once per campaign, at the start of any stage while Denethor leads an army, Good may light the beacons: Denethor is replaced as leader for good, and at the start of the 3rd turn after, a 1000 point Kingdom of Rohan army appears next to Good's capital. Not allowed while the capital is in enemy hands or while Good holds East or West Osgiliath." },
    },
    Evil = {
      { key = "hordes", name = "The Hordes of Mordor", text = "Legion of Mordor armies with no Monsters or named Heroes take 1 turn less to raise and cost 50 RP less." },
      { key = "corsairs", name = "The Corsairs of Umbar", text = "When an Umbar army finishes raising, Evil may move it at once to a territory next to the sea or the Anduin. Umbar armies treat territories next to the sea or the Anduin as if they had Roads." },
      { key = "eye", name = "The Eye of Sauron", declare = "Logistics", text = "At the start of the Logistics stage Evil may pick a territory: armies that start in it, pass through it or stop in it are revealed. In a battle there, the Good army has +1 Courage and the Evil army -1 Courage." },
    },
  },
}

-- Rules of the theatre as a whole, not a benefit for either side.
THEATRE_RULES = {
  Rohan = {
    { key = "ents", name = "Wrath of the Ents",
      text = "If Evil holds two or more of Wellinghall, Treegarth, Limsworth and Entmoot, a 600 point Fangorn army awakens in Entmoot, controlled by Good. It may only move into those four territories and must be used to retake them. Once none of them is left in Evil hands, the territories it retook return to Neutral and the Ents go home." },
    { key = "industry", name = "Powers of Industry",
      text = "Wellinghall, Treegarth, Limsworth and Entmoot each produce 25 extra RP a turn while Evil holds them. This stacks with any City built there." },
  },
}
THEATRE_RULES.Gondor = {
  { key = "established", name = "Established Power",
    text = "Both Good and Evil start the campaign holding 12 territories. Minas Morgul begins with Fortifications." },
  { key = "anduin", name = "The River Anduin",
    text = "An army in a territory on the Anduin or the sea may pay 25 RP per territory, from the RP it carries, to sail up to 4 territories along the shore. Sailing uses 1 point of its movement. It may instead cross the Anduin to a territory on the far bank for a flat 75 RP, which ends its movement. Example: 100 RP to sail from Belfalas to the Crossings of Erui; 75 RP to cross from Emyn Arnen to the Crossings of Erui." },
  { key = "osgiliath", name = "Osgiliath Fatigue",
    text = "An army that stays in East or West Osgiliath for more than 1 turn starts to lose heart: its Courage is worsened by 2, then by 1 more for each further turn, to a worst of 4. It recovers after a turn in a friendly territory." },
}
ENT_LANDS = { "Wellinghall", "Treegarth", "Limsworth", "Entmoot" }

do  -- keep helpers out of the main chunk (Lua allows 200 locals there)

local function theatre() return (S and S.settings.theatre) or "Eriador" end

function theatreRules() return THEATRE_RULES[theatre()] or {} end
function theatreRule(key)
  for _, r in ipairs(theatreRules()) do if r.key == key then return r end end
  return nil
end
function entLand(n)
  if theatre() ~= "Rohan" then return false end
  local nm = NAMES[n]
  for _, e in ipairs(ENT_LANDS) do if e == nm then return true end end
  return false
end
-- Powers of Industry: +25 RP for Evil in the Ent lands
function industryBonus(n)
  if theatreRule("industry") and S.terr[n] and S.terr[n].owner == "Evil" and entLand(n) then return 25 end
  return 0
end
function fangornArmy()
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if a.fangorn then return a end end
end
-- Wrath of the Ents: wake them, or send them home. Called whenever a territory changes hands and each turn.
local entsBusy = false
function checkEnts()
  if entsBusy or not S.started or not theatreRule("ents") then return end
  entsBusy = true
  local ok, err = pcall(function()
    S.ents = S.ents or {}
    local lands, held = {}, 0
    for n = 1, #NAMES do if entLand(n) then table.insert(lands, n); if S.terr[n].owner == "Evil" then held = held + 1 end end end
    local f = fangornArmy()
    if S.ents.status == "awake" and not f then
      S.ents.status = "fallen"
      clog("The Ents of Fangorn have fallen. They will not march again.", nil, "armies")
      return
    end
    if not f and held >= 2 and S.ents.status ~= "fallen" then
      local en = terrByName("Entmoot")
      local a = { id = newId(), side = "Good", name = "The Ents of Fangorn", size = 600, leader = "", named = false, list = "Fangorn",
                  merc = false, cavalry = false, canMarch = false, allied = false, fangorn = true, units = "", loc = en, status = "ready",
                  buildLeft = 0, damage = 0, carried = 0, seenLeader = {}, seenUnits = {}, wounds = {} }
      table.insert(S.armies, a)
      numberArmy(a)
      S.ents = { status = "awake", t = S.turn, won = {} }
      clog("Wrath of the Ents! Evil holds " .. held .. " of the Ent lands, and Fangorn has woken: a 600 point Fangorn army (" .. pubName(a) ..
           ") stands in " .. NAMES[en] .. " under Good's command. It may only march into the Ent lands, and must retake them.", nil, "armies")
      pieceHook("created", "army", a)
    elseif f and held == 0 then
      local back = {}
      for _, n in ipairs(lands) do
        if S.ents.won and S.ents.won[tostring(n)] and S.terr[n].owner == "Good" then setOwner(n, "Neutral", "the Ents went home"); table.insert(back, NAMES[n]) end
      end
      removeArmy(f)
      S.ents.status = "home"
      clog("The Ent lands are free of Evil. The Ents return to Fangorn" .. ((#back > 0) and (", and " .. table.concat(back, ", ") .. " return" .. ((#back == 1) and "s" or "") .. " to Neutral.") or "."), nil, "armies")
    end
  end)
  entsBusy = false
  if not ok then print("War Council ents: " .. tostring(err)) end
end
-- a territory changed hands by conquest: note what the Ents retook
function theatreConquered(n, owner)
  if owner == "Good" and entLand(n) and S.ents and S.ents.status == "awake" then
    for _, a in ipairs(armiesIn(n, "Good")) do if a.fangorn then S.ents.won = S.ents.won or {}; S.ents.won[tostring(n)] = true end end
  end
end
-- The Fangorn army only goes into the Ent lands.
function fangornBlocked(a, dest) return a and a.fangorn and not entLand(dest) end

-- ---------- Gondor: the River Anduin ----------
-- Where an army may sail: { n, cost, cross }. Steps along one bank cost 25 each (up to 4); crossing to the far bank is a flat 75.
function sailOptions(a)
  if not theatreRule("anduin") or not a or not WATER[a.loc] or besieging(a) then return {} end
  local out, best = {}, { [a.loc] = 0 }
  local frontier = { a.loc }
  for step = 1, 4 do
    local nxt = {}
    for _, n in ipairs(frontier) do
      for _, e in ipairs(WATER[n] or {}) do
        local m, cross = e[1], e[2]
        if cross == 0 and best[m] == nil then best[m] = step; table.insert(nxt, m); table.insert(out, { n = m, cost = 25 * step, cross = false, steps = step }) end
      end
    end
    frontier = nxt
  end
  for _, e in ipairs(WATER[a.loc]) do
    if e[2] == 1 then table.insert(out, { n = e[1], cost = 75, cross = true, steps = 1 }) end
  end
  return out
end
function sailArmy(a, dest)
  local ok = stageOk("Logistics"); if ok ~= true then return ok end
  dest = tonumber(dest)
  if not a then return "Choose an army." end
  if a.status == "building" and not S.settings.override then return a.name .. " is still being raised." end
  if a.moved and not S.settings.override then return a.name .. " has already moved this turn." end
  if a.sailT == S.turn and not S.settings.override then return a.name .. " has already taken to the water this turn." end
  local held = eventNoMove(a)
  if held and not S.settings.override then return a.name .. " can't move this turn: " .. held .. "." end
  if armyMove(a) < 1 and not S.settings.override then return a.name .. " has no movement left to sail with." end
  if besieging(a) and not S.settings.override then return a.name .. " is lifting the siege of " .. tname(a.loc) .. ": it can only march 1 territory this turn, not sail." end
  if fangornBlocked(a, dest) then return "That army can't go there." end
  local madness = madnessBlocks(a, dest)
  if madness and not S.settings.override then return madness end
  local opt
  for _, o in ipairs(sailOptions(a)) do if o.n == dest then opt = o end end
  if not opt then return tname(dest) .. " can't be reached by water from " .. tname(a.loc) .. "." end
  if (a.carried or 0) < opt.cost and not S.settings.override then return a.name .. " carries " .. (a.carried or 0) .. " RP and needs " .. opt.cost .. " RP to make that voyage." end
  local from = a.loc
  a.carried = math.max(0, (a.carried or 0) - opt.cost)
  a.loc = dest
  a.sailT = S.turn
  if opt.cross then a.moved = true end
  armyLeft(a, from)
  if #armiesIn(dest, OTHER[a.side]) > 0 then a.moved = true; clog(a.name .. " landed among an enemy army at " .. tname(dest) .. ".", nil, "armies") end
  clog(a.side .. " army " .. a.name .. (opt.cross and " crossed the Anduin" or " sailed") .. " from " .. tname(from) .. " to " .. tname(dest) .. " for " .. opt.cost .. " RP" ..
       (opt.cross and "; its movement is over for this turn." or " (1 movement used)."), a.side)
  pieceHook("moved", "army", a, { from, dest })
  armyArrivedCaptures(a)
  if S.terr[dest].owner ~= a.side then clog("An army has entered " .. tname(dest) .. ".") end
  return true
end
-- movement left after a voyage along the shore
function sailCost(a) return (a and a.sailT == S.turn) and 1 or 0 end

-- ---------- Gondor: Osgiliath Fatigue ----------
local function inOsgiliath(a) local nm = NAMES[a.loc] or ""; return nm == "East Osgiliath" or nm == "West Osgiliath" end
function theatreTurnStart()
  if not theatreRule("osgiliath") then return end
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
    if a.status ~= "building" then
      if inOsgiliath(a) then
        a.osg = (a.osg or 0) + 1
        if a.osg == 2 then clog(pubName(a) .. " has lingered in " .. NAMES[a.loc] .. " too long: Osgiliath Fatigue sets in (Courage worsened by 2).", nil, "armies") end
      elseif S.terr[a.loc] and S.terr[a.loc].owner == a.side then a.osg = nil end
    end
  end
end
function osgFatigue(a)
  if not theatreRule("osgiliath") or not a or (a.osg or 0) < 2 then return 0 end
  return math.min(4, a.osg)
end
-- Splendor of the White City: the capital's City gives +50 instead of +25
function whiteCityBonus(n)
  if n == S.settings.capGood and S.terr[n].b.city and S.terr[n].owner == "Good" and hasRule("Good", "whitecity") then return 25 end
  return 0
end

-- The Hornburg: Helm's Deep's garrison
-- The garrison: 400 pts, +200 for each Fortress Upgrade, to at most 1000
function garrisonSize(n)
  local base = 400
  if hasRule("Good", "hornburg") and S.terr[n].owner == "Good" and NAMES[n] == "Helm's Deep" then base = 600 end
  return math.min(1000, base + 200 * (S.terr[n].fortup or 0))
end
-- Swiftness of the Riddermark: a Heroic March gives +2 regions
function marchBonus(a)
  if a and a.side == "Good" and hasRule("Good", "swiftness") then return 2 end
  return 1
end
-- what the special rules set up at the start of a campaign
function rulesAtStart()
  if hasRule("Good", "whitecity") then
    local cap = S.settings.capGood
    if cap and not S.terr[cap].b.city then S.terr[cap].b.city = true; pieceHook("built", cap, "city") end
    clog("Splendor of the White City: " .. tname(cap) .. " begins with Fortifications and a City; its City gives +50 RP.")
  end
  if hasRule("Good", "hornburg") then
    local hd = terrByName("Helm's Deep")
    if hd and not S.terr[hd].b.fort then S.terr[hd].b.fort = true; pieceHook("built", hd, "fort") end
    if hd then clog("The Hornburg: Helm's Deep begins with Fortifications, and its garrison is 600 points.") end
  end
  if theatreRule("established") then   -- Gondor: the Dead City is walled from the start
    local mm = terrByName("Minas Morgul")
    if mm and not S.terr[mm].b.fort then S.terr[mm].b.fort = true; pieceHook("built", mm, "fort") end
    if mm then clog("Established Power: Minas Morgul begins with Fortifications.") end
  end
  if hasRule("Evil", "whitehand") then
    local cap = S.settings.capEvil
    local a = { id = newId(), side = "Evil", name = "Army of the White Hand", size = 400, leader = "", named = false, list = "",
                merc = false, cavalry = false, canMarch = false, allied = false, units = "", loc = cap, status = "ready",
                buildLeft = 0, damage = 0, carried = 0, seenLeader = {}, seenUnits = {}, wounds = {} }
    table.insert(S.armies, a)
    numberArmy(a)
    clog("An Army Worthy of Mordor: Evil begins with a free 400 point Army of the White Hand at " .. tname(cap) .. ".", "Evil", "armies")
    pieceHook("created", "army", a)
  end
end

-- Is special rule `key` in force for `side` in this campaign?
function hasRule(side, key)
  for _, r in ipairs(((SPECIAL_RULES[theatre()] or {})[side]) or {}) do if r.key == key then return r end end
  return nil
end

function rulesFor(side) return ((SPECIAL_RULES[theatre()] or {})[side]) or {} end

-- Turns a declared rule still has to wait. 0 = ready.
function ruleCooldown(side, key)
  local r = hasRule(side, key)
  local used = S.ruleUsed and S.ruleUsed[side .. ":" .. key]
  if not r or not used or not r.cooldown then return 0 end
  return math.max(0, used + r.cooldown - S.turn)
end

-- Build time after special rules (Hobbit Laborers).
function buildTurns(side, key, n)
  local t = BUILDING_BY_KEY[key].turns
  if key == "roads" and side == "Good" and hasRule("Good", "hobbits") then t = t - 1 end
  if side == "Evil" and hasRule("Evil", "teardown") and (n == nil or n == S.settings.capEvil) and n ~= nil then t = t - 1 end
  return math.max(1, t)
end
