-- Scout roll needed to learn an army's units (Dunedain Scouts).
function scoutNeed(side)
  return (side == "Good" and hasRule("Good", "dunedain")) and 2 or 3
end

-- Moria armies slip past enemy armies (Goblin Cunning).
function passesEnemies(a)
  return a.side == "Evil" and hasRule("Evil", "goblins") and string.find(string.lower(a.list or ""), "moria", 1, true) ~= nil
end

-- Extra pillage loot (Rhudaur Raiders).
function pillageBonus(a)
  local b = 0
  if a.side == "Evil" and (a.size or 0) <= 400 and hasRule("Evil", "raiders") then b = b + 50 end
  if a.side == "Evil" and S.dunland == S.turn then b = b + 50 end    -- Dunland's Rage (random event)
  return b
end
function pillageBonusWhy(a)
  local w = {}
  if a.side == "Evil" and (a.size or 0) <= 400 and hasRule("Evil", "raiders") then table.insert(w, "Rhudaur Raiders") end
  if a.side == "Evil" and S.dunland == S.turn then table.insert(w, "Dunland's Rage") end
  return table.concat(w, " and ")
end

-- ---------- Gondor: The Eye of Sauron ----------
function eyeOn(n) return S.eye and S.eye.t == S.turn and S.eye.n == n end
function eyeReveal(a)
  if not a or a.side ~= "Good" or a.status == "building" then return end
  a.seenLeader = a.seenLeader or {}; a.seenUnits = a.seenUnits or {}
  if a.seenLeader.Evil and a.seenUnits.Evil then return end
  local pub = publicName(a)
  a.seenLeader.Evil = true; a.seenUnits.Evil = true
  clog("The Eye of Sauron sees " .. pub .. " at " .. tname(S.eye.n) .. ": it is " .. a.name ..
       ((a.leader and a.leader ~= "") and (", led by " .. a.leader) or "") .. ".", nil, "armies")
end
-- an army that moved: did its road take it into the Eye's sight?
function eyeAfterMove(a, from, path, dest)
  if not (S.eye and S.eye.t == S.turn) then return end
  local seen = (from == S.eye.n) or (dest == S.eye.n)
  for _, m in ipairs(path or {}) do if m == S.eye.n then seen = true end; if m == dest then break end end
  if seen then eyeReveal(a) end
end

-- Extra movement this turn (Iron Fist of Angmar).
function ruleMoveBonus(a)
  if a.side == "Evil" and S.ironFist == S.turn then return 1 end
  return 0
end

local function ledByWitchKing(a)
  local l = string.lower(a.leader or "")
  return string.find(l, "witch", 1, true) ~= nil
end

-- Has anyone done anything this Logistics stage yet? (Wagon routes run on their own and don't count.)
local function logisticsUntouched()
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if a.moved or a.order then return false end end
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do if g.type ~= "Supply Wagon" and (g.moved or g.acted) then return false end end
  return not S.logTouched
end

-- Can `side` declare rule `key` right now? true or a reason.
function canDeclare(side, key)
  local r = hasRule(side, key)
  if not r or not r.declare then return "That isn't one of your special rules." end
  if not S.started then return "Start the campaign first." end
  if STAGES[S.stage] ~= r.declare and not S.settings.override then return r.name .. " is declared in the " .. r.declare .. " stage." end
  local cd = ruleCooldown(side, key)
  if cd > 0 then return r.name .. " is ready again in " .. cd .. " turn" .. ((cd == 1) and "" or "s") .. " (turn " .. (S.turn + cd) .. ")." end
  if key == "palantir" and S.prio then return "Priority has already been rolled this turn." end
  if key == "eye" then
    if S.eye and S.eye.t == S.turn then return "The Eye is already on " .. tname(S.eye.n) .. " this turn." end
    if not logisticsUntouched() and not S.settings.override then return "It must be used at the start of the Logistics stage, before either player does anything." end
  end
  if key == "ironfist" then
    if S.ironFist == S.turn then return "Already declared this turn." end
    if not logisticsUntouched() and not S.settings.override then return "It must be declared before either player does anything this Logistics stage." end
  end
  return true
end

function declareRule(side, key, n)
  local ok = canDeclare(side, key)
  if ok ~= true then return ok end
  if key == "eye" and not (n and S.terr[n]) then return "Pick the territory the Eye looks upon." end
  local r = hasRule(side, key)
  S.ruleUsed = S.ruleUsed or {}
  S.ruleUsed[side .. ":" .. key] = S.turn
  S.ruleLog = S.ruleLog or {}
  table.insert(S.ruleLog, { t = S.turn, s = side, k = key })
  if key == "palantir" then
    S.prio = { Good = "-", Evil = "-", winner = "Good", palantir = true }
    S.first = nil
    clog("Good gazed into Círdan's Palantír and wins priority this turn. Good chooses who acts first.", nil, "armies")
    pcall(rollEvents)
  elseif key == "eye" then
    S.eye = { t = S.turn, n = n }
    clog("Evil turned the Eye of Sauron on " .. tname(n) .. ": armies that start in it, pass through it or stop in it this turn are revealed. In a battle there, the Good army has +1 Courage and the Evil army -1 Courage.", nil, "armies")
    for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if a.loc == n then eyeReveal(a) end end
  elseif key == "ironfist" then
    S.ironFist = S.turn
    local wk = {}
    for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if a.side == "Evil" and ledByWitchKing(a) then table.insert(wk, a.name) end end
    clog("Evil declared the Iron Fist of Angmar: every Evil army moves 1 extra region this turn." ..
         ((#wk > 0) and (" " .. table.concat(wk, ", ") .. " (led by the Witch-king) moves first, whatever the priority.") or ""), nil, "armies")
  end
  return true
end

end

--[[ ===================================================================
     WAR COUNCIL - random events
     After priority is decided, the side that won priority rolls a D6. On a 3 or lower each side
     rolls on its own Random Event table (one of ten events, equally likely).
     =================================================================== ]]

EVENT_TABLES = {
  Eriador = {
    Good = {
      { key = "holiday", name = "Hobbit Holiday", text = "The Hobbits are celebrating! Joyous news spreads through Eriador, but the feasting empties the coffers: four random Good territories lose 25 RP, and any construction under way in them moves 1 turn closer to completion." },
      { key = "elves", name = "Passing of the Elves", text = "A fresh Crossing sets sail into the West, a day of celebration and mourning for every Elf. Armies led by Elves cannot move this turn, but if they fight a battle this turn they start it with 4 victory points already achieved." },
      { key = "dunedain", name = "Dúnedain Resurgence", text = "More of the Dúnedain have come of age: Good gains 1 free Scout at its capital." },
      { key = "drought", name = "Great Drought", text = "A drought has ruined the farms and the territories cling to their stores: this turn no wagon or army may pick up RP from a Good territory." },
      { key = "snow", name = "Snowed In", text = "A snowstorm buries one Good army's camp: it cannot move this turn, and nobody can enter or pass through its territory until the end of the turn." },
      { key = "mercs", name = "Passing Mercenaries", text = "A mercenary company seeks an employer. This turn, a Good territory with enough RP may hire a 400 point Mercenary army for the price of a normal 400 point army (150 RP). It is ready at once." },
      { key = "bandits", name = "Bandit Raid", text = "Bandits raid a random Good territory: it is pillaged, losing half its stockpile and its next turn of supplies. The plunder simply vanishes." },
      { key = "dwarves", name = "Dwarven Gifts", text = "The Dwarves of Ered Luin send a gift of gems: a random Good territory gains 100 RP." },
      { key = "illness", name = "Sweeping Illness", text = "A swift plague strikes one Good army: it suffers 25% casualties." },
      { key = "tomb", name = "Tomb of Kings", text = "A Good army stumbles on a tomb of the old kings and is inspired: -2 Courage until the end of its next battle." },
    },
    Evil = {
      { key = "missive", name = "Missive from Mordor", text = "Mordor names a Good territory that must be seized: if Evil conquers it within the next 8 turns, Evil gains 300 RP at its capital. Happens once per campaign; no penalty for failing." },
      { key = "troll", name = "Troll Hoard Discovered", text = "A random Evil army finds an old Troll hoard. If it raids it, roll a D6: on a 4+ it gains 200 RP; otherwise it suffers 25% casualties and must retreat in the Retreat stage, even if it fights and wins this turn." },
      { key = "rhudaur", name = "Rhudaur Discontent", text = "The Men of Rhudaur chafe under the Dark Lord's cruelty: Rhudaur units have +2 Courage until the end of the turn." },
      { key = "craving", name = "Craving Man-flesh", text = "The Orcs and Goblins hunger for man-flesh: any Evil army with Orcs or Goblins that does not fight a battle this turn refuses to move next turn; any that does fight may declare a free army-wide Heroic March on the first turn of the battle." },
      { key = "eclipse", name = "Eclipse", text = "An eclipse darkens the hated Sun: the first army Evil moves this turn gets +1 movement." },
      { key = "illness", name = "Sweeping Illness", text = "A swift plague strikes one Evil army: it suffers 25% casualties." },
      { key = "bandits", name = "Bandit Raid", text = "Bandits raid a random Evil territory: it is pillaged, losing half its stockpile and its next turn of supplies. The plunder simply vanishes." },
      { key = "drought", name = "Great Drought", text = "A drought has ruined the farms and the territories cling to their stores: this turn no wagon or army may pick up RP from an Evil territory." },
      { key = "snow", name = "Snowed In", text = "A snowstorm buries one Evil army's camp: it cannot move this turn, and nobody can enter or pass through its territory until the end of the turn." },
      { key = "mercs", name = "Passing Mercenaries", text = "A mercenary company seeks an employer. This turn, an Evil territory with enough RP may hire a 400 point Mercenary army for the price of a normal 400 point army (150 RP). It is ready at once." },
    },
  },
}

-- Rohan: five events of its own for each side, plus the five both sides share in Eriador.
do
  local function shared(side)
    local out = {}
    for _, k in ipairs({ "drought", "snow", "mercs", "bandits", "illness" }) do
      for _, d in ipairs(EVENT_TABLES.Eriador[side]) do if d.key == k then table.insert(out, d) end end
    end
    return out
  end
  local good = {
    { key = "peasants", name = "Peasants' Pride", text = "The peasants of Rohan are a proud people who will not be subjugated by evil easily: no Good territory can be conquered this turn, and any conquest of a Good territory already under way is paused." },
    { key = "gondor", name = "Gondor's Relief", text = "Gondor has sent supplies in our dire need: Good gains 100 RP at its capital." },
    { key = "theoden", name = "Sickness of Théoden", text = "Théoden King has briefly fallen under the evil spell of Saruman: every Good army has +1 Courage in battles this turn." },
    { key = "horses", name = "Sick Horses", text = "The horse plague has returned: cavalry-only Good armies cannot move this turn." },
    { key = "eorl", name = "Eorl's Birthday", text = "The birthday of our honoured ancestor has arrived, a joyous occasion. Good may spend 100 RP from any of its territories to give every Good army -2 Courage in battles this turn." },
  }
  local evil = {
    { key = "bread", name = "Maggoty Bread", text = "The Orcs don't like the maggoty bread they are served and have become enraged: one random Evil army refuses to move this turn, and if it fights a battle this turn it has +2 Courage." },
    { key = "ilk", name = "Mordor Ilk", text = "The proud Uruk-hai do not like working with the Mordor rabble and a brawl has broken out: one random Evil army rolls a D6. On a 3+ nothing happens; otherwise it suffers 25% casualties." },
    { key = "dunland", name = "Dunland's Rage", text = "The Men of Dunland are eager to slaughter the people of Rohan: this turn Evil armies gain an extra 50 RP from pillaging, and an Evil army that pillages this turn has -1 Courage in battles this turn." },
    { key = "saruman", name = "Fell Speech of Saruman", text = "Saruman has harnessed the power of his voice: Evil chooses one Good army, and it cannot move this turn." },
    { key = "goblinraid", name = "Goblins Come", text = "Goblins have come down from the Misty Mountains to deal with the White Wizard. Evil may pay them 100 RP from any of its territories; they then run amok in Rohan, and one Good army that Evil chooses, standing in a Good territory, suffers 25% casualties." },
  }
  for _, d in ipairs(shared("Good")) do table.insert(good, d) end
  for _, d in ipairs(shared("Evil")) do table.insert(evil, d) end
  EVENT_TABLES.Rohan = { Good = good, Evil = evil }
  local ggood = {
    { key = "boromir", name = "Boromir's Courage", text = "The Hero of Gondor's courage spreads through the forces of Good: every Good army has -2 Courage in battles this turn, and an army led by Boromir has -3." },
    { key = "insight", name = "Denethor's Insight", text = "Denethor has long probed the enemy's defences: one random enemy territory bordering Good land takes 1 turn less to conquer, provided a Good army starts conquering it this turn." },
    { key = "madness", name = "Denethor's Madness", text = "Seeing the Legions of Evil, Denethor's failing will gives way to madness and he refuses to strike at Mordor: this turn no Good army may enter enemy territory or a territory holding an Evil army, except an army led by Aragorn." },
    { key = "warfatigue", name = "War Fatigue", text = "The war has gone on too long and the people of Gondor are weary: roll a D6 for each Good army. On a 2+ nothing happens; otherwise it suffers 25% casualties as its men give up the fight." },
    { key = "king", name = "Return of the King", text = "If Aragorn is not yet leading an army, Good may at once raise a 400 point Men of the West army led by him at its capital. If it does, no army may contain Denethor from then on. Optional; happens once per campaign." },
  }
  local gevil = {
    { key = "sauronwill", name = "Sauron's Will", text = "Sauron bends his worshippers to his mighty will: Evil chooses one Evil army, which gets +1 movement this turn. If it fights a battle this turn, it may declare a free army-wide Heroic March on the first turn of that battle." },
    { key = "tribute", name = "Tribute to the Dark Lord", text = "Rhûn has sent a tribute of gold to show its loyalty: Evil gains 100 RP at its capital." },
    { key = "rebellion", name = "Slave Rebellion", text = "The slaves are revolting: every Evil territory loses 25 RP, and construction in Evil territories is paused this turn." },
    { key = "paranoia", name = "Sauron's Paranoia", text = "Sauron grows paranoid at rumours of the return of Isildur's line and wants the war over: one random Evil army must move as close as it can to the nearest Good army this turn, and if it can reach that army it must. It need not use a Heroic March." },
    { key = "ritual", name = "Dark Rituals", text = "Fell things stir in the darkest pits of Mordor. Evil may attempt a Dark Ritual: roll a D6. On a 3+ every Evil army has -3 Courage in battles this turn; otherwise no Evil army can move this turn. Optional." },
  }
  for _, d in ipairs(shared("Good")) do table.insert(ggood, d) end
  for _, d in ipairs(shared("Evil")) do table.insert(gevil, d) end
  EVENT_TABLES.Gondor = { Good = ggood, Evil = gevil }
end

do  -- keep helpers out of the main chunk (Lua allows 200 locals there)

local ELF_NAMES = { "elrond", "gil-galad", "glorfindel", "cirdan", "círdan", "elladan", "elrohir", "arwen", "erestor", "gildor", "legolas", "thranduil",
                    "galadriel", "celeborn", "haldir", "lindir", "elf", "elven" }

local function pick(list) if #list == 0 then return nil end return list[math.random(1, #list)] end
local function shuffled(list)
  local t = {}
  for i, v in ipairs(list) do t[i] = v end
  for i = #t, 2, -1 do local j = math.random(1, i); t[i], t[j] = t[j], t[i] end
  return t
end
local function readyArmies(side, test)
  local out = {}
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if a.side == side and a.status ~= "building" and (not test or test(a)) then table.insert(out, a) end end
  return out
end
local function armyText(a)
  local s = string.lower((a.units or "") .. " " .. (a.list or ""))
  if a.roster and a.roster.lines then for _, l in pairs(a.roster.lines) do s = s .. " " .. string.lower(tostring(l.name or l)) end end
  return s
end
function ledByElf(a)
  local l = string.lower(a.leader or "")
  for _, nm in ipairs(ELF_NAMES) do if l ~= "" and string.find(l, nm, 1, true) then return true end end
  return false
end
function hasRhudaur(a) return string.find(armyText(a), "rhudaur", 1, true) ~= nil end
function hasOrcs(a)
  local s = armyText(a)
  return string.find(s, "orc", 1, true) ~= nil or string.find(s, "goblin", 1, true) ~= nil or string.find(s, "moria", 1, true) ~= nil
end
local function capitalOf(side) return (side == "Good") and S.settings.capGood or S.settings.capEvil end
local function ledBy(a, who) return string.find(string.lower(a.leader or ""), who, 1, true) ~= nil end
function hasHero(a, who) return ledBy(a, who) or string.find(armyText(a), who, 1, true) ~= nil end
-- Denethor's Madness: a reason the Good army can't go there this turn, or nil
function madnessBlocks(a, dest)
  if not (S.madness == S.turn and a and a.side == "Good" and not ledBy(a, "aragorn")) then return nil end
  dest = tonumber(dest)
  if not dest or not S.terr[dest] then return nil end
  if S.terr[dest].owner == "Evil" or #armiesIn(dest, "Evil") > 0 then return "Denethor's Madness: no Good army may enter enemy land or a territory holding an Evil army this turn (unless Aragorn leads it)." end
  return nil
end
-- Denethor's Insight: the conquest it speeds up
function insightCut(n, attacker)
  if attacker == "Good" and S.insight and S.insight.t == S.turn and S.insight.n == n then return 1 end
  return nil
end
-- Sauron's Paranoia: the territories the chosen army may move to (closest to its quarry), or nil when it isn't bound
function paranoiaAllowed(a, options)
  if not (a and a.paranoiaT == S.turn) then return nil end
  local target = a.paranoiaTarget and armyById(a.paranoiaTarget)
  if not target then return nil end
  local dist = { [target.loc] = 0 }
  local q, qi = { target.loc }, 1
  while q[qi] do local n = q[qi]; qi = qi + 1; for _, m in ipairs(ADJ[n] or {}) do if dist[m] == nil then dist[m] = dist[n] + 1; table.insert(q, m) end end end
  local best
  for _, m in ipairs(options) do if dist[m] and (best == nil or dist[m] < best) then best = dist[m] end end
  if best == nil then return nil end
  local ok = {}
  for _, m in ipairs(options) do if dist[m] == best then ok[m] = true end end
  return ok
end

function eventsOn() return S.settings.randomEvents ~= false and EVENT_TABLES[S.settings.theatre or "Eriador"] ~= nil end

-- ---------- what the rest of the War Council asks ----------
function eventNoMove(a)
  if a.noMoveTurn == S.turn then return a.noMoveWhy or "an event" end
  if S.snow and S.snow.t == S.turn and S.snow.army == a.id and not mappedFor(a.loc, a.side) then return "it is snowed in" end
  return nil
end
function isSnowed(n) return S.snow and S.snow.t == S.turn and S.snow.n == n end
function droughtAt(n) local o = S.terr[n] and S.terr[n].owner; return S.drought and o and S.drought[o] == S.turn end
function eventMoveBonus(a)
  local b = 0
  if a.side == "Evil" and S.eclipse and S.eclipse.t == S.turn and not S.eclipse.used then b = b + 1 end
  if a.willT == S.turn then b = b + 1 end
  return b
end
function eventAfterMove(a)
  if a.side == "Evil" and S.eclipse and S.eclipse.t == S.turn and not S.eclipse.used then
    S.eclipse.used = true
    clog(pubName(a) .. " marched under the Eclipse (+1 movement).", nil, "armies")
  end
end
function eventBattleLines(n, a, lines)
  if not a then return end
  if (a.tomb or 0) > 0 then table.insert(lines, pubName(a) .. ": Tomb of Kings, -" .. a.tomb .. " Courage this battle.") end
  if a.elvesTurn == S.turn then table.insert(lines, pubName(a) .. ": Passing of the Elves, starts the battle with 4 victory points.") end
  if S.rhudaurT == S.turn and a.side == "Evil" and hasRhudaur(a) then table.insert(lines, pubName(a) .. ": Rhudaur Discontent, +2 Courage for its Rhudaur units.") end
  if a.craving == S.turn then table.insert(lines, pubName(a) .. ": Craving Man-flesh, may declare a free army-wide Heroic March on the first turn.") end
  if a.forceRetreat == S.turn then table.insert(lines, pubName(a) .. " must retreat after this battle (Troll Hoard), win or lose.") end
  if a.side == "Good" and S.boromir == S.turn then table.insert(lines, pubName(a) .. ": Boromir's Courage, -" .. (ledBy(a, "boromir") and 3 or 2) .. " Courage this battle.") end
  if a.willT == S.turn then table.insert(lines, pubName(a) .. ": Sauron's Will, may declare a free army-wide Heroic March on the first turn.") end
  if a.side == "Evil" and S.ritualT == S.turn then table.insert(lines, pubName(a) .. ": Dark Rituals, -3 Courage this battle.") end
  if a.side == "Good" and S.theoden == S.turn then table.insert(lines, pubName(a) .. ": Sickness of Théoden, +1 Courage this battle.") end
  if a.side == "Good" and S.eorl == S.turn then table.insert(lines, pubName(a) .. ": Eorl's Birthday, -2 Courage this battle.") end
  if a.breadT == S.turn then table.insert(lines, pubName(a) .. ": Maggoty Bread, +2 Courage this battle.") end
  if a.side == "Evil" and S.dunland == S.turn and (a.pillagedT == S.turn or (a.order and a.order.k == "pillage")) then table.insert(lines, pubName(a) .. ": Dunland's Rage, pillaged this turn, -1 Courage this battle.") end
end
-- a battle uses up the Tomb of Kings
function eventAfterBattle(...)
  for _, a in ipairs({ ... }) do if a then a.tomb = nil end end
end
-- Missive from Mordor
function eventConquered(n, owner)
  local m = S.missive
  if m and not m.done and owner == "Evil" and m.n == n and S.turn <= m.untilT then
    m.done = true
    local cap = capitalOf("Evil")
    if cap and S.terr[cap] then S.terr[cap].stock = S.terr[cap].stock + 300 end
    clog("Missive from Mordor fulfilled: " .. tname(n) .. " has fallen. Evil gains 300 RP at its capital.", nil, "territories")
  end
end
-- end of turn: hungry orcs that didn't fight sulk; Troll hoard raiders are sent back
function eventsTurnEnd()
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
    if a.craving == S.turn then
      local fought = false
      for _, b in ipairs(S.battles or {}) do if b.t == S.turn and (b.atkId == a.id or b.defId == a.id) then fought = true end end
      if not fought then
        a.noMoveTurn, a.noMoveWhy = S.turn + 1, "its Orcs refuse to march (Craving Man-flesh)"
        clog(pubName(a) .. " found no man-flesh this turn and refuses to move next turn.", nil, "armies")
      end
    end
  end
end
function eventsRetreatStage()
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
    if a.forceRetreat == S.turn and not a.needsRetreat then
      a.needsRetreat = true
      clog(pubName(a) .. " must retreat (Troll Hoard).", nil, "armies")
    end
  end
end

-- ---------- each event ----------
local function apply(side, ev)
  local k = ev.key
  local d
  local info = {}
  ev.info = info
  if k == "holiday" then
    local hit = {}
    for i, n in ipairs(shuffled(ownedBy(side))) do
      if i > 4 then break end
      local t = S.terr[n]
      t.stock = math.max(0, t.stock - 25)
      for qi = #t.q, 1, -1 do
        local item = t.q[qi]
        item.left = item.left - 1
        if item.left <= 0 then
          local extra = finishBuild(n, item)
          table.remove(t.q, qi)
          clog(BUILDING_BY_KEY[item.key].name .. " completed at " .. tname(n) .. extra .. " (Hobbit Holiday).", nil, "construction")
        end
      end
      table.insert(hit, NAMES[n])
      info.ns = info.ns or {}; table.insert(info.ns, n)
    end
    d = (#hit > 0) and ("-25 RP and +1 turn of construction at " .. table.concat(hit, ", ") .. ".") or "No territories to celebrate in."
  elseif k == "elves" then
    local names = {}
    for _, a in ipairs(readyArmies(side, ledByElf)) do
      a.noMoveTurn, a.noMoveWhy, a.elvesTurn = S.turn, "its Elves are at the Crossing (Passing of the Elves)", S.turn
      table.insert(names, pubName(a))
    end
    d = (#names > 0) and (table.concat(names, ", ") .. " cannot move this turn.") or "No army is led by an Elf."
  elseif k == "dunedain" then
    local cap = capitalOf(side)
    if cap and S.terr[cap] and S.terr[cap].owner == side then
      local g = { id = newId(), side = side, type = "Scout", loc = cap, carried = 0 }
      info.n = cap
      table.insert(S.agents, g)
      pieceHook("created", "agent", g)
      d = "A free Scout joins Good at " .. NAMES[cap] .. "."
    else d = "Good holds no capital to send the Scout to." end
  elseif k == "drought" then
    S.drought = S.drought or {}
    S.drought[side] = S.turn
    info.side = side
    d = "No RP can be picked up from " .. side .. " territories this turn."
  elseif k == "snow" then
    local a = pick(readyArmies(side))
    if a then
      S.snow = { t = S.turn, n = a.loc, army = a.id }
      info.n, info.army = a.loc, a.id
      d = pubName(a) .. " is snowed in at " .. NAMES[a.loc] .. ": it can't move, and nobody can enter or cross " .. NAMES[a.loc] .. " this turn."
    else d = "No army to snow in." end
  elseif k == "mercs" then
    local any = false
    for _, n in ipairs(ownedBy(side)) do if S.terr[n].stock >= ARMY_COST[400][1] then any = true end end
    if any then ev.pending = "mercs"; d = "A territory with " .. ARMY_COST[400][1] .. " RP can hire them this turn (Turn tab)."
    else d = "No " .. side .. " territory has the " .. ARMY_COST[400][1] .. " RP to hire them." end
  elseif k == "bandits" then
    local n = pick(ownedBy(side))
    if n then
      local t = S.terr[n]
      local lost = math.floor(t.stock / 2)
      t.stock = t.stock - lost
      t.noIncome = math.max(t.noIncome or 0, 1)
      t.noIncomeBy = { k = "pillage", s = OTHER[side] }
      info.n = n
      d = "Bandits pillaged " .. NAMES[n] .. ": " .. lost .. " RP lost, and it produces nothing next turn."
    else d = "No territory to raid." end
  elseif k == "dwarves" then
    local n = pick(ownedBy(side))
    if n then S.terr[n].stock = S.terr[n].stock + 100; info.n = n; d = NAMES[n] .. " gains 100 RP." else d = "No territory to receive the gift." end
  elseif k == "illness" then
    local a = pick(readyArmies(side))
    if a then info.army = a.id; info.n = a.loc; a.damage = math.min(75, (a.damage or 0) + 25); d = pubName(a) .. " suffers 25% casualties (now -" .. a.damage .. "%)."
    else d = "No army falls ill." end
  elseif k == "tomb" then
    local a = pick(readyArmies(side))
    if a then info.army = a.id; info.n = a.loc; a.tomb = 2; d = pubName(a) .. " gains -2 Courage until the end of its next battle." else d = "No army to find the tomb." end
  elseif k == "missive" then
    local n = pick(ownedBy("Good"))
    if n then
      S.missive = { n = n, untilT = S.turn + 8, done = false }
      info.n, info.untilT = n, S.turn + 8
      d = "Target: " .. NAMES[n] .. ". Conquer it by turn " .. (S.turn + 8) .. " for 300 RP."
    else d = "Good holds no territory to target." end
  elseif k == "troll" then
    local a = pick(readyArmies(side))
    if a then ev.pending = "troll"; ev.army = a.id; info.army = a.id; info.n = a.loc; d = pubName(a) .. " has found the hoard. Raid it or leave it (Turn tab)."
    else d = "No army to find the hoard." end
  elseif k == "rhudaur" then
    S.rhudaurT = S.turn
    local names = {}
    for _, a in ipairs(readyArmies(side, hasRhudaur)) do table.insert(names, pubName(a)) end
    d = (#names > 0) and ("+2 Courage this turn for the Rhudaur units of " .. table.concat(names, ", ") .. ".") or "No army contains Rhudaur units."
  elseif k == "craving" then
    local names = {}
    for _, a in ipairs(readyArmies(side, hasOrcs)) do a.craving = S.turn; table.insert(names, pubName(a)) end
    d = (#names > 0) and (table.concat(names, ", ") .. " must find a battle this turn or refuse to move next turn.") or "No army contains Orcs or Goblins."
  elseif k == "peasants" then
    S.peasants = S.turn
    d = "No Good territory can be conquered this turn; conquests already under way are paused."
  elseif k == "gondor" then
    local cap = capitalOf(side)
    if cap and S.terr[cap] and S.terr[cap].owner == side then S.terr[cap].stock = S.terr[cap].stock + 100; info.n = cap; d = NAMES[cap] .. " gains 100 RP."
    else d = "Good holds no capital to receive the supplies." end
  elseif k == "theoden" then
    S.theoden = S.turn
    d = "Every Good army has +1 Courage in battles this turn."
  elseif k == "horses" then
    local names = {}
    for _, a in ipairs(readyArmies(side, isCavalry)) do
      a.noMoveTurn, a.noMoveWhy = S.turn, "its horses are sick (Sick Horses)"
      table.insert(names, pubName(a))
    end
    d = (#names > 0) and (table.concat(names, ", ") .. " cannot move this turn.") or "No cavalry-only army is affected."
  elseif k == "eorl" then
    local any = false
    for _, n in ipairs(ownedBy(side)) do if S.terr[n].stock >= 100 then any = true end end
    if any then ev.pending = "eorl"; d = "Good may spend 100 RP from one of its territories to celebrate (Turn tab)."
    else d = "No Good territory has the 100 RP to spend on the feast." end
  elseif k == "bread" then
    local a = pick(readyArmies(side))
    if a then
      a.noMoveTurn, a.noMoveWhy, a.breadT = S.turn, "its Orcs are enraged by the bread (Maggoty Bread)", S.turn
      info.army, info.n = a.id, a.loc
      d = pubName(a) .. " refuses to move this turn, and has +2 Courage if it fights."
    else d = "No army to feed." end
  elseif k == "ilk" then
    local a = pick(readyArmies(side))
    if a then
      info.army, info.n = a.id, a.loc
      local r = d6(side, "Mordor Ilk")
      if r >= 3 then d = pubName(a) .. " rolled " .. r .. ": the brawl dies down."
      else a.damage = math.min(75, (a.damage or 0) + 25); d = pubName(a) .. " rolled " .. r .. ": 25% casualties (now -" .. a.damage .. "%)." end
    else d = "No army to quarrel." end
  elseif k == "dunland" then
    S.dunland = S.turn
    d = "Evil armies gain +50 RP from pillaging this turn; any that pillage have -1 Courage in battles this turn."
  elseif k == "saruman" then
    if #readyArmies("Good") > 0 then ev.pending = "saruman"; d = "Evil chooses one Good army that cannot move this turn (Turn tab)."
    else d = "Good has no army to bewitch." end
  elseif k == "goblinraid" then
    local any, targets = false, false
    for _, n in ipairs(ownedBy(side)) do if S.terr[n].stock >= 100 then any = true end end
    for _, a in ipairs(readyArmies("Good")) do if S.terr[a.loc].owner == "Good" then targets = true end end
    if any and targets then ev.pending = "goblinraid"; d = "Evil may pay the Goblins 100 RP and choose a Good army in Good land (Turn tab)."
    elseif not any then d = "No Evil territory has the 100 RP the Goblins want."
    else d = "No Good army stands in Good land for the Goblins to raid." end
  elseif k == "boromir" then
    S.boromir = S.turn
    d = "Every Good army has -2 Courage in battles this turn; an army led by Boromir has -3."
  elseif k == "insight" then
    local list = {}
    for _, n in ipairs(ownedBy("Evil")) do
      for _, m in ipairs(ADJ[n] or {}) do if S.terr[m].owner == "Good" then table.insert(list, n); break end end
    end
    local n = pick(list)
    if n then S.insight = { t = S.turn, n = n }; info.n = n; d = NAMES[n] .. " takes 1 turn less to conquer if a Good army starts conquering it this turn."
    else d = "No enemy territory borders Good land." end
  elseif k == "madness" then
    S.madness = S.turn
    d = "No Good army may enter enemy land or a territory holding an Evil army this turn, unless Aragorn leads it."
  elseif k == "warfatigue" then
    local hit, rolls = {}, {}
    for _, a in ipairs(readyArmies(side)) do
      local r = d6(side, "War Fatigue: " .. pubName(a))
      table.insert(rolls, pubName(a) .. " " .. r)
      if r < 2 then a.damage = math.min(75, (a.damage or 0) + 25); table.insert(hit, pubName(a)) end
    end
    d = (#rolls == 0) and "Good has no army to tire." or ("Rolled: " .. table.concat(rolls, ", ") .. ". " .. ((#hit > 0) and (table.concat(hit, ", ") .. " suffer" .. ((#hit == 1) and "s" or "") .. " 25% casualties.") or "Every army holds firm."))
  elseif k == "king" then
    local led = false
    for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if a.side == "Good" and ledBy(a, "aragorn") then led = true end end
    local cap = capitalOf(side)
    if S.kingDone then d = "The King has already returned."
    elseif led then d = "Aragorn already leads an army."
    elseif not (cap and S.terr[cap] and S.terr[cap].owner == side) then d = "Good holds no capital to raise the army in."
    else ev.pending = "king"; d = "Good may raise a 400 point Men of the West army led by Aragorn at " .. NAMES[cap] .. " (Turn tab). No army may then contain Denethor." end
  elseif k == "sauronwill" then
    if #readyArmies(side) > 0 then ev.pending = "sauronwill"; d = "Evil chooses one of its armies to receive +1 movement this turn (Turn tab)."
    else d = "Evil has no army to bend to his will." end
  elseif k == "tribute" then
    local cap = capitalOf(side)
    if cap and S.terr[cap] and S.terr[cap].owner == side then S.terr[cap].stock = S.terr[cap].stock + 100; info.n = cap; d = NAMES[cap] .. " gains 100 RP."
    else d = "Evil holds no capital to receive the tribute." end
  elseif k == "rebellion" then
    local names = {}
    for _, n in ipairs(ownedBy(side)) do
      local t = S.terr[n]
      t.stock = math.max(0, t.stock - 25)
      for _, item in ipairs(t.q or {}) do item.halt = true end
      table.insert(names, NAMES[n])
    end
    d = "Every Evil territory loses 25 RP, and construction in Evil land is paused this turn."
  elseif k == "paranoia" then
    local a = pick(readyArmies(side))
    if a then
      -- the nearest Good army, by territories
      local dist = { [a.loc] = 0 }
      local q, qi = { a.loc }, 1
      while q[qi] do local n = q[qi]; qi = qi + 1; for _, m in ipairs(ADJ[n] or {}) do if dist[m] == nil then dist[m] = dist[n] + 1; table.insert(q, m) end end end
      local best, bd
      for _, g in ipairs(readyArmies("Good")) do if dist[g.loc] and (bd == nil or dist[g.loc] < bd) then best, bd = g, dist[g.loc] end end
      if best then
        a.paranoiaT, a.paranoiaTarget = S.turn, best.id
        info.army, info.n = a.id, a.loc
        d = pubName(a) .. " must march as close as it can to " .. pubName(best) .. " at " .. NAMES[best.loc] .. " this turn, and attack it if it can reach it."
      else d = "There is no Good army for " .. pubName(a) .. " to hunt." end
    else d = "Evil has no army to send." end
  elseif k == "ritual" then
    ev.pending = "ritual"
    d = "Evil may attempt the Dark Ritual (Turn tab): D6, on a 3+ every Evil army has -3 Courage this turn, otherwise no Evil army can move this turn."
  elseif k == "eclipse" then
    S.eclipse = { t = S.turn, used = false }
    d = "The first army Evil moves this turn gets +1 movement."
  end
  ev.detail = d
  return ev
end

-- After priority is decided: the winner rolls for events.
function rollEvents()
  if not eventsOn() or not S.prio then return end
  if S.events and S.events.t == S.turn then return end
  local roller = S.prio.winner or "Good"
  local r = d6(roller, "Random events")
  S.events = { t = S.turn, roller = roller, roll = r }
  S.eventLog = S.eventLog or {}
  table.insert(S.eventLog, { t = S.turn, roller = roller, roll = r })
  while #S.eventLog > 120 do table.remove(S.eventLog, 1) end
  if r >= 4 then
    clog("Random events: " .. roller .. " rolled " .. r .. ". Nothing happens this turn.", nil, "territories")
    return
  end
  local tables = EVENT_TABLES[S.settings.theatre or "Eriador"]
  clog("Random events: " .. roller .. " rolled " .. r .. "! Both sides roll on their Random Event tables.", nil, "territories")
  for _, side in ipairs(SIDES) do
    local list = tables[side] or {}
    local ev
    for _ = 1, 10 do   -- Missive from Mordor only happens once: roll again if it comes up a second time
      local def = list[dN(#list, side, "Event table")]
      if not ((def.key == "missive" and S.missive) or (def.key == "king" and S.kingDone)) then ev = def; break end
    end
    if ev then
      local e = apply(side, { key = ev.key, name = ev.name, text = ev.text })
      S.events[side] = e
      eventRecord(side, e)
      clog(side .. " event - " .. e.name .. ": " .. (e.detail or ""), nil, "territories")
    end
  end
end

-- Keep this turn's entry in the event history up to date (choices change the outcome).
function eventRecord(side, e)
  S.eventLog = S.eventLog or {}
  local last = S.eventLog[#S.eventLog]
  if not last or last.t ~= S.turn then last = { t = S.turn, roller = side, roll = 1 }; table.insert(S.eventLog, last) end
  last[side] = { key = e.key, name = e.name, detail = e.detail, info = e.info }
end

-- Run one particular event now (for testing, or a host settling a dispute).
function applyEventKey(side, key)
  for _, def in ipairs((EVENT_TABLES[S.settings.theatre or "Eriador"] or {})[side] or {}) do
    if def.key == key then
      S.events = (S.events and S.events.t == S.turn) and S.events or { t = S.turn, roller = side, roll = 1 }
      local e = apply(side, { key = def.key, name = def.name, text = def.text })
      S.events[side] = e
      eventRecord(side, e)
      clog(side .. " event - " .. e.name .. ": " .. (e.detail or ""), nil, "territories")
      return e
    end
  end
end

-- ---------- choices some events offer ----------
function hireMercs(side, n, d)
  d = d or {}
  local e = S.events and S.events.t == S.turn and S.events[side]
  if not e or e.pending ~= "mercs" then return "There's no mercenary company to hire right now." end
  n = tonumber(n)
  if not n or S.terr[n].owner ~= side then return "Choose one of your territories." end
  local cost = ARMY_COST[400][1]
  if S.terr[n].stock < cost then return NAMES[n] .. " needs " .. cost .. " RP." end
  S.terr[n].stock = S.terr[n].stock - cost
  local name = d.name
  local a = { id = newId(), side = side, name = (name and name ~= "") and name or ("Mercenary Company of " .. NAMES[n]), size = 400,
              leader = d.leader or "", named = d.named and true or false, list = d.list or "", merc = true, cavalry = d.cavalry and true or false,
              canMarch = d.canMarch and true or false, allied = false, units = d.units or "", roster = d.roster,
              loc = n, status = "ready", buildLeft = 0, damage = 0, carried = 0, paid = cost, raisedAt = n, seenLeader = {}, seenUnits = {}, wounds = {} }
  table.insert(S.armies, a)
  numberArmy(a)
  e.pending = nil
  e.detail = pubName(a) .. " was hired at " .. NAMES[n] .. " for " .. cost .. " RP and is ready."
  e.info = e.info or {}; e.info.n = n; e.info.army = a.id
  eventRecord(side, e)
  clog(side .. " hired " .. pubName(a) .. " (400 pts, Mercenary) at " .. tname(n) .. " for " .. cost .. " RP.", nil, "armies")
  pieceHook("created", "army", a)
  return true
end

-- Eorl's Birthday, Fell Speech of Saruman, Goblins Come: the side's choice
function eventChoose(side, payN, armyId)
  local e = S.events and S.events.t == S.turn and S.events[side]
  if not e or not (e.pending == "eorl" or e.pending == "saruman" or e.pending == "goblinraid" or e.pending == "king" or e.pending == "sauronwill" or e.pending == "ritual") then return "There's nothing to choose right now." end
  payN = tonumber(payN)
  local a = armyId and armyById(tonumber(armyId)) or nil
  if e.pending == "eorl" or e.pending == "goblinraid" then
    if not payN or not S.terr[payN] or S.terr[payN].owner ~= side then return "Choose one of your territories to pay from." end
    if S.terr[payN].stock < 100 then return NAMES[payN] .. " doesn't have 100 RP." end
  end
  if e.pending == "saruman" or e.pending == "goblinraid" then
    if not a or a.side ~= "Good" or a.status == "building" then return "Choose a Good army." end
    if e.pending == "goblinraid" and S.terr[a.loc].owner ~= "Good" then return "The Goblins can only raid a Good army standing in a Good territory." end
  end
  if e.pending == "sauronwill" and (not a or a.side ~= "Evil" or a.status == "building") then return "Choose one of Evil's armies." end
  e.info = e.info or {}
  if e.pending == "king" then
    local cap = capitalOf("Good")
    local g = { id = newId(), side = "Good", name = "Men of the West", size = 400, leader = "Aragorn", named = true, list = "Men of the West",
                merc = false, cavalry = false, canMarch = true, allied = false, units = "", loc = cap, status = "ready", buildLeft = 0, damage = 0,
                carried = 0, seenLeader = {}, seenUnits = {}, wounds = {} }
    table.insert(S.armies, g)
    numberArmy(g)
    pieceHook("created", "army", g)
    S.kingDone, S.noDenethor = true, true
    e.info.n, e.info.army = cap, g.id
    e.detail = "The King has returned: a 400 point Men of the West army led by Aragorn stands ready at " .. NAMES[cap] .. ". No army may contain Denethor from now on."
    e.pending = nil
    eventRecord(side, e)
    clog("Good event - " .. e.name .. ": " .. e.detail, nil, "armies")
    return true
  elseif e.pending == "sauronwill" then
    a.willT = S.turn
    e.info.army, e.info.n = a.id, a.loc
    e.detail = pubName(a) .. " has +1 movement this turn, and a free army-wide Heroic March on turn 1 of any battle it fights."
    e.pending = nil
    eventRecord(side, e)
    clog("Evil event - " .. e.name .. ": " .. e.detail, nil, "armies")
    return true
  elseif e.pending == "ritual" then
    local r = d6(side, "Dark Rituals")
    if r >= 3 then S.ritualT = S.turn; e.detail = "Evil rolled " .. r .. ": the ritual succeeds. Every Evil army has -3 Courage in battles this turn."
    else
      for _, x in ipairs(S.armies) do if x.side == "Evil" then x.noMoveTurn, x.noMoveWhy = S.turn, "the Dark Ritual went wrong" end end
      e.detail = "Evil rolled " .. r .. ": the ritual fails. No Evil army can move this turn."
    end
    e.pending = nil
    eventRecord(side, e)
    clog("Evil event - " .. e.name .. ": " .. e.detail, nil, "armies")
    return true
  end
  if e.pending == "eorl" then
    S.terr[payN].stock = S.terr[payN].stock - 100
    S.eorl = S.turn
    e.info.n = payN
    e.detail = "Good spent 100 RP from " .. NAMES[payN] .. " on the feast: every Good army has -2 Courage in battles this turn."
  elseif e.pending == "saruman" then
    a.noMoveTurn, a.noMoveWhy = S.turn, "Saruman's voice holds it (Fell Speech of Saruman)"
    e.info.army, e.info.n = a.id, a.loc
    e.detail = pubName(a) .. " at " .. NAMES[a.loc] .. " cannot move this turn."
  else
    S.terr[payN].stock = S.terr[payN].stock - 100
    a.damage = math.min(75, (a.damage or 0) + 25)
    e.info.army, e.info.n = a.id, a.loc
    e.detail = "Evil paid the Goblins 100 RP from " .. NAMES[payN] .. ". They raided " .. pubName(a) .. " at " .. NAMES[a.loc] .. ": 25% casualties (now -" .. a.damage .. "%)."
  end
  e.pending = nil
  eventRecord(side, e)
  clog(side .. " event - " .. e.name .. ": " .. e.detail, nil, "armies")
  return true
end

function declineEvent(side)
  local e = S.events and S.events.t == S.turn and S.events[side]
  if not e or not e.pending then return "Nothing to decide." end
  if e.pending == "troll" then
    local a = armyById(e.army)
    e.detail = (a and pubName(a) or "The army") .. " left the Troll hoard alone."
  elseif e.pending == "eorl" then e.detail = "Good kept its RP; the birthday passed quietly."
  elseif e.pending == "saruman" then e.detail = "Saruman held his tongue this turn."
  elseif e.pending == "goblinraid" then e.detail = "Evil sent the Goblins away unpaid."
  elseif e.pending == "king" then e.detail = "Aragorn bides his time; the King has not yet returned."
  elseif e.pending == "sauronwill" then e.detail = "Sauron's will passed unused."
  elseif e.pending == "ritual" then e.detail = "Evil left the Dark Ritual alone."
  else e.detail = "The mercenaries moved on." end
  e.pending = nil
  eventRecord(side, e)
  clog(side .. ": " .. e.detail, nil, "armies")
  return true
end

function raidTrollHoard(side)
  local e = S.events and S.events.t == S.turn and S.events[side]
  if not e or e.pending ~= "troll" then return "There's no hoard to raid." end
  local a = armyById(e.army)
  e.pending = nil
  if not a then e.detail = "The army is gone."; return true end
  local r = d6(side, "Troll Hoard")
  if r >= 4 then
    local room = math.max(0, a.size - (a.carried or 0))
    local got = math.min(200, room)
    a.carried = (a.carried or 0) + got
    e.detail = pubName(a) .. " raided the hoard (rolled " .. r .. ") and gained 200 RP" .. ((got < 200) and (" (carried " .. got .. ", the rest left behind)") or "") .. "."
  else
    a.damage = math.min(75, (a.damage or 0) + 25)
    a.forceRetreat = S.turn
    e.detail = pubName(a) .. " raided the hoard (rolled " .. r .. ") and the Trolls woke: 25% casualties, and it must retreat in the Retreat stage."
  end
  eventRecord(side, e)
  clog(e.detail, nil, "armies")
  return true
end

end

--[[ ===================================================================
     WAR COUNCIL - physical dice
     Every roll is made with real dice in the "Dice Tray". An action that needs dice is first run
     quietly to find out which dice it needs; those dice are thrown (by the War Council, or by the
     player when their side is set to "players throw"); then the action runs for real using the faces
     that came up. Settings > Display chooses digital dice or physical dice per side.
     =================================================================== ]]

DICE = { mode = "live", queue = {}, qi = 0, want = {}, short = false, busy = false, pending = nil }
QUIET = false            -- true while an action is being run just to count its dice: no pieces, no messages
DICE_TAG = "wc:die"
DIE_RGB = { Good = { 0.20, 0.52, 0.30 }, Evil = { 0.62, 0.16, 0.13 }, Neutral = { 0.55, 0.50, 0.42 } }

-- The one place a die is rolled. side = whose die it is; why = what it's for (shown on the die and in prompts).
-- Every die that counts is remembered (for the Chronicle).
local function logDie(sides, side, why, v)
  if QUIET or not S then return v end
  S.diceLog = S.diceLog or {}
  table.insert(S.diceLog, { t = S.turn, s = side or "Neutral", w = why or "Roll", n = sides, v = v })
  while #S.diceLog > 400 do table.remove(S.diceLog, 1) end
  return v
end

function dN(sides, side, why)
  if DICE.mode == "replay" then
    DICE.qi = DICE.qi + 1
    local v = DICE.queue[DICE.qi]
    if v and v.sides == sides then return logDie(sides, side, why, v.value) end
    -- dice beyond what has been thrown: collect one kind of roll at a time (later rolls may depend on these)
    DICE.short = true
    why = why or "Roll"
    DICE.batch = DICE.batch or why
    if why == DICE.batch then table.insert(DICE.want, { sides = sides, side = side or "Neutral", why = why }) end
    return logDie(sides, side, why, math.random(1, sides))
  end
  return logDie(sides, side, why, math.random(1, sides))
end
function d6(side, why) return dN(6, side, why) end

function physicalDice() return S and S.started and S.settings.dice ~= "digital" end
function diceMode(side) return ((S.settings.diceBy or {})[side]) or "auto" end   -- "auto" = War Council throws, "player" = the player throws

do  -- keep helpers out of the main chunk (Lua allows 200 locals there)

-- quiet passes don't talk
local rawBroadcast, rawBroadcastColor = broadcastToAll, broadcastToColor
broadcastToAll = function(...) if QUIET then return end return rawBroadcast(...) end
broadcastToColor = function(...) if QUIET then return end return rawBroadcastColor(...) end

local function tray()
  for _, o in ipairs(getAllObjects()) do
    if string.lower(o.getName() or "") == "dice tray" then return o end
  end
  return nil
end

local function trayPoint(i, n)
  local t = tray()
  if t then
    local b = t.getBounds()
    local w, d = (b.size.x or 6) * 0.55, (b.size.z or 6) * 0.55
    local cols = math.max(1, math.ceil(math.sqrt(n)))
    local cx = ((i - 1) % cols + 0.5) / cols - 0.5
    local cz = (math.floor((i - 1) / cols) + 0.5) / math.ceil(n / cols) - 0.5
    return { b.center.x + cx * w, b.center.y + (b.size.y or 1) / 2 + 2.5, b.center.z + cz * d }
  end
  -- no tray: beside the War Council
  local ok, p = pcall(function() return self.getPosition() end)
  if not ok or not p then p = { x = 0, y = 1, z = -36 } end
  return { p.x + 6 + (i - 1) * 1.6, p.y + 3, p.z }
end

local function clearDice()
  for _, o in ipairs(getAllObjects()) do
    if (o.getGMNotes and o.getGMNotes() or "") == DICE_TAG then o.destruct() end
  end
end

-- A D6 resting on an edge or corner: its tilt isn't close to a flat face.
local function cocked(o, sides)
  if sides ~= 6 then return false end
  local r = o.getRotation()
  local function off(a) local m = a % 90; return math.min(m, 90 - m) end
  return off(r.x) > 12 or off(r.z) > 12
end

local function sideColor(side) return (side == "Good" and S.settings.goodColor) or (side == "Evil" and S.settings.evilColor) or nil end

-- Throw (or ask for) the dice in `reqs`; done(values) gets one number per request, in order.
local function throwDice(reqs, done)
  clearDice()
  local dice = {}
  DICE.pending = { list = dice, why = {} }
  for i, q in ipairs(reqs) do
    local d = { sides = q.sides, side = q.side, why = q.why, auto = diceMode(q.side) == "auto", thrown = false, value = nil, settleAt = nil }
    table.insert(dice, d)
    local pos = trayPoint(i, #reqs)
    local ok = pcall(function()
      d.obj = spawnObject({ type = (q.sides == 10) and "Die_10" or "Die_6", position = pos, rotation = { math.random(0, 359), math.random(0, 359), math.random(0, 359) },
                            callback_function = function(o)
                              o.setGMNotes(DICE_TAG)
                              o.setName(q.side .. " D" .. q.sides .. " · " .. q.why)
                              o.setColorTint(DIE_RGB[q.side] or DIE_RGB.Neutral)
                              if d.auto then d.thrown = true; o.roll() end
                            end })
    end)
    if not ok or not d.obj then d.value = math.random(1, q.sides); d.digital = true end
  end
  -- who needs to throw
  local asks = {}
  for _, d in ipairs(dice) do if not d.auto and not d.digital then asks[d.side] = (asks[d.side] or 0) + 1 end end
  for side, n in pairs(asks) do
    local c = sideColor(side)
    local msg = side .. ": throw " .. n .. " die" .. ((n == 1) and "" or "s") .. " in the Dice Tray (pick each up and drop it, or hover and press R)."
    if c then pcall(function() broadcastToColor(msg, c, { 0.95, 0.85, 0.55 }) end) end
    pcall(function() broadcastToAll(msg, { 0.95, 0.85, 0.55 }) end)
  end
  if rebuild then pcall(rebuild) end
  local waited = 0
  local function poll()
    waited = waited + 0.25
    local all = true
    for _, d in ipairs(dice) do
      if not d.value then
        local o = d.obj
        if o == nil then d.value = math.random(1, d.sides); d.digital = true
        else
          if o.held_by_color then d.thrown = true; d.settleAt = nil end
          if d.thrown and o.resting and not o.held_by_color and not o.isSmoothMoving() then
            if cocked(o, d.sides) then o.roll()
            else
              d.settleAt = d.settleAt or waited
              if waited - d.settleAt >= 0.5 then
                local v = tonumber(o.getValue()) or math.random(1, d.sides)
                if d.sides == 10 and v == 0 then v = 10 end
                d.value = math.max(1, math.min(d.sides, v))
                pcall(function() local p = o.getPosition(); floatText(p, tostring(d.value), DIE_RGB[d.side] or DIE_RGB.Neutral) end)
              end
            end
          else
            d.settleAt = nil
          end
          -- the War Council's own dice that refuse to settle get thrown again
          if d.auto and d.thrown and not d.value and waited > 12 and (math.floor(waited * 4) % 40 == 0) then o.roll() end
        end
      end
      if not d.value then all = false end
    end
    if all then
      local vals = {}
      for i, d in ipairs(dice) do vals[i] = d.value end
      DICE.pending = nil
      Wait.time(function() done(vals) end, 0.4)
      return
    end
    Wait.time(poll, 0.25)
  end
  Wait.time(poll, 0.5)
end

-- "Throw for me": the War Council throws a side's waiting dice.
function throwForMe(side)
  local p = DICE.pending
  if not p then return "No dice are waiting." end
  local n = 0
  for _, d in ipairs(p.list) do
    if not d.value and not d.thrown and (side == nil or d.side == side) and d.obj then d.thrown = true; d.obj.roll(); n = n + 1 end
  end
  return (n > 0) or "Nothing to throw."
end

-- The waiting dice, for the panel: { side, sides, why, thrown } for each one not yet read.
function diceWaiting()
  local out = {}
  for _, d in ipairs((DICE.pending and DICE.pending.list) or {}) do
    if not d.value then table.insert(out, { side = d.side, sides = d.sides, why = d.why, thrown = d.thrown, auto = d.auto }) end
  end
  return out
end

-- Called by a player pressing R on a die (TTS randomise).
function diceRandomized(obj, color)
  for _, d in ipairs((DICE.pending and DICE.pending.list) or {}) do
    if d.obj == obj then d.thrown = true; d.settleAt = nil end
  end
end

-- Run fn with physical dice. fn may run several times: quietly to learn which dice it needs, then for real.
function withDice(fn)
  if not physicalDice() or DICE.inside then return fn() end
  if DICE.busy then broadcastToAll("The dice are still rolling - wait a moment.", { 0.95, 0.75, 0.45 }); return end
  local snap = stateSnap()
  -- the panels' forms too: a quiet pass that succeeds clears its form (winner, target...), and the real pass needs it back
  local function copyT(t, seen)
    if type(t) ~= "table" then return t end
    seen = seen or {}
    if seen[t] then return seen[t] end
    local o = {}
    seen[t] = o
    for k, v in pairs(t) do o[k] = copyT(v, seen) end
    return o
  end
  local uSnap = copyT(U)
  local undoLen = #UNDO
  local queue = {}
  local function pass(quiet)
    DICE.mode, DICE.queue, DICE.qi, DICE.want, DICE.short, DICE.batch = "replay", queue, 0, {}, false, nil
    DICE.inside, QUIET = true, quiet
    local ok, err = pcall(fn)
    DICE.inside, QUIET, DICE.mode = false, false, "live"
    if not ok then print("War Council dice: " .. tostring(err)) end
    return DICE.want
  end
  local function restore()
    local okR, data = pcall(stateRestore, snap)
    if okR and type(data) == "table" then S = data end
    if type(uSnap) == "table" then
      local fresh = copyT(uSnap)
      for k in pairs(U) do U[k] = nil end
      for k, v in pairs(fresh) do U[k] = v end
    end
    while #UNDO > undoLen do table.remove(UNDO) end
  end
  local function step()
    local want = pass(true)
    restore()
    if #want == 0 then
      DICE.busy = false
      pass(false)                              -- the real run, with every die already thrown
      return
    end
    DICE.busy = true
    throwDice(want, function(vals)
      for i, q in ipairs(want) do table.insert(queue, { sides = q.sides, value = vals[i] }) end
      step()                                   -- a tie or a follow-up roll may need more dice
    end)
  end
  local ok, err = pcall(step)
  if not ok then DICE.busy = false; print("War Council dice: " .. tostring(err)); fn() end
end

end

     WAR COUNCIL - Part 3: physical pieces on the map
       * Finished buildings are copied from template objects onto building slots.
       * Armies and agents are linked to tokens; dropping a token in a territory is a move.
     =================================================================== ]]
