  { buildings = { {0.75846, 0.80373}, {0.72201, 0.81671}, {0.74284, 0.82320}, {0.76367, 0.82968}, {0.77930, 0.81022}, {0.70508, 0.84590}, {0.72721, 0.84266}, {0.78451, 0.83617}, {0.80534, 0.84266}, {0.77409, 0.88159}, {0.78971, 0.86212}, {0.80404, 0.88321} }, units = { {0.75326, 0.87510}, {0.74805, 0.84915}, {0.76888, 0.85564}, {0.73242, 0.86861} } }
}
local NB_SLOTS, NU_SLOTS = 12, 4

local TAG_PREFIX = "wc:"
local warnedMissing = {}
local editMarkers = {}
local pollBusy = false

-- ===================================================================
-- tags and lookup
-- ===================================================================
local function buildingTag(n, key) return "wc:b:" .. n .. ":" .. key end
local function tokenTag(kind, ent) return "wc:" .. ((kind == "army") and "a" or "g") .. ":" .. ent.id end

-- Snapshot of all objects, safe to destroy from while looping.
local function allObjects()
  local out = {}
  for _, o in ipairs(getObjects()) do table.insert(out, o) end
  return out
end

local function isOurs(obj)
  local g = obj.getGMNotes and obj.getGMNotes() or ""
  return string.sub(g, 1, #TAG_PREFIX) == TAG_PREFIX
end

function findByTag(tag)
  for _, o in ipairs(allObjects()) do
    if o.getGMNotes and o.getGMNotes() == tag then return o end
  end
end

-- A template is any object with one of these names that we didn't spawn ourselves.
function findTemplate(names)
  local objs = allObjects()
  for _, want in ipairs(names) do
    local lw = string.lower(want)
    for _, o in ipairs(objs) do
      if o ~= self and not isOurs(o) and string.lower(o.getName() or "") == lw then return o end
    end
  end
end

function buildingTemplateNames(key) return { BUILDING_BY_KEY[key].name } end

function tokenTemplateNames(kind, ent)
  if kind == "army" then return { ent.side .. " Army", "Army" } end
  return { ent.side .. " " .. ent.type, ent.type }
end

-- ===================================================================
-- slots
-- ===================================================================
function slotUV(n, kind, i)
  local ov = S.slotOv[n]
  local list = ov and ov[kind]
  if list and (list[i] or list[tostring(i)]) then
    local p = list[i] or list[tostring(i)]
    return p[1] or p["1"], p[2] or p["2"]
  end
  local p = SLOTS[n][kind][i]
  return p[1], p[2]
end

function slotWorld(n, kind, i)
  local b = getBoard()
  if not b then return nil end
  local u, v = slotUV(n, kind, i)
  return b.call("uvToWorld", { u = u, v = v })
end

-- Building slots are handed out in construction order. Overflow shares a slot, offset sideways.
function assignBuildingSlot(n, key)
  local t = S.terr[n]
  if t.bslot[key] then return t.bslot[key] end
  local used = {}
  for _, i in pairs(t.bslot) do used[i] = (used[i] or 0) + 1 end
  for i = 1, NB_SLOTS do
    if not used[i] then t.bslot[key] = i; return i end
  end
  local count = 0
  for _ in pairs(t.bslot) do count = count + 1 end
  t.bslot[key] = (count % NB_SLOTS) + 1
  return t.bslot[key]
end

local function buildingWorld(n, key)
  local i = assignBuildingSlot(n, key)
  local p = slotWorld(n, "buildings", i)
  if not p then return nil end
  local shared = 0
  for k, j in pairs(S.terr[n].bslot) do
    if j == i and k ~= key and k < key then shared = shared + 1 end
  end
  if shared > 0 then p = { x = p.x + 0.9 * shared, y = p.y, z = p.z } end
  return p
end

-- ===================================================================
-- spawning
-- ===================================================================
local function seat(o, surfaceY, lock)
  -- Sit the object's base on the board surface.
  local b = o.getBounds()
  local bottom = b.center.y - b.size.y / 2
  local p = o.getPosition()
  o.setPosition({ p.x, p.y + (surfaceY - bottom) + 0.02, p.z })
  if lock ~= nil then o.setLock(lock) end
end

-- Copy a template with our tag written into every state (so flipping states keeps the link).
function spawnCopy(template, pos, tag, opts)
  opts = opts or {}
  local data = template.getData()
  data.GMNotes = tag
  data.Locked = opts.lock and true or false
  if opts.name and not data.States then data.Nickname = opts.name end
  if opts.desc and not data.States then data.Description = opts.desc end
  if data.States then
    for _, st in pairs(data.States) do st.GMNotes = tag end
  end
  local surfaceY = pos.y
  local spawnPos = { pos.x, pos.y + 2, pos.z }
  local o = spawnObjectData({
    data = data, position = spawnPos, rotation = template.getRotation(),
    callback_function = function(obj) seat(obj, surfaceY, opts.lock and true or false); if opts.done then opts.done(obj) end end,
  })
  return o
end

local function warnOnce(what)
  if warnedMissing[what] then return end
  warnedMissing[what] = true
  broadcastToAll("War Council: no template object named '" .. what .. "' found - skipping that piece. (Settings lists the template names.)", { 0.95, 0.75, 0.45 })
end

-- ===================================================================
-- buildings
-- ===================================================================
function placeBuilding(n, key)
  local tag = buildingTag(n, key)
  local pos = buildingWorld(n, key)
  if not pos then return end
  local existing = findByTag(tag)
  if existing then
    existing.setLock(true)
    existing.setPositionSmooth({ pos.x, existing.getPosition().y, pos.z }, false, true)
    return existing
  end
  local tpl = findTemplate(buildingTemplateNames(key))
  if not tpl then
    if key ~= "roads" then warnOnce(BUILDING_BY_KEY[key].name) end   -- roads are drawn on the map, a piece is optional
    return
  end
  local b = BUILDING_BY_KEY[key]
  return spawnCopy(tpl, pos, tag, { lock = true, name = b.name,
    desc = b.name .. " - " .. tname(n) .. "\n" .. b.rules })
end

-- Make the table match the ledger: every finished building has its piece, nothing extra.
function reconcileBuildings()
  local want = {}
  for n, t in ipairs(S.terr) do
    for key, has in pairs(t.b) do
      if has then
        want[buildingTag(n, key)] = true
        if not findByTag(buildingTag(n, key)) then placeBuilding(n, key) end
      end
    end
  end
  for _, o in ipairs(allObjects()) do
    local g = o.getGMNotes and o.getGMNotes() or ""
    if string.sub(g, 1, 5) == "wc:b:" and not want[g] then o.destruct() end
  end
end

-- Place any finished building whose piece is missing, as soon as a template with its name appears on the table.
-- Pieces already known are checked by their GUID, so the table is only searched when one is actually missing.
BUILD_GUID, PC = {}, { lastMissing = nil, pending = {}, objDirty = true, buildDirty = true, tickN = 0, sweepPhase = 0 }
function fillMissingBuildings(force)
  if not S or not S.started then return end
  local missing = {}
  for n, t in ipairs(S.terr) do
    for key, has in pairs(t.b) do
      if has then
        local tag = buildingTag(n, key)
        local g = BUILD_GUID[tag]
        local o = g and getObjectFromGUID(g)
        if not (o and o.getGMNotes() == tag) then table.insert(missing, tag) end
      end
    end
  end
  if #missing == 0 then PC.lastMissing = nil; return end
  local sig = table.concat(missing, ",")
  if sig == PC.lastMissing and not force then return end   -- same pieces missing as last time, nothing new on the table
  PC.lastMissing = sig
  local have, names = {}, {}
  for _, o in ipairs(allObjects()) do
    local g = o.getGMNotes and o.getGMNotes() or ""
    if string.sub(g, 1, 5) == "wc:b:" then have[g] = true; BUILD_GUID[g] = o.getGUID()
    elseif string.sub(g, 1, 3) ~= "wc:" then names[string.lower(o.getName() or "")] = true end
  end
  for n, t in ipairs(S.terr) do
    for key, has in pairs(t.b) do
      if has and not have[buildingTag(n, key)] then
        local bname = BUILDING_BY_KEY[key].name
        if names[string.lower(bname)] then
          warnedMissing[bname] = nil
          local o = placeBuilding(n, key)
          if o then pcall(function() BUILD_GUID[buildingTag(n, key)] = o.getGUID() end) end
        end
      end
    end
  end
end

-- ===================================================================
-- tokens (armies and agents)
-- ===================================================================
local function entLoc(kind, ent)
  if kind == "army" then return ent.loc end
  return agentLoc(ent)
end

-- Tokens searched for and not found (in a bag, deleted): not searched again until something new appears on the table.
TOKEN_MISS = {}
function tokenObject(kind, ent)
  if not ent.tok then return nil end
  local tag = tokenTag(kind, ent)
  local o = ent.tok.guid and getObjectFromGUID(ent.tok.guid)
  if o and o.getGMNotes() == tag then return o end
  if TOKEN_MISS[tag] then return nil end
  o = findByTag(tag)
  if o then ent.tok.guid = o.getGUID() else TOKEN_MISS[tag] = true end
  return o
end

-- First free unit slot in a territory (not occupied by another tracked token).
function freeUnitSlot(n, except)
  local taken = {}
  local function mark(kind, list)
    for _, e in ipairs(list) do
      if e ~= except and e.tok then
        local o = tokenObject(kind, e)
        if o then
          for i = 1, NU_SLOTS do
            local p = slotWorld(n, "units", i)
            local q = o.getPosition()
            if p and (p.x - q.x) ^ 2 + (p.z - q.z) ^ 2 < 0.36 then taken[i] = true end
          end
        end
      end
    end
  end
  mark("army", S.armies)
  mark("agent", S.agents)
  for i = 1, NU_SLOTS do if not taken[i] then return slotWorld(n, "units", i) end end
  local p = slotWorld(n, "units", 1)
  return p and { x = p.x + 0.5, y = p.y, z = p.z + 0.5 }
end

function placeToken(kind, ent)
  local o = tokenObject(kind, ent)
  local n = entLoc(kind, ent)
  if not o or not n then return end
  local p = freeUnitSlot(n, ent)
  if not p then return end
  local y = o.getPosition().y
  o.setPositionSmooth({ p.x, math.max(y, p.y + 0.5), p.z }, false, true)
  ent.tok.pos = { p.x, p.z }
  ent.tok.terr = n
end

function linkToken(kind, ent, o)
  ent.noAutoLink = nil
  ent.tok = { guid = o.getGUID(), spawned = false }
  o.setGMNotes(tokenTag(kind, ent))
  local p = o.getPosition()
  ent.tok.pos = { p.x, p.z }
  local b = getBoard()
  ent.tok.terr = b and b.call("territoryAt", { position = p }) or nil
  return true
end

function spawnToken(kind, ent)
  if kind == "agent" and ent.type == "Surgeon" then return "Surgeons travel with their army and don't need a token." end
  local n = entLoc(kind, ent)
  local tpl = findTemplate(tokenTemplateNames(kind, ent))
  if not tpl then return "No template found. Name an object '" .. tokenTemplateNames(kind, ent)[1] .. "' (or '" .. tokenTemplateNames(kind, ent)[2] .. "')." end
  local p = freeUnitSlot(n, ent)
  if not p then return "Couldn't find the map board." end
  local name = entName(kind, ent)
  ent.tok = { guid = nil, spawned = true, pos = { p.x, p.z }, terr = n }
  local o = spawnCopy(tpl, p, tokenTag(kind, ent), { lock = false, name = name,
    done = function(obj) if ent.tok then ent.tok.guid = obj.getGUID() end end })
  if o then ent.tok.guid = o.getGUID() end
  return true
end

function unlinkToken(kind, ent)
  local o = tokenObject(kind, ent)
  if o and o.getGMNotes() == tokenTag(kind, ent) then o.setGMNotes("") end
  ent.tok = nil
  ent.noAutoLink = true
  return true
end

-- What a token is called on the table: an army shows its public number until its leader is revealed.
function entName(kind, ent)
  if kind == "army" then return armyRevealed(ent) and ent.name or publicName(ent) end
  return ent.name or (ent.side .. " " .. ent.type .. " #" .. ent.id)
end

function stateList(o)
  local ok, st = pcall(function() return o.getStates() end)
  return (ok and type(st) == "table") and st or {}
end

-- An untagged object named exactly like this army/agent, if there is one. For an army, a model whose
-- other state carries the army's name counts too (state 1 "Good Army 3", state 2 "I Arnorian Legion").
function findNamedToken(kind, ent)
  local want = string.lower((kind == "army") and ent.name or entName(kind, ent))
  for _, o in ipairs(allObjects()) do
    if not isOurs(o) then
      if string.lower(o.getName() or "") == want then return o end
      if kind == "army" then
        for _, st in ipairs(stateList(o)) do if string.lower(st.name or "") == want then return o end end
      end
    end
  end
end

-- Show an army's model as the enemy knows it: the numbered state until its leader is revealed, then the
-- state named after the army. Single-state tokens are simply renamed.
function armyLook(a)
  if QUIET or not a.tok or a.tok.animating then return end
  local o = tokenObject("army", a)
  if not o or o.held_by_color ~= nil then return end
  local rv, pub = armyRevealed(a), publicName(a)
  local key = o.getGUID() .. (rv and ":r:" or ":h:") .. pub .. ":" .. a.name
  if a.tok.look == key then return end
  local others = stateList(o)
  if #others == 0 then
    local want = rv and a.name or pub
    if o.getName() ~= want then o.setName(want) end
    a.tok.look = key
    return
  end
  local cur = o.getStateId()
  local lw = string.lower(a.name)
  local realId = (string.lower(o.getName() or "") == lw) and cur or nil
  for _, st in ipairs(others) do if string.lower(st.name or "") == lw then realId = st.id end end
  realId = realId or 2
  local want = rv and realId or ((realId == 1) and 2 or 1)
  if cur ~= want then
    local n2 = o.setState(want)
    if n2 then
      a.tok.guid = n2.getGUID()
      n2.setGMNotes(tokenTag("army", a))
      if not rv then n2.setName(pub) end
      a.tok.look = n2.getGUID() .. (rv and ":r:" or ":h:") .. pub .. ":" .. a.name
    end
  else
    if not rv and o.getName() ~= pub then o.setName(pub) end
    if rv and cur == realId and o.getName() ~= a.name then o.setName(a.name) end   -- renamed since
    a.tok.look = key
  end
end

-- Name match first (your own named token); otherwise copy a template.
local function autoToken(kind, ent)
  if kind == "agent" and ent.type == "Surgeon" then return end
  local o = findNamedToken(kind, ent)
  if o then linkToken(kind, ent, o); return end
  if findTemplate(tokenTemplateNames(kind, ent)) then spawnToken(kind, ent) end
end

-- Armies/agents without a token keep watching for an object named after them. One pass over the table for all of
-- them; a model's other state names are read once per object and remembered.
STATE_NAMES = {}
local function linkByName()
  local cands, anyArmy = {}, false
  for _, spec in ipairs({ { "army", S.armies }, { "agent", S.agents } }) do
    for _, ent in ipairs(spec[2]) do
      if not ent.tok and not ent.noAutoLink and not (spec[1] == "agent" and ent.type == "Surgeon") then
        table.insert(cands, { spec[1], ent })
        if spec[1] == "army" then anyArmy = true end
      end
    end
  end
  if #cands == 0 then return end
  local list, byName = allObjects(), {}
  local free = {}
  for _, o in ipairs(list) do
    if o ~= self and not isOurs(o) then
      free[#free + 1] = o
      local nm = string.lower(o.getName() or "")
      if nm ~= "" and not byName[nm] then byName[nm] = o end
    end
  end
  local taken, linked = {}, false
  for _, c in ipairs(cands) do
    local kind, ent = c[1], c[2]
    local want = string.lower((kind == "army") and ent.name or entName(kind, ent))
    local o = byName[want]
    if o and taken[o] then o = nil end
    if not o and kind == "army" and anyArmy then
      for _, o2 in ipairs(free) do
        if not taken[o2] then
          local g = o2.getGUID()
          local sn = STATE_NAMES[g]
          if sn == nil then
            sn = {}
            for _, st in ipairs(stateList(o2)) do sn[string.lower(st.name or "")] = true end
            STATE_NAMES[g] = sn
          end
          if sn[want] then o = o2; break end
        end
      end
    end
    if o then
      taken[o] = true
      linkToken(kind, ent, o)
      broadcastToAll("War Council: linked " .. ((S.settings.hidden and kind == "army") and "a model" or ("'" .. (o.getName() or "") .. "'")) .. " to " .. entName(kind, ent) .. ".", { 0.6, 0.9, 0.6 })
      if kind == "army" then pcall(armyLook, ent) end
      linked = true
    end
  end
  if linked then rebuild() end
end

-- ===================================================================
-- drop-to-move
-- ===================================================================
local function snapBack(ent, o, why, name)
  if QUIET then return end
  if ent.tok and ent.tok.pos then
    o.setPositionSmooth({ ent.tok.pos[1], o.getPosition().y + 0.3, ent.tok.pos[2] }, false, true)
  end
  broadcastToAll(name .. ": " .. why .. " Token returned.", { 0.95, 0.6, 0.5 })
end

local function handleDrop(kind, ent, o, n)
  local name = (kind == "army") and ent.name or (ent.side .. " " .. ent.type)
  local cur = entLoc(kind, ent)
  local p = o.getPosition()
  if n == cur then
    ent.tok.pos = { p.x, p.z }
    ent.tok.terr = n
    return
  end
  if not S.settings.dropMove then
    ent.tok.pos = { p.x, p.z }
    ent.tok.terr = n
    ent.tok.mismatch = n
    return
  end
  if not n then return snapBack(ent, o, "that isn't inside a territory.", name) end
  local r
  local snap = pushUndo and stateSnap() or nil
  local turnBefore, stageBefore = S.turn, S.stage
  PIECE_DROP = true
  if kind == "army" then
    if ent.needsRetreat then
      r = retreatArmy(ent, n)
    else
      local march = false
      local inRange = false
      for _, x in ipairs(reachable(ent.loc, armyMove(ent) - (ent.slow or 0), false, nil, ent.side)) do if x == n then inRange = true end end
      if not inRange and ent.canMarch then march = true end
      r = moveArmy(ent, n, march)
    end
  else
    r = moveAgent(ent, n)
  end
  PIECE_DROP = false
  if r == true then
    ent.tok.mismatch = nil
    -- put it back where it started and drive it there along the roads, as if moved from the panel
    local start, path = ent.tok.pos, shortestPath(cur, n)
    local drove = false
    if start and cur and #path > 0 and not QUIET then
      ent.tok.terr = cur
      drove = pcall(function()
        o.setPosition({ start[1], p.y, start[2] })
        driveToken(kind, ent, path)
      end)
    end
    if not drove then
      if ent.tok then ent.tok.animating = nil end
      ent.tok.pos = { p.x, p.z }
      ent.tok.terr = n
    end
    if snap then pushUndo(snap, "Token move: " .. name, S.settings.hidden and ent.side or "All") end
    if not QUIET then
      if afterAction then pcall(afterAction, S.settings.hidden and ent.side or "All") end
      rebuild()
    end
  else
    snapBack(ent, o, r, name)
  end
end

-- Has this token been moved by hand since the War Council last saw it? Then treat it as a move.
function checkToken(kind, ent, b, color)
  if not ent.tok then return end
  local o = tokenObject(kind, ent)
  if not (o and not ent.tok.animating and o.held_by_color == nil and o.resting and not o.isSmoothMoving()) then return end
  local p = o.getPosition()
  local last = ent.tok.pos
  if last and (p.x - last[1]) ^ 2 + (p.z - last[2]) ^ 2 <= 0.09 then return end
  local n = b and b.call("territoryAt", { position = p }) or nil
  local id = ent.id
  local prev = ACT_PK
  ACT_PK = color and viewerKeyOf and viewerKeyOf(color) or nil
  -- look the army/agent up again inside: a dice roll may swap in a fresh copy of the campaign state
  withDice(function()
    local e2 = (kind == "army") and armyById(id) or agentById(id)
    if e2 then handleDrop(kind, e2, o, n) end
  end)
  ACT_PK = prev
end

-- Every army model shows the right name and state (cheap when nothing changed).
function lookAll()
  if not S or not S.started or QUIET then return end
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do pcall(armyLook, a) end
end

-- Background checks. The table tells the War Council when something is dropped, spawned or deleted, so it reacts
-- to that instead of looking over every object twice a second. A slow sweep, a part per tick, catches anything
-- the events miss (a model moved with the gizmo, or by another script).
-- PC.pending: tokens just dropped, waiting to come to rest. 4 ticks a second, a sweep every 10 seconds.
PIECE_TICK, SWEEP_TICKS = 0.25, 40

function piecesDirty(objects, buildings)
  if objects then PC.objDirty = true; TOKEN_MISS = {} end
  if buildings then PC.buildDirty = true end
end

-- Everything again, now (the Refresh button).
function piecesRefresh()
  TOKEN_MISS, STATE_NAMES = {}, {}
  PC.objDirty, PC.buildDirty = true, true
  PC.sweepPhase = 1
end

function sweepStep(b)
  if PC.sweepPhase == 1 then
    TOKEN_MISS = {}
    for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do checkToken("army", a, b) end
  elseif PC.sweepPhase == 2 then
    for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do checkToken("agent", g, b) end
  elseif PC.sweepPhase == 3 then
    linkByName()
  elseif PC.sweepPhase == 4 then
    lookAll()
  elseif PC.sweepPhase == 5 then
    fillMissingBuildings(false)
  end
  PC.sweepPhase = (PC.sweepPhase >= 5) and 0 or (PC.sweepPhase + 1)
end

function pieceTick()
  if pollBusy or not S or not S.started or DICE.busy then return end
  if mapMigrating and mapMigrating() then return end
  pollBusy = true
  local ok, err = pcall(function()
    PC.tickN = PC.tickN + 1
    local b = nil
    -- tokens just dropped: once they've settled, see where they landed
    if next(PC.pending) then
      b = getBoard()
      local keys, any = {}, false
      for guid in pairs(PC.pending) do table.insert(keys, guid) end
      for _, guid in ipairs(keys) do
        local p = PC.pending[guid]
        local o = getObjectFromGUID(guid)
        p.n = p.n + 1
        if not o or p.n > 80 then PC.pending[guid] = nil
        elseif o.held_by_color == nil and o.resting and not o.isSmoothMoving() then
          PC.pending[guid] = nil
          local ent = (p.kind == "army") and armyById(p.id) or agentById(p.id)
          if ent then checkToken(p.kind, ent, b, p.color); any = true end
        end
      end
      if any then lookAll() end
      return
    end
    -- something new on the table: a model named after an unlinked army, a building template
    if PC.objDirty then PC.objDirty = false; linkByName(); return end
    if PC.buildDirty then PC.buildDirty = false; fillMissingBuildings(true); return end
    -- the slow sweep
    if PC.sweepPhase == 0 and PC.tickN % SWEEP_TICKS == 0 then PC.sweepPhase = 1 end
    if PC.sweepPhase > 0 then sweepStep(b or getBoard()) end
  end)
  pollBusy = false
  if not ok then print("War Council token check: " .. tostring(err)) end
end

-- The old full check, kept for anything that wants every token looked at right now.
function pollTokens()
  if pollBusy or not S or not S.started or DICE.busy then return end
  local b = getBoard()
  pcall(function()
    TOKEN_MISS = {}
    linkByName()
    for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do checkToken("army", a, b) end
    for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do checkToken("agent", g, b) end
    lookAll()
  end)
end

-- Picking up a linked token outlines where it can go (same rules as the panel's map highlights).
local function entForObject(obj)
  local g = obj and obj.getGMNotes and obj.getGMNotes() or ""
  local k, id = string.match(g, "^wc:([ag]):(%d+)$")
  if not k then return nil end
  if k == "a" then return "army", armyById(tonumber(id)) end
  return "agent", agentById(tonumber(id))
end

local function seatSide(color)
  if color == S.settings.goodColor then return "Good" elseif color == S.settings.evilColor then return "Evil" end
end

function onObjectPickUp(color, obj)
  if not S or not S.started or not showRange then return end
  local kind, ent = entForObject(obj)
  if not ent then return end
  if S.settings.hidden then
    local sd = seatSide(color)
    if sd and sd ~= ent.side then return end
  end
  local list = {}
  local from
  if kind == "army" then
    from = ent.loc
    if ent.needsRetreat then list = retreatOptions(ent)
    elseif not ent.moved or S.settings.override then
      local budget = armyMove(ent) - (ent.slow or 0) + (ent.canMarch and 1 or 0)
      list = moveRangeFor(ent, true, budget)
    end
  else
    if ent.army then return end
    from = ent.loc
    if not ent.moved or S.settings.override then list = moveRangeFor(ent, false, math.max(agentMoveLeft(ent), S.settings.override and AGENTS[ent.type].move or 0)) end
  end
  if from then pcall(showRange, ent.side, from, list, "pick:" .. obj.getGUID() .. ":" .. tostring(from)) end
end

function onObjectDrop(color, obj)
  if not S then return end
  local kind, ent = entForObject(obj)
  if not ent then PC.objDirty = true; return end   -- maybe a model just renamed after an army
  PC.pending[obj.getGUID()] = { kind = kind, id = ent.id, color = color, n = 0 }
  Wait.time(function()
    if updateHints and LAST_HINT_PK then pcall(updateHints, LAST_HINT_PK) elseif clearHints then pcall(clearHints) end
  end, 1.2)
end

-- A flipped state is a new object: re-point the link and re-apply our tag.
function onObjectSpawn(obj)
  PC.objDirty, PC.buildDirty = true, true
  TOKEN_MISS = {}
end

function onObjectDestroy(obj)
  PC.buildDirty = true
  pcall(function() STATE_NAMES[obj.getGUID()] = nil end)
end

function onObjectStateChange(obj, oldGuid)
  if not S then return end
  PC.objDirty = true
  TOKEN_MISS = {}
  local function fix(kind, list)
    for _, ent in ipairs(list) do
      if ent.tok and ent.tok.guid == oldGuid then
        ent.tok.guid = obj.getGUID()
        obj.setGMNotes(tokenTag(kind, ent))
      end
    end
  end
  fix("army", S.armies)
  fix("agent", S.agents)
end

-- ===================================================================
-- slot editor
-- ===================================================================
function editSlots(n)
  cancelSlotEdit()
  local b = getBoard()
  if not b then return "Couldn't find the map board." end
  S.editing = n
  for _, spec in ipairs({ { "buildings", NB_SLOTS, { 0.8, 0.25, 0.2 }, "Building slot " }, { "units", NU_SLOTS, { 0.2, 0.35, 0.85 }, "Unit slot " } }) do
    local kind, count, color, label = spec[1], spec[2], spec[3], spec[4]
    for i = 1, count do
      local p = slotWorld(n, kind, i)
      local o = spawnObject({ type = (kind == "buildings") and "BlockSquare" or "Checker_white",
                              position = { p.x, p.y + 0.6, p.z }, scale = { 0.35, 0.35, 0.35 } })
      o.setColorTint(color)
      o.setName(label .. i)
      o.setGMNotes("wc:edit:" .. kind .. ":" .. i)
      table.insert(editMarkers, o)
    end
  end
  return true
end

function saveSlotEdit()
  local n = S.editing
  if not n then return "Nothing is being edited." end
  local b = getBoard()
  local ov = { buildings = {}, units = {} }
  for i = 1, NB_SLOTS do local u, v = slotUV(n, "buildings", i); ov.buildings[i] = { u, v } end
  for i = 1, NU_SLOTS do local u, v = slotUV(n, "units", i); ov.units[i] = { u, v } end
  for _, o in ipairs(allObjects()) do
    local kind, i = string.match(o.getGMNotes() or "", "^wc:edit:(%a+):(%d+)$")
    if kind then
      local uv = b.call("worldToUv", { position = o.getPosition() })
      ov[kind][tonumber(i)] = { uv.u, uv.v }
    end
  end
  S.slotOv[n] = ov
  cancelSlotEdit()
  -- move this territory's buildings onto the new slots
  for key, has in pairs(S.terr[n].b) do if has then placeBuilding(n, key) end end
  clog("Slots for " .. tname(n) .. " updated.")
  return true
end

function resetSlotEdit(n)
  S.slotOv[n] = nil
  cancelSlotEdit()
  for key, has in pairs(S.terr[n].b) do if has then placeBuilding(n, key) end end
  return true
end

function cancelSlotEdit()
  for _, o in ipairs(allObjects()) do
    if string.sub(o.getGMNotes() or "", 1, 8) == "wc:edit:" then o.destruct() end
  end
  editMarkers = {}
  if S then S.editing = nil end
end

-- ===================================================================
-- hook from the rules engine
-- ===================================================================
-- ===================================================================
-- driving: tokens glide stop by stop instead of jumping
-- ===================================================================
local function yawToward(from, to)
  local dx, dz = to.x - from.x, to.z - from.z
  if dx * dx + dz * dz < 0.0001 then return nil end
  local a = math.atan2 and math.atan2(dx, dz) or math.atan(dx, dz)
  return math.deg(a)
end

-- path: territories visited in order (not including the start). The last one gets a free unit slot.
-- Drive speed in table units per second (the map is about 140 units wide).
DRIVE_SPEEDS = { Slow = 2.5, Normal = 5, Fast = 10 }

-- ===================================================================
-- floating cargo numbers
-- ===================================================================
function floatText(pos, text, rgb)
  local yaw = (mapYaw and mapYaw()) or 0
  local o = spawnObject({ type = "3DText", position = { pos.x, pos.y + 1.0, pos.z }, rotation = { 60, yaw, 0 } })
  if not o then return end
  pcall(function()
    o.setGMNotes("wc:fx")
    o.TextTool.setValue(text)
    o.TextTool.setFontSize(72)
    o.TextTool.setFontColor(rgb)
  end)
  local f = 0
  local function rise()
    f = f + 1
    if f > 70 or o == nil then
      pcall(function() o.destruct() end)
      return
    end
    pcall(function() o.setPosition({ pos.x, pos.y + 1.0 + f * 0.025, pos.z }) end)
    Wait.frames(rise, 1)
  end
  Wait.frames(rise, 1)
end

-- Show queued cargo changes for wagon g that happened in territory n (all of them when n is nil).
function playCargo(g, n)
  local list = CARGO_FX and CARGO_FX[g.id]
  if not list or #list == 0 then return end
  local o = tokenObject("agent", g)
  local keep, show = {}, {}
  for _, e in ipairs(list) do
    if n == nil or e.n == n then table.insert(show, e) else table.insert(keep, e) end
  end
  CARGO_FX[g.id] = keep
  if not o then return end
  local delay = 0
  for _, e in ipairs(show) do
    Wait.time(function()
      local ok, p = pcall(function() return o.getPosition() end)
      if ok and p then
        floatText(p, ((e.d > 0) and "+" or "") .. e.d .. " RP", (e.d > 0) and { 0.45, 0.95, 0.55 } or { 1.0, 0.45, 0.4 })
      end
    end, delay)
    delay = delay + 0.5
  end
end

do  -- keep helpers out of the main chunk (Lua allows 200 locals there)
-- The way a token travels from territory a into b: along the road out of a, then the road into b.
local function legPoints(a, b, target)
  local out = {}
  local ra = a and roadWorld and roadWorld(a, b) or nil
  local rb = roadWorld and roadWorld(b, a) or nil
  if ra and rb then
    for _, p in ipairs(ra) do table.insert(out, p) end
    for k = #rb - 1, 1, -1 do table.insert(out, rb[k]) end   -- skip the shared border point
  end
  table.insert(out, target)
  return out
end

-- Drop the start of the road if the token is already further along it (no doubling back to the hub).
local function joinRoad(from, pts)
  local best, bi = nil, 1
  for k = 1, #pts - 1 do
    local p = pts[k]
    local d = (p.x - from.x) ^ 2 + (p.z - from.z) ^ 2
    if not best or d < best then best, bi = d, k end
  end
  local out = {}
  for k = bi, #pts do table.insert(out, pts[k]) end
  return out
end

local function angleLerp(a, b, t)
  local d = ((b - a + 540) % 360) - 180
  return a + d * t
end

function driveToken(kind, ent, path)
  local o = tokenObject(kind, ent)
  if not o or not path or #path == 0 then return end
  if kind == "agent" and isWagon(ent) and ent.tok then playCargo(ent, ent.tok.terr) end
  local legs = {}
  local prev = ent.tok and ent.tok.terr
  for i, n in ipairs(path) do
    local p = (i < #path) and slotWorld(n, "units", 1) or freeUnitSlot(n, ent)
    if p then
      table.insert(legs, { n = n, pts = legPoints(prev, n, p) })
    end
    prev = n
  end
  if #legs == 0 then return end
  DRIVE_ID = (DRIVE_ID or 0) + 1
  local myId = DRIVE_ID
  local wasDriving = ent.tok.animating and ent.tok.driveLock
  ent.tok.driveId = myId            -- a newer move of the same token takes over from this one
  ent.tok.animating = true
  local final = legs[#legs]
  local wasLocked = o.getLock and o.getLock() or false
  if wasDriving ~= nil then wasLocked = wasDriving end   -- taking over: keep the lock state from before the first move
  ent.tok.driveLock = wasLocked
  o.setLock(true) -- hold it steady while we move it frame by frame
  local speed = DRIVE_SPEEDS[S.settings.driveSpeed or "Slow"] or DRIVE_SPEEDS.Slow
  local cruiseY = o.getPosition().y + ((wasDriving ~= nil) and 0 or 0.15)
  local face = S.settings.wagonFace ~= false
  local function current() return ent.tok and ent.tok.driveId == myId end
  local function finish()
    if not current() then return end
    pcall(function()
      if not wasLocked then o.setLock(false) end
    end)
    if ent.tok then
      local q = o.getPosition()
      ent.tok.pos = { q.x, q.z }
      ent.tok.terr = final.n
      ent.tok.animating = nil
      ent.tok.driveLock = nil
    end
    if kind == "agent" and isWagon(ent) then pcall(playCargo, ent, nil) end
  end
  local step, stepOnce
  step = function(i)
    if i > #legs or o == nil then finish(); return end
    local okStep = pcall(function() stepOnce(i) end)
    if not okStep then
      ent.tok.animating = nil
      pcall(placeToken, kind, ent)
      finish()
    end
  end
  -- one territory: follow the road at a steady speed, easing in and out, turning to face the way it goes
  stepOnce = function(i)
    local from = o.getPosition()
    local pts = { { x = from.x, z = from.z } }
    for _, p in ipairs((i == 1) and joinRoad(from, legs[i].pts) or legs[i].pts) do table.insert(pts, p) end
    local cum = { 0 }
    for k = 2, #pts do
      local dx, dz = pts[k].x - pts[k - 1].x, pts[k].z - pts[k - 1].z
      cum[k] = cum[k - 1] + math.sqrt(dx * dx + dz * dz)
    end
    local total = cum[#cum]
    local frames = math.max(1, math.floor(total / speed * 60 + 0.5))
    local yaw = o.getRotation().y
    local f, seg = 0, 2
    local function posAt(dist)
      while seg < #pts and cum[seg] < dist do seg = seg + 1 end
      local a, b = pts[seg - 1], pts[seg]
      local len = cum[seg] - cum[seg - 1]
      local t = (len > 0) and (dist - cum[seg - 1]) / len or 1
      return a.x + (b.x - a.x) * t, a.z + (b.z - a.z) * t
    end
    local function tick()
      if o == nil then finish(); return end
      if not current() then return end
      f = f + 1
      local t = f / frames
      local e = t * t * (3 - 2 * t)
      local x, z = posAt(total * e)
      if face then
        -- look a little way ahead so it turns smoothly through the bends
        local s0 = seg
        local ax, az = posAt(math.min(total, total * e + 1.2))
        seg = s0
        local want = yawToward({ x = x, z = z }, { x = ax, z = az })
        if want then yaw = angleLerp(yaw, want, 0.15) end
        local r = o.getRotation()
        o.setRotation({ r.x, yaw, r.z })
      end
      o.setPosition({ x, cruiseY, z })
      if f < frames then
        Wait.frames(tick, 1)
      else
        if kind == "agent" and isWagon(ent) and i < #legs then pcall(playCargo, ent, legs[i].n) end
        Wait.time(function() step(i + 1) end, (i < #legs) and 0.6 or 0.05)
      end
    end
    tick()
  end
  step(1)
end
end

function piecesOn(event, a, b, c)
  if event == "cargo" then
    playCargo(b, nil)
  elseif event == "routed" then
    if PIECE_DROP then playCargo(b, nil); return end
    local ok = pcall(driveToken, a, b, c)
    if not ok then
      if b.tok then b.tok.animating = nil end
      placeToken(a, b)
    end
  elseif event == "built" then
    placeBuilding(a, b)
  elseif event == "created" then
    -- an army appears on the map once it's raised, not while it's still being raised
    if not (a == "army" and b.status == "building") then autoToken(a, b) end
  elseif event == "ready" then
    if not b.tok and not b.noAutoLink then autoToken(a, b) else placeToken(a, b) end
  elseif event == "moved" then
    if PIECE_DROP then return end   -- a dropped token drives itself once the move is accepted
    -- every move travels along the roads, like the wagons
    if c and #c > 0 and b.tok and b.tok.terr and b.tok.terr ~= c[#c] and tokenObject(a, b) then
      local ok = pcall(driveToken, a, b, c)
      if not ok then
        if b.tok then b.tok.animating = nil end
        placeToken(a, b)
      end
    else
      placeToken(a, b)
    end
  elseif event == "captured" then
    -- a wagon that changed hands: a copy of the old side's template is swapped for the new side's; your own model stays
    if b.tok and b.tok.spawned then
      local o = tokenObject(a, b)
      if o then o.destruct() end
      b.tok = nil
      if findTemplate(tokenTemplateNames(a, b)) then spawnToken(a, b) end
    end
  elseif event == "removed" then
    local o = tokenObject(a, b)
    if o then
      if b.tok.spawned then o.destruct() else o.setGMNotes("") end
    end
  elseif event == "reset" then
    for _, o in ipairs(allObjects()) do
      local g = o.getGMNotes and o.getGMNotes() or ""
      if string.sub(g, 1, 5) == "wc:b:" then o.destruct()
      elseif string.match(g, "^wc:[ag]:") then
        local spawned = false
        for _, e in ipairs(S.armies) do if e.tok and e.tok.spawned and tokenTag("army", e) == g then spawned = true end end
        for _, e in ipairs(S.agents) do if e.tok and e.tok.spawned and tokenTag("agent", e) == g then spawned = true end end
        if spawned then o.destruct() else o.setGMNotes("") end
      end
    end
  end
end

function templateStatus()
  local out = {}
  for _, b in ipairs(BUILDINGS) do table.insert(out, { b.name, findTemplate({ b.name }) ~= nil }) end
  for _, side in ipairs(SIDES) do
    table.insert(out, { side .. " Army", findTemplate({ side .. " Army", "Army" }) ~= nil })
  end
  for _, t in ipairs(AGENT_TYPES) do
    if t ~= "Surgeon" then table.insert(out, { t, findTemplate({ "Good " .. t, "Evil " .. t, t }) ~= nil }) end
  end
  return out
end

function startPieces()
  for _, list in ipairs({ S.armies or {}, S.agents or {} }) do
    for _, e in ipairs(list) do if e.tok then e.tok.animating = nil end end
  end
  Wait.time(function() pieceTick() end, PIECE_TICK, -1)
  Wait.frames(function()
    if S.started then
      local ok, err = pcall(reconcileBuildings)
      if not ok then print("War Council buildings: " .. tostring(err)) end
      -- tokens we spawned for armies still being raised come off the map until they're ready
      pcall(function()
        for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
          if a.status == "building" and a.tok and a.tok.spawned then
            local o = tokenObject("army", a)
            if o then o.destruct() end
            a.tok = nil
          end
        end
      end)
    end
  end, 60)
end


-- ===================================================================
-- profile cards on the table
-- ===================================================================
local function cardTag(id) return "wc:card:" .. id end
local CARD_SCALE = 1.7

function clearCards(id)
  local n = 0
  for _, o in ipairs(getAllObjects()) do
    if o.getGMNotes and o.getGMNotes() == cardTag(id) then o.destruct(); n = n + 1 end
  end
  return n
end

local function handOf(color)
  if not color or color == "Grey" or color == "Black" then return nil end
  local ok, t = pcall(function() return Player[color].getHandTransform() end)
  if ok and type(t) == "table" and t.position then return t end
  return nil
end

-- Deal an army's profile cards: into the player's hand when hidden information is on, otherwise face up in front of them.
function dealCards(color, a)
  local cards = armyCards(a)
  if #cards == 0 then return false, "No profile cards found for this army's roster." end
  clearCards(a.id)
  local hand = handOf(color)
  local toHand = S.settings.hidden and hand ~= nil
  local origin, right, toward, yaw
  if hand then
    local p = hand.position
    local d = { x = -p.x, z = -p.z }
    local len = math.sqrt(d.x * d.x + d.z * d.z)
    if len < 0.01 then d = { x = 0, z = 1 }; len = 1 end
    toward = { x = d.x / len, z = d.z / len }
    right = { x = toward.z, z = -toward.x }
    origin = { x = p.x + toward.x * 9, z = p.z + toward.z * 9 }
    yaw = (hand.rotation and hand.rotation.y) or 0
  else
    local o = tokenObject("army", a)
    local p = o and o.getPosition() or { x = 0, y = 2, z = 0 }
    origin = { x = p.x + 6, z = p.z }; right = { x = 1, z = 0 }; toward = { x = 0, z = -1 }; yaw = 0
  end
  local per = 4
  local cw, ch = 3.4 * CARD_SCALE / 1.5, 2.4 * CARD_SCALE / 1.5   -- footprint of a sideways card
  for i, c in ipairs(cards) do
    local col = (i - 1) % per
    local rowN = math.floor((i - 1) / per)
    local off = (col - (math.min(per, #cards) - 1) / 2) * (cw + 0.4)
    local pos
    if toHand then pos = { hand.position.x + right.x * off * 0.3, hand.position.y + 0.5, hand.position.z + right.z * off * 0.3 }
    else pos = { origin.x + right.x * off - toward.x * rowN * (ch + 0.4), 2.5, origin.z + right.z * off - toward.z * rowN * (ch + 0.4) } end
    local url = cardUrl(c)
    local o = spawnObject({ type = "CardCustom", position = pos, rotation = { 0, yaw, 0 }, scale = { CARD_SCALE, 1, CARD_SCALE }, sound = false })
    o.setCustomObject({ face = url, back = url, sideways = true })
    o.setName(c.n)
    o.setDescription(a.name .. "  ·  " .. c.o)
    o.setGMNotes(cardTag(a.id))
  end
  local where = toHand and "into your hand" or (hand and "on the table in front of you" or "next to the army's token")
  return true, "Dealt " .. #cards .. " profile card" .. ((#cards == 1) and "" or "s") .. " for " .. a.name .. " " .. where .. ". The pictures take a moment to load the first time."
end

--[[ ===================================================================
     WAR COUNCIL - army lists pasted from the MESBG List Builder (v2024)
     Accepts: the Summary text ("Copy to clipboard"), the Export JSON, the Tabletop Simulator export,
     or a Tabletop Admiral list (the same bracketed lines, which name heroes by their full title).
     =================================================================== ]]

local MOUNT_WORDS = { "horse", "warg", "rider", "cavalry", "knight", "mount", "steed", "chariot", "pony", "elk", "boar",
                      "goat", "fell beast", "mumak", "mûmak", "drake", "great beast", "camel", "spider", "bear", "eagle" }

local ACCENTS = { ["û"] = "u", ["ú"] = "u", ["ü"] = "u", ["é"] = "e", ["ê"] = "e", ["ë"] = "e", ["ó"] = "o", ["ô"] = "o",
                  ["â"] = "a", ["á"] = "a", ["í"] = "i", ["î"] = "i" }
local LIST_ALIAS = { buhdurs = "buhrdurshorde", carndum = "armyofcarndum", fornost = "battleoffornost" }

-- Trim without heavy patterns (Tabletop Simulator's Lua gives up on long backtracking matches).
local function trim(s)
  s = s or ""
  local i = string.find(s, "%S")
  if not i then return "" end
  local j = #s
  while j > i do
    local c = string.byte(s, j)
    if c == 32 or c == 9 or c == 10 or c == 13 then j = j - 1 else break end
  end
  return string.sub(s, i, j)
end

function normList(s)
  s = string.lower(s or "")
  for k, v in pairs(ACCENTS) do s = string.gsub(s, k, v) end
  s = string.gsub(s, "[^%w]", "")
  return s
end

local function slugName(id)
  local slug = string.match(id or "", "%]%s*(.+)$") or id or "?"
  slug = string.gsub(slug, "%-", " ")
  return (string.gsub(slug, "(%a)([%w']*)", function(a, b) return string.upper(a) .. b end))
end

local function tierFor(points)
  if not points then return nil end
  for _, s in ipairs({ 400, 600, 800, 1000 }) do if points <= s then return s end end
  return 1000
end

-- ===================================================================
-- profile cards (images hosted by the MESBG List Builder)
-- ===================================================================
CARD_BASE = "https://resources.mesbg-list-builder.com/v2024/images/profiles/"

local function uriEnc(s)
  return (string.gsub(s, "[^%w%-_%.!~%*'%(%)]", function(c) return string.format("%%%02X", string.byte(c)) end))
end

-- Find the card for a unit name (as written in any List Builder export). Returns { n = name, o = sourcebook } or nil.
function cardFor(name, list)
  local k = normList(name)
  local e = CARD_BY[k]
  if not e then
    k = normList(string.gsub(name or "", "%b()", ""))
    e = CARD_BY[k]
  end
  if not e then   -- Tabletop Admiral writes "Faramir, Captain of Gondor" where the List Builder has "Faramir"
    local short = string.match(name or "", "^([^,]+),")
    if short then k = normList(short); e = CARD_BY[k] end
  end
  if not e then return nil end
  local o = (list and CARD_OVER[normList(list) .. "|" .. k]) or e[2]
  return { n = e[1], o = CARD_ORIGINS[o], h = (e[3] == 1) or nil }
end

function cardUrl(c) return CARD_BASE .. uriEnc(c.o) .. "/cards/" .. uriEnc(c.n) .. ".jpg" end
function cardAsset(c) return "mlbc_" .. normList(c.o) .. "_" .. normList(c.n) end

-- Cards for a list of roster entries: heroes first, each profile once, plus extra profiles (e.g. a hero's companion).
function cardsForEntries(entries, list)
  local out, seen, miss = {}, {}, {}
  local function add(c)
    if c and not seen[c.o .. "|" .. c.n] then seen[c.o .. "|" .. c.n] = true; table.insert(out, c) end
  end
  for pass = 1, 2 do
    for _, e in ipairs(entries or {}) do
      local c = cardFor(e.name, list)
      local hero = c and c.h or e.hero
      if (pass == 1) == (hero and true or false) then
        if c then
          add(c)
          for _, x in ipairs(CARD_EX[normList(list or "") .. "|" .. normList(c.n)] or {}) do
            local xc = cardFor(x, list)
            if xc then xc.o = c.o; add(xc) end
          end
        elseif pass == 1 or not e.hero then table.insert(miss, e.name) end
      end
    end
  end
  for _, c in ipairs(out) do c.h = nil end
  return out, miss
end

-- Cards for an army: saved with its roster, or worked out from the roster lines of older saves.
function armyCards(a)
  local r = a and a.roster
  if not r then return {} end
  if r.cards then return r.cards end
  local entries = {}
  for _, l in ipairs(r.lines or {}) do
    local hero = string.sub(l, 1, 2) == "* "
    local s = hero and string.sub(l, 3) or l
    s = string.gsub(s, "^%d+x%s+", "")
    s = string.gsub(s, "%s+%d+ pts$", "")
    s = string.gsub(s, "%s*%b()$", "")
    table.insert(entries, { name = s, hero = hero })
  end
  r.cards = cardsForEntries(entries, r.list)
  return r.cards
end

local function finish(r)
  r.broken = r.broken or ((r.units and r.units > 0) and (math.floor(r.units * 0.5) + 1) or nil)
  r.quartered = r.quartered or (r.units and math.floor(r.units * 0.25) or nil)
  r.size = tierFor(r.points)
  local lines, mounted, any = {}, true, false
  for _, e in ipairs(r.entries) do
    local opts = (#e.opts > 0) and (" (" .. table.concat(e.opts, ", ") .. ")") or ""
    local qty = (e.qty and e.qty > 1) and (e.qty .. "x ") or ""
    table.insert(lines, (e.hero and "* " or "") .. qty .. e.name .. opts .. (e.points and ("  " .. e.points .. " pts") or ""))
    local txt = string.lower(e.name .. " " .. table.concat(e.opts, " "))
    local m = false
    for _, w in ipairs(MOUNT_WORDS) do if string.find(txt, w, 1, true) then m = true end end
    if not m then mounted = false end
    any = true
  end
  r.lines = lines
  r.mounted = any and mounted
  if not r.general then
    for _, e in ipairs(r.entries) do if e.hero then r.general = e.name; break end end
  end
  -- short summary for the army's notes field
  local parts = {}
  for _, e in ipairs(r.entries) do
    local opts = (#e.opts > 0) and (" (" .. table.concat(e.opts, ", ") .. ")") or ""
    table.insert(parts, ((e.qty and e.qty > 1) and (e.qty .. "x ") or "") .. e.name .. opts)
  end
  r.summary = table.concat(parts, "; ")
  r.cards = cardsForEntries(r.entries, r.list)
  r.entries = nil
  return r
end

local function parseJson(text)
  local ok, d = pcall(JSON.decode, text)
  if not ok or type(d) ~= "table" or not d.warbands then return nil end
  local r = { source = "json", list = d.armyList, name = d.name, points = 0, units = 0, entries = {} }
  local leaderWb = d.metadata and d.metadata.leader
  for _, wb in ipairs(d.warbands) do
    if wb.meta then
      r.points = r.points + (tonumber(wb.meta.points) or 0)
      r.units = r.units + (tonumber(wb.meta.units) or 0) + ((wb.hero and 1) or 0)
    end
    if type(wb.hero) == "table" and wb.hero.model_id then
      local e = { name = slugName(wb.hero.model_id), qty = 1, opts = {}, hero = true }
      for _, o in ipairs(wb.hero.options or {}) do table.insert(e.opts, ((tonumber(o.quantity) or 1) > 1 and (o.quantity .. " ") or "") .. (o.name or "")) end
      table.insert(r.entries, e)
      if leaderWb and wb.id == leaderWb then r.general = e.name end
    end
    for _, u in ipairs(wb.units or {}) do
      if u.model_id then
        local e = { name = slugName(u.model_id), qty = tonumber(u.quantity) or 1, opts = {} }
        for _, o in ipairs(u.options or {}) do table.insert(e.opts, o.name or "") end
        table.insert(r.entries, e)
      end
    end
  end
  if r.points == 0 then r.points = nil end
  if r.units == 0 then r.units = nil end
  return finish(r)
end

local function parseTts(lines)
  local r = { source = "tts", entries = {}, units = 0, approx = true }
  for _, l in ipairs(lines) do
    -- a heading above the units (a list name, perhaps with its points)
    if l ~= "" and string.sub(l, 1, 1) ~= "(" and #r.entries == 0 then
      local pts = tonumber(string.match(l, "(%d+)%s*[Pp]") or "")
      if pts and pts >= 50 and pts <= 3000 then r.points = pts; r.approx = nil end
      local nm = trim((string.gsub(string.gsub(l, "%b()", ""), "[%-|:]?%s*%d+%s*[Pp][%a]*", "")))
      if nm ~= "" and not r.list then r.list = nm end
    end
    local q, name, opts = string.match(l, "^%((%d+)x%s+(.-):%s*(.-)%)$")
    local hero = false
    if not q then
      name, opts = string.match(l, "^%((.-):%s*(.-)%)$")
      hero = name ~= nil
    end
    if name then
      local e = { name = trim(name), qty = tonumber(q) or 1, opts = {}, hero = hero }
      for o in string.gmatch(opts or "", "[^,]+") do if trim(o) ~= "" then table.insert(e.opts, trim(o)) end end
      table.insert(r.entries, e)
      r.units = r.units + e.qty
    end
  end
  if #r.entries == 0 then return nil end
  return finish(r)
end

local function parseText(lines)
  local r = { source = "text", entries = {} }
  local last, expectHero = nil, false
  for _, l in ipairs(lines) do
    if string.find(l, "Army Bonuses", 1, true) or string.find(l, "Created with", 1, true) then break end
    if not r.list and l ~= "" and not string.match(l, "^[%-|=]") then r.list = l
    elseif string.match(l, "^|") then
      r.points = r.points or tonumber(string.match(l, "Points:%s*(%d+)"))
      r.units = r.units or tonumber(string.match(l, "Units:%s*(%d+)"))
      r.broken = r.broken or tonumber(string.match(l, "Broken:%s*(%d+)"))
      r.quartered = r.quartered or tonumber(string.match(l, "Quartered:%s*(%d+)"))
    elseif string.match(l, "^Warband%s+%d+") then
      expectHero = true
    elseif string.match(l, "^~") then
      if last then table.insert(last.opts, trim(string.sub(l, 2))) end
    else
      local q, name, pts = string.match(l, "^(%d+)x%s+(.-)%s*%((%d+) points%)")
      if not q then name, pts = string.match(l, "^(.-)%s*%((%d+) points%)") end
      if name then
        local general = false
        if string.find(name, "*GENERAL*", 1, true) then
          general = true
          name = trim(string.gsub(name, "%*GENERAL%*", ""))
        end
        local e = { name = trim(name), qty = tonumber(q) or 1, opts = {}, points = tonumber(pts), hero = expectHero or general }
        if general then r.general = e.name end
        table.insert(r.entries, e)
        last = e
        expectHero = false
      end
    end
  end
  if #r.entries == 0 then return nil end
  return finish(r)
end

-- Returns a roster table, or nil and a reason.
function parseRoster(text)
  text = string.gsub(text or "", "\r", "")
  if trim(text) == "" then return nil, "Paste a list first." end
  if string.match(trim(text), "^{") then
    local r = parseJson(trim(text))
    if r then return r end
    return nil, "That looks like JSON but isn't a List Builder export."
  end
  local lines = {}
  for l in string.gmatch(text .. "\n", "([^\n]*)\n") do table.insert(lines, trim(l)) end
  local tts = 0
  for _, l in ipairs(lines) do if string.match(l, "^%(.-:.*%)$") then tts = tts + 1 end end
  local r = (tts > 0) and parseTts(lines) or parseText(lines)
  if not r then return nil, "Couldn't find any units. Paste the List Builder's 'Copy to clipboard' text or JSON, or a Tabletop Admiral list." end
  return r
end

-- How the pasted list fits `side` in the current theatre: "faction", "allied", or "other".
function rosterFit(r, side)
  if not r or not r.list then return "other" end
  local L = normList(r.list)
  local al = allianceFor(side)
  if al then
    local A = normList(al.list)
    if A ~= "" and (string.find(L, A, 1, true) or string.find(A, L, 1, true)) then return "allied" end
  end
  local th = FACTION_LISTS[(S and S.settings.theatre) or "Eriador"]
  local lists = th and th[side] or ""
  if string.find(string.lower(lists), "all ", 1, true) then return "unknown" end
  for tok in string.gmatch(lists, "[^,]+") do
    local T = normList(tok)
    local alias = LIST_ALIAS[T]
    -- whole list names only, so "Moria" doesn't also take in "Reclamation of Moria" (Good) or "The Shire" "Ravagers of the Shire" (Evil)
    if T ~= "" and (L == T or (alias and L == alias)) then return "faction" end
  end
  return "other"
end

-- Compact copy kept on the army.
function rosterForArmy(r)
  if not r then return nil end
  return { list = r.list, points = r.points, units = r.units, broken = r.broken, quartered = r.quartered,
           general = r.general, lines = r.lines, approx = r.approx, cards = r.cards }
end

--[[ ===================================================================
     WAR COUNCIL - sound effects (played through Tabletop Simulator's Music Player)
     Each event has a link to an MP3 set in Settings > Sounds. Everyone hears them.
     =================================================================== ]]

SOUND_SLOTS = {
  { key = "newturn",   label = "New turn",            prio = 10 },
  { key = "report",    label = "Turn report",         prio = 9 },
  { key = "battle",    label = "Battle begins",       prio = 8 },
  { key = "battlegood", label = "Good wins a battle", prio = 7 },
  { key = "battleevil", label = "Evil wins a battle", prio = 7 },
  { key = "battleend", label = "Battle drawn",        prio = 7 },
  { key = "conquer",   label = "Territory conquered", prio = 6 },
  { key = "raise",     label = "Army raised",         prio = 5 },
  { key = "recruit",   label = "Agent recruited",     prio = 5 },
  { key = "built",     label = "Building finished",   prio = 5 },
  { key = "build",     label = "Building started",    prio = 4 },
  { key = "move",      label = "Army moves",          prio = 3 },
  { key = "wagon",     label = "Wagon delivers",      prio = 2 },
  { key = "stage",     label = "Stage change",        prio = 1 },
}
do  -- keep helpers out of the main chunk (Lua allows 200 locals there)
local SLOT_BY = {}
for _, s in ipairs(SOUND_SLOTS) do SLOT_BY[s.key] = s end

local SND_PENDING, SND_PREV = {}, nil
local sndToken = 0
local MUSIC_SAVED = nil     -- the track that was playing before a burst of sounds
local resumeToken = 0
local SFX_PREFIX = "War Council: "

local function isSfx(clip) return type(clip) == "table" and type(clip.title) == "string" and string.sub(clip.title, 1, #SFX_PREFIX) == SFX_PREFIX end
local function curClip() local c; pcall(function() c = MusicPlayer.getCurrentAudioclip() end); return c end
local function status() local s; pcall(function() s = MusicPlayer.player_status end); return s end

-- Remember the music that is playing (only real music, never one of our own sounds).
local function rememberMusic()
  if S.settings.musicResume == false then MUSIC_SAVED = nil; return end
  local c = curClip()
  if c and not isSfx(c) and c.url and c.url ~= "" and status() == "Play" then
    MUSIC_SAVED = { url = c.url, title = c.title }
  elseif not isSfx(c) then
    MUSIC_SAVED = nil   -- music was stopped or paused: leave it that way
  end                   -- (if one of our sounds is still playing, keep what was saved before it)
end

-- Once the last sound of a burst has finished, put the music back on.
local function resumeMusicLater()
  if not MUSIC_SAVED then return end
  resumeToken = resumeToken + 1
  local tok, waited, heard = resumeToken, 0, false
  local function tick()
    if tok ~= resumeToken then return end                        -- a newer sound took over
    local c, st = curClip(), status()
    if not isSfx(c) then MUSIC_SAVED = nil; return end           -- someone picked music themselves
    if st == "Play" then heard = true end
    waited = waited + 0.5
    local done = (heard and st ~= "Play" and st ~= "Loading") or (not heard and waited >= 10) or waited >= 120
    if done then
      local m = MUSIC_SAVED
      MUSIC_SAVED = nil
      if m and S.settings.musicResume ~= false then
        pcall(function()
          MusicPlayer.setCurrentAudioclip({ url = m.url, title = m.title })
          Wait.condition(function() pcall(function() MusicPlayer.play() end) end,
                         function() local l = true; pcall(function() l = MusicPlayer.loaded end); return l ~= false end,
                         10, function() pcall(function() MusicPlayer.play() end) end)
        end)
      end
      return
    end
    pcall(function() Wait.time(tick, 0.5) end)
  end
  pcall(function() Wait.time(tick, 1) end)
end

function soundUrl(key)
  local u = S.settings.sounds and S.settings.sounds[key]
  if type(u) == "string" and string.find(u, "%S") then return u end
  return nil
end

-- the sound actually played for an event (a victory with no sound of its own falls back to "Battle drawn")
local function playableKey(key)
  if soundUrl(key) then return key end
  if (key == "battlegood" or key == "battleevil") and soundUrl("battleend") then return "battleend" end
  return nil
end

-- Play one sound now.
-- last=false: more sounds follow in this burst, so don't resume the music after this one
function playSound(key, last)
  local url = soundUrl(key)
  if not url then return false end
  local title = SFX_PREFIX .. ((SLOT_BY[key] and SLOT_BY[key].label) or key)
  rememberMusic()
  resumeToken = resumeToken + 1     -- cancels any resume still waiting for an earlier sound
  local ok = pcall(function()
    pcall(function() MusicPlayer.repeat_track = false end)
    MusicPlayer.setCurrentAudioclip({ url = url, title = title })
  end)
  if not ok then return false end
  pcall(function()
    Wait.condition(function() pcall(function() MusicPlayer.play() end); if last ~= false then resumeMusicLater() end end,
                   function() local l = true; pcall(function() l = MusicPlayer.loaded end); return l ~= false end,
                   6, function() pcall(function() MusicPlayer.play() end); if last ~= false then resumeMusicLater() end end)
  end)
  return true
end

function soundEvent(key) if not QUIET then SND_PENDING[key] = true end end

-- What the sound system compares between actions.
function soundSnap()
  local s = { turn = S.turn, stage = S.stage, started = S.started, army = {}, agent = {}, battles = #(S.battles or {}),
              bp = S.bpTurn, owner = {}, nb = {}, nq = {} }
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies or {}) do s.army[a.id] = a.loc end
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents or {}) do s.agent[g.id] = true end
  for n, t in pairs(S.terr or {}) do
    s.owner[n] = t.owner
    local nb = 0
    for _, has in pairs(t.b or {}) do if has then nb = nb + 1 end end   -- buildings are stored by name, not as a list
    local nq = 0
    for _ in pairs(t.q or {}) do nq = nq + 1 end
    s.nb[n], s.nq[n] = nb, nq
  end
  return s
end

local function contested()
  local by = {}
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies or {}) do
    by[a.loc] = by[a.loc] or {}
    by[a.loc][a.side] = true
    if by[a.loc].Good and by[a.loc].Evil then return true end
  end
  return false
end

-- Compare with the last snapshot, queue sounds for what changed, and play the most important (up to three, spaced out).
function soundCheck(silent)
  local now = soundSnap()
  local p = SND_PREV
  SND_PREV = now
  if silent or not p or not now.started or not p.started then SND_PENDING = {}; return end
  if now.turn > p.turn then soundEvent("newturn"); soundEvent("report")
  elseif now.turn == p.turn and now.stage ~= p.stage then soundEvent("stage") end
  if now.turn < p.turn then SND_PENDING = {}; return end
  for id, loc in pairs(now.army) do
    if p.army[id] == nil then soundEvent("raise") elseif p.army[id] ~= loc then soundEvent("move") end
  end
  for id in pairs(now.agent) do if not p.agent[id] then soundEvent("recruit") end end
  if now.battles > p.battles then
    local b = S.battles[#S.battles] or {}
    soundEvent((b.win == "Good" and "battlegood") or (b.win == "Evil" and "battleevil") or "battleend")
  end
  if now.bp ~= p.bp and now.bp ~= nil and contested() then soundEvent("battle") end
  for n, o in pairs(now.owner) do
    if p.owner[n] and p.owner[n] ~= o and o ~= "Neutral" then soundEvent("conquer") end
    if (now.nb[n] or 0) > (p.nb[n] or 0) then soundEvent("built") end
    if (now.nq[n] or 0) > (p.nq[n] or 0) then soundEvent("build") end
  end
  soundFlush()
end

function soundFlush()
  local list = {}
  -- each sound makes Tabletop Simulator's music player load a file (and the music again afterwards), which can
  -- make the game hitch; the everyday ones can be switched off
  local minor = { move = true, build = true, stage = true, wagon = true }
  for key in pairs(SND_PENDING) do
    local k = SLOT_BY[key] and not (S.settings.minorSounds == false and minor[key]) and playableKey(key)
    if k then table.insert(list, { key = k, prio = SLOT_BY[key].prio }) end
  end
  SND_PENDING = {}
  if S.settings.sound == false or #list == 0 then return end
  -- the stage-change sound only plays on its own (it would cut off e.g. the battle sound)
  if #list > 1 then
    for i = #list, 1, -1 do if list[i].key == "stage" then table.remove(list, i) end end
  end
  table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.prio > b.prio end)
  sndToken = sndToken + 1
  local tok = sndToken
  local n = math.min(3, #list)
  playSound(list[1].key, n == 1)
  local gapS = tonumber(S.settings.soundGap) or 4
  for i = 2, n do
    local key, isLast = list[i].key, (i == n)
    pcall(function()
      Wait.time(function() if tok == sndToken then playSound(key, isLast) end end, gapS * (i - 1))
    end)
  end
end

end

     =================================================================== ]]

MAP_VERSION = 2

local GRID_CHARS = "0123456789abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDE"

state = {}

local function defaults()
  state.owners = state.owners or {}
  for n = 1, #RECTS do
    local k = tostring(n)
    if state.owners[k] == nil then state.owners[k] = START[n] or "Neutral" end
  end
  state.urls = state.urls or {}
  if state.mapVer ~= MAP_VERSION then
    -- new map image and border images: forget old uploaded links and the measured size
    state.urls = {}
    state.size = nil
    state.mapVer = MAP_VERSION
  end
  if state.flipX == nil then state.flipX = false end
  if state.flipZ == nil then state.flipZ = false end
  if state.rot180 == nil then state.rot180 = false end
  if state.faceFlip == nil then state.faceFlip = false end
  if state.mode == nil then state.mode = 1 end
  state.size = nil
end

-- Map image aspect (height / width). Used when the engine can't report the tile size yet.
local ASPECT = 0.802734

local function decalName(n, owner) return string.format("C%02d_%s", n, owner) end

local function urlFor(name)
  return state.urls[name] or (IMG_BASE .. "/" .. name .. ".png")
end

-- Size of the board in its own local units.
local function localSize()
  local b = self.getBoundsNormalized()
  local s = self.getScale()
  local W, H, D = b.size.x / s.x, b.size.y / s.y, b.size.z / s.z
  if W > 0.05 and D > 0.05 then
    state.size = { W, H, D }
    return W, H, D
  end
  if state.size then return state.size[1], state.size[2], state.size[3] end
  -- Fallback: custom tiles are 2 units wide at scale 1.
  return 2, 0.1, 2 * ASPECT
end

local function makeDecal(n, owner)
  local r = RECTS[n]
  local W, H, D = localSize()
  local cu, cv = (r[1] + r[3]) / 2, (r[2] + r[4]) / 2
  local x = (0.5 - cu) * W
  local z = (cv - 0.5) * D
  if state.flipX then x = -x end
  if state.flipZ then z = -z end
  local name = decalName(n, owner)
  -- Modes: 1 local units, 2 world units (decals don't inherit object scale), 3/4 = same with the face flipped.
  local m = state.mode
  local k = 1
  local yk = 1
  if m == 2 or m == 4 then
    local s = self.getScale()
    k = s.x
    yk = s.y
  end
  local faceDown = state.faceFlip
  if m == 3 or m == 4 then faceDown = not faceDown end
  local y = (H * 0.5 + 0.12) * yk
  return {
    name = name,
    url = urlFor(name),
    position = { x * k, y, z * k },
    rotation = { faceDown and 270 or 90, state.rot180 and 0 or 180, 0 },
    scale = { (r[3] - r[1]) * W * k, (r[4] - r[2]) * D * k, 1 },
  }
end

-- The board keeps ALL 120 border images attached at all times: the one for each territory's
-- current owner sits on the map, the other two are tucked face-down under the board.
-- Image links are always read back from the board's own decals, so when TTS uploads the
-- files ("Upload All Loaded Files" or a Workshop upload) and rewrites those links, we keep them.
local function readUrls()
  for _, d in ipairs(self.getDecals() or {}) do
    if d.name and d.url and string.match(d.name, "^C%d%d_") then state.urls[d.name] = d.url end
  end
end

local function stashed(dec)
  local p = dec.position
  dec.position = { p[1], -p[2], p[3] }          -- below the board
  dec.rotation = { 270, dec.rotation[2], 0 }     -- facing down
  return dec
end

function applyAll()
  readUrls()
  local list = {}
  for n = 1, #RECTS do
    local owner = state.owners[tostring(n)] or "Neutral"
    if not FACTIONS[owner] then owner = "Neutral" end
    for _, o in ipairs(OWNER_LIST) do
      local dec = makeDecal(n, o)
      if o ~= owner then dec = stashed(dec) end
      table.insert(list, dec)
    end
  end
  self.setDecals(list)
end

function countCloud()
  local c = 0
  for _, u in pairs(state.urls) do if string.sub(u, 1, 4) == "http" then c = c + 1 end end
  return c
end

function onLoad(saved)
  if saved and saved ~= "" then
    local ok, data = pcall(JSON.decode, saved)
    -- (a War Council save left here by mistake is not the board's own: start the board fresh)
    if ok and type(data) == "table" and not data.armies and not data.terr then state = data end
  end
  defaults()
  addHotkey("Eriador: cycle owner under cursor", hotkeyCycle)
  addHotkey("Eriador: flip overlays left-right", function() state.flipX = not state.flipX; applyAll() end)
  addHotkey("Eriador: flip overlays top-bottom", function() state.flipZ = not state.flipZ; applyAll() end)
  addHotkey("Eriador: rotate overlay images 180", function() state.rot180 = not state.rot180; applyAll() end)
  addHotkey("Eriador: next border mode", function() calibrate({ what = "mode" }) end)
  self.addContextMenuItem("Open in War Council", openInCouncil)
  -- Wait until the map image has finished loading so the board reports its real size.
  Wait.condition(function() firstApply() end,
    function() return not self.loading_custom and not self.spawning end,
    20, function() firstApply() end)
end

local applied = false
function firstApply()
  if applied then return end
  applied = true
  Wait.frames(function()
    state.preparing = nil
    applyAll()
  end, 10)
end

function onSave()
  return JSON.encode(state)
end

-- ===== Public API =====

function setOwner(p)
  local n = tonumber(p.id)
  if not n or not RECTS[n] then return false end
  local owner = p.owner
  if owner ~= "Good" and owner ~= "Evil" then owner = "Neutral" end
  state.owners[tostring(n)] = owner
  applyAll()
  return true
end

-- p.what: "flipX", "flipZ", "rot180" or "local" (finish setup without cloud images)
function calibrate(p)
  local w = p and p.what
  if w == "flipX" then state.flipX = not state.flipX
  elseif w == "flipZ" then state.flipZ = not state.flipZ
  elseif w == "rot180" then state.rot180 = not state.rot180
  elseif w == "face" then state.faceFlip = not state.faceFlip
  elseif w == "mode" then
    state.mode = (state.mode % 4) + 1
    local desc = { "local units", "world units", "local units, flipped face", "world units, flipped face" }
    broadcastToAll("Eriador borders: mode " .. state.mode .. " of 4 (" .. desc[state.mode] .. "). Look at the map.", {0.9, 0.8, 0.5})
  elseif w == "online" then
    readUrls()
    local n = countCloud()
    local total = #OWNER_LIST * #RECTS
    local img = self.getCustomObject and self.getCustomObject().image or "?"
    broadcastToAll(string.format("Online check: %d of %d border images are uploaded.", n, total), (n >= total) and {0.6, 0.9, 0.6} or {0.95, 0.6, 0.5})
    broadcastToAll("Map image: " .. ((string.sub(img or "", 1, 4) == "http") and "uploaded" or "NOT uploaded (still on this PC)"),
      (string.sub(img or "", 1, 4) == "http") and {0.6, 0.9, 0.6} or {0.95, 0.6, 0.5})
    return true
  elseif w == "diag" then
    local W, H, D = localSize()
    local ds = self.getDecals() or {}
    local first = ds[1] and ds[1].url or "none"
    local sc = self.getScale()
    broadcastToAll(string.format("Board size %.2f x %.2f (thick %.2f) | scale %.1f | decals: %d | mode %d | uploaded border images: %d",
      W, D, H, sc.x, #ds, state.mode, countCloud()), {0.9, 0.8, 0.5})
    if ds[1] then
      local p, q = ds[1].position, ds[1].scale
      broadcastToAll(string.format("First decal at (%.2f, %.2f, %.2f) size %.2f x %.2f", p.x, p.y, p.z, q.x, q.y), {0.9, 0.8, 0.5})
    end
    broadcastToAll("First decal image: " .. first, {0.9, 0.8, 0.5})
    return true
  end
  applyAll()
  return true
end

function mapVersion() return MAP_VERSION end

function getOwners()
  local out = {}
  for n = 1, #RECTS do out[n] = state.owners[tostring(n)] end
  return out
end

function getTerritories()
  local out = {}
  for n = 1, #RECTS do out[n] = { name = NAMES[n], owner = state.owners[tostring(n)] } end
  return out
end

-- Map image coordinates (u, v in 0..1, top-left origin) -> world position on the board's top surface.
function uvToWorld(p)
  local W, H, D = localSize()
  local x = (0.5 - p.u) * W
  local z = (p.v - 0.5) * D
  if state.flipX then x = -x end
  if state.flipZ then z = -z end
  return self.positionToWorld({ x, H / 2, z })
end

-- World position -> map image coordinates.
function worldToUv(p)
  local l = self.positionToLocal(p.position)
  local W, _, D = localSize()
  local lx, lz = l.x, l.z
  if state.flipX then lx = -lx end
  if state.flipZ then lz = -lz end
  return { u = 0.5 - lx / W, v = 0.5 + lz / D }
end

function territoryAt(p)
  local pos = p.position
  if not pos then return nil end
  local uv = worldToUv({ position = pos })
  local u, v = uv.u, uv.v
  if u < 0 or u >= 1 or v < 0 or v >= 1 then return nil end
  local gx = math.floor(u * GRID_W)
  local gy = math.floor(v * GRID_H)
  local ch = string.sub(GRID, gy * GRID_W + gx + 1, gy * GRID_W + gx + 1)
  local idx = string.find(GRID_CHARS, ch, 1, true)
  if not idx or idx == 1 then return nil end
  return idx - 1
end

-- Right-click the map > "Open in War Council": shows the territory under the cursor in that player's panel.
function openInCouncil(color)
  local ok, pos = pcall(function() return Player[color].getPointerPosition() end)
  local n = ok and pos and territoryAt({ position = pos }) or nil
  if not n then
    broadcastToColor("No territory under the cursor.", color, { 0.9, 0.6, 0.5 })
    return
  end
  local wc = getObjectFromGUID("c0c0a1")
  if not wc then
    for _, o in ipairs(getAllObjects()) do if o.getName() == "War Council" then wc = o end end
  end
  if wc then wc.call("openTerritoryFor", { color = color, n = n }) end
end

-- Testing aid until the War Council exists: hover a territory and press the hotkey.
function hotkeyCycle(color, hovered, pointer)
  local n = territoryAt({ position = pointer })
  if not n then
    broadcastToColor("Eriador: no territory under the cursor.", color, {0.9, 0.6, 0.5})
    return
  end
  local cur = state.owners[tostring(n)]
  local nxt = (cur == "Neutral" and "Good") or (cur == "Good" and "Evil") or "Neutral"
  setOwner({ id = n, owner = nxt })
  broadcastToAll(string.format("Eriador: %d %s is now %s.", n, NAMES[n], nxt), {0.9, 0.8, 0.5})
end
]==]
do  -- keep helpers out of the main chunk (Lua allows 200 locals there)
local migrating = false

function mapMigrating() return migrating end

-- old map (u, v) -> new map (u, v)
local function cropUV(u, v)
  local c = MAP_CROP
  return (u * c.w - c.x0) / c.cw, (v * c.h - c.y0) / c.ch
end

local function dirOf(url)
  if type(url) ~= "string" then return nil end
  return string.match(url, "^(.*)/[^/]+$")
end

-- Where this PC keeps the campaign's images: from the board's own image, the UI icons, or Settings.
local function imageFolder(b)
  local cands = {}
  local ok, co = pcall(function() return b.getCustomObject() end)
  if ok and co and co.image then table.insert(cands, co.image) end
  pcall(function() for _, a in ipairs(UI.getCustomAssets() or {}) do if a.url then table.insert(cands, a.url) end end end)
  for _, u in ipairs(cands) do
    if string.sub(string.lower(u), 1, 5) == "file:" then return dirOf(u) end
  end
  local s = S.settings.imgFolder
  if type(s) == "string" and string.find(s, "%S") then
    s = string.gsub(s, "\\", "/")
    s = string.gsub(s, "/+$", "")
    if not string.find(s, "^%a+:/") or string.find(s, "^%a:/") then s = "file:///" .. s end
    return s
  end
  return nil
end

local function boardIsV2(b)
  local ok, v = pcall(function() return b.call("mapVersion") end)
  return ok and v == MAP_VERSION
end

-- Step 2: once the new board is up, move everything onto it.
local function settlePieces()
  local c = MAP_CROP
  -- custom slots from the Slot editor follow the map
  for n, ov in pairs(S.slotOv or {}) do
    for _, kind in ipairs({ "buildings", "units" }) do
      for i, p in pairs(ov[kind] or {}) do
        local u, v = cropUV(p[1] or p["1"], p[2] or p["2"])
        ov[kind][i] = { u, v }
      end
    end
  end
  -- armies, agents and wagons: one per unit slot in their territory
  local used = {}
  for _, pair in ipairs({ { "army", S.armies or {} }, { "agent", S.agents or {} } }) do
    for _, ent in ipairs(pair[2]) do
      local o = tokenObject(pair[1], ent)
      local n = (pair[1] == "army") and ent.loc or agentLoc(ent)
      if o and n then
        used[n] = (used[n] or 0) + 1
        local i = ((used[n] - 1) % NU_SLOTS) + 1
        local p = slotWorld(n, "units", i)
        if p then
          if used[n] > NU_SLOTS then p = { x = p.x + 0.5, y = p.y, z = p.z + 0.5 } end
          local y = math.max(o.getPosition().y, p.y + 0.5)
          o.setPosition({ p.x, y, p.z })
          ent.tok.pos = { p.x, p.z }
          ent.tok.terr = n
        end
      end
    end
  end
  -- buildings onto their (new) slots
  for n, t in ipairs(S.terr) do
    for key, has in pairs(t.b or {}) do if has then pcall(placeBuilding, n, key) end end
  end
  pcall(reconcileMarkers, true)
  pcall(clearHints)
  pcall(reconcileRoads, true)
  S.mapVer = MAP_VERSION
  migrating = false
  broadcastToAll("War Council: the campaign is on the new map. Every piece has been moved to its territory.", { 0.6, 0.9, 0.6 })
  if rebuild then pcall(rebuild) end
end

-- Step 1: swap the board's image and script (the board keeps its owners), then settle the pieces.
function migrateMap()
  if migrating then return true end
  local b = getBoard()
  if not b then return false, "Couldn't find the map board." end
  if boardIsV2(b) then
    if (S.mapVer or 1) < MAP_VERSION then migrating = true; settlePieces() end
    return true
  end
  local base = imageFolder(b)
  if not base then
    return false, "Couldn't find where the campaign's images are on this PC. Fill in 'Image folder' in Settings > Map & pieces, then press Update map."
  end
  migrating = true
  broadcastToAll("War Council: moving the campaign onto the new map...", { 0.9, 0.8, 0.5 })
  local script = string.gsub(BOARD_SCRIPT_V2, "{{IMG}}", function() return base end)
  b.setLuaScript(script)
  local co = b.getCustomObject() or {}
  co.image = base .. "/Eriador_Board_v2.jpg"
  b.setCustomObject(co)
  b.reload()
  Wait.condition(function()
      Wait.time(function()
        local ok, err = pcall(settlePieces)
        if not ok then migrating = false; print("War Council map update: " .. tostring(err)) end
      end, 2)
    end,
    function()
      local nb = getBoard()
      return nb ~= nil and not nb.loading_custom and not nb.spawning and boardIsV2(nb)
    end,
    30,
    function()
      migrating = false
      broadcastToAll("War Council: the new map didn't load. Check Eriador_Board_v2.jpg is in the campaign's image folder (run update_map.bat), then press Update map in Settings.", { 0.95, 0.6, 0.5 })
    end)
  return true
end

end
