}         -- per territory: { neighbour, {u1, v1, u2, v2, ...} } from its hub to that border

local HINT_T = 0.1871     -- our line thickness; identifies our lines among the table's vector lines
local hintKey = nil

-- World frame of the map: P(u, v) = O + u*U + v*V (lifted slightly above the surface)
local function frame()
  local b = getBoard()
  if not b then return nil end
  local o = b.call("uvToWorld", { u = 0, v = 0 })
  local pu = b.call("uvToWorld", { u = 1, v = 0 })
  local pv = b.call("uvToWorld", { u = 0, v = 1 })
  if type(o) ~= "table" or type(pu) ~= "table" or type(pv) ~= "table" then return nil end
  return { o = { o.x, o.y, o.z }, u = { pu.x - o.x, pu.y - o.y, pu.z - o.z }, v = { pv.x - o.x, pv.y - o.y, pv.z - o.z } }
end

local function at(F, u, v, lift)
  return { F.o[1] + u * F.u[1] + v * F.v[1], F.o[2] + u * F.u[2] + v * F.v[2] + (lift or 0.3), F.o[3] + u * F.u[3] + v * F.v[3] }
end

-- Yaw that makes flat text read with its top toward the top of the map.
function mapYaw()
  local F = frame()
  if not F then return 0 end
  return math.deg((math.atan2 or math.atan)(-F.v[1], -F.v[3])) + ((S.settings.markerFlip and 180) or 0)
end

local function isOurs(line) return line.thickness and math.abs(line.thickness - HINT_T) < 0.0005 end

-- Roads: two lines per stretch (dark edge, light top), told apart from other lines by their thickness.
do  -- keep helpers out of the main chunk (Lua allows 200 locals there)
local ROAD_EDGE_T, ROAD_TOP_T = 0.2217, 0.1263
local ROAD_EDGE_C, ROAD_TOP_C = { 0.24, 0.16, 0.09, 0.6 }, { 0.77, 0.66, 0.46, 0.7 }
local function isRoad(line)
  local t = line.thickness
  return t and (math.abs(t - ROAD_EDGE_T) < 0.0005 or math.abs(t - ROAD_TOP_T) < 0.0005
                or math.abs(t - 0.3417) < 0.0005 or math.abs(t - 0.1953) < 0.0005)   -- also clears the first, thicker roads
end
local roadKey = nil

function roadsOn() return S and S.settings.roadLines ~= false end

-- World points of the road from territory a's hub to the shared border with b (nil if they don't touch).
function roadWorld(a, b)
  for _, seg in ipairs(ROADS[a] or {}) do
    if seg[1] == b then
      local F = frame(); if not F then return nil end
      local c, out = seg[2], {}
      for i = 1, #c - 1, 2 do
        local p = at(F, c[i], c[i + 1], 0)
        table.insert(out, { x = p[1], y = p[2], z = p[3] })
      end
      return out
    end
  end
  return nil
end

-- Draw a road in every territory with finished Roads, reaching out to each neighbouring territory.
function reconcileRoads(force)
  if not S then return end
  local list = {}
  if S.started and roadsOn() then
    for n, t in ipairs(S.terr or {}) do if t.b and t.b.roads then table.insert(list, n) end end
  end
  local key = table.concat(list, ",")
  if key == roadKey and not force then return end
  local F = frame(); if not F then return end
  local edge, top = {}, {}
  for _, n in ipairs(list) do
    for _, seg in ipairs(ROADS[n] or {}) do
      local c = seg[2]
      local pe, pt = {}, {}
      for i = 1, #c - 1, 2 do
        table.insert(pe, at(F, c[i], c[i + 1], 0.08))
        table.insert(pt, at(F, c[i], c[i + 1], 0.09))
      end
      table.insert(edge, { points = pe, color = ROAD_EDGE_C, thickness = ROAD_EDGE_T })
      table.insert(top, { points = pt, color = ROAD_TOP_C, thickness = ROAD_TOP_T })
    end
  end
  local keep = {}
  local ok, cur = pcall(function() return Global.getVectorLines() end)
  if ok and cur then
    for _, l in ipairs(cur) do if not isRoad(l) then table.insert(keep, l) end end
  end
  for _, l in ipairs(edge) do table.insert(keep, l) end   -- edges first so the light tops draw over them
  for _, l in ipairs(top) do table.insert(keep, l) end
  pcall(function() Global.setVectorLines(keep) end)
  roadKey = key
end
end

local function setLines(ours)
  local keep = {}
  local ok, cur = pcall(function() return Global.getVectorLines() end)
  if ok and cur then
    for _, l in ipairs(cur) do if not isOurs(l) then table.insert(keep, l) end end
  end
  for _, l in ipairs(ours) do table.insert(keep, l) end
  pcall(function() Global.setVectorLines(keep) end)
end

function hintsAllowed()
  local m = S.settings.mapHints or "auto"
  if m == "off" then return false end
  if m == "on" then return true end
  return not S.settings.hidden
end

function clearHints()
  if hintKey == nil then return end
  hintKey = nil
  setLines({})
end

local function outline(F, n, color, out)
  for _, poly in ipairs(OUTLINES[n] or {}) do
    local pts = {}
    for _, p in ipairs(poly) do table.insert(pts, at(F, p[1], p[2])) end
    table.insert(out, { points = pts, color = color, thickness = HINT_T, loop = true })
  end
end

local GOLD = { 0.96, 0.80, 0.35 }
local SIDE_RGB = { Good = { 0.40, 0.85, 0.50 }, Evil = { 0.95, 0.40, 0.35 }, Neutral = { 0.85, 0.82, 0.75 } }

-- Outline where something at `from` can move to (list of territory numbers).
function showRange(side, from, list, key)
  if not hintsAllowed() then return clearHints() end
  if key and key == hintKey then return end
  local F = frame(); if not F then return end
  local out = {}
  for _, n in ipairs(list) do if n ~= from then outline(F, n, GOLD, out) end end
  outline(F, from, SIDE_RGB[side] or GOLD, out)
  hintKey = key
  setLines(out)
end

-- Dashed line along a wagon's route.
function showRoute(g, key)
  if not hintsAllowed() then return clearHints() end
  if key and key == hintKey then return end
  local r = g.route
  if not r or not r.legs then return clearHints() end
  local F = frame(); if not F then return end
  local seq = { r.start }
  for _, leg in ipairs(r.legs) do for _, n in ipairs(leg.steps or {}) do table.insert(seq, n) end end
  local out = {}
  local col = SIDE_RGB[g.side] or GOLD
  for i = 1, #seq - 1 do
    local a, b = LABELPOS[seq[i]], LABELPOS[seq[i + 1]]
    if a and b and seq[i] ~= seq[i + 1] then
      local steps = 8
      for s = 0, steps - 1, 2 do
        local t0, t1 = s / steps, (s + 1) / steps
        table.insert(out, { points = { at(F, a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * t0, a[2] + (b[2] - a[2]) * t0, 0.35),
                                       at(F, a[1] + (b[1] - a[1]) * t1, a[2] + (b[2] - a[2]) * t1, 0.35) },
                            color = col, thickness = HINT_T })
      end
    end
  end
  for _, n in ipairs(seq) do outline(F, n, GOLD, out) end
  hintKey = key
  setLines(out)
end

-- ===================================================================
-- conquest markers (3D text on the map)
-- ===================================================================
local function markerTag(n) return "wc:m:" .. n end

local function markerText(n)
  local c = S.terr[n].conq
  local lines, by = {}, nil
  if c then
    by = c.by
    table.insert(lines, c.held and (c.by .. ": blocked by fortress") or (c.by .. " conquering " .. c.prog .. "/" .. c.need))
  end
  -- public territory actions and their effects (secret ones only once discovered)
  for _, l in ipairs(actionLines and actionLines(n) or {}) do
    table.insert(lines, l[2])
    by = by or l[1]
  end
  if #lines == 0 then return nil end
  return table.concat(lines, "\n"), by
end

-- Territory markers (3D text over the map). Rewriting all forty of them after every click made the game hitch,
-- so each marker remembers what it shows and is only touched when that changes. The table is only searched
-- when a marker has gone missing.
MARKERS = MARKERS or {}
function reconcileMarkers(force)
  if not S or not S.started then return end
  local F = frame(); if not F then return end
  local nx, nz = -F.v[1], -F.v[3]
  local yaw = math.deg((math.atan2 or math.atan)(nx, nz)) + ((S.settings.markerFlip and 180) or 0)
  local want = {}
  local needScan = force or MARKERS_SCANNED == nil
  for n = 1, #S.terr do
    local txt, by = markerText(n)
    want[n] = txt and { txt = txt, by = by } or nil
    local m = MARKERS[n]
    if (txt or (m and m.guid)) and m and m.guid and not getObjectFromGUID(m.guid) then needScan = true end
    if txt and not (m and m.guid) then needScan = true end
  end
  if needScan then
    local have = {}
    for _, o in ipairs(getAllObjects()) do
      local g = o.getGMNotes and o.getGMNotes() or ""
      local n = tonumber(string.match(g, "^wc:m:(%d+)$"))
      if n then
        if have[n] then o.destruct() else have[n] = o end
      end
    end
    for n = 1, #S.terr do
      local o = have[n]
      if o then
        if not MARKERS[n] or MARKERS[n].guid ~= o.getGUID() then MARKERS[n] = { guid = o.getGUID() } end
      else
        MARKERS[n] = nil
      end
    end
    MARKERS_SCANNED = true
  end
  for n = 1, #S.terr do
    local w, m = want[n], MARKERS[n]
    local o = m and m.guid and getObjectFromGUID(m.guid)
    if not w then
      if o then o.destruct() end
      MARKERS[n] = nil
    else
      local lp = LABELPOS[n] or { 0.5, 0.5 }
      local pos = at(F, lp[1], lp[2] + 0.018, 0.15)
      local placeKey = string.format("%.2f,%.2f,%.2f,%.1f", pos[1], pos[2], pos[3], yaw)
      if not o then
        o = spawnObject({ type = "3DText", position = pos, rotation = { 90, yaw, 0 } })
        o.setGMNotes(markerTag(n))
        m = { guid = o.getGUID() }
        MARKERS[n] = m
      elseif m.place ~= placeKey then
        o.setPosition(pos)
        o.setRotation({ 90, yaw, 0 })
      end
      m.place = placeKey
      local rgb = SIDE_RGB[w.by] or GOLD
      local look = w.txt .. "|" .. tostring(w.by)
      if m.look ~= look then
        pcall(function()
          o.TextTool.setValue(w.txt)
          o.TextTool.setFontSize(48)
          o.TextTool.setFontColor({ rgb[1], rgb[2], rgb[3] })
        end)
        o.setLock(true)
        m.look = look
      end
    end
  end
end

--[[ ===================================================================
     WAR COUNCIL - Part 2: screen overlay UI  (v2 look: header + side menu + cards)
     =================================================================== ]]

local PW, PH = 1200, 780
local NAV_W, HEAD_H = 196, 64
local MAIN_W = PW - NAV_W - 48          -- usable content width
local BODY_H = PH - HEAD_H - 34 - 26    -- usable content height under the message line
local SIZES = { Compact = { 1040, 690 }, Normal = { 1200, 780 }, Large = { 1380, 880 } }
local function applySize()
  local s = SIZES[(S and S.settings.panelSize) or "Normal"] or SIZES.Normal
  PW, PH = s[1], s[2]
  MAIN_W = PW - NAV_W - 48
  BODY_H = PH - HEAD_H - 34 - 26
end
-- custom UI images (wordmark, icons) live in the save's Custom UI Assets; fall back to text without them
local ASSETS = {}
local function loadAssets()
  ASSETS = {}
  local ok, list = pcall(function() return UI.getCustomAssets() end)
  if ok and type(list) == "table" then
    for _, a in ipairs(list) do if a.name then ASSETS[a.name] = true end end
  end
end
local function hasAsset(n) return ASSETS[n] == true end
local COLORS = { "White", "Brown", "Red", "Orange", "Yellow", "Green", "Teal", "Blue", "Purple", "Pink", "Grey", "Black" }
local C = {
  bg = "#1C150F", head = "#140F0A", nav = "#17110C", card = "#241B13", deep = "#17110C", line = "#4A3B2A", line2 = "#3A2E21",
  raised = "#3A2C1B", frame = "#6E5530",
  gold = "#E9C877", goldHi = "#F0D08A", ink = "#EFE4CC", dim = "#B7A687", dim2 = "#D8C9A8",
  good = "#5DBB7A", goodBg = "#20382A", goodBg2 = "#1F2A1F", goodInk = "#A9DFB8",
  evil = "#E06A5F", evilBg = "#2C1C1A", evilInk = "#F0B2AA",
  neutral = "#8C8576", warn = "#F0C27A", warnBg = "#2E2414", err = "#F0A59B", ok = "#9FD4AE", okBg = "#1C2A20",
}
-- Button colour sets: normal | highlighted | pressed | disabled
local BTN       = "#2E241A|#3A2E21|#241B13|#211912"   -- secondary
local BTN_GHOST = "#241B13|#33281C|#1C150F|#201810"   -- quiet, on cards
local BTN_SEL   = "#3A2C1B|#4A3822|#2E2216|#3A2C1B"   -- selected chip / list row
local BTN_GOLD  = "#C9A24F|#D9B566|#A8873E|#4A3D27"   -- primary action
local BTN_GOOD  = "#2E6B40|#3A7D4D|#245633|#24332A"
local BTN_EVIL  = "#7A2E28|#8C3A33|#5E231E|#3A2522"
local BTN_DANGER = "#5A2420|#6E2E28|#461C19|#2E1E1B"
local BTN_NAV   = "#17110C|#2A2016|#3A2C1B|#17110C"
local BTN_ON    = BTN_SEL
-- non-interactive chips use the same colour for every state
local function flat(c) return c .. "|" .. c .. "|" .. c .. "|" .. c end

U = {}
local baseXml = {}
local GP = "" -- "<guid>/"
-- Settings every Text and Button share, sent once instead of on each of them (fewer to send, compare and redraw).
UI_DEFAULTS = { tag = "Defaults", attributes = {}, children = {
  { tag = "Text", attributes = { horizontalOverflow = "Wrap", verticalOverflow = "Overflow" }, children = {} },
  { tag = "Button", attributes = { tooltipPosition = "Above" }, children = {} },
} }

local function sideColor(side)
  if side == "Good" then return C.good elseif side == "Evil" then return C.evil end
  return C.neutral
end
local function sideInk(side)
  if side == "Good" then return C.goodInk elseif side == "Evil" then return C.evilInk end
  return C.dim2
end
local function sideBg(side)
  if side == "Good" then return C.goodBg2 elseif side == "Evil" then return C.evilBg end
  return C.card
end
local function sideBtn(side)
  if side == "Good" then return BTN_GOOD elseif side == "Evil" then return BTN_EVIL end
  return BTN_SEL
end

local function uiState(pk)
  if not U[pk] then U[pk] = { tab = "Turn", open = true, acting = (pk == "Evil") and "Evil" or "Good", sel = {}, f = {}, msg = nil, msgOk = false } end
  return U[pk]
end

-- Each seat has its own panel (its own tab, selection and half-typed forms), so two people using the War Council
-- at once don't pull each other's view around. Anyone not in a seat shares the "All" panel.
function panelKeys() return { "Good", "Evil", "All" } end

local function visFor(pk)
  if pk == "Good" then return S.settings.goodColor end
  if pk == "Evil" then return S.settings.evilColor end
  local out = {}
  for _, c in ipairs(COLORS) do
    if c ~= S.settings.goodColor and c ~= S.settings.evilColor then table.insert(out, c) end
  end
  return table.concat(out, "|")
end

function actingSide(pk)
  if pk == "All" or not S.settings.hidden then return uiState(pk).acting end
  return pk
end

-- Can the viewer of panel pk see private information belonging to `side`?
function canSee(pk, side)
  if not S.settings.hidden or pk == "All" then return true end
  return side == nil or side == "Neutral" or side == pk
end

local function stageAllowed(stage)
  return S.settings.override or (S.started and STAGES[S.stage] == stage)
end

-- ===================================================================
-- element helpers
-- ===================================================================
local NO_FLEX = { Option = true }
-- The game turns the UI table into XML; a stray "<" in any text can break the whole panel, so swap it out.
local function safeText(s)
  if type(s) ~= "string" or not string.find(s, "<", 1, true) then return s end
  return (string.gsub(s, "<", "\226\128\185"))
end
local TEXT_ATTRS = { tooltip = true, text = true, placeholder = true }
local function el(tag, attrs, children, value)
  local a = {}
  for k, v in pairs(attrs or {}) do a[k] = TEXT_ATTRS[k] and safeText(tostring(v)) or tostring(v) end
  value = safeText(value)
  if not NO_FLEX[tag] and a.flexibleHeight == nil then a.flexibleHeight = "0" end
  return { tag = tag, attributes = a, children = children or {}, value = value }
end

local function filler() return { tag = "Panel", attributes = { flexibleHeight = "1", preferredHeight = "0" }, children = {} } end
local function hOf(e) return tonumber(e and e.attributes and e.attributes.preferredHeight) or 24 end

-- rough line count for wrapped text: chars per line ~ width / (size * 0.5)
local function wrapH(s, size, width, lineH)
  size = size or 14
  local per = math.max(10, math.floor((width or MAIN_W) / (size * 0.52)))
  local lines = 0
  for part in string.gmatch((s or "") .. "\n", "([^\n]*)\n") do lines = lines + math.max(1, math.ceil(string.len(part) / per)) end
  return math.max(1, lines) * (lineH or math.floor(size * 1.45))
end

local function txt(s, o)
  o = o or {}
  local size = o.size or 14
  return el("Text", {
    fontSize = size, color = o.color or C.ink, alignment = o.align or "MiddleLeft",
    fontStyle = o.style or "Normal", preferredHeight = o.h or ((o.wrap and wrapH(s, size, o.ww or o.w)) or math.max(20, math.floor(size * 1.6))),
    preferredWidth = o.w or -1, flexibleWidth = o.w and 0 or 1,   -- overflow: see UI_DEFAULTS
  }, nil, s)
end

-- uppercase small label (section kicker)
local function cap(s, color, o)
  o = o or {}
  return txt(string.upper(s), { size = o.size or 11, style = "Bold", color = color or C.dim, h = o.h or 16, w = o.w, align = o.align })
end

local function act(pk, ...)
  local parts = { pk }
  for _, v in ipairs({ ... }) do table.insert(parts, tostring(v)) end
  return GP .. "ui(" .. table.concat(parts, "~") .. ")"
end

local function btn(pk, label, o, ...)
  o = o or {}
  local colors = o.colors or BTN
  local tc = o.textColor
  if not tc then
    if colors == BTN_GOLD then tc = (o.enabled == false) and "#8C7A55" or "#1C150F"
    elseif colors == BTN_SEL then tc = C.goldHi
    elseif colors == BTN_GOOD then tc = "#F2FBF4"
    elseif colors == BTN_EVIL then tc = "#FBE9E6"
    elseif colors == BTN_DANGER then tc = "#F8D6D1"
    else tc = (o.enabled == false) and "#7D6F57" or C.ink end
  end
  return el("Button", {
    onClick = act(pk, ...), fontSize = o.size or 13, textColor = tc,
    fontStyle = o.style or ((colors == BTN_GOLD or colors == BTN_SEL) and "Bold" or "Normal"),
    textAlignment = o.align or "MiddleCenter",
    colors = colors, preferredHeight = o.h or 32, preferredWidth = o.w or -1,
    flexibleWidth = o.w and 0 or 1, interactable = (o.enabled == false) and "false" or "true",
    tooltip = o.tip or "",
    icon = (o.icon and hasAsset(o.icon)) and o.icon or nil, iconWidth = (o.icon and hasAsset(o.icon)) and (o.iconWidth or 20) or nil,
    iconColor = (o.icon and hasAsset(o.icon)) and (o.iconColor or tc) or nil,
  }, nil, label)
end

-- non-interactive pill / chip
local function chip(label, fg, bg, o)
  o = o or {}
  return el("Button", { interactable = "false", colors = flat(bg or C.raised), textColor = fg or C.dim2, fontSize = o.size or 12,
                        fontStyle = o.style or "Bold", preferredHeight = o.h or 26, preferredWidth = o.w or -1,
                        flexibleWidth = o.w and 0 or 1, tooltip = o.tip or "" }, nil, label)
end

local function spacer(w) return el("Panel", { preferredWidth = w or 0, flexibleWidth = w and 0 or 1, preferredHeight = 1 }) end
local function gap(h) return el("Panel", { preferredHeight = h or 6, flexibleWidth = 1 }) end
local function swatch(color, w, h) return el("Panel", { color = color, preferredWidth = w or 4, flexibleWidth = 0, preferredHeight = h or 20 }) end

local function maxH(children, min)
  local h = min or 0
  for _, c in ipairs(children) do h = math.max(h, hOf(c)) end
  return h
end

local function row(children, h, spacing, fw)
  return el("HorizontalLayout", { spacing = spacing or 8, preferredHeight = h or maxH(children, 28), childForceExpandWidth = "false",
                                  flexibleWidth = fw or 1,
                                  childForceExpandHeight = "true", childAlignment = "MiddleLeft" }, children)
end

local function stackH(children, spacing, padV)
  local h = padV or 0
  for i, c in ipairs(children) do h = h + hOf(c) + ((i > 1) and (spacing or 0) or 0) end
  return h
end

local function col(children, w, spacing)
  return el("VerticalLayout", { spacing = spacing or 8, preferredWidth = w or -1, flexibleWidth = w and 0 or 1,
                                preferredHeight = stackH(children, spacing or 8),
                                childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true",
                                childAlignment = "UpperLeft" }, children)
end

-- a bordered card; o.bg, o.border, o.pad ("l r t b"), o.spacing, o.w, o.h
local function card(children, o)
  o = o or {}
  local sp = o.spacing or 8
  local pt, pb = 14, 14
  local h = o.h or stackH(children, sp, pt + pb)
  if o.h and stackH(children, sp, pt + pb) + sp <= o.h then
    local kids = {}
    for _, c in ipairs(children) do table.insert(kids, c) end
    table.insert(kids, filler())
    children = kids
  end
  return el("VerticalLayout", { color = o.bg or C.card, outline = o.border or C.line, outlineSize = "1 -1",
                                padding = o.pad or ("16 16 " .. pt .. " " .. pb), spacing = sp,
                                preferredHeight = h, preferredWidth = o.w or -1, flexibleWidth = o.w and 0 or 1,
                                childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true", childAlignment = "UpperLeft" }, children)
end

-- coloured notice strip
local function strip(s, tone, o)
  o = o or {}
  local fg, bg = C.warn, C.warnBg
  if tone == "ok" then fg, bg = C.ok, C.okBg elseif tone == "err" then fg, bg = C.err, C.evilBg
  elseif tone == "dim" then fg, bg = C.dim2, C.deep end
  local h = o.h or (wrapH(s, 13, (o.w or MAIN_W) - 40) + 16)
  return el("HorizontalLayout", { color = bg, padding = "12 12 6 6", spacing = 10, preferredHeight = h, childForceExpandWidth = "false",
                                  childForceExpandHeight = "true" }, {
    swatch(fg, 3, h - 12), txt(s, { size = 13, color = fg, h = h - 12 }) })
end

local function heading(s, o)
  o = o or {}
  return txt(s, { size = o.size or 16, color = C.gold, style = "Bold", h = o.h or 24, w = o.w })
end
local header = heading

-- capacity bar
local function bar(frac, color, h)
  frac = math.max(0, math.min(1, frac or 0))
  local kids = {}
  if frac > 0 then table.insert(kids, el("Panel", { color = color or C.gold, flexibleWidth = frac, preferredWidth = 0 })) end
  if frac < 1 then table.insert(kids, el("Panel", { flexibleWidth = 1 - frac, preferredWidth = 0 })) end
  return el("HorizontalLayout", { color = C.deep, spacing = 0, preferredHeight = h or 8, childForceExpandWidth = "false",
                                  childForceExpandHeight = "true" }, kids)
end

-- stat tile: big value + label (+ optional sub line)
local function tile(value, label, o)
  o = o or {}
  local kids = { txt(tostring(value), { size = o.size or 24, style = "Bold", color = o.color or C.ink, h = 32 }),
                 txt(label, { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 }) }
  if o.sub then table.insert(kids, txt(o.sub, { size = 12, color = o.subColor or C.dim2, h = 18 })) end
  if o.frac then table.insert(kids, bar(o.frac, o.barColor or C.gold, 6)) end
  local h = stackH(kids, 2, 20)
  return el("VerticalLayout", { color = o.bg or C.deep, padding = "12 12 10 10", spacing = 2, preferredHeight = o.h or h,
                                preferredWidth = o.w or -1, flexibleWidth = o.w and 0 or 1,
                                childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true" }, kids)
end

local function scroll(children, h, w, spacing)
  spacing = spacing or 8
  local kids = {}
  for _, c in ipairs(children) do table.insert(kids, c) end
  table.insert(kids, filler())
  local inner = el("VerticalLayout", { height = math.max(h - 4, stackH(children, spacing, 4) + spacing), spacing = spacing, padding = "0 12 0 4",
                                       childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true", childAlignment = "UpperLeft" }, kids)
  return el("VerticalScrollView", { preferredHeight = h, preferredWidth = w or -1, flexibleWidth = w and 0 or 1,
                                    color = "#00000000", scrollSensitivity = 40, verticalScrollbarVisibility = "AutoHideAndExpandViewport",
                                    horizontalScrollbarVisibility = "Hidden", scrollbarColors = "#3A2E21|#6E5530|#8A6A2E|#3A2E21",
                                    scrollbarBackgroundColor = "#17110C" }, { inner })
end

local function inputId(pk, field) return "wc_" .. pk .. "_" .. field end

local function input(pk, field, placeholder, o)
  o = o or {}
  local f = uiState(pk).f
  return el("InputField", {
    id = inputId(pk, field), onEndEdit = GP .. "uiInput", text = f[field] or o.default or "",
    placeholder = placeholder or "", fontSize = o.size or 14, preferredHeight = o.h or 32, preferredWidth = o.w or -1,
    flexibleWidth = o.w and 0 or 1, characterValidation = o.int and "Integer" or "None",
    colors = "#F3E7C9|#FFF5DC|#FFF5DC|#B8AC90", textColor = "#2A1C10",
  })
end

-- A choice box. Tabletop Simulator's own dropdowns can't change their options without redrawing the whole screen,
-- so this is a button showing the current choice; clicking it opens the panel's chooser list (see pickerModal).
PICK_COLORS = "#F3E7C9|#FFF5DC|#FFF5DC|#B8AC90"
local function dropdown(pk, field, options, o)
  o = o or {}
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local f = u.f
  local cur = f[field]
  local found = false
  for _, opt in ipairs(options) do if opt == cur then found = true end end
  if not found then cur = options[1]; f[field] = cur end
  u.opts = u.opts or {}
  u.opts[field] = options
  if OPT_REC then OPT_REC[field] = options end
  return btn(pk, "  " .. tostring(cur or "") .. "   ▼", { w = o.w, h = o.h or 32, size = 14, align = "MiddleLeft", colors = PICK_COLORS,
                                                                 textColor = "#2A1C10", enabled = #options > 0 }, "pickopen", field)
end

-- on/off switch drawn as a small pill button
local function switch(pk, on, o, ...)
  o = o or {}
  return btn(pk, on and "ON" or "OFF", { w = o.w or 58, h = o.h or 28, size = 12, style = "Bold",
                                       colors = on and ((o.danger and BTN_DANGER) or BTN_GOOD) or BTN, textColor = on and "#F2FBF4" or C.dim,
                                       tip = o.tip, enabled = o.enabled }, ...)
end

-- a form field toggle stored in u.f (value "True"/"False") drawn as a check button
local function toggle(pk, field, label, o)
  o = o or {}
  local on = uiState(pk).f[field] == "True"
  return btn(pk, (on and "[x]  " or "[  ]  ") .. label, { w = o.w, h = o.h or 30, align = "MiddleLeft", size = 13,
                                                     colors = on and BTN_SEL or BTN_GHOST, textColor = on and C.goldHi or C.dim2,
                                                     tip = o.tip, style = "Normal" }, "ftog", field)
end

-- segmented choice stored in u.f
local function choice(pk, field, options, o)
  o = o or {}
  local kids = {}
  local cur = uiState(pk).f[field] or o.default
  for _, opt in ipairs(options) do
    local val, label = opt, opt
    if type(opt) == "table" then val, label = opt[1], opt[2] end
    local on = tostring(cur) == tostring(val)
    table.insert(kids, btn(pk, label, { colors = on and (o.onColors or BTN_SEL) or BTN_GHOST, textColor = (not on) and C.dim2 or nil,
                                        w = o.w, h = o.h or 32, tip = o.tips and o.tips[tostring(val)] or nil }, "set", field, val))
  end
  return row(kids, o.h or 32, 4)
end

-- label column + control row used by forms
local function field(label, control, o)
  o = o or {}
  return row({ txt(label, { w = o.lw or 120, color = C.dim, size = 13 }), control }, o.h or hOf(control))
end

-- two-line selectable list row with a coloured edge
local function listRow(pk, title, sub, selected, edge, o, ...)
  o = o or {}
  local label = title .. ((sub and sub ~= "") and ("\n" .. sub) or "")
  local h = o.h or ((sub and sub ~= "") and 54 or 38)
  return row({ swatch(edge or C.line, 4, h),
               btn(pk, "  " .. string.gsub(label, "\n", "\n  "), { h = h, align = "MiddleLeft", size = 13, colors = selected and BTN_SEL or BTN_GHOST,
                                                                   textColor = selected and C.goldHi or C.ink, style = selected and "Bold" or "Normal",
                                                                   tip = o.tip }, ...) }, h, 0)
end

local function numOf(s) return tonumber(string.match(s or "", "^(%d+)")) end
local function idOf(s) return tonumber(string.match(s or "", "#(%d+)")) end

local function terrOpt(n) return tostring(n) .. " " .. NAMES[n] end
local function terrDot(n) return tostring(n) .. " · " .. NAMES[n] end

-- ===================================================================
-- visibility of armies
-- ===================================================================
local function armyKnown(pk, a)
  if canSee(pk, a.side) then return true, true end
  local viewer = pk
  local tower = towerSees(viewer, a.loc)
  return (a.seenLeader[viewer] or tower) and true or false, (a.seenUnits[viewer] or tower) and true or false
end

local function armyLabel(pk, a)
  if canSee(pk, a.side) then return a.name end
  -- once its leader has been seen the enemy knows the army by name (its model on the table shows it too)
  if a.seenLeader and a.seenLeader[pk] then return a.name .. ((a.leader ~= "") and (" (" .. a.leader .. ")") or "") end
  local leader = armyKnown(pk, a)
  return publicName(a) .. (leader and (" (" .. (a.leader ~= "" and a.leader or "unnamed leader") .. ")") or "")
end

local function armyStatus(a)
  local bits = {}
  if a.status == "building" then table.insert(bits, "raising, " .. a.buildLeft .. "t") end
  if a.upLeft then table.insert(bits, "upgrading") end
  if (a.damage or 0) > 0 then table.insert(bits, "-" .. a.damage .. "%") end
  if a.replenishing then table.insert(bits, "replenishing") end
  if a.needsRetreat then table.insert(bits, "MUST RETREAT") end
  if (a.exh or 0) > 0 then table.insert(bits, "exhausted") end
  if (a.absent or 0) > 0 then table.insert(bits, "leader absent") end
  if outOfSupplies(a) then table.insert(bits, "OUT OF SUPPLIES") end
  if a.moved then table.insert(bits, "moved") end
  if #bits == 0 then return "ready" end
  return table.concat(bits, ", ")
end

local function escPat(s) return (string.gsub(s, "[%^%$%(%)%%%.%[%]%*%+%-%?]", "%%%0")) end

-- Replace enemy army names the viewer shouldn't know with how they know them.
function maskNames(pk, line, armies)
  for _, a in ipairs(armies or S.armies) do
    if not canSee(pk, a.side) and a.name and a.name ~= "" then
      local lbl = armyLabel(pk, a)
      line = string.gsub(line, escPat(a.name), function() return lbl end)
    end
  end
  return line
end

function fieldable(a) return math.floor(a.size * (1 - (a.damage or 0) / 100)) end

local function rosterOver(a)
  local r = a.roster
  if r and r.points and r.points > fieldable(a) then
    return a.name .. "'s roster is " .. r.points .. " pts, but it can only field " .. fieldable(a) .. " pts right now" ..
           (((a.damage or 0) > 0) and (" (-" .. a.damage .. "%)") or "") .. "."
  end
end

-- "11 of 31 lost = 35% · broken"
function lostInfo(a, v)
  local r = a and a.roster
  local lost = tonumber(v)
  if not r or not r.units or not lost then return nil end
  local pct = math.floor(lost * 100 / math.max(1, r.units) + 0.5)
  local st = ""
  if r.broken and lost >= r.broken then st = " · broken" end
  if r.quartered and (r.units - lost) <= r.quartered then st = " · quartered" end
  return pct, lost .. " of " .. r.units .. " lost = " .. pct .. "%" .. st
end

local function sideTotals(side)
  local t = { terrs = #ownedBy(side), stock = 0, carried = 0, wagons = 0, income = 0, armies = 0, raising = 0, agents = 0 }
  for _, n in ipairs(ownedBy(side)) do t.stock = t.stock + S.terr[n].stock; t.income = t.income + income(n) end
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
    if a.side == side then
      t.carried = t.carried + (a.carried or 0)
      if a.status == "building" then t.raising = t.raising + 1 else t.armies = t.armies + 1 end
    end
  end
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do
    if g.side == side then
      t.agents = t.agents + 1
      if isWagon(g) then t.wagons = t.wagons + (g.carried or 0) end
    end
  end
  return t
end

-- ===================================================================
-- tabs
-- ===================================================================

  outcome = {
    Broken = "Loses half its carried RP (the winner takes half of that; twice as much if it was conquering the winner's land). Must retreat 2+ territories. Disbanding later refunds 25%.",
    Quartered = "Loses 75% of its carried RP (the winner takes half of that; all of it if it was conquering the winner's land). Must retreat 2+ territories. No Warband Leaders left at the end? Record it as Destroyed.",
    Destroyed = "The army is removed. The winner takes half of everything it carried.",
  },
  duel = {
    none = "No Heroic Duel was fought.",
    atk = "The defender's Leader lost the duel and is absent until the end of next turn.",
    def = "The attacker's Leader lost the duel and is absent until the end of next turn.",
  },
}

local TABS = { "Turn", "Territories", "Armies", "Agents", "Wagons", "Battles", "Reports", "Supplies", "Log", "History", "Settings" }
local RULE_PAGES = { turn = 5, armies = 6, battles = 8, movement = 7, agents = 9, resources = 10, buildings = 11, conquest = 12, actions = 12, specials = 13, events = 16, campaigns = 3, map = 4 }
-- small "Rules" button that turns the campaign rulebook to the right page
local function ruleBtn(pk, key)
  return btn(pk, "Rules p" .. RULE_PAGES[key], { w = 84, h = 26, size = 11, colors = BTN_GHOST, textColor = C.dim,
                                                 tip = "Turn the campaign rulebook on the table to page " .. RULE_PAGES[key] }, "rule", key)
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- Turn
local function tabTurn(pk)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local out = {}
  if not S.started then
    table.insert(out, card({
      heading("No campaign is running", { size = 20, h = 30 }),
      txt("Set the seat colours, theatre, capitals and starting resources in Settings, then press Start new campaign.", { color = C.dim2, h = 24 }),
      row({ btn(pk, "Open Settings", { w = 200, h = 40, colors = BTN_GOLD }, "tab", "Settings"), spacer() }, 40),
    }))
    return out
  end
  if S.winner then
    table.insert(out, card({ txt(S.winner .. " has won the campaign!", { size = 24, color = sideColor(S.winner), style = "Bold", h = 36, align = "MiddleCenter" }) },
                           { bg = sideBg(S.winner), border = sideColor(S.winner) }))
  end

  -- turn + stage track + ready
  local top = {}
  table.insert(top, row({
    txt("Turn " .. S.turn, { size = 28, color = C.gold, style = "Bold", w = 150, h = 38 }),
    txt(stageName() .. " stage", { size = 16, color = C.dim2, w = 240, h = 38 }),
    spacer(),
    S.first and txt(S.first .. " acts first this turn", { size = 14, color = sideInk(S.first), w = 260, h = 38, align = "MiddleRight" }) or spacer(1),
    ruleBtn(pk, "turn"),
  }, 38))
  local chips = {}
  for i, s in ipairs(STAGES) do
    local past, now = i < S.stage, i == S.stage
    table.insert(chips, chip(i .. " · " .. s, now and "#1C150F" or (past and C.dim or C.dim2),
                             now and "#C9A24F" or (past and C.raised or C.deep), { h = 34, size = 13, tip = TIPS.stage[s] }))
  end
  table.insert(top, row(chips, 34, 6))

  if STAGES[S.stage] == "Priority" then
    if not S.prio then
      table.insert(top, row({ btn(pk, "Roll for priority", { w = 220, h = 36, colors = BTN_GOLD }, "prio"), spacer() }, 36))
    else
      table.insert(top, strip(S.prio.palantir and "Good used Círdan's Palantír and wins priority without a roll. Good chooses who acts first."
                              or ("Good rolled " .. S.prio.Good .. ", Evil rolled " .. S.prio.Evil .. ". " .. S.prio.winner .. " won and chooses who acts first."), "dim", { h = 34 }))
      if not S.first then
        local mine = (pk == "All") or not S.settings.hidden or (pk == S.prio.winner)
        table.insert(top, row({ btn(pk, "Good acts first", { w = 200, h = 36, colors = BTN_GOOD, enabled = mine }, "first", "Good"),
                                btn(pk, "Evil acts first", { w = 200, h = 36, colors = BTN_EVIL, enabled = mine }, "first", "Evil"), spacer() }, 36))
      end
    end
  end

  local nextLabel = (S.stage == #STAGES) and ("End turn - start Turn " .. (S.turn + 1)) or ("Advance to " .. STAGES[S.stage + 1])
  if S.settings.readyCheck then
    local kids = {}
    for _, sd in ipairs(SIDES) do
      local r = S.ready[sd]
      table.insert(kids, chip(r and (sd .. " is ready") or ("Waiting for " .. sd), r and "#F2FBF4" or C.dim,
                              r and ((sd == "Good") and "#2E6B40" or "#7A2E28") or C.deep, { w = 180, h = 38, size = 14 }))
    end
    table.insert(kids, spacer())
    local confirm = u.confirm == "force"
    table.insert(kids, btn(pk, confirm and "Click again to force" or "Force advance", { w = 170, h = 38, colors = confirm and BTN_DANGER or BTN,
                                                                                     tip = "Skips the ready check: " .. nextLabel }, "force"))
    if pk ~= "All" then
      table.insert(kids, btn(pk, S.ready[pk] and "Cancel ready" or ("I'm ready - " .. nextLabel), { w = 280, h = 38, size = 14,
                             colors = S.ready[pk] and BTN or BTN_GOLD }, "ready", pk))
    else
      for _, sd in ipairs(SIDES) do
        table.insert(kids, btn(pk, S.ready[sd] and ("Cancel " .. sd) or (sd .. " ready"), { w = 140, h = 38,
                               colors = S.ready[sd] and BTN or sideBtn(sd) }, "ready", sd))
      end
    end
    table.insert(top, row(kids, 38, 8))
    table.insert(top, txt("The stage advances to " .. (STAGES[S.stage + 1] or ("Turn " .. (S.turn + 1))) .. " once both sides are ready.", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 }))
  else
    table.insert(top, row({ spacer(), btn(pk, nextLabel, { w = 320, h = 40, size = 15, colors = BTN_GOLD }, "adv") }, 40))
  end
  table.insert(out, card(top, { spacing = 12 }))

  -- side summaries
  local cards = {}
  for _, side in ipairs(SIDES) do
    local t = sideTotals(side)
    local see = canSee(pk, side)
    local capN = (side == "Good") and S.settings.capGood or S.settings.capEvil
    local kids = {
      row({ swatch(sideColor(side), 10, 10), heading("Forces of " .. side, { size = 18, h = 26, w = 200 }), spacer(),
            txt("Capital: " .. (NAMES[capN] or "?"), { size = 13, color = C.dim, w = 170, align = "MiddleRight" }) }, 26),
      row({ tile(t.terrs, "Territories", { size = 22 }), tile(see and t.stock or "?", "RP stored", { size = 22 }),
            tile(see and t.armies or (t.armies + t.raising), "Armies", { size = 22 }), tile(see and t.agents or "?", "Agents", { size = 22 }) }, 72, 8),
      txt(see and ("+" .. t.income .. " RP next turn  ·  " .. t.raising .. ((t.raising == 1) and " army" or " armies") .. " being raised")
              or "Hidden information is on - details unknown", { size = 13, color = sideInk(side), h = 20 }),
    }
    table.insert(cards, card(kids, { bg = sideBg(side), border = (side == "Good") and "#36593F" or "#6B3631", spacing = 10 }))
  end
  table.insert(out, row(cards, maxH(cards), 14))

  -- coming up
  local soon = {}
  for n, t in ipairs(S.terr) do
    for _, q in ipairs(t.q) do
      if q.left == 1 and canSee(pk, t.owner) then table.insert(soon, { BUILDING_BY_KEY[q.key].name .. " finishes at " .. NAMES[n] .. (q.halt and " (halted)" or ""), t.owner }) end
    end
    if t.conq then
      table.insert(soon, { NAMES[n] .. ": " .. (t.conq.held and ("conquest by " .. t.conq.by .. " blocked by the fortress at " .. NAMES[t.conq.held])
                                                          or ("conquest by " .. t.conq.by .. " " .. t.conq.prog .. " of " .. t.conq.need)), t.conq.by })
    end
  end
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
    if canSee(pk, a.side) then
      if a.status == "building" and a.buildLeft == 1 then table.insert(soon, { a.name .. " is ready next turn", a.side }) end
      if a.upLeft == 1 then table.insert(soon, { a.name .. " finishes upgrading", a.side }) end
      if a.needsRetreat then table.insert(soon, { a.name .. " must retreat (Retreat stage)", a.side }) end
      if a.replenishing then table.insert(soon, { a.name .. " replenishes at the End stage", a.side }) end
    end
  end
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do
    if isWagon(g) and g.route and g.route.on and canSee(pk, g.side) then
      if isArmyRoute(g.route) then
        table.insert(soon, { wagonLabel(g) .. ": " .. armyRouteStatus(g), g.side })
      else
        table.insert(soon, { wagonLabel(g) .. ": route turn " .. (g.route.idx or 1) .. " of " .. #g.route.legs, g.side })
      end
    end
  end
  local list = { heading("Coming up") }
  if #soon == 0 then table.insert(list, txt("Nothing finishing next turn.", { color = C.dim })) end
  for i, s in ipairs(soon) do
    if i > 12 then table.insert(list, txt("... and " .. (#soon - 12) .. " more", { color = C.dim, size = 12 })); break end
    table.insert(list, row({ swatch(sideColor(s[2]), 4, 22), txt(s[1], { size = 14, h = 22 }) }, 22, 10))
  end
  table.insert(out, card(list, { spacing = 6 }))
  -- this turn's random events
  if S.events and S.events.t == S.turn then
    local E = S.events
    local k = { row({ heading("Random events"), ruleBtn(pk, "events"), spacer(), txt(E.roller .. " rolled " .. E.roll .. ((E.roll >= 4) and ": nothing happens" or ": both sides roll!"),
                                                           { size = 13, color = (E.roll >= 4) and C.dim or C.warn, w = 300, align = "MiddleRight" }) }, 28) }
    for _, sd in ipairs(SIDES) do
      local e = E[sd]
      if e then
        table.insert(k, row({ swatch(sideColor(sd), 4, 40), col({ txt(sd .. " · " .. e.name, { size = 15, style = "Bold", color = sideInk(sd), h = 22 }),
                                                                  txt(e.text, { size = 12, color = C.dim2, wrap = true, ww = MAIN_W - 320 }),
                                                                  txt(e.detail or "", { size = 13, color = C.ink, wrap = true, ww = MAIN_W - 320 }) }, nil, 2) }, nil, 10))
        local mine = canSee(pk, sd)
        if e.pending == "mercs" then
          local opts = {}
          for _, n in ipairs(ownedBy(sd)) do if S.terr[n].stock >= ARMY_COST[400][1] then table.insert(opts, terrOpt(n)) end end
          if #opts == 0 then opts = { "(no territory has the RP)" } end
          table.insert(k, row({ dropdown(pk, "evMercT", opts, { w = 240 }), input(pk, "evMercName", "army name (optional)"), input(pk, "evMercLeader", "leader"), input(pk, "evMercList", "list") }, 32, 8))
          table.insert(k, row({ spacer(), btn(pk, "Hire them (" .. ARMY_COST[400][1] .. " RP)", { w = 200, colors = BTN_GOLD, enabled = mine }, "evmerc", sd),
                                btn(pk, "Let them go", { w = 140, enabled = mine }, "evno", sd) }, 32, 8))
        elseif e.pending == "troll" then
          table.insert(k, row({ spacer(), btn(pk, "Raid the hoard (D6, 4+)", { w = 220, colors = BTN_GOLD, enabled = mine }, "evtroll", sd),
                                btn(pk, "Leave it alone", { w = 160, enabled = mine }, "evno", sd) }, 32, 8))
        end
      end
    end
    -- right under the turn track, so nobody misses it
    table.insert(out, math.min(#out + 1, (S.winner and 3 or 2)), card(k, { spacing = 8, border = (E.roll < 4) and C.warn or nil }))
  end

  -- this campaign's special rules for each side
  do
    local k = { row({ heading("Special rules · " .. (S.settings.theatre or "Eriador")), ruleBtn(pk, "specials"), spacer() }, 28) }
    for _, sd in ipairs(SIDES) do
      local rs = rulesFor(sd)
      if #rs > 0 then
        table.insert(k, cap(sd, sideInk(sd), { h = 18 }))
        for _, r in ipairs(rs) do
          local kids = { swatch(sideColor(sd), 4, 44), col({ txt(r.name, { size = 14, style = "Bold", h = 20 }), txt(r.text, { size = 12, color = C.dim2, wrap = true, ww = MAIN_W - 520 }) }, nil, 2) }
          if r.declare then
            local okD = canDeclare(sd, r.key)
            local cd = ruleCooldown(sd, r.key)
            local status = (cd > 0) and ("Ready on turn " .. (S.turn + cd)) or ((STAGES[S.stage] == r.declare) and "Ready" or ("Ready · " .. r.declare .. " stage"))
            table.insert(kids, col({ chip(string.upper(status), (cd > 0) and C.dim or C.ok, (cd > 0) and C.deep or C.okBg, { w = 190, h = 22, size = 10 }),
                                     btn(pk, "Use " .. r.name, { w = 190, h = 30, size = 12, colors = sideBtn(sd), enabled = okD == true and canSee(pk, sd),
                                                                 tip = (okD == true) and r.text or okD }, "rule", sd, r.key) }, 190, 4))
          else
            table.insert(kids, chip(r.auto and "ALWAYS ON" or "BY HAND", r.auto and C.dim2 or C.warn, r.auto and C.deep or C.warnBg,
                                    { w = 120, h = 22, size = 10, tip = r.auto and "The War Council applies this for you." or "Not automated on this map yet: apply it at the table." }))
          end
          table.insert(k, row(kids, nil, 10))
        end
      end
    end
    table.insert(out, card(k, { spacing = 8 }))
  end
  return out
end

function terrTip(pk, n)
  local t = S.terr[n]
  local lines = { tname(n) .. "  -  " .. t.owner, "Income +" .. income(n) .. " RP per turn" }
  local bl = {}
  for _, b in ipairs(BUILDINGS) do if t.b[b.key] then table.insert(bl, b.name) end end
  if #bl > 0 then table.insert(lines, "Buildings: " .. table.concat(bl, ", ")) end
  if t.conq then table.insert(lines, "Being conquered by " .. t.conq.by .. " (" .. t.conq.prog .. "/" .. t.conq.need .. ")") end
  local sh = fortShield(n)
  if sh then
    table.insert(lines, "Protected by the fortress at " .. NAMES[sh] .. ": can't be conquered while it holds.")
  else
    local m = nil
    if t.owner == "Neutral" then
      for _, x in ipairs(ADJ[n] or {}) do if S.terr[x].b.fort and S.terr[x].owner ~= "Neutral" then m = x end end
    end
    table.insert(lines, "Takes " .. conquestNeed(n) .. " End stages to conquer" ..
      (m and (" (+1 for " .. OTHER[S.terr[m].owner] .. ": next to the fortress at " .. NAMES[m] .. ")") or "") .. ".")
  end
  local nb = {}
  for _, m in ipairs(ADJ[n] or {}) do table.insert(nb, tostring(m)) end
  table.insert(lines, "Borders: " .. table.concat(nb, ", "))
  return table.concat(lines, "\n")
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- Territories
local function tabTerritories(pk)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local side = actingSide(pk)
  local sel = u.sel.terr or 1
  local filt = u.f.tFilter or "All"
  local counts = { All = #S.terr, Good = 0, Evil = 0, Neutral = 0 }
  for _, t in ipairs(S.terr) do counts[t.owner] = (counts[t.owner] or 0) + 1 end
  local fchips = {}
  for _, k in ipairs({ "All", "Good", "Evil", "Neutral" }) do
    table.insert(fchips, btn(pk, k .. " " .. counts[k], { h = 30, size = 12, colors = (filt == k) and BTN_SEL or BTN_GHOST,
                                                         textColor = (filt ~= k) and C.dim2 or nil }, "set", "tFilter", k))
  end
  local rows = {}
  for n, t in ipairs(S.terr) do
    if filt == "All" or t.owner == filt then
      local stockTxt = canSee(pk, t.owner) and (t.stock .. " RP") or "? RP"
      local on = n == sel
      table.insert(rows, row({
        swatch(sideColor(t.owner), 4, 34),
        btn(pk, "  " .. string.format("%2d", n) .. "   " .. NAMES[n], { h = 34, align = "MiddleLeft", colors = on and BTN_SEL or BTN_GHOST,
            textColor = on and C.goldHi or C.ink, style = on and "Bold" or "Normal", tip = terrTip(pk, n) }, "selT", n),
        txt(stockTxt, { w = 64, size = 12, color = C.dim, align = "MiddleRight", h = 34 }),
      }, 34, 0))
    end
  end
  local left = col({ row(fchips, 30, 4), scroll(rows, BODY_H - 38, nil, 4) }, 300, 8)

  local t = S.terr[sel]
  local d = {}
  -- hero card
  local isCap = (sel == S.settings.capGood) or (sel == S.settings.capEvil)
  local titleRow = { txt(terrDot(sel), { size = 24, style = "Bold", color = C.gold, h = 36 }) }
  if isCap then table.insert(titleRow, chip("CAPITAL", C.goldHi, C.raised, { w = 76, h = 24, size = 11 })) end
  for _, o in ipairs({ "Good", "Neutral", "Evil" }) do
    table.insert(titleRow, btn(pk, o, { w = 74, h = 32, colors = (t.owner == o) and sideBtn(o) or BTN_GHOST,
                                       textColor = (t.owner ~= o) and sideInk(o) or nil, tip = "Set the owner of " .. NAMES[sel] }, "own", sel, o))
  end
  local hero = { row(titleRow, 36, 6) }
  local see = canSee(pk, t.owner)
  local shieldOthers = {}
  for _, m in ipairs(ADJ[sel] or {}) do if fortShield(m) == sel then table.insert(shieldOthers, NAMES[m]) end end
  table.insert(hero, row({ tile(see and t.stock or "?", "RP in stockpile", { color = C.gold }), tile("+" .. income(sel), "Income per turn"),
                           tile(conquestNeed(sel), "Turns to conquer"), tile(#(ADJ[sel] or {}), "Bordering regions") }, nil, 8))
  if #shieldOthers > 0 then table.insert(hero, strip("Fortress: " .. table.concat(shieldOthers, ", ") .. " can't be conquered while " .. NAMES[sel] .. " holds.", "ok")) end
  local sh = fortShield(sel)
  if sh then table.insert(hero, strip("Shielded by the fortress at " .. NAMES[sh] .. ": can't be conquered while it holds.", "ok")) end
  if t.conq then
    if t.conq.held then
      table.insert(hero, strip(t.conq.by .. " is here but can't conquer it while the fortress at " .. NAMES[t.conq.held] .. " holds.", "warn"))
    else
      table.insert(hero, strip("Being conquered by " .. t.conq.by .. ": " .. t.conq.prog .. " of " .. t.conq.need .. " turns.", "warn"))
    end
  end
  for _, l in ipairs(actionLines(sel)) do table.insert(hero, strip(l[2] .. (l[1] and (" (" .. l[1] .. ")") or ""), "warn", { h = 32 })) end
  if t.owner == "Neutral" then
    for _, x in ipairs(ADJ[sel] or {}) do
      if S.terr[x].b.fort and S.terr[x].owner ~= "Neutral" then
        table.insert(hero, strip("Next to the " .. S.terr[x].owner .. " fortress at " .. NAMES[x] .. ": takes " .. OTHER[S.terr[x].owner] .. " 1 extra turn to conquer.", "dim"))
      end
    end
  end
  table.insert(d, card(hero, { spacing = 12 }))

  -- buildings + construct
  local bk = { heading("Buildings") }
  local any = false
  for _, b in ipairs(BUILDINGS) do
    if t.b[b.key] then
      any = true
      table.insert(bk, row({ swatch(C.gold, 4, 30), btn(pk, "  " .. b.name, { h = 30, align = "MiddleLeft", colors = flat(C.deep), tip = b.name .. ": " .. b.rules }, "noop") }, 30, 0))
    end
  end
  if not any then table.insert(bk, txt("None yet.", { color = C.dim, size = 13 })) end
  if see then
    for _, q in ipairs(t.q) do
      local b = BUILDING_BY_KEY[q.key]
      local total = q.total or b.turns
      local done = (total - q.left)
      table.insert(bk, row({ txt("Building: " .. b.name .. (q.halt and " (halted)" or ""), { size = 13, color = C.warn }),
                             txt(done .. " of " .. total .. " turns", { size = 12, color = C.dim, w = 100, align = "MiddleRight" }) }, 20))
      table.insert(bk, bar(done / total, C.warn, 6))
    end
    table.insert(bk, gap(4))
    table.insert(bk, cap("Stockpile correction"))
    table.insert(bk, row({ input(pk, "stockAdj", "amount", { int = true, w = 90 }),
                           btn(pk, "Add", { w = 64 }, "stock", sel, 1), btn(pk, "Remove", { w = 80 }, "stock", sel, -1), spacer() }, 32))
  end
  local cons = { row({ heading("Construct"), ruleBtn(pk, "buildings") }, 26) }
  if t.owner == side or S.settings.override then
    local full = buildingCount(sel) >= MAX_BUILDINGS and not S.settings.override
    local can = stageAllowed("Building") and not full
    local grid = {}
    for _, b in ipairs(BUILDINGS) do
      local queued = false
      for _, q in ipairs(t.q) do if q.key == b.key then queued = true end end
      if not t.b[b.key] and not queued then
        local bt = buildTurns((t.owner ~= "Neutral") and t.owner or side, b.key)
        table.insert(grid, btn(pk, "  " .. b.name .. "\n  " .. b.cost .. " RP · " .. bt .. " turns",
                               { enabled = can, h = 50, align = "MiddleLeft", colors = BTN, size = 13,
                                 tip = b.name .. ": " .. b.rules .. "\nCost " .. b.cost .. " RP, " .. bt .. " turns." .. ((bt < b.turns) and " (Hobbit Laborers)" or "") }, "build", sel, b.key))
      end
    end
    for i = 1, #grid, 2 do table.insert(cons, row({ grid[i], grid[i + 1] or spacer() }, 50, 8)) end
    if #grid == 0 then table.insert(cons, txt("Everything is built here.", { color = C.dim })) end
    table.insert(cons, txt(full and ("This territory holds " .. MAX_BUILDINGS .. " buildings (counting any being built): it can't take another.")
                               or (can and ("Hover a building for its full rule. " .. buildingCount(sel) .. " of " .. MAX_BUILDINGS .. " building slots used.")
                               or "Construction happens in the Building stage."), { size = 12, color = full and C.warn or C.dim, h = 18 }))
  else
    table.insert(cons, txt("Only " .. t.owner .. " can build here.", { color = C.dim }))
  end
  local bc, cc = card(bk, { spacing = 8 }), card(cons, { spacing = 8 })
  table.insert(d, row({ bc, cc }, math.max(hOf(bc), hOf(cc)), 14))

  -- presence
  local here = {}
  for _, a in ipairs(armiesIn(sel)) do table.insert(here, armyLabel(pk, a) .. " (" .. a.side .. ")") end
  for _, g in ipairs(agentsIn(sel)) do if canSee(pk, g.side) then table.insert(here, (g.name or (g.side .. " " .. g.type))) end end
  local nb = {}
  for _, m in ipairs(ADJ[sel] or {}) do table.insert(nb, NAMES[m]) end
  local pres = "Present: " .. (#here > 0 and table.concat(here, ", ") or "nobody")
  local bord = "Borders: " .. table.concat(nb, ", ")
  table.insert(d, card({ txt(pres, { size = 13, wrap = true, ww = MAIN_W - 360 }), txt(bord, { size = 12, color = C.dim, wrap = true, ww = MAIN_W - 360 }) }, { spacing = 4 }))
  do
    local notes = t.notes or {}
    local nk = { row({ heading("Notes", { size = 15 }), spacer(),
                       txt(S.settings.hidden and ("Only " .. side .. " sees these") or ("Written as " .. side), { size = 12, color = C.dim, w = 200, align = "MiddleRight" }) }, 24),
                 el("InputField", { id = "wc_" .. pk .. "_note_" .. side, onValueChanged = GP .. "uiNote", text = notes[side] or "",
                                    placeholder = "Your notes about " .. NAMES[sel], fontSize = 13, preferredHeight = 64, flexibleWidth = 1,
                                    lineType = "MultiLineNewline", colors = "#F3E7C9|#FFF5DC|#FFF5DC|#B8AC90", textColor = "#2A1C10" }) }
    local o2 = OTHER[side]
    if canSee(pk, o2) and notes[o2] and notes[o2] ~= "" then
      table.insert(nk, txt(o2 .. "'s notes: " .. notes[o2], { size = 12, color = sideInk(o2), wrap = true, ww = MAIN_W - 380 }))
    end
    table.insert(d, card(nk, { spacing = 6 }))
  end
  local right = col({ scroll(d, BODY_H, nil, 14) })
  return { row({ left, right }, BODY_H, 18) }, true
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- token strip (armies, agents, wagons)
function tokenBlock(pk, kind, ent)
  if kind == "agent" and ent.type == "Surgeon" then
    return { strip("Surgeons ride with their army - no token needed.", "dim") }
  end
  local o = tokenObject(kind, ent)
  local d = {}
  local msg, tone
  if o then
    local b = getBoard()
    local n = b and b.call("territoryAt", { position = o.getPosition() })
    msg, tone = "Token linked: '" .. (o.getName() ~= "" and o.getName() or "unnamed object") .. "' on " .. (n and NAMES[n] or "no territory"), "ok"
  elseif ent.tok then
    msg, tone = "Linked token is missing from the table.", "warn"
  else
    msg, tone = ent.name and ("No token linked. Name an object '" .. ent.name .. "' or link one.") or "No token linked.", "dim"
  end
  table.insert(d, strip(msg, tone, { h = 34 }))
  if o and ent.tok and ent.tok.mismatch and S.settings.dropMove == false then
    table.insert(d, strip("Token is in " .. NAMES[ent.tok.mismatch] .. " but the War Council has it in " ..
                          NAMES[(kind == "army") and ent.loc or agentLoc(ent)] .. ".", "warn", { h = 34 }))
  end
  table.insert(d, row({
    btn(pk, "Spawn token", { h = 30, size = 12, tip = "Copies the template object for this " .. kind }, "tokspawn", kind, ent.id),
    btn(pk, "Link selected object", { h = 30, size = 12, tip = "Select an object on the table first, then click this" }, "toklink", kind, ent.id),
    btn(pk, "Bring token here", { h = 30, size = 12, enabled = o ~= nil }, "tokplace", kind, ent.id),
    btn(pk, "Unlink", { h = 30, size = 12, w = 90, enabled = ent.tok ~= nil }, "tokunlink", kind, ent.id),
  }, 30, 6))
  return d
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- Armies
local FIT_TEXT = {
  faction = { "One of your faction's lists for this theatre.", "ok" },
  allied = { "This is your allied list - tick 'Allied army'.", "ok" },
  other = { "Not one of your faction lists here: raise it as a Mercenary, or check the theatre in Settings.", "warn" },
  unknown = { "This theatre's lists can't be checked automatically.", "dim" },
}

function rosterLine(r)
  local bits = { r.list or "Unknown list" }
  table.insert(bits, r.points and (r.points .. " pts") or "points unknown")
  if r.units then table.insert(bits, r.units .. " models") end
  if r.broken then table.insert(bits, "broken at " .. r.broken .. " dead") end
  if r.quartered then table.insert(bits, "quartered at " .. r.quartered .. " left") end
  return table.concat(bits, "  ·  ")
end

local function pasteCard(pk, a)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local side = a and a.side or actingSide(pk)
  local fld = a and "r_pasteA" or "r_paste"
  local k = {
    heading(a and "Replace the roster from the List Builder" or "Import from the MESBG List Builder", { size = 15 }),
    txt("In the List Builder open your list, choose Summary (or Tabletop Simulator export) and Copy to clipboard. Click the box below and press Ctrl+V.",
        { size = 12, color = C.dim, wrap = true, ww = MAIN_W - 380 }),
    el("InputField", { id = inputId(pk, fld), onValueChanged = GP .. "uiPaste", text = u.f[fld] or "", placeholder = "Paste the list here",
                       fontSize = 12, preferredHeight = 70, flexibleWidth = 1, lineType = "MultiLineNewline", characterLimit = 0,
                       colors = "#F3E7C9|#FFF5DC|#FFF5DC|#B8AC90", textColor = "#2A1C10" }),
    row({ btn(pk, "Read pasted list", { w = 180, h = 32, colors = (u.paste and u.paste[fld]) and BTN or BTN_GOLD }, "pasteread", fld), spacer() }, 32),
  }
  u.paste = u.paste or {}
  u.pasteErr = u.pasteErr or {}
  local r, err = u.paste[fld], u.pasteErr[fld]
  if err then
    table.insert(k, strip(err, "err", { h = 34 }))
  elseif r then
    table.insert(k, txt(rosterLine(r) .. "  ·  General: " .. (r.general or "?"), { size = 13, color = C.goldHi, wrap = true, ww = MAIN_W - 380 }))
    local fit = (r.list and rosterFit(r, side)) or "unknown"
    local ft = FIT_TEXT[fit] or FIT_TEXT.unknown
    table.insert(k, strip(ft[1], ft[2], { h = 34 }))
    if r.approx then table.insert(k, strip("The Tabletop Simulator export has no points - set the army size yourself.", "dim", { h = 34 })) end
    if r.points and r.points > 1000 then table.insert(k, strip("This list is over 1000 points, the largest army size.", "warn", { h = 34 })) end
    if r.mounted then table.insert(k, strip("Every model looks mounted - tick 'Cavalry only' if that's right.", "dim", { h = 34 })) end
    local kids = {}
    if a then
      table.insert(kids, btn(pk, "Replace roster - Edit, " .. EDIT_COST .. " RP", { h = 34, colors = BTN_GOLD, enabled = stageAllowed("Building"),
                                                                                tip = "Updates leader, list, units and roster. Building stage." }, "pasteedit", a.id))
    else
      table.insert(kids, btn(pk, "Fill in the form", { h = 34, colors = BTN_GOLD }, "pasteuse"))
    end
    table.insert(kids, btn(pk, "Clear", { w = 90, h = 34 }, "pasteclr", fld))
    table.insert(k, row(kids, 34, 8))
  end
  return card(k, { spacing = 8 })
end

-- Profile card pictures are loaded as custom UI assets (only the ones on screen are kept).
local function ensureCardAssets(cards)
  local ok, list = pcall(function() return UI.getCustomAssets() end)
  if not ok or type(list) ~= "table" then list = {} end
  local keep, have, want = {}, {}, {}
  for _, c in ipairs(cards) do want[cardAsset(c)] = c end
  for _, x in ipairs(list) do
    if x.name and string.sub(x.name, 1, 5) ~= "mlbc_" then table.insert(keep, x)
    elseif x.name and want[x.name] then table.insert(keep, x); have[x.name] = true end
  end
  local added = false
  for name, c in pairs(want) do
    if not have[name] then table.insert(keep, { name = name, url = cardUrl(c) }); added = true end
  end
  if added or #keep ~= #list then pcall(function() UI.setCustomAssets(keep) end) end
  for _, x in ipairs(keep) do ASSETS[x.name] = true end
end

local CARD_RATIO = 1835 / 2604   -- height / width of a List Builder profile card

local function cardsView(pk, a, cards)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local W = MAIN_W - 370   -- army detail column, inside the card's padding
  local k = {}
  local big = tonumber(u.cardBig)
  if big and cards[big] then
    local c = cards[big]
    local bh = math.floor(W * CARD_RATIO)
    table.insert(k, row({ txt(c.n, { size = 15, style = "Bold", color = C.goldHi }),
                          btn(pk, "<  Prev", { w = 90, h = 28, enabled = big > 1 }, "cardbig", big - 1),
                          btn(pk, "Next  >", { w = 90, h = 28, enabled = big < #cards }, "cardbig", big + 1),
                          btn(pk, "All cards", { w = 110, h = 28 }, "cardbig", 0) }, 28, 6))
    table.insert(k, el("Image", { image = cardAsset(c), preferredWidth = W, preferredHeight = bh, flexibleWidth = 1, preserveAspect = "true" }))
    return k
  end
  local per = 2
  local tw = math.floor((W - (per - 1) * 10) / per)
  local th = math.floor(tw * CARD_RATIO)
  for i = 1, #cards, per do
    local cells = {}
    for j = i, math.min(#cards, i + per - 1) do
      local c = cards[j]
      table.insert(cells, el("VerticalLayout", { preferredWidth = tw, flexibleWidth = 0, preferredHeight = th + 24, spacing = 2,
                                                 childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true" }, {
        el("Button", { onClick = act(pk, "cardbig", j), image = cardAsset(c), colors = "#FFFFFF|#E8DCC0|#C9B994|#FFFFFF",
                       preferredWidth = tw, preferredHeight = th, flexibleWidth = 0, tooltip = "Open " .. c.n }, nil, ""),
        txt(c.n, { size = 11, color = C.dim2, h = 20, w = tw }) }))
    end
    for _ = #cells + 1, per do table.insert(cells, spacer(tw)) end
    table.insert(k, row(cells, th + 24, 10))
  end
  return k
end

local function rosterCard(pk, a, title)
  local r = a.roster
  if not r then return nil end
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local cards = armyCards(a)
  local open = u.cardsFor == a.id
  local head = { heading(title or "Roster", { size = 15 }) }
  if #cards > 0 then
    table.insert(head, btn(pk, open and "Hide profile cards" or ("Profile cards (" .. #cards .. ")"), { w = 180, h = 28, colors = open and BTN_SEL or BTN,
                 tip = "The List Builder's profile cards for every hero and unit in this list." }, "cards", a.id))
    table.insert(head, btn(pk, "Deal cards", { w = 120, h = 28,
                 tip = S.settings.hidden and "Puts this army's profile cards into your hand." or "Lays this army's profile cards on the table in front of you." }, "deal", a.id))
    table.insert(head, btn(pk, "Clear", { w = 70, h = 28, colors = BTN_GHOST, tip = "Removes this army's profile cards from the table." }, "cardsclr", a.id))
  end
  local k = { row(head, 28, 6), txt(rosterLine(r), { size = 13, color = C.goldHi, wrap = true, ww = MAIN_W - 380 }) }
  if open then
    for _, e in ipairs(cardsView(pk, a, cards)) do table.insert(k, e) end
  else
    for _, l in ipairs(r.lines or {}) do table.insert(k, txt(l, { size = 12, color = C.dim2, h = 18 })) end
  end
  return card(k, { spacing = 6 })
end

local function armyCreateForm(pk)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local side = actingSide(pk)
  local f = u.f
  local d = {}
  local a1 = { row({ heading("Raise a new " .. side .. " army", { size = 20, h = 30 }), ruleBtn(pk, "armies") }, 30), txt("Building stage", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 16 }) }
  table.insert(a1, field("Name", input(pk, "aName", side .. " Army")))
  table.insert(a1, field("Size", choice(pk, "aSize", { "400", "600", "800", "1000" }, { default = "400", w = 90, tips = TIPS.size })))
  table.insert(a1, field("Leader", input(pk, "aLeader", "e.g. Aragorn or Captain of Gondor")))
  table.insert(a1, field("", toggle(pk, "aNamed", "Leader is a named hero (can only lead one army)", { tip = "Named heroes (e.g. Aragorn) can lead only one army at a time. Unnamed heroes (e.g. a Captain of Gondor) can lead any number." })))
  table.insert(a1, field("Army list", input(pk, "aList", "e.g. Arnor, Rivendell, Carn Dum")))
  do
    local al = allianceFor(side)
    local cur = alliedArmy(side)
    if cur then
      table.insert(a1, field("", txt(al.name .. ": already used by " .. cur.name .. ".", { color = C.dim, size = 12 })))
    else
      table.insert(a1, field("", toggle(pk, "aAllied", "Allied army - " .. al.name .. " (" .. al.list .. ")",
                     { tip = "Not a Mercenary: no surcharge, normal replenish, doesn't count toward the Mercenary limit. One at a time." })))
    end
  end
  table.insert(a1, field("", row({
    toggle(pk, "aMerc", "Mercenary", { tip = "From a list outside your faction (same alignment). Max 2, one per faction list. +50 RP for the first, +75 for the second; replenishing costs +25/+50." }),
    isWolfPack(f.aList) and chip("Cavalry (Wolf Pack)", C.dim2, C.deep, { w = 170, h = 30, size = 12, tip = "The Wolf Pack of Angmar always counts as a cavalry-only army." })
      or toggle(pk, "aCav", "Cavalry only", { tip = "Cavalry-only armies move 3 regions at 400 pts, 2 at 600+." }),
    toggle(pk, "aMarchOK", "Heroic March", { tip = "Leader has Heroic March: move 1 extra region, then the whole army is -1\" movement for 2 turns." }) }, 30, 6)))
  local own = {}
  for _, n in ipairs(ownedBy(side)) do table.insert(own, terrOpt(n)) end
  if #own == 0 then own = { "(no territory)" } end
  table.insert(a1, field("Raise at", dropdown(pk, "aTerr", own)))
  table.insert(a1, field("Units / notes", input(pk, "aUnits", "roster summary (hidden from the enemy)")))
  do
    local have, capN = armyCount(side), armyCap(side)
    table.insert(a1, field("Army Cap", txt(have .. " of " .. capN .. " armies" .. ((have >= capN) and " - at the cap: build a City to raise more." or "") ..
                                           "   (5, +1 for each City " .. side .. " has built)", { size = 13, color = (have >= capN) and C.warn or C.dim2 })))
  end
  local size = tonumber(f.aSize or "400") or 400
  local cost = ARMY_COST[size][1]
  local merc = f.aMerc == "True" and f.aAllied ~= "True"
  if merc then cost = cost + MERC[math.min(2, mercCount(side) + 1)].cost end
  local n = numOf(f.aTerr)
  local turns = ARMY_COST[size][2] - ((n and S.terr[n].b.mustering) and 1 or 0)
  local stock = n and S.terr[n].stock or 0
  table.insert(a1, row({ tile(cost .. " RP", "Cost (stockpile there: " .. stock .. ")", { size = 20, color = (stock >= cost) and C.ok or C.warn }),
                         tile(math.max(1, turns), "Turns to raise", { size = 20 }),
                         tile(((f.aCav == "True") and ((size == 400) and 3 or 2) or ((size == 400) and 2 or 1)), "Regions per move", { size = 20 }) }, nil, 8))
  table.insert(a1, row({ spacer(), btn(pk, "Raise army", { w = 240, h = 42, size = 15, colors = BTN_GOLD, enabled = stageAllowed("Building") }, "raise") }, 42))
  if u.pasteRoster then
    table.insert(a1, #a1, strip("The pasted roster (" .. rosterLine(u.pasteRoster) .. ") will be saved with this army.", "ok"))
    if u.pasteRoster.points and u.pasteRoster.points > size then
      table.insert(a1, #a1, strip("The roster is " .. u.pasteRoster.points .. " pts - more than a " .. size .. " pt army can field.", "warn", { h = 34 }))
    end
  end
  table.insert(d, pasteCard(pk, nil))
  table.insert(d, card(a1, { spacing = 10 }))
  return d
end

local function armyDetail(pk, a)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local f = u.f
  local d = {}
  local own = canSee(pk, a.side)
  if not own then
    local leaderK, unitsK = armyKnown(pk, a)
    local known = a.seenLeader and a.seenLeader[pk]
    local k = { txt((known and a.name or publicName(a)) .. " at " .. NAMES[a.loc], { size = 22, style = "Bold", color = sideInk(a.side), h = 32 }),
                txt("Leader: " .. (leaderK and (a.leader ~= "" and a.leader or "unnamed") or "unknown (send a Scout)"), { color = C.dim2 }) }
    if unitsK then
      table.insert(k, txt("Size: " .. a.size .. " pts  ·  List: " .. (a.list ~= "" and a.list or "?") .. (isCavalry(a) and "  ·  cavalry" or ""), { color = C.dim2 }))
      table.insert(k, txt("Units: " .. (a.units ~= "" and a.units or "(no notes)"), { wrap = true, ww = MAIN_W - 340, size = 13 }))
      if a.roster then table.insert(k, txt(rosterLine(a.roster), { size = 13, color = C.dim2, wrap = true, ww = MAIN_W - 340 })) end
    else
      table.insert(k, strip("Units unknown: a Scout needs a 3+, or a Scout Tower next door.", "dim"))
    end
    table.insert(d, card(k, { bg = sideBg(a.side), border = (a.side == "Good") and "#36593F" or "#6B3631" }))
    return d
  end
  -- hero
  local sub = "Led by " .. (a.leader ~= "" and a.leader or "-") .. (a.named and " (named hero)" or "") .. "  ·  " ..
              (a.list ~= "" and (a.list .. " list") or "no list") .. "  ·  at " .. NAMES[a.loc]
  local tags = string.upper(a.side) .. " · " .. a.size .. " PTS"
  if S.settings.hidden then
    sub = sub .. "  ·  " .. (armyRevealed(a) and "known to the enemy" or ("the enemy sees " .. publicName(a)))
  end
  local hero = {
    row({ col({ txt(a.name, { size = 24, style = "Bold", color = C.gold, h = 34 }), txt(sub, { size = 13, color = C.dim2, h = 20 }) }, nil, 2),
          chip(tags, sideInk(a.side), (a.side == "Good") and C.goodBg or C.evilBg, { w = 150, h = 28 }) }, 56),
  }
  local pills = {}
  local bad = a.needsRetreat or outOfSupplies(a)
  table.insert(pills, chip(string.upper(armyStatus(a)), bad and C.err or C.dim2, bad and C.evilBg or C.deep, { w = 260, h = 24, size = 11 }))
  if a.allied then table.insert(pills, chip("ALLIED · " .. string.upper((allianceFor(a.side) or {}).name or ""), C.goldHi, C.raised, { w = 260, h = 24, size = 11 })) end
  if a.merc then table.insert(pills, chip("MERCENARY", C.warn, C.warnBg, { w = 110, h = 24, size = 11 })) end
  if isCavalry(a) then table.insert(pills, chip("CAVALRY ONLY", C.dim2, C.deep, { w = 120, h = 24, size = 11 })) end
  table.insert(pills, spacer())
  table.insert(hero, row(pills, 24, 6))
  local surgeon = false
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do if g.army == a.id then surgeon = true end end
  table.insert(hero, row({
    tile((a.carried or 0) .. " / " .. a.size, "RP carried", { size = 20, frac = (a.carried or 0) / a.size, barColor = outOfSupplies(a) and C.err or C.good,
         sub = inFriendlyLand(a) and "No upkeep in your land" or ("Upkeep " .. armyUpkeep(a) .. " RP / turn here") }),
    tile(((a.damage or 0) > 0) and ("-" .. a.damage .. "%") or "None", "Losses", { size = 20, color = ((a.damage or 0) > 0) and C.warn or C.ink,
         sub = ((a.damage or 0) > 0) and ("Field as " .. math.floor(a.size * (1 - a.damage / 100)) .. " pts") or "Full strength" }),
    tile(armyMove(a) .. " region" .. ((armyMove(a) == 1) and "" or "s"), "Movement", { size = 20, sub = "Heroic March: " .. (a.canMarch and "yes" or "no") }),
    tile(surgeon and "Surgeon" or "-", "Attached", { size = 20, sub = surgeon and "-5% / -10% casualties" or "no agent" }),
  }, nil, 8))
  do local w = rosterOver(a); if w then table.insert(hero, strip(w, "warn", { h = 32 })) end end
  for _, w in ipairs(a.wounds or {}) do table.insert(hero, strip(w.who .. " is wounded until the next Logistics stage.", "warn", { h = 32 })) end
  local knows = {}
  if a.seenLeader[OTHER[a.side]] then table.insert(knows, "leader") end
  if a.seenUnits[OTHER[a.side]] then table.insert(knows, "units") end
  if #knows > 0 then table.insert(hero, strip("The enemy has scouted this army's " .. table.concat(knows, " and ") .. ".", "dim", { h = 32 })) end
  for _, line in ipairs(tokenBlock(pk, "army", a)) do table.insert(hero, line) end
  table.insert(d, card(hero, { spacing = 10 }))

  -- supplies from the stockpile it stands on, and its name
  do
    local t = S.terr[a.loc]
    local here = (t.owner == a.side)
    local logi = stageAllowed("Logistics")
    local room = math.max(0, a.size - (a.carried or 0))
    local sk = {
      row({ heading("Supplies", { w = 120 }), spacer(), chip(logi and "NOW" or "LOGISTICS STAGE", logi and C.ok or C.dim, logi and C.okBg or C.deep, { w = 130, h = 22, size = 10 }) }, 24),
      txt(here and ("Stockpile at " .. NAMES[a.loc] .. ": " .. (t.stock or 0) .. " RP  ·  carrying " .. (a.carried or 0) .. " / " .. a.size .. " RP (room for " .. room .. ")")
               or ("No stockpile to use: " .. NAMES[a.loc] .. " isn't yours. Carrying " .. (a.carried or 0) .. " / " .. a.size .. " RP."), { size = 13, color = C.dim2, h = 20 }),
      row({ txt("Amount", { w = 60, color = C.dim, size = 13 }), input(pk, "aRP", "all", { w = 110, int = true }),
            btn(pk, "Pick up", { w = 120, h = 32, colors = BTN_GOLD, enabled = here and logi }, "apick", a.id),
            btn(pk, "Fill up", { w = 110, h = 32, enabled = here and logi and room > 0, tip = "Take as much as it can carry" }, "afill", a.id),
            btn(pk, "Drop off", { w = 120, h = 32, enabled = here and logi and (a.carried or 0) > 0, tip = "Leave RP in this territory's stockpile" }, "adrop", a.id),
            spacer() }, 32, 8),
      row({ txt("Name", { w = 60, color = C.dim, size = 13 }), input(pk, "aRename", "new name for " .. a.name), btn(pk, "Rename", { w = 120, h = 32 }, "arename", a.id) }, 32, 8),
    }
    table.insert(d, card(sk, { spacing = 8 }))
  end

  -- move + muster
  local canMove = stageAllowed("Logistics") and a.status ~= "building" and not a.moved and (not outOfSupplies(a) or S.settings.override)
  local budget = armyMove(a) + ((f.mMarch == "True") and 1 or 0) - (a.slow or 0)
  local dests = {}
  for _, n in ipairs(moveRangeFor(a, true, budget)) do table.insert(dests, terrOpt(n)) end
  if #dests == 0 then dests = { "(nowhere in range)" } end
  local mv = {
    row({ heading("Move", { w = 80 }), ruleBtn(pk, "movement"), spacer(), chip(stageAllowed("Logistics") and "NOW" or "LOGISTICS STAGE", stageAllowed("Logistics") and C.ok or C.dim,
                                                        stageAllowed("Logistics") and C.okBg or C.deep, { w = 130, h = 22, size = 10 }) }, 24),
    field("Destination", dropdown(pk, "mDest", dests), { lw = 90 }),
    toggle(pk, "mMarch", "Heroic March (+1 region, exhausted 2 turns)"),
    btn(pk, a.canMarch and "Leader has Heroic March: yes" or "Leader has Heroic March: no", { h = 28, size = 12, colors = BTN_GHOST, textColor = C.dim2,
        tip = "Lets a dropped token use Heroic March when the move needs it" }, "canmarch", a.id),
    btn(pk, "Move army", { h = 40, size = 14, colors = BTN_GOLD, enabled = canMove }, "move", a.id),
    txt("Range " .. budget .. ((a.slow or 0) > 0 and " (slowed)" or "") .. ". Or drop its token in a territory on the map.", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 }),
  }
  local can = stageAllowed("Building")
  local rc = replenishCost(a)
  local confirm = u.confirm == ("disband:" .. a.id)
  local ms = {
    row({ heading("Muster", { w = 90 }), spacer(), chip(can and "NOW" or "BUILDING STAGE", can and C.ok or C.dim, can and C.okBg or C.deep, { w = 130, h = 22, size = 10 }) }, 24),
    btn(pk, (a.damage or 0) > 0 and ("  Replenish losses  -  " .. rc .. " RP") or "  Replenish (no losses)", { align = "MiddleLeft", h = 34, enabled = can and (a.damage or 0) > 0 and not a.replenishing }, "rep", a.id),
    btn(pk, a.size < 1000 and ("  Upgrade to " .. (a.size + 200) .. " pts  -  " .. UPGRADE_COST .. " RP") or "  Already at maximum size", { align = "MiddleLeft", h = 34, enabled = can and a.size < 1000 and not a.upLeft }, "up", a.id),
    row({ input(pk, "eLeader", "leader", { default = a.leader, h = 30 }), input(pk, "eList", "list", { default = a.list, h = 30 }) }, 30, 6),
    row({ input(pk, "eUnits", "units / notes", { default = a.units, h = 30 }), btn(pk, "Edit - " .. EDIT_COST .. " RP", { w = 120, h = 30, enabled = can }, "edit", a.id) }, 30, 6),
    btn(pk, confirm and "Click again to confirm disband" or ("Disband (refund " .. disbandRefund(a) + (a.carried or 0) .. " RP)"), { h = 32, colors = BTN_DANGER, enabled = can }, "disband", a.id),
  }
  local c1, c2 = card(mv), card(ms)
  table.insert(d, row({ c1, c2 }, math.max(hOf(c1), hOf(c2)), 14))
  -- territory actions (public ones; the secret ones are in each player's Secret orders panel)
  do
    local ak = { row({ heading("Territory actions", { w = 220 }), ruleBtn(pk, "actions"), spacer(), chip(stageAllowed("Logistics") and "NOW" or "LOGISTICS STAGE", stageAllowed("Logistics") and C.ok or C.dim,
                                                                      stageAllowed("Logistics") and C.okBg or C.deep, { w = 130, h = 22, size = 10 }) }, 24) }
    local o = a.order
    if o and (not o.secret or o.revealed) then
      table.insert(ak, strip(a.name .. " is " .. ACTIONS[o.k].verb .. " " .. NAMES[o.n] .. ": completes at the end of the turn unless it's caught in a battle first.", "warn", { h = 34 }))
      table.insert(ak, row({ spacer(), btn(pk, "Cancel " .. ACTIONS[o.k].name, { w = 200, h = 30, size = 12, colors = BTN_DANGER, enabled = canSee(pk, a.side) }, "actcancel", a.id) }, 30))
    else
      local kids = {}
      for _, k in ipairs(PUBLIC_ACTIONS) do
        -- an undiscovered secret order looks like an ordinary finished move here
        local okA = o and (a.name .. " has already used its move this turn.") or actionAllowed(a, k)
        local key = "act:" .. k .. ":" .. a.id
        local label = (k == "scorch" and u.confirm == key) and "Click again to burn it" or ACTIONS[k].name
        table.insert(kids, btn(pk, label, { h = 34, colors = (k == "scorch") and BTN_DANGER or BTN, enabled = (okA == true) and canSee(pk, a.side),
                                            tip = ACTIONS[k].rules .. ((okA ~= true) and ("\n" .. okA) or "") }, "act", k, a.id))
      end
      table.insert(ak, row(kids, 34, 8))
      local t = S.terr[a.loc]
      if #(t.q or {}) > 0 and t.owner ~= a.side then
        local opts = {}
        for _, item in ipairs(t.q) do table.insert(opts, BUILDING_BY_KEY[item.key].name .. " (" .. item.left .. "t left)") end
        table.insert(ak, field("Sabotage target", dropdown(pk, "actSab", opts), { lw = 120 }))
      end
      table.insert(ak, txt("An action ends the army's movement and completes at the end of the turn. A battle first cancels it. Secret actions (Road Blockage, Dig In, Sow Discontent) are in each player's Secret orders panel.",
                           { size = 12, color = C.dim, wrap = true }))
    end
    table.insert(d, card(ak, { spacing = 8 }))
  end

  if a.needsRetreat or S.settings.override then
    local opts = {}
    for _, n in ipairs(retreatOptions(a)) do table.insert(opts, terrOpt(n)) end
    if #opts == 0 then opts = { "(no friendly or neutral neighbour)" } end
    table.insert(d, card({ row({ heading("Retreat", { w = 100 }), spacer(), chip("RETREAT STAGE", C.dim, C.deep, { w = 130, h = 22, size = 10 }) }, 24),
                           row({ dropdown(pk, "rDest", opts), btn(pk, "Retreat", { w = 110, enabled = stageAllowed("Retreat"), colors = BTN_GOLD }, "retreat", a.id),
                                 btn(pk, "No retreat: destroyed", { w = 190, colors = BTN_DANGER }, "destroy", a.id) }, 32) }, { border = C.evil }))
  end
  local rc2 = rosterCard(pk, a)
  if rc2 then table.insert(d, rc2) end
  table.insert(d, pasteCard(pk, a))
  return d
end

local function tabArmies(pk)
