  local u = uiState(pk)
  local side = actingSide(pk)
  local rows = { btn(pk, "+  Raise a new army", { colors = (u.sel.army == nil) and BTN_GOLD or BTN, h = 40, size = 14 }, "selA", "new") }
  for _, s in ipairs({ side, OTHER[side] }) do
    local any = false
    for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
      if a.side == s then
        if not any then table.insert(rows, cap((s == side) and "Your armies" or "Enemy armies", nil, { h = 22 })); any = true end
        local mine = canSee(pk, a.side)
        local title = armyLabel(pk, a) .. (mine and ("   ·   " .. a.size .. " pts") or "")
        local sub = NAMES[a.loc] .. "  ·  " .. (mine and armyStatus(a) or "units unknown")
        table.insert(rows, listRow(pk, title, sub, u.sel.army == a.id, sideColor(a.side), nil, "selA", a.id))
      end
    end
  end
  local left = col({ scroll(rows, BODY_H, nil, 6) }, 300)
  local a = u.sel.army and armyById(u.sel.army)
  local right = col({ scroll(a and armyDetail(pk, a) or armyCreateForm(pk), BODY_H, nil, 14) })
  return { row({ left, right }, BODY_H, 18) }, true
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- Agents
local AGENT_ODDS = {
  agent = { "Needs a 6. A natural 1 and the Assassin is caught.", "~17%" },
  hero = { "Needs a 6, then a 4+. The wound lasts until the next Logistics stage.", "~8%" },
  leader = { "Needs a 6, then a 5+. The wound lasts until the next Logistics stage.", "~6%" },
  build = { "4+ halts it for a turn; a 6 also sets it back a turn.", "50%" },
  army = { "Needs a 6 to slow it 1 region. A 1 and the Saboteur dies.", "~17%" },
}

local function tabAgents(pk)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local side = actingSide(pk)
  local f = u.f
  local out = {}
  -- roster by type
  local tiles = {}
  for _, typ in ipairs({ "Scout", "Assassin", "Saboteur", "Surgeon" }) do
    local def = AGENTS[typ]
    local have, cap2 = countAgents(side, typ), def.building and countBuildings(side, def.building) or nil
    table.insert(tiles, tile(have .. (cap2 and (" of " .. cap2) or ""), typ .. "s", { size = 20, sub = def.building and ("one per " .. BUILDING_BY_KEY[def.building].name) or "no limit",
                                                                                      color = (cap2 and have >= cap2) and C.warn or C.ink }))
  end
  table.insert(tiles, tile(countAgents(side, "Supply Wagon"), "Wagons", { size = 20, sub = "no limit" }))
  local tilesRow = row(tiles, nil, 8)
  table.insert(out, tilesRow)

  local rows = { btn(pk, "+  Recruit an agent", { colors = (u.sel.agent == nil) and BTN_GOLD or BTN, h = 40, size = 14 }, "selG", "new") }
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do
    if canSee(pk, g.side) and not isWagon(g) then
      local where = g.army and ("with " .. (armyById(g.army) and armyById(g.army).name or "?")) or NAMES[g.loc]
      local title = g.name or (g.side .. " " .. g.type .. " #" .. g.id)
      local sub = g.type .. "  ·  " .. where .. ((g.moved or g.acted) and "  ·  done" or "")
      table.insert(rows, listRow(pk, title, sub, u.sel.agent == g.id, sideColor(g.side), nil, "selG", g.id))
    end
  end
  table.insert(rows, txt("Supply Wagons are in the Wagons tab.", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 }))
  local listH = BODY_H - hOf(tilesRow) - 14
  local left = col({ scroll(rows, listH, nil, 6) }, 300)

  local d = {}
  local g = u.sel.agent and agentById(u.sel.agent)
  if not g then
    local k = { row({ heading("Recruit a " .. side .. " agent", { size = 20, h = 30 }), ruleBtn(pk, "agents") }, 30), txt("Building stage", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 16 }) }
    table.insert(k, choice(pk, "gType", AGENT_TYPES, { default = "Scout", tips = TIPS.agent }))
    local typ = f.gType or "Scout"
    local def = AGENTS[typ]
    if typ == "Surgeon" then
      local opts = {}
      for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if a.side == side then table.insert(opts, a.name .. " #" .. a.id) end end
      if #opts == 0 then opts = { "(no armies)" } end
      table.insert(k, field("Attach to", dropdown(pk, "gArmy", opts)))
    else
      local own = {}
      for _, n in ipairs(ownedBy(side)) do table.insert(own, terrOpt(n)) end
      if #own == 0 then own = { "(no territory)" } end
      table.insert(k, field("Recruit at", dropdown(pk, "gTerr", own)))
      if typ == "Supply Wagon" then
        local n = numOf(f.gTerr)
        if n then table.insert(k, strip("It will be called '" .. wagonName(n) .. "' (manage wagons in the Wagons tab).", "ok", { h = 34 })) end
      else
        table.insert(k, field("Name", input(pk, "gName", "optional - name your token the same to link it")))
      end
    end
    local capTxt = "No building needed."
    if def.building then
      capTxt = countAgents(side, typ) .. " of " .. countBuildings(side, def.building) .. " allowed (one per " .. BUILDING_BY_KEY[def.building].name .. ")"
    end
    table.insert(k, row({ tile(def.cost .. " RP", "Cost", { size = 20 }), tile(capTxt, "Limit", { size = 14 }) }, nil, 8))
    local info = {
      ["Scout"] = "Entering a territory with an enemy army reveals its leader; on a 3+ its units too.",
      ["Assassin"] = "Kills enemy agents on a 6 (dies on a 1). Wounds a Hero on 6 then 4+, a Leader on 6 then 5+. Dies on a natural 1 first roll.",
      ["Surgeon"] = "Rides with an army. After each battle: 3-4 casualties -5%, 5-6 -10%. Dies on a 1 when the army retreats.",
      ["Saboteur"] = "Construction: 4+ halts it a turn, 6 sets it back a turn. Army: 6 slows it 1 region; dies on a 1.",
      ["Supply Wagon"] = "Carries up to 400 RP and moves 3 regions. Captured if it meets an enemy army.",
    }
    table.insert(k, strip(info[typ], "dim"))
    table.insert(k, row({ spacer(), btn(pk, "Recruit " .. typ, { w = 240, h = 42, size = 15, colors = BTN_GOLD, enabled = stageAllowed("Building") }, "recruit") }, 42))
    table.insert(d, card(k, { spacing = 10 }))
  else
    local here = agentLoc(g)
    local title = g.name or (g.side .. " " .. g.type .. " #" .. g.id)
    local sub = g.type .. "  ·  " .. (g.army and ("with " .. (armyById(g.army) and armyById(g.army).name or "?") .. " at " .. NAMES[here]) or ("at " .. NAMES[here])) ..
                "  ·  " .. ((g.acted and "has acted this turn") or (g.moved and "has moved this turn") or (((g.mvUsed or 0) > 0) and (agentMoveLeft(g) .. " movement left")) or "hasn't acted this turn")
    local k = { col({ txt(title, { size = 24, style = "Bold", color = C.gold, h = 34 }), txt(sub, { size = 13, color = C.dim2, h = 20 }) }, nil, 2) }
    if not g.army then
      local dests = {}
      for _, n in ipairs(reachable(g.loc, agentMoveLeft(g), g.type == "Supply Wagon", agentRoadFree(g), g.side)) do table.insert(dests, terrOpt(n)) end
      if #dests == 0 then dests = { "(nowhere in range)" } end
      table.insert(k, row({ txt("Move to", { w = 70, color = C.dim, size = 13 }), dropdown(pk, "gDest", dests),
                            btn(pk, "Move agent", { w = 130, enabled = stageAllowed("Logistics") and not g.moved }, "gmove", g.id) }, 32))
    end
    local targets = agentTargets(g)
    if #targets > 0 then
      table.insert(k, cap("Choose a target here", C.gold, { h = 20, size = 12 }))
      local curKey = f.gTargetKey
      local valid = false
      for _, t in ipairs(targets) do if t[1] == curKey then valid = true end end
      if not valid then curKey = targets[1][1]; f.gTargetKey = curKey end
      for _, t in ipairs(targets) do
        local kind = string.match(t[1], "^(%a+):")
        local od = AGENT_ODDS[kind] or { "", "" }
        local on = t[1] == curKey
        table.insert(k, row({
          btn(pk, (on and "  (o)  " or "  ( )  ") .. t[2] .. "\n          " .. od[1], { h = 54, align = "MiddleLeft", colors = on and BTN_SEL or BTN_GHOST,
              textColor = on and C.goldHi or C.ink, style = on and "Bold" or "Normal", size = 13 }, "gtgt", t[1]),
          txt(od[2], { w = 60, size = 16, style = "Bold", color = on and C.gold or C.dim, align = "MiddleCenter", h = 54 }),
        }, 54, 6))
      end
      if g.type == "Assassin" then table.insert(k, strip("A natural 1 on the first die and the Assassin is caught and killed.", "err", { h = 34 })) end
      table.insert(k, btn(pk, (g.type == "Assassin") and "Attempt the strike" or "Attempt", { h = 42, size = 15, colors = BTN_GOLD,
                                                                                             enabled = stageAllowed("Logistics") and not g.acted }, "gact", g.id))
    elseif g.type ~= "Surgeon" and g.type ~= "Scout" then
      table.insert(k, strip("No targets here. Move into a territory with enemy agents, armies or construction.", "dim", { h = 34 }))
    elseif g.type == "Scout" then
      table.insert(k, strip("Scouts report automatically when they enter a territory with an enemy army.", "dim", { h = 34 }))
    end
    for _, line in ipairs(tokenBlock(pk, "agent", g)) do table.insert(k, line) end
    local confirm = u.confirm == ("dismiss:" .. g.id)
    table.insert(k, row({ spacer(), btn(pk, confirm and "Click again to confirm" or "Dismiss agent", { w = 200, colors = BTN_DANGER }, "gdis", g.id) }, 32))
    table.insert(d, card(k, { spacing = 10 }))
  end
  local right = col({ scroll(d, listH, nil, 14) })
  table.insert(out, row({ left, right }, listH, 18))
  return out, true
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- Battles
-- Who fights: the army that moved in this turn attacks, the one already there (or the territory's owner, or its
-- garrison) defends. f.bSwap turns it round.
function battleSides(n, swap)
  local g, e = armiesIn(n, "Good"), armiesIn(n, "Evil")
  local t = S.terr[n]
  local function pick(list)
    for _, a in ipairs(list) do if not a.moved then return a end end
    return list[1]
  end
  local atkSide
  if #g > 0 and #e > 0 then
    local gm, em = false, false
    for _, a in ipairs(g) do if a.moved then gm = true end end
    for _, a in ipairs(e) do if a.moved then em = true end end
    if gm ~= em then atkSide = gm and "Good" or "Evil"
    elseif t.owner ~= "Neutral" then atkSide = OTHER[t.owner]
    else atkSide = "Evil" end
  else
    atkSide = (#g > 0) and "Good" or "Evil"   -- one side only: it's attacking a fortress
  end
  if swap and #g > 0 and #e > 0 then atkSide = OTHER[atkSide] end
  local atkList = (atkSide == "Good") and g or e
  local defList = (atkSide == "Good") and e or g
  local A = atkList[1]
  for _, a in ipairs(atkList) do if a.moved then A = a; break end end
  local D = pick(defList)
  return A, D, atkList, defList
end

local function tabBattles(pk)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local f = u.f
  local d = {}
  local bt = battleTerritories()
  local isBattle = {}
  for _, n in ipairs(bt) do isBattle[n] = true end
  if u.bopen and not isBattle[u.bopen] and not S.settings.override then u.bopen = nil end
  if not u.bopen then
    -- the list: every battle happening now, as a card to open
    local head = { heading((#bt == 0) and "No battles right now" or (#bt .. " battle" .. ((#bt == 1) and "" or "s") .. " to fight")), spacer(),
                   btn(pk, "Announce battles again", { w = 200, h = 30, enabled = stageAllowed("Battle") and #bt > 0,
                                                       tip = "Reveal the armies and show everyone the battle pop-up again" }, "bprompt"),
                   btn(pk, "Refresh", { w = 100, h = 30, colors = BTN_GHOST, textColor = C.dim2 }, "refresh") }
    table.insert(d, row(head, 30, 8))
    if #bt == 0 then
      table.insert(d, card({ txt("When opposing armies share a territory (or an army stands at an enemy fortress), the battle appears here.", { color = C.dim2, wrap = true, ww = MAIN_W - 340 }) }))
    end
    for _, n in ipairs(bt) do
      local A, D = battleSides(n)
      local siege = S.terr[n].b.fort and S.terr[n].owner ~= "Neutral"
      local function who(a, side)
        if a then
          local others = #armiesIn(n, a.side) - 1
          return txt(armyLabel(pk, a) .. ((others > 0) and ("  +" .. others .. " more") or ""), { size = 16, style = "Bold", color = sideInk(a.side), h = 24 })
        end
        return txt((siege and side) and ("Garrison of " .. NAMES[n]) or "-", { size = 16, style = "Bold", color = sideInk(side or "Neutral"), h = 24 })
      end
      local defSide = D and D.side or S.terr[n].owner
      local done
      for i = #S.battles, 1, -1 do
        local b = S.battles[i]
        if b.t ~= S.turn then break end
        if b.n == n then done = b; break end
      end
      local kids = {
        row({ txt(terrDot(n), { size = 18, style = "Bold", color = C.gold, h = 26 }), siege and chip("Siege", C.gold, C.head, { w = 70, h = 24, size = 12 }) or spacer(1),
              done and chip("Result recorded", "#F2FBF4", "#2E6B40", { w = 140, h = 24, size = 12 }) or spacer(1), spacer(),
              btn(pk, done and "Open again" or "Open battle", { w = 150, h = 34, colors = done and BTN_GHOST or BTN_GOLD, textColor = done and C.dim2 or nil }, "bopen", n) }, 34, 10),
        row({ cap("Attacker", nil, { w = 80, h = 24 }), who(A), cap("Defender", nil, { w = 80, h = 24 }), who(D, defSide) }, 24, 8),
      }
      if done then
        local tx = ((pk ~= "All" and S.settings.hidden) and done["t" .. pk]) or done.text or ""
        table.insert(kids, txt(tx, { size = 13, color = C.dim2, wrap = true, ww = MAIN_W - 340 }))
      end
      table.insert(d, card(kids, { spacing = 8 }))
    end
    if S.settings.override then
      local terrs = {}
      for m = 1, #NAMES do table.insert(terrs, terrOpt(m)) end
      table.insert(d, card({ row({ txt("Override: record a battle anywhere", { size = 13, color = C.dim, w = 260 }), dropdown(pk, "r_bAny", terrs),
                                   btn(pk, "Open", { w = 90, h = 32 }, "bopen", "any") }, 32, 8) }))
    end
  else
    local n = u.bopen
    f.r_bTerr = terrOpt(n)
    local siege = S.terr[n].b.fort and S.terr[n].owner ~= "Neutral"
    local autoA, autoD, atkList, defList = battleSides(n, f.bSwap == "True")
    table.insert(d, row({ btn(pk, "< All battles", { w = 120, h = 32, colors = BTN_GHOST, textColor = C.dim2 }, "bback"),
                          txt(terrDot(n) .. (siege and "  (siege)" or ""), { w = 240, size = 17, style = "Bold", color = C.gold, h = 32 }), spacer(),
                          btn(pk, "Swap sides", { w = 120, h = 32, enabled = #armiesIn(n, "Good") > 0 and #armiesIn(n, "Evil") > 0,
                                                                tip = "The army that moved in this turn attacks. Swap if it was the other way round." }, "bswap"),
                          btn(pk, "Announce again", { w = 130, h = 32, enabled = stageAllowed("Battle"), tip = "Reveal the armies and show everyone the battle pop-up again" }, "bprompt") }, 32, 8))
    -- who: chosen for you; a choice only appears when a side has more than one army here
    local function opt(a) return armyLabel(pk, a) .. " [" .. a.side .. "] #" .. a.id end
    local function valid(v, list)
      for _, a in ipairs(list) do if opt(a) == v then return true end end
      return false
    end
    if not valid(f.r_bAtk, atkList) then f.r_bAtk = autoA and opt(autoA) or nil end
    local defOpts = {}
    for _, a in ipairs(defList) do table.insert(defOpts, opt(a)) end
    if siege then table.insert(defOpts, "Garrison only (400 pts)") end
    local defOk = false
    for _, v in ipairs(defOpts) do if v == f.r_bDef then defOk = true end end
    if not defOk then f.r_bDef = autoD and opt(autoD) or defOpts[1] end
    if #atkList > 1 or #defOpts > 1 then
      local atkOpts = {}
      for _, a in ipairs(atkList) do table.insert(atkOpts, opt(a)) end
      local kids = {}
      if #atkList > 1 then table.insert(kids, txt("Attacking army", { w = 110, color = C.dim, size = 13 })); table.insert(kids, dropdown(pk, "r_bAtk", atkOpts)) end
      if #defOpts > 1 then table.insert(kids, txt("Defending", { w = 80, color = C.dim, size = 13 })); table.insert(kids, dropdown(pk, "r_bDef", defOpts)) end
      table.insert(d, row(kids, 32, 8))
    end
    local A = armyById(idOf(f.r_bAtk))
    local D = armyById(idOf(f.r_bDef))
    local garrison = string.find(f.r_bDef or "", "Garrison", 1, true) ~= nil
    -- matchup
    local function fighter(role, a, gside)
      local sd = a and a.side or gside or "Neutral"
      local k = { cap(role .. " · " .. sd, sideInk(sd), { h = 16 }) }
      if a then
        local hasSurgeon = false
        for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do if g.army == a.id then hasSurgeon = true end end
        local lk, uk = armyKnown(pk, a)
        local mine = canSee(pk, a.side)
        table.insert(k, txt(armyLabel(pk, a), { size = 22, style = "Bold", color = sideInk(sd), h = 30 }))
        table.insert(k, txt((lk and (a.leader ~= "" and a.leader or "No leader") or "Leader unknown") .. " · " .. (uk and (a.size .. " pts · ") or "") ..
                            (uk and (((a.damage or 0) > 0) and ("field as " .. math.floor(a.size * (1 - a.damage / 100)) .. " (-" .. a.damage .. "%)") or "full strength") or "units unknown"),
                            { size = 13, color = C.dim2, h = 20 }))
        local third = {}
        if mine and hasSurgeon then table.insert(third, "Surgeon attached") end
        if mine then table.insert(third, "Carrying " .. (a.carried or 0) .. " RP") end
        if uk and a.roster and a.roster.units then table.insert(third, a.roster.units .. " models, broken at " .. (a.roster.broken or "?") .. " dead") end
        table.insert(k, txt(table.concat(third, " · "), { size = 13, color = C.dim2, h = 20 }))
      elseif garrison and role == "Defender" then
        table.insert(k, txt("Garrison", { size = 22, style = "Bold", color = sideInk(sd), h = 30 }))
        table.insert(k, txt("400 pt garrison of " .. NAMES[n], { size = 13, color = C.dim2, h = 20 }))
        table.insert(k, txt("Siege battle", { size = 13, color = C.dim2, h = 20 }))
      else
        table.insert(k, txt("Choose an army", { size = 18, color = C.dim, h = 30 }))
        table.insert(k, gap(20)); table.insert(k, gap(20))
      end
      return card(k, { bg = sideBg(sd), border = (sd == "Good") and "#36593F" or ((sd == "Evil") and "#6B3631" or C.line), spacing = 4 })
    end
    local fa = fighter("Attacker", A)
    local fd = fighter("Defender", D, garrison and S.terr[n].owner or nil)
    table.insert(d, row({ fa, chip("VS", C.gold, C.head, { w = 50, h = 50, size = 15 }), fd }, hOf(fa), 8))
    -- conditions
    local lines = battleModifiers(n, A, D)
    local ck = { row({ heading("Conditions"), btn(pk, "Roll battle priority", { w = 200, h = 32, enabled = stageAllowed("Battle") }, "bprio") }, 32) }
    local here = armiesIn(n)
    for _, l in ipairs(lines) do table.insert(ck, row({ swatch(C.gold, 3, 20), txt(maskNames(pk, l, here), { size = 13, h = 20 }) }, 20, 10)) end
    for _, a in ipairs({ A, D }) do
      if a and canSee(pk, a.side) then
        local w = rosterOver(a)
        if w then table.insert(ck, strip(w, "warn", { h = 32 })) end
      end
    end
    table.insert(d, card(ck, { spacing = 6 }))
    -- result
    local an = A and armyLabel(pk, A) or "Attacker"
    local dn = (D and armyLabel(pk, D)) or (garrison and "Garrison") or "Defender"
    local function lead(a, fallback)
      if not a then return fallback end
      local lk = armyKnown(pk, a)
      return (lk and a.leader ~= "" and a.leader) or fallback
    end
    local out = f.bOut or "Broken"
    local rk = { row({ heading("Result"), ruleBtn(pk, "battles") }, 26) }
    table.insert(rk, field("Winner", choice(pk, "bWin", { { "atk", an }, { "def", dn } }, { onColors = BTN_GOOD })))
    table.insert(rk, field("Loser was", choice(pk, "bOut", { "Broken", "Quartered", "Destroyed" }, { default = "Broken", tips = TIPS.outcome })))
    table.insert(rk, field("", txt(TIPS.outcome[out] or "", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 })))
    table.insert(rk, field("", toggle(pk, "bRetreat", "Loser called the retreat" .. ((f.bRetreat == "True" and out == "Quartered") and "  - counts as Broken, not Quartered" or ""),
                                      { tip = "An army that called the retreat counts as Broken even if it fell below 25% - unless it was destroyed." })))
    table.insert(rk, field("Casualties %", row({ txt(an, { size = 13, color = A and sideInk(A.side) or C.dim2 }), input(pk, "bCasA", "0", { int = true, w = 64 }),
                                                 spacer(16), txt(dn, { size = 13, color = D and sideInk(D.side) or C.dim2 }), input(pk, "bCasD", "0", { int = true, w = 64 }) }, 32, 6)))
    if (A and A.roster and A.roster.units) or (D and D.roster and D.roster.units) then
      local _, la = lostInfo(A, f.r_bLostA)
      local _, ld = lostInfo(D, f.r_bLostD)
      table.insert(rk, field("Models lost", row({
        (A and A.roster and A.roster.units) and input(pk, "r_bLostA", "0", { int = true, w = 64 }) or spacer(64),
        txt(la or ((A and A.roster and A.roster.units) and ("of " .. A.roster.units .. " models") or "no roster"), { size = 12, color = C.dim }),
        spacer(16),
        (D and D.roster and D.roster.units) and input(pk, "r_bLostD", "0", { int = true, w = 64 }) or spacer(64),
        txt(ld or ((D and D.roster and D.roster.units) and ("of " .. D.roster.units .. " models") or "no roster"), { size = 12, color = C.dim }) }, 32, 6)))
      table.insert(rk, txt("Entering models lost fills in the casualty % for you.", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 }))
    end
    table.insert(rk, field("Heroic Duel", choice(pk, "bDuel", { { "none", "None" }, { "atk", lead(A, "Attacker") .. " won" },
                                                                { "def", lead(D, "Defender") .. " won" } }, { default = "none", tips = TIPS.duel })))
    table.insert(rk, txt("Casualties are the share of the army's points lost. Surgeons reduce them; the army then drops to the 25/50/75% tier.", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 }))
    table.insert(rk, btn(pk, "Apply battle result", { h = 44, size = 15, colors = BTN_GOLD, enabled = stageAllowed("Battle") }, "bapply"))
    table.insert(d, card(rk, { spacing = 10 }))
  end
  local hist = { heading("Recent battles", { size = 15 }) }
  if #S.battles == 0 then table.insert(hist, txt("None yet.", { color = C.dim, size = 13 })) end
  for i = #S.battles, math.max(1, #S.battles - 15), -1 do
    local b = S.battles[i]
    table.insert(hist, cap("Turn " .. b.t .. (b.n and (" · " .. NAMES[b.n]) or ""), nil, { h = 16 }))
    table.insert(hist, txt(((pk ~= "All" and S.settings.hidden) and b["t" .. pk]) or b.text, { size = 13, wrap = true, ww = 210 }))
    table.insert(hist, swatch(C.line2, 1, 1))
  end
  local left = col({ scroll(d, BODY_H, nil, 14) })
  local right = card({ scroll(hist, BODY_H - 30, nil, 6) }, { w = 260, bg = C.deep, border = C.line2, h = BODY_H })
  return { row({ left, right }, BODY_H, 18) }, true
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- Supplies
local function tabSupplies(pk)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local side = actingSide(pk)
  local f = u.f
  local d = {}
  local places, seen = {}, {}
  local function addPlace(n) if n and not seen[n] then seen[n] = true; table.insert(places, n) end end
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if a.side == side then addPlace(a.loc) end end
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do if g.side == side and isWagon(g) then addPlace(g.loc) end end
  for _, n in ipairs(ownedBy(side)) do addPlace(n) end
  table.sort(places)
  local opts = {}
  for _, n in ipairs(places) do table.insert(opts, terrOpt(n)) end
  local t = sideTotals(side)
  -- treasury aside
  local rich = {}
  for _, m in ipairs(ownedBy(side)) do table.insert(rich, m) end
  table.sort(rich, function(a, b) return S.terr[a].stock > S.terr[b].stock end)
  local function kv(k, v, c) return row({ txt(k, { size = 14, color = C.dim2 }), txt(tostring(v), { size = 14, w = 80, align = "MiddleRight", color = c or C.ink }) }, 22) end
  local aside = { heading(side .. "'s treasury", { size = 15 }),
                  txt((t.stock + t.carried + t.wagons) .. " RP", { size = 30, style = "Bold", color = sideInk(side), h = 42 }),
                  kv("In stockpiles", t.stock), kv("With armies", t.carried), kv("On wagons", t.wagons),
                  swatch(C.line2, 1, 1), kv("Income next turn", "+" .. t.income, C.goodInk), gap(10), cap("Richest stores") }
  for i = 1, math.min(5, #rich) do
    if S.terr[rich[i]].stock > 0 then table.insert(aside, kv(NAMES[rich[i]], S.terr[rich[i]].stock, C.gold)) end
  end
  local right = card(aside, { w = 260, bg = C.deep, border = C.line2, h = BODY_H, spacing = 8 })

  if #opts == 0 then
    table.insert(d, card({ heading("Nothing to manage"), txt("You have nothing that can carry or store resources.", { color = C.dim2 }) }))
    return { row({ col(d), right }, BODY_H, 18) }, true
  end
  local tsel = dropdown(pk, "r_sTerr", opts, { w = 260, h = 36 })
  local n = numOf(f.r_sTerr)
  local tt = S.terr[n]
  local bl = {}
  for _, b in ipairs(BUILDINGS) do if tt.b[b.key] then table.insert(bl, b.name) end end
  table.insert(d, row({ txt("Territory", { w = 74, color = C.dim, size = 13 }), tsel,
                        chip(tt.owner .. ((#bl > 0) and (" · " .. #bl .. " building" .. ((#bl == 1) and "" or "s")) or ""), sideInk(tt.owner), sideBg(tt.owner),
                             { w = 200, h = 28, size = 12, tip = (#bl > 0) and table.concat(bl, ", ") or "No buildings" }),
                        spacer() }, 36))
  -- endpoints
  local eps = {}
  if tt.owner == side then table.insert(eps, { key = "stock:" .. n, kind = "Stockpile", name = NAMES[n] .. " stores", amt = tt.stock }) end
  for _, a in ipairs(armiesIn(n, side)) do table.insert(eps, { key = "army:" .. a.id, kind = "Army", name = a.name, amt = a.carried or 0, cap = a.size }) end
  for _, g in ipairs(agentsIn(n, side, "Supply Wagon")) do table.insert(eps, { key = "wagon:" .. g.id, kind = "Wagon", name = wagonLabel(g), amt = g.carried or 0, cap = WAGON_CAP }) end
  for _, m in ipairs(ADJ[n] or {}) do
    for _, a in ipairs(armiesIn(m, side)) do table.insert(eps, { key = "army:" .. a.id, kind = "Army next door", name = a.name .. " (" .. NAMES[m] .. ")", amt = a.carried or 0, cap = a.size }) end
  end
  local byKey = {}
  for _, e in ipairs(eps) do byKey[e.key] = e; e.label = e.name .. "  [" .. e.key .. "]" end
  local fromK = string.match(f.sFrom or "", "%[(.-)%]$")
  local toK = string.match(f.sTo or "", "%[(.-)%]$")
  if not byKey[fromK] then fromK = eps[1] and eps[1].key; f.sFrom = fromK and byKey[fromK].label or nil end
  if not byKey[toK] or toK == fromK then
    toK = nil
    for _, e in ipairs(eps) do if e.key ~= fromK then toK = e.key; break end end
    f.sTo = toK and byKey[toK].label or nil
  end
  table.insert(d, heading("Holdings at " .. NAMES[n]))
  if #eps == 0 then table.insert(d, txt("Nothing of yours is here.", { color = C.dim })) end
  local cards = {}
  for _, e in ipairs(eps) do
    local isF, isT = e.key == fromK, e.key == toK
    local k = {
      cap(e.kind .. (isF and " · from" or (isT and " · to" or "")), isF and C.goldHi or (isT and C.goodInk or C.dim), { h = 16 }),
      txt(e.name, { size = 15, style = "Bold", h = 22 }),
      txt(e.amt .. (e.cap and (" / " .. e.cap) or "") .. " RP", { size = 22, style = "Bold", color = isF and C.gold or C.ink, h = 30 }),
      e.cap and bar(e.amt / e.cap, isT and C.good or (isF and "#C9A24F" or C.neutral), 8) or gap(8),
      row({ btn(pk, "From", { h = 26, size = 12, colors = isF and BTN_SEL or BTN_GHOST }, "sep", "from", e.key),
            btn(pk, "To", { h = 26, size = 12, colors = isT and BTN_GOOD or BTN_GHOST }, "sep", "to", e.key) }, 26, 6),
    }
    table.insert(cards, card(k, { bg = isF and C.raised or C.card, border = isF and "#C9A24F" or (isT and C.good or C.line), spacing = 6 }))
  end
  for i = 1, #cards, 3 do
    local part = { cards[i], cards[i + 1] or spacer(), cards[i + 2] or spacer() }
    table.insert(d, row(part, hOf(cards[i]), 12))
  end
  -- transfer
  if fromK and toK then
    local fe, te = endpoint(fromK), endpoint(toK)
    local fill = 0
    if fe and te then fill = math.max(0, math.min(fe.get(), (te.cap == math.huge) and fe.get() or (te.cap - te.get()))) end
    local k = { row({ heading("Move supplies"), ruleBtn(pk, "resources") }, 26),
      row({ chip(byKey[fromK].name, C.goldHi, C.raised, { h = 44, size = 14 }), txt("->", { w = 30, size = 18, color = C.gold, align = "MiddleCenter", style = "Bold" }),
            chip(byKey[toK].name, "#CDEBD6", C.goodBg, { h = 44, size = 14 }),
            input(pk, "sAmt", "RP", { int = true, w = 90, h = 44, size = 16 }),
            btn(pk, "Transfer", { w = 130, h = 44, colors = BTN_GOLD, enabled = stageAllowed("Logistics") }, "xfer") }, 44, 10),
      row({ txt("Quick amount", { w = 100, size = 12, color = C.dim }),
            btn(pk, "25", { w = 56, h = 28, size = 12, colors = BTN_GHOST }, "qamt", 25), btn(pk, "50", { w = 56, h = 28, size = 12, colors = BTN_GHOST }, "qamt", 50),
            btn(pk, "100", { w = 56, h = 28, size = 12, colors = BTN_GHOST }, "qamt", 100),
            btn(pk, "Fill (" .. fill .. ")", { w = 110, h = 28, size = 12, colors = BTN_SEL }, "qamt", fill), spacer() }, 28, 6),
      txt("Armies carry up to their points value, wagons up to " .. WAGON_CAP .. ". Stockpiles and wagons only trade within a territory; armies can also trade with adjacent armies.",
          { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 34, wrap = true }),
    }
    table.insert(d, card(k, { spacing = 10 }))
  end
  return { row({ col({ scroll(d, BODY_H, nil, 12) }), right }, BODY_H, 18) }, true
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- Log
local LOG_CATS = { { "all", "All" }, { "territories", "Territories" }, { "armies", "Armies" }, { "agents", "Agents" },
                   { "captures", "Captures" }, { "construction", "Construction" }, { "routes", "Routes" } }
local function logCat(e)
  if e.c and e.c ~= "war" then return e.c end
  local m = string.lower(e.m or "")
  if string.find(m, "captur") or string.find(m, "caught") or string.find(m, "raid") then return "captures" end
  if string.find(m, "wagon") or string.find(m, "route") then return "routes" end
  if string.find(m, "finished") or string.find(m, "construct") or string.find(m, "sabotag") then return "construction" end
  if string.find(m, "conquer") or string.find(m, "conquest") or string.find(m, "fortress") or string.find(m, " is now ") then return "territories" end
  if string.find(m, "scout") or string.find(m, "assassin") or string.find(m, "saboteur") or string.find(m, "surgeon") or string.find(m, "agent") then return "agents" end
  if string.find(m, "army") or string.find(m, "battle") or string.find(m, "retreat") or string.find(m, "host") or string.find(m, "raised") then return "armies" end
  return "other"
end

local function tabLog(pk)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local f = u.f
  local cat = f.logCat or "all"
  local q = string.lower(f.r_logQ or "")
  local kids = {}
  for _, c in ipairs(LOG_CATS) do
    table.insert(kids, btn(pk, c[2], { w = 96, h = 32, size = 12, colors = (cat == c[1]) and BTN_SEL or BTN_GHOST, textColor = (cat ~= c[1]) and C.dim2 or nil }, "set", "logCat", c[1]))
    if f.logCatWas ~= cat then f.logCatWas = cat; f.logPage = nil end
  end
  table.insert(kids, spacer())
  table.insert(kids, input(pk, "r_logQ", "Search the log", { w = 190, h = 32 }))
  local out = { row(kids, 32, 6) }
  -- a page of entries at a time: a long log is slow to draw in Tabletop Simulator
  local PER = 40
  local page = math.max(1, tonumber(f.logPage) or 1)
  local rows, shown, total, lastKey, matching = {}, 0, 0, nil, 0
  for i = #S.log, 1, -1 do
    local e = S.log[i]
    if e.v == nil or canSee(pk, e.v) then
      total = total + 1
      local ec = logCat(e)
      local hit = (cat == "all" or ec == cat) and (q == "" or string.find(string.lower(e.m or ""), q, 1, true))
      if hit then matching = matching + 1 end
      if hit and matching > (page - 1) * PER and shown < PER then
        local key = e.t .. ":" .. e.s
        if key ~= lastKey then
          lastKey = key
          table.insert(rows, row({ txt("Turn " .. e.t, { w = 70, size = 15, style = "Bold", color = C.gold, h = 30 }),
                                   cap(STAGES[e.s] or "?", C.dim, { w = 90, h = 30 }), spacer() }, 30, 8))
        end
        local h = wrapH(e.m, 14, MAIN_W - 170) + 6
        local hl = ec == "captures"
        local r = row({ swatch(e.v and sideColor(e.v) or C.neutral, 4, h - 6), cap(ec, hl and C.err or C.dim, { w = 100, h = h }),
                        txt(e.m, { size = 14, h = h }) }, h, 12)
        if hl then r.attributes.color = C.evilBg end
        table.insert(rows, r)
        shown = shown + 1
      end
    end
  end
  if shown == 0 then table.insert(rows, txt((page > 1 and matching > 0) and "No more entries." or "Nothing matches.", { color = C.dim })) end
  local pages = math.max(1, math.ceil(matching / PER))
  local logH = BODY_H - 32 - 14 - 14 - 30 - 4
  table.insert(out, card({ scroll(rows, logH - 20, nil, 4) }, { bg = C.deep, border = C.line2, pad = "18 8 10 10", h = logH }))
  local first = (shown > 0) and ((page - 1) * PER + 1) or 0
  table.insert(out, row({
    txt("Entries " .. first .. "-" .. ((page - 1) * PER + shown) .. " of " .. matching .. ((matching ~= total) and (" (" .. total .. " in all)") or "") ..
        (S.settings.hidden and " · hidden-information entries only show to their own side" or ""), { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 30 }),
    spacer(),
    btn(pk, "Newest", { w = 80, h = 28, size = 12, colors = BTN_GHOST, textColor = C.dim2, enabled = page > 1 }, "set", "logPage", "1"),
    btn(pk, "< Newer", { w = 90, h = 28, size = 12, colors = BTN_GHOST, textColor = C.dim2, enabled = page > 1 }, "set", "logPage", tostring(page - 1)),
    txt("Page " .. math.min(page, pages) .. " of " .. pages, { w = 90, size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 30, align = "MiddleCenter" }),
    btn(pk, "Older >", { w = 90, h = 28, size = 12, colors = BTN_GHOST, textColor = C.dim2, enabled = page < pages }, "set", "logPage", tostring(page + 1)),
  }, 30, 6))
  return out, true
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- Settings
local function settingRow(pk, label, sub, control)
  local h = sub and 40 or 30
  return row({ col({ txt(label, { size = 14, h = 20 }), sub and txt(sub, { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 }) or nil }, nil, 2), control }, h, 10)
end

local function tabSettings(pk)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local f = u.f
  local st = S.settings
  local out = {}
  -- campaign
  local confirm = u.confirm == "start"
  local th = {}
  for i, t in ipairs(THEATRES) do
    table.insert(th, btn(pk, t, { w = 96, h = 32, colors = (st.theatre == t) and BTN_SEL or BTN_GHOST, textColor = (st.theatre ~= t) and C.dim2 or nil,
      tip = "Good: " .. FACTION_LISTS[t].Good .. "\nEvil: " .. FACTION_LISTS[t].Evil ..
            "\nAllies - Good: " .. ALLIANCES[t].Good.name .. " (" .. ALLIANCES[t].Good.list .. "); Evil: " ..
            ALLIANCES[t].Evil.name .. " (" .. ALLIANCES[t].Evil.list .. ")" }, "theatre", t))
  end
  local all = {}
  for n = 1, #NAMES do table.insert(all, terrOpt(n)) end
  f.capGood = f.capGood or terrOpt(st.capGood)
  f.capEvil = f.capEvil or terrOpt(st.capEvil)
  local thName = st.theatre or "Eriador"
  local camp = {
    row({ heading("Campaign"), btn(pk, confirm and "Click again: this wipes the current campaign" or "Start new campaign",
                                   { w = confirm and 360 or 200, h = 36, colors = BTN_DANGER }, "start") }, 36),
    row({ txt("Theatre", { w = 64, color = C.dim, size = 13 }), row(th, 32, 0, 0), spacer(16),
          txt("Starting RP", { w = 84, color = C.dim, size = 13 }), input(pk, "startRP", tostring(st.startRP), { int = true, w = 80, default = tostring(st.startRP) }), spacer() }, 32, 8),
    row({ txt("Good capital", { w = 96, color = C.goodInk, size = 13 }), dropdown(pk, "capGood", all, { w = 240 }), spacer(16),
          txt("Evil capital", { w = 90, color = C.evilInk, size = 13 }), dropdown(pk, "capEvil", all, { w = 240 }), spacer() }, 32, 8),
    txt("Good lists: " .. FACTION_LISTS[thName].Good .. "   ·   Evil lists: " .. FACTION_LISTS[thName].Evil, { size = 12, color = C.dim, wrap = true }),
    txt("Owners reset to the starting map (8 Good, 8 Evil). Capitals get the starting RP and Fortifications.", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 }),
  }
  table.insert(out, card(camp, { spacing = 10 }))

  -- seats + rules
  f.r_setGood = st.goodColor
  f.r_setEvil = st.evilColor
  local seats = {
    heading("Seats & secrecy"),
    row({ txt("Good", { w = 40, color = C.goodInk, size = 13 }), dropdown(pk, "r_setGood", COLORS), spacer(8),
          txt("Evil", { w = 34, color = C.evilInk, size = 13 }), dropdown(pk, "r_setEvil", COLORS) }, 32, 6),
    settingRow(pk, "Lock seats to Good and Evil", "Grey spectators allowed", switch(pk, st.lockSeats, nil, "lockseats")),
    settingRow(pk, "Hidden information", "Each side sees only its own armies and agents", switch(pk, st.hidden, nil, "hidden")),
  }
  local rules = {
    heading("Table rules"),
    settingRow(pk, "Ready check before each stage", nil, switch(pk, st.readyCheck, nil, "readycheck")),
    settingRow(pk, "Dispatch pop-ups", nil, switch(pk, st.popups, nil, "popups")),
    settingRow(pk, "Move tokens by dropping them", nil, switch(pk, st.dropMove, nil, "dropmove")),
    settingRow(pk, "Rule override (fixing mistakes)", "Ignores stage, range and cost checks", switch(pk, st.override, { danger = true }, "override")),
  }
  local c1, c2 = card(seats, { spacing = 10 }), card(rules, { spacing = 10 })
  table.insert(out, row({ c1, c2 }, math.max(hOf(c1), hOf(c2)), 14))

  -- map & pieces + online/backup
  local cur = st.driveSpeed or "Slow"
  local spd = {}
  for _, s in ipairs({ "Slow", "Normal", "Fast" }) do
    table.insert(spd, btn(pk, s, { w = 70, h = 28, size = 12, colors = (cur == s) and BTN_SEL or BTN_GHOST, textColor = (cur ~= s) and C.dim2 or nil }, "drivespeed", s))
  end
  local mp = {
    heading("Map & pieces"),
    settingRow(pk, "Wagon speed", nil, row(spd, 28, 0, 0)),
    settingRow(pk, "Wagons face where they drive", nil, switch(pk, st.wagonFace ~= false, nil, "wagonface")),
    row({ btn(pk, "Rebuild building pieces", { h = 32, tip = "Puts every finished building's piece on its slot (and removes stray ones)." }, "rebuildpieces") }, 32),
  }
  if (S.mapVer or 1) < MAP_VERSION then
    f.imgFolder = st.imgFolder
    table.insert(mp, strip("This campaign is still on the old map. Run update_map.bat, then press Update map. Only fill in the folder if it says it can't find the images.", "warn"))
    table.insert(mp, row({ input(pk, "imgFolder", "Image folder, e.g. C:\\Users\\you\\Documents\\My Games\\Tabletop Simulator\\Mods\\Images\\EriadorCampaign", { h = 30, size = 11 }),
                           btn(pk, "Update map", { w = 120, h = 30, colors = BTN_GOLD }, "mapupdate") }, 30, 6))
  end
  local all2 = {}
  for n = 1, #NAMES do table.insert(all2, terrOpt(n)) end
  if S.editing then
    table.insert(mp, strip("Editing " .. NAMES[S.editing] .. ": drag the red (building) and blue (army/agent) markers, then Save.", "warn", { h = 40 }))
    table.insert(mp, row({ btn(pk, "Save slots", { colors = BTN_GOLD }, "slotsave"), btn(pk, "Reset", {}, "slotreset"), btn(pk, "Cancel", {}, "slotcancel") }, 32, 6))
  else
    table.insert(mp, row({ dropdown(pk, "slotTerr", all2), btn(pk, "Slot editor", { w = 120, tip = "12 building + 4 army/agent slots per territory" }, "slotedit") }, 32, 6))
  end
  local online = {
    heading("Online & backup"),
    txt("Other players can't load files from your PC. Once the map and borders show: Modding > Upload All Loaded Files, save, then check here.",
        { size = 12, color = C.dim2, wrap = true, ww = MAIN_W / 2 - 40 }),
    btn(pk, "Check online status", { h = 34, colors = BTN_GOLD, tip = "Prints to chat how many border images and whether the map image are uploaded" }, "cal", "online"),
    row({ btn(pk, "Export to Notebook", { h = 32 }, "export"), btn(pk, "Import from Notebook", { h = 32 }, "import") }, 32, 6),
    txt("Backups use the Notebook tab 'War Council Save'.", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 }),
  }
  local c3, c4 = card(mp, { spacing = 10 }), card(online, { spacing = 10 })
  table.insert(out, row({ c3, c4 }, math.max(hOf(c3), hOf(c4)), 14))

  -- display + backups
  local function seg(field2, options, action)
    local kids = {}
    for _, o in ipairs(options) do
      local val, label = o[1], o[2]
      local on = (st[field2] or options[1][1]) == val
      table.insert(kids, btn(pk, label, { w = o[3] or 80, h = 28, size = 12, colors = on and BTN_SEL or BTN_GHOST, textColor = (not on) and C.dim2 or nil }, action, val))
    end
    return row(kids, 28, 0, 0)
  end
  local disp = {
    heading("Display"),
    settingRow(pk, "Panel size", "Compact suits laptop screens", seg("panelSize", { { "Compact", "Compact", 76 }, { "Normal", "Normal", 70 }, { "Large", "Large", 60 } }, "psize")),
    settingRow(pk, "Map highlights", "Range and route outlines (everyone sees them)", seg("mapHints", { { "auto", "Auto", 56 }, { "on", "On", 46 }, { "off", "Off", 46 } }, "maphints")),
    settingRow(pk, "Dispatch pop-ups appear", nil, seg("newsTiming", { { "now", "As it happens", 110 }, { "turn", "Each new turn", 110 } }, "newstiming")),
    settingRow(pk, "Stage banners", "A big title across the screen when the stage changes", switch(pk, st.banners ~= false, nil, "bannertog")),
    settingRow(pk, "Map hover info", "Shows what's in the territory under your pointer", switch(pk, st.hoverInfo ~= false, nil, "hovertog")),
    settingRow(pk, "Roads on the map", "Territories with Roads get a road drawn out to each neighbour", switch(pk, st.roadLines ~= false, nil, "roadtog")),
    settingRow(pk, "Random events", "After priority, the winner rolls a D6: on a 1-3 both sides roll on their event tables", switch(pk, st.randomEvents ~= false, nil, "evtog")),
    settingRow(pk, "Physical dice", "Every roll is made with real dice in the object named 'Dice Tray'", switch(pk, st.dice ~= "digital", nil, "dicetog")),
    settingRow(pk, "Good's dice", "Who throws Good's dice", row({ btn(pk, "War Council", { w = 120, h = 28, size = 12, colors = (diceMode("Good") == "auto") and BTN_SEL or BTN_GHOST }, "diceby", "Good", "auto"),
                                                                  btn(pk, "Player", { w = 90, h = 28, size = 12, colors = (diceMode("Good") == "player") and BTN_SEL or BTN_GHOST }, "diceby", "Good", "player") }, 28, 6, 0)),
    settingRow(pk, "Evil's dice", "Who throws Evil's dice", row({ btn(pk, "War Council", { w = 120, h = 28, size = 12, colors = (diceMode("Evil") == "auto") and BTN_SEL or BTN_GHOST }, "diceby", "Evil", "auto"),
                                                                  btn(pk, "Player", { w = 90, h = 28, size = 12, colors = (diceMode("Evil") == "player") and BTN_SEL or BTN_GHOST }, "diceby", "Evil", "player") }, 28, 6, 0)),
    row({ btn(pk, "Turn conquest markers 180", { h = 30, size = 12, tip = "Use if the conquest text on the map reads upside down" }, "markerflip") }, 30),
    txt("Auto shows map highlights only while hidden information is off.", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 }),
  }
  local bnames = backupList()
  local bk2 = {
    heading("Turn backups"),
    settingRow(pk, "Back up at the start of every turn", "Keeps the last 5 turns in the Notebook", switch(pk, st.backups ~= false, nil, "backups")),
  }
  if #bnames == 0 then
    table.insert(bk2, txt("No backups yet - one is made when the next turn starts.", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 }))
  else
    table.insert(bk2, dropdown(pk, "backupSel", bnames))
    local confirmB = u.confirm == "brestore"
    table.insert(bk2, btn(pk, confirmB and "Click again: replace the campaign with this backup" or "Restore this backup",
                          { h = 32, colors = confirmB and BTN_DANGER or BTN }, "brestore"))
    table.insert(bk2, txt("Restoring can be undone with Undo.", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 }))
  end
  local c5, c6 = card(disp, { spacing = 10 }), card(bk2, { spacing = 10 })
  table.insert(out, row({ c5, c6 }, math.max(hOf(c5), hOf(c6)), 14))

  -- end of the campaign
  local endc = {
    row({ heading("End of the campaign"), spacer(), btn(pk, "Campaign summary", { w = 180, h = 32, colors = BTN_GOLD }, "sumshow") }, 32),
  }
  if S.winner then
    table.insert(endc, strip(S.winner .. " has won the campaign.", "ok"))
    table.insert(endc, row({ btn(pk, "Resume the campaign (undo the win)", { h = 32, colors = BTN_GHOST }, "unendwar") }, 32))
  else
    table.insert(endc, txt("The campaign ends by itself when a side loses all its territory. To finish early, declare a winner:", { size = 12, color = C.dim, wrap = true, ww = MAIN_W - 60 }))
    local cg, ce = u.confirm == "endwar:Good", u.confirm == "endwar:Evil"
    table.insert(endc, row({ btn(pk, cg and "Click again: Good wins" or "Declare Good the winner", { h = 32, colors = cg and BTN_GOOD or BTN }, "endwar", "Good"),
                             btn(pk, ce and "Click again: Evil wins" or "Declare Evil the winner", { h = 32, colors = ce and BTN_EVIL or BTN }, "endwar", "Evil") }, 32, 8))
  end
  table.insert(out, card(endc, { spacing = 10 }))

  -- advanced
  local okList, missList = {}, {}
  local okT, status = pcall(templateStatus)
  if okT then for _, e in ipairs(status) do table.insert(e[2] and okList or missList, e[1]) end end
  local adv = {
    heading("Templates & borders"),
    txt("Template objects found: " .. (#okList > 0 and table.concat(okList, ", ") or "none"), { size = 12, color = C.ok, wrap = true }),
    txt("Missing: " .. (#missList > 0 and table.concat(missList, ", ") or "none"), { size = 12, color = (#missList > 0) and C.warn or C.ok, wrap = true }),
    txt("Armies and agents first link to an object named exactly like them. Only if there is none is a template ('Good Army', 'Scout', ...) copied. Keep templates on the storage tray.",
        { size = 12, color = C.dim, wrap = true }),
    row({ btn(pk, "Refresh borders", { h = 30, size = 12 }, "cal", "refresh"), btn(pk, "Diagnose (chat)", { h = 30, size = 12 }, "cal", "diag"),
          btn(pk, "Next border mode", { h = 30, size = 12 }, "cal", "mode"), btn(pk, "Flip left-right", { h = 30, size = 12 }, "cal", "flipX"),
          btn(pk, "Flip top-bottom", { h = 30, size = 12 }, "cal", "flipZ"), btn(pk, "Rotate 180", { h = 30, size = 12 }, "cal", "rot180"),
          btn(pk, "Flip face", { h = 30, size = 12 }, "cal", "face") }, 30, 6),
    txt("Border buttons are only needed if the borders are missing or don't line up with the map.", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 }),
  }
  table.insert(out, card(adv, { spacing = 8 }))

  -- sounds
  local gaps = {}
  for _, g in ipairs({ 2, 3, 4, 6 }) do
    local on = (tonumber(st.soundGap) or 4) == g
    table.insert(gaps, btn(pk, g .. "s", { w = 48, h = 28, size = 12, colors = on and BTN_SEL or BTN_GHOST, textColor = (not on) and C.dim2 or nil }, "sndgap", g))
  end
  local snd = {
    row({ heading("Sounds"), spacer(), txt("Gap between two sounds", { w = 170, size = 12, color = C.dim, align = "MiddleRight" }), row(gaps, 28, 0, 0), spacer(12),
          switch(pk, st.sound ~= false, nil, "sndtog") }, 30, 8),
    row({ txt("Put the music back on after a sound", { size = 13 }), spacer(), switch(pk, st.musicResume ~= false, nil, "musictog") }, 30, 8),
    row({ txt("Sounds for everyday actions (moves, building, wagons, stage changes)", { size = 13 }), spacer(), switch(pk, st.minorSounds ~= false, nil, "minortog") }, 30, 8),
    txt("If a track from the Music Player was playing, it starts again (from the beginning) once the sounds finish. TTS can't continue a track from the middle.",
        { size = 12, color = C.dim2, wrap = true, ww = MAIN_W - 60 }),
    txt("Upload MP3s with Modding > Cloud Manager, copy each link and paste it below (press Enter to save). Sounds play through the Music Player, so everyone hears them and each player's music volume sets how loud they are. Leave a box empty for no sound (a victory with no sound of its own uses Battle drawn). If several things happen at once, the most important plays first and up to two more follow, one gap apart.",
        { size = 12, color = C.dim2, wrap = true, ww = MAIN_W - 60 }),
  }
  st.sounds = st.sounds or {}
  for _, sl in ipairs(SOUND_SLOTS) do
    f["snd_" .. sl.key] = st.sounds[sl.key]
    table.insert(snd, row({ txt(sl.label, { w = 160, size = 13 }), input(pk, "snd_" .. sl.key, "https://steamusercontent-a.akamaihd.net/ugc/...", { h = 30, size = 12 }),
                            btn(pk, "Test", { w = 70, h = 30, enabled = st.sounds[sl.key] ~= nil }, "sndtest", sl.key) }, 30, 8))
  end
  table.insert(out, card(snd, { spacing = 8 }))
  -- performance
  local pon = PERF and PERF.on
  table.insert(out, card({
    row({ heading("Performance"), spacer(), txt("Time what the War Council does", { w = 230, size = 12, color = C.dim, align = "MiddleRight" }), spacer(12),
          switch(pk, pon, nil, "perftog") }, 30, 8),
    row({ txt("Prepare tabs in the background (fewer full redraws when you open a tab)", { size = 13 }), spacer(),
          switch(pk, S.settings.prewarm ~= false, nil, "prewarmtog") }, 30, 8),
    row({ txt(pon and "Timing is on. Play normally for a minute or two, then press Print report: the timings appear in the chat."
                  or "Switch timing on, play normally for a minute or two, then press Print report to see what takes the time.",
              { size = 12, color = C.dim2, wrap = true, ww = MAIN_W - 220 }), spacer(),
          btn(pk, "Print report", { w = 130, h = 30, size = 13, enabled = pon and true or false }, "perfrep") }, 40, 8),
  }, { spacing = 8 }))
  return out
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- Wagons
local ACT_TIPS = {
  none = "Do nothing here.",
  load = "Load from your stockpile here. Leave the amount blank for as much as fits.",
  unload = "Unload into your stockpile here. Leave the amount blank to unload everything.",
}

local function actRow(pk, g, k, i, a, can)
  local f = uiState(pk).f
  local kids = {
    btn(pk, "Nothing", { w = 80, h = 30, size = 12, colors = (a.k == "none") and BTN_SEL or BTN_GHOST, textColor = (a.k ~= "none") and C.dim2 or nil, tip = ACT_TIPS.none, enabled = can }, "sact", g.id, k, i, "none"),
    btn(pk, "Load", { w = 66, h = 30, size = 12, colors = (a.k == "load") and BTN_GOOD or BTN_GHOST, textColor = (a.k ~= "load") and C.dim2 or nil, tip = ACT_TIPS.load, enabled = can }, "sact", g.id, k, i, "load"),
    btn(pk, "Unload", { w = 74, h = 30, size = 12, colors = (a.k == "unload") and BTN_EVIL or BTN_GHOST, textColor = (a.k ~= "unload") and C.dim2 or nil, tip = ACT_TIPS.unload, enabled = can }, "sact", g.id, k, i, "unload"),
  }
  if a.k ~= "none" then
    local fld = "r_amt_" .. g.id .. "_" .. k .. "_" .. i
    f[fld] = a.a and tostring(a.a) or ""
    table.insert(kids, txt("Amount", { w = 54, color = C.dim, size = 12 }))
    table.insert(kids, input(pk, fld, "all", { int = true, w = 70, h = 30 }))
  end
  return kids
end

local function stopBadge(label, on)
  return chip(label, on and "#1C150F" or C.gold, on and "#C9A24F" or C.raised, { w = 34, h = 34, size = 13 })
end

local function relation(g, n)
  local o = S.terr[n].owner
  if o == g.side then return "yours" elseif o == "Neutral" then return "neutral" end
  return "enemy"
end

local function routeEditor(pk, g, d)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local f = u.f
  local r = g.route
  local can = canSee(pk, g.side)
  -- choosing an army and a depot (used when setting up or changing a supply run)
  local function supplyPicker(k, label)
    local armies, depots = {}, {}
    for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if a.side == g.side then table.insert(armies, a.name .. " #" .. a.id) end end
    for _, n in ipairs(ownedBy(g.side)) do table.insert(depots, terrOpt(n)) end
    if #armies == 0 then armies = { "(no armies)" } end
    if #depots == 0 then depots = { "(no territory)" } end
    if r and isArmyRoute(r) then
      local a = armyById(r.army)
      if a and not f.sArmy then f.sArmy = a.name .. " #" .. a.id end
      if not f.sDepot and r.depot then f.sDepot = terrOpt(r.depot) end
    elseif not f.sDepot and S.terr[g.loc].owner == g.side then f.sDepot = terrOpt(g.loc) end
    table.insert(k, row({ txt("Army", { w = 60, color = C.dim, size = 13 }), dropdown(pk, "sArmy", armies),
                          txt("Load at", { w = 60, color = C.dim, size = 13 }), dropdown(pk, "sDepot", depots),
                          btn(pk, label, { w = 170, colors = BTN_GOLD, enabled = can }, "rsupply", g.id) }, 32, 8))
  end
  if not r then
    local k = { heading("Supply route"),
      txt("No route. Each turn of a route the wagon makes up to 3 moves and can load or unload at any stop; the route then repeats.", { color = C.dim2, size = 13, wrap = true }),
      row({ btn(pk, "Create a route starting at " .. NAMES[g.loc], { w = 360, h = 38, colors = BTN_GOLD, enabled = can }, "rnew", g.id), spacer() }, 38),
      heading("Or supply an army"),
      txt("The wagon shuttles on its own: it loads at a territory of yours, takes the supplies to the army wherever it is, and comes back for more. Armies outside your land use 25 RP a turn (50 for 800-1000 pts).",
          { color = C.dim2, size = 13, wrap = true }) }
    supplyPicker(k, "Start supply run")
    table.insert(d, card(k, { spacing = 10 }))
    return
  end
  if isArmyRoute(r) then
    local a = armyById(r.army)
    local k = { row({ heading("Supplying " .. (a and a.name or "an army")), spacer(),
                      btn(pk, (u.confirm == ("rclear:" .. g.id)) and "Click again to delete" or "Delete supply run", { w = 190, h = 32, size = 12, colors = BTN_DANGER, enabled = can }, "rclear", g.id) }, 32, 8) }
    for _, p in ipairs(armyRouteProblems(r)) do table.insert(k, strip(p, "warn", { h = 32 })) end
    if a then
      table.insert(k, row({ tile((a.carried or 0) .. " / " .. a.size, a.name .. " carries", { size = 18, frac = (a.carried or 0) / a.size, barColor = outOfSupplies(a) and C.err or C.good,
                                 sub = inFriendlyLand(a) and "In your land: no upkeep" or ("Uses " .. armyUpkeep(a) .. " RP a turn at " .. NAMES[a.loc]) }),
                            tile(NAMES[r.depot] or "-", "Loads at", { size = 18, sub = "Stockpile " .. ((S.terr[r.depot] or {}).stock or 0) .. " RP" }) }, nil, 8))
    end
    table.insert(k, strip(armyRouteStatus(g), "dim", { h = 32 }))
    table.insert(k, txt("Change who it supplies or where it loads:", { color = C.dim, size = 12, h = 18 }))
    supplyPicker(k, "Update")
    table.insert(d, card(k, { spacing = 10 }))
    return
  end
  fixRoute(r)
  local sel = u.sel.leg or 1
  if sel > #r.legs then sel = math.max(1, #r.legs) end
  u.sel.leg = sel
  local k = {}
  -- turn tabs
  local tabs = {}
  for i = 1, #r.legs do
    local nxt = r.on and r.idx == i
    table.insert(tabs, btn(pk, "Turn " .. i .. (nxt and " · next" or ""), { w = nxt and 120 or 90, h = 32, size = 13, colors = (i == sel) and BTN_SEL or BTN_GHOST,
                                                                          textColor = (i ~= sel) and C.dim2 or nil, tip = legSummary(r, i) }, "rsel", i))
  end
  table.insert(tabs, btn(pk, "+ Turn", { w = 80, h = 32, size = 13, enabled = can, tip = "Adds a turn that starts where the last one ends." }, "radd", g.id))
  table.insert(tabs, btn(pk, "- Last", { w = 70, h = 32, size = 13, enabled = can and #r.legs > 0, tip = "Removes the last turn" }, "rdel", g.id))
  table.insert(tabs, spacer())
  table.insert(tabs, btn(pk, (u.confirm == ("rclear:" .. g.id)) and "Click again to delete" or "Delete route", { w = 170, h = 32, size = 12, colors = BTN_DANGER, enabled = can }, "rclear", g.id))
  table.insert(k, row(tabs, 32, 6))
  local probs = routeProblems(r)
  if #probs > 0 then for _, p in ipairs(probs) do table.insert(k, strip(p, "warn", { h = 32 })) end end
  if #r.legs > 0 then
    local leg = r.legs[sel]
    local startN = legStart(r, sel)
    -- start
    local kids = { stopBadge("S", true), col({ txt(NAMES[startN], { size = 15, style = "Bold", h = 20 }), txt("Start of turn - before leaving", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 16 }) }, 220, 0) }
    for _, x in ipairs(actRow(pk, g, sel, 0, leg.depart, can)) do table.insert(kids, x) end
    table.insert(kids, spacer())
    table.insert(k, row(kids, 40, 8))
    for i, n in ipairs(leg.steps) do
      local opts = {}
      for _, x in ipairs(stepOptions(r, sel, i)) do table.insert(opts, terrOpt(x)) end
      local fld = "r_stop_" .. g.id .. "_" .. sel .. "_" .. i
      f[fld] = terrOpt(n)
      local last = i == #leg.steps
      local kids2 = { stopBadge(tostring(i)),
                      col({ dropdown(pk, fld, (#opts > 0) and opts or { terrOpt(n) }, { w = 230, h = 32 }),
                            txt(n .. " · " .. relation(g, n) .. (last and " · turn ends here" or ""), { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 16 }) }, 230, 2) }
      for _, x in ipairs(actRow(pk, g, sel, i, leg.acts[i], can)) do table.insert(kids2, x) end
      table.insert(kids2, spacer())
      table.insert(kids2, btn(pk, "Remove", { w = 76, h = 30, size = 12, enabled = can, tip = "Removes this stop and every stop after it." }, "rmstop", g.id, sel, i))
      table.insert(k, row(kids2, 50, 8))
    end
    local nextOpts = stepOptions(r, sel, #leg.steps + 1)
    if #nextOpts > 0 then
      local labels = {}
      for _, x in ipairs(nextOpts) do table.insert(labels, terrOpt(x)) end
      table.insert(k, row({ stopBadge("+"), txt("Next stop", { w = 70, color = C.dim, size = 13 }), dropdown(pk, "addStop", labels, { w = 240 }),
                            btn(pk, "Add stop", { w = 110, colors = BTN_GOLD, enabled = can }, "addstop", g.id, sel), spacer() }, 34, 8))
    else
      table.insert(k, txt("No movement left this turn.", { color = C.dim, size = 12, h = 18 }))
    end
  end
  table.insert(k, row({ input(pk, "routeName", "name this route"), btn(pk, "Save to library", { w = 160, enabled = can }, "rsave", g.id) }, 32, 8))
  table.insert(d, card(k, { spacing = 10 }))
end

local function tabWagons(pk)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local side = actingSide(pk)
  local f = u.f
  local rows = { btn(pk, "+  Recruit a Supply Wagon", { colors = (u.sel.wagon == nil) and BTN_GOLD or BTN, h = 40, size = 14 }, "selW", "new") }
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents) do
    if isWagon(g) and canSee(pk, g.side) then
      local rt = g.route and (isArmyRoute(g.route) and (((armyById(g.route.army) or {}).name and ("supplying " .. armyById(g.route.army).name) or "supply run") .. (g.route.on and " · on" or " · off"))
                 or (g.route.on and ("route on · next turn " .. (g.route.idx or 1) .. " of " .. #g.route.legs) or "route off")) or "no route"
      local on = u.sel.wagon == g.id
      local lr = listRow(pk, wagonLabel(g), NAMES[g.loc] .. "  ·  " .. rt, on, sideColor(g.side), nil, "selW", g.id)
      table.insert(rows, lr)
      table.insert(rows, row({ spacer(4), bar((g.carried or 0) / WAGON_CAP, g.route and g.route.on and C.good or C.neutral, 6),
                               txt((g.carried or 0) .. "/" .. WAGON_CAP, { w = 64, size = 11, color = C.dim, align = "MiddleRight", h = 12 }) }, 12, 6))
    end
  end
  local lib = { cap("Route library", C.gold, { h = 18 }) }
  if #S.routes == 0 then
    table.insert(lib, txt("No saved routes yet.", { color = C.dim, size = 12, h = 18 }))
  else
    local names = {}
    for i, rt in ipairs(S.routes) do table.insert(names, i .. ". " .. rt.name .. " (" .. #rt.legs .. " turns)") end
    table.insert(lib, dropdown(pk, "libSel", names))
    local w = u.sel.wagon and agentById(u.sel.wagon)
    table.insert(lib, row({ btn(pk, "Apply to wagon", { h = 30, size = 12, enabled = w ~= nil }, "rapply"), btn(pk, "Delete", { w = 70, h = 30, size = 12, colors = BTN_DANGER }, "rlibdel") }, 30, 6))
  end
  table.insert(lib, row({ btn(pk, "Pause all", { h = 30, size = 12 }, "rall", 0), btn(pk, "Resume all", { h = 30, size = 12 }, "rall", 1) }, 30, 6))
  local libCard = card(lib, { spacing = 8, bg = C.deep, border = C.line2 })
  local left = col({ scroll(rows, BODY_H - hOf(libCard) - 12, nil, 6), libCard }, 300, 12)

  local d = {}
  local g = u.sel.wagon and agentById(u.sel.wagon)
  if not g then
    local k = { row({ heading("Recruit a " .. side .. " Supply Wagon", { size = 20, h = 30 }), ruleBtn(pk, "resources") }, 30), txt("Building stage", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 16 }) }
    local own = {}
    for _, n in ipairs(ownedBy(side)) do table.insert(own, terrOpt(n)) end
    if #own == 0 then own = { "(no territory)" } end
    table.insert(k, field("Recruit at", dropdown(pk, "wTerr", own)))
    local n = numOf(f.wTerr)
    if n then table.insert(k, strip("It will be called '" .. wagonName(n) .. "' - name its token the same to link it.", "ok", { h = 34 })) end
    table.insert(k, row({ tile(AGENTS["Supply Wagon"].cost .. " RP", "Cost", { size = 20 }), tile("3", "Regions per move", { size = 20 }), tile(WAGON_CAP, "RP capacity", { size = 20 }) }, nil, 8))
    table.insert(k, txt("A wagon that meets an enemy army (either one moving in) loses all its cargo; the army takes half. Wagons in a territory can pay for building, raising, recruiting and replenishing there.",
                        { color = C.dim, size = 12, wrap = true }))
    table.insert(k, row({ spacer(), btn(pk, "Recruit Supply Wagon", { w = 260, h = 42, size = 15, colors = BTN_GOLD, enabled = stageAllowed("Building") }, "wrecruit") }, 42))
    table.insert(d, card(k, { spacing = 10 }))
  else
    local can = canSee(pk, g.side)
    local r = g.route
    local mvl = agentMoveLeft(g)
    local sub = "At " .. NAMES[g.loc] .. "  ·  carrying " .. (g.carried or 0) .. " / " .. WAGON_CAP .. " RP" ..
                (g.moved and "  ·  moved this turn" or (((g.mvUsed or 0) > 0) and ("  ·  " .. mvl .. " movement left") or ""))
    if r and isArmyRoute(r) then
      sub = sub .. "\n" .. armyRouteStatus(g) .. "  ·  runs at the start of each Logistics stage"
    elseif r then
      local probs = routeProblems(r)
      sub = sub .. "\nRoute loops from " .. NAMES[r.start] .. "  ·  " .. ((#probs == 0) and "valid" or "needs fixing") .. "  ·  runs at the start of each Logistics stage"
    end
    local k = {
      row({ col({ txt(wagonLabel(g), { size = 22, style = "Bold", color = C.gold, h = 32 }), txt(sub, { size = 12, color = C.dim2, h = r and 34 or 18 }) }, nil, 2),
            r and btn(pk, r.on and "Route ON" or "Route OFF", { w = 120, h = 34, colors = r.on and BTN_GOOD or BTN, enabled = can,
                                                               tip = "Active routes move automatically at the start of every Logistics stage." }, "rtoggle", g.id) or spacer(1) }, r and 68 or 52, 8),
      row({ btn(pk, "Load all", { h = 32, enabled = can and stageAllowed("Logistics"), tip = "Fill the wagon from your stockpile in this territory." }, "wload", g.id),
            btn(pk, "Unload all", { h = 32, enabled = can and stageAllowed("Logistics"), tip = "Empty the wagon into your stockpile in this territory." }, "wunload", g.id),
            btn(pk, g.autoUnload and "Auto-unload: ON" or "Auto-unload: OFF", { h = 32, colors = g.autoUnload and BTN_SEL or BTN, enabled = can,
                tip = "When ON, the wagon empties itself whenever it arrives in one of your territories." }, "wauto", g.id) }, 32, 8),
    }
    local dests = {}
    for _, n in ipairs(moveRangeFor(g, false, agentMoveLeft(g))) do table.insert(dests, terrOpt(n)) end
    if #dests == 0 then dests = { "(nowhere in range)" } end
    table.insert(k, row({ txt("Move now", { w = 70, color = C.dim, size = 13 }), dropdown(pk, "wDest", dests),
                          btn(pk, "Move", { w = 100, enabled = can and stageAllowed("Logistics") and not g.moved }, "wmove", g.id) }, 32, 8))
    for _, line in ipairs(tokenBlock(pk, "agent", g)) do table.insert(k, line) end
    table.insert(d, card(k, { spacing = 10 }))
    routeEditor(pk, g, d)
  end
  local right = col({ scroll(d, BODY_H, nil, 14) })
  return { row({ left, right }, BODY_H, 18) }, true
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- History
local function barChart(title, rows, getter, maxV, see, note)
  local n = #rows
  local first = math.max(1, n - 19)
  local cols = {}
  local CH = 150
  for i = first, n do
    local r = rows[i]
    local bars = {}
    for _, side in ipairs(SIDES) do
      local v = see[side] and getter(r[side] or {}) or nil
      local h = v and math.max(2, math.floor(CH * v / math.max(1, maxV))) or 2
      table.insert(bars, el("Panel", { color = v and sideColor(side) or C.line2, preferredWidth = 12, flexibleWidth = 0, preferredHeight = h }))
    end
    table.insert(cols, el("VerticalLayout", { preferredWidth = 34, flexibleWidth = 1, spacing = 4, childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "false",
                                              childAlignment = "LowerCenter", preferredHeight = CH + 22 }, {
      el("HorizontalLayout", { preferredHeight = CH, spacing = 3, childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "false",
                               childAlignment = "LowerCenter", flexibleWidth = 1 }, bars),
      txt("T" .. r.t, { size = 11, color = C.dim, h = 14, align = "MiddleCenter" }),
    }))
  end
  local k = { row({ heading(title, { size = 15 }), spacer(),
                    chip("Good", C.goodInk, C.goodBg, { w = 64, h = 22, size = 11 }), chip("Evil", C.evilInk, C.evilBg, { w = 64, h = 22, size = 11 }) }, 24) }
  if n == 0 then table.insert(k, txt("History starts with the first turn.", { color = C.dim }))
  else table.insert(k, row(cols, CH + 22, 4)) end
  if note then table.insert(k, txt(note, { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 })) end
  return card(k, { spacing = 10 })
end

local function tabHistory(pk)
  local out = {}
  local rows = S.hist or {}
  local see = { Good = canSee(pk, "Good"), Evil = canSee(pk, "Evil") }
  local maxT, maxR = 1, 1
  for _, r in ipairs(rows) do
    for _, side in ipairs(SIDES) do
      if r[side] then
        maxT = math.max(maxT, r[side].terr or 0)
        if see[side] then maxR = math.max(maxR, r[side].rp or 0) end
      end
    end
  end
  local allSee = { Good = true, Evil = true }
  table.insert(out, barChart("Territories held", rows, function(x) return x.terr end, maxT, allSee, "Counted at the start of each turn."))
  table.insert(out, barChart("Total resources (RP)", rows, function(x) return x.rp end, maxR, see,
                             (see.Good and see.Evil) and "Stockpiles + supplies carried by armies and wagons." or "The enemy's resources are hidden."))
  -- battle record
  local wins = { Good = 0, Evil = 0 }
  for _, b in ipairs(S.battles) do if b.win and wins[b.win] then wins[b.win] = wins[b.win] + 1 end end
  local k = { row({ heading("Battle record", { size = 15 }), spacer(),
                    chip("Good won " .. wins.Good, C.goodInk, C.goodBg, { w = 110, h = 24, size = 12 }),
                    chip("Evil won " .. wins.Evil, C.evilInk, C.evilBg, { w = 110, h = 24, size = 12 }) }, 26) }
  if #S.battles == 0 then table.insert(k, txt("No battles yet.", { color = C.dim })) end
  for i = #S.battles, 1, -1 do
    local b = S.battles[i]
    local text = ((pk ~= "All" and S.settings.hidden) and b["t" .. pk]) or b.text
    local h = wrapH(text, 13, MAIN_W - 200) + 4
    table.insert(k, row({ swatch(b.win and sideColor(b.win) or C.neutral, 4, h),
                          txt("Turn " .. b.t .. (b.n and ("\n" .. NAMES[b.n]) or ""), { w = 120, size = 12, color = C.dim, h = h }),
                          txt(text, { size = 13, h = h }) }, h, 10))
  end
  table.insert(out, card(k, { spacing = 6 }))
  return out
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- Reports
-- Public, compact copy of the campaign for the web page (no hidden information when hidden info is on).
function webExport()
  local hidden = S.settings.hidden and true or false
  local out = { v = 2, pointCap = { base = POINT_CAP_BASE, city = POINT_CAP_CITY }, theatre = S.settings.theatre or "Eriador", turn = S.turn, stage = stageName(), started = S.started and true or false,
                hidden = hidden, caps = { Good = S.settings.capGood, Evil = S.settings.capEvil }, winner = S.winner, winnerNote = S.winnerNote,
                terr = {}, hist = {}, battles = {}, reports = S.reports or {}, armies = {} }
  pcall(function() out.exported = os.date("%d %b %Y %H:%M") end)
  for n, t in ipairs(S.terr) do
    local bl = {}
    for _, b in ipairs(BUILDINGS) do if t.b[b.key] then table.insert(bl, b.name) end end
    local e = { n = n, name = NAMES[n], owner = t.owner, b = bl }
    if t.conq then e.conq = { by = t.conq.by, prog = t.conq.prog, need = t.conq.need, held = t.conq.held } end
    if not hidden then e.stock = t.stock end
    table.insert(out.terr, e)
  end
  for _, r in ipairs(S.hist or {}) do
    local h = { t = r.t }
    for _, side in ipairs(SIDES) do
      local x = r[side] or {}
      h[side] = { terr = x.terr, rp = (not hidden) and x.rp or nil, armies = (not hidden) and x.armies or nil }
    end
    table.insert(out.hist, h)
  end
  for _, b in ipairs(S.battles or {}) do
    local text = b.text
    local nm = b.names and b.names.All
    if hidden then
      -- armies that fight see each other's leaders, so after a battle both are known by name
      local an, dn = nm and nm[1] or b.atkSide or "?", nm and nm[2] or b.defSide or "?"
      text = "Battle at " .. tname(b.n) .. ": " .. an .. " (" .. (b.atkSide or "?") .. ") attacked " .. dn .. " (" .. (b.defSide or "?") .. "). " ..
             (b.win and (b.win .. " won") or "Result recorded") .. "."
      local fates = {}
      for _, x in ipairs(b.armies or {}) do
        if x.fate ~= "won" then fates[#fates + 1] = x.nm .. ((x.fate == "Retreated") and " called the retreat" or (" was " .. x.fate)) end
      end
      if #fates > 0 then text = text .. " " .. table.concat(fates, "; ") .. "."
      elseif b.outcome then text = text .. " The loser was " .. b.outcome .. "." end
    end
    local fought = {}
    for _, x in ipairs(b.armies or {}) do
      fought[#fought + 1] = { id = x.id, s = x.s, nm = x.nm, sz = x.sz, cas = x.cas, fate = x.fate, atk = x.atk, fg = x.fg }
    end
    local e = { t = b.t, n = b.n, win = b.win, outcome = b.outcome, atkSide = b.atkSide, defSide = b.defSide, text = text,
                atkSize = b.atkSize, defSize = b.defSize, atkId = b.atkId, defId = b.defId, names = nm,
                armies = (#fought > 0) and fought or nil, garrison = b.garrison,
                atkIds = b.atkIds, defIds = b.defIds, cas = b.cas }
    table.insert(out.battles, e)
  end
  -- per-turn snapshots plus the live state as the last frame
  out.snaps = {}
  -- turns recorded before figures were kept: an army's figure as it is now
  local figNow = {}
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if a.figure and a.figure ~= "" then figNow[a.id] = a.figure end end
  local function cleanSnap(sn)
    local c = { t = sn.t, st = sn.st, o = sn.o, b = sn.b, c = sn.c, a = {}, w = {}, x = sn.x }
    if not c.x then pcall(function() c.x = pastActs(sn.t) end) end   -- turns recorded before actions were kept
    if hidden and c.x then
      -- action notes written before names were kept public: swap in the names everyone knows
      local xs = {}
      for _, x in ipairs(c.x) do
        local y = {}
        for k, v in pairs(x) do y[k] = v end
        if type(y.d) == "string" then for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do y.d = pubMsg(a, y.d) end end
        table.insert(xs, y)
      end
      c.x = xs
    end
    if not hidden then c.q = sn.q end   -- construction (private when hidden information is on)
    for _, x in ipairs(sn.w or {}) do table.insert(c.w, { id = x.id, s = x.s, l = x.l, c = (not hidden) and x.c or nil, sr = (not hidden) and x.sr or nil }) end
    for _, x in ipairs(sn.a or {}) do
      if hidden then
        -- the public only knows an army by its number until its leader is revealed
        local no = x.no or (S.armyNoById and S.armyNoById[tostring(x.id)])
        local pub = x.rv and x.nm or (x.s .. " Army " .. tostring(no or "?"))
        table.insert(c.a, { id = x.id, s = x.s, l = x.l, r = x.r, d = x.d, m = x.m, nm = pub, ld = x.rv and x.ld or nil, fg = x.rv and (x.fg or figNow[x.id]) or nil })
      else table.insert(c.a, { id = x.id, s = x.s, l = x.l, nm = x.nm, sz = x.sz, ld = x.ld, r = x.r, d = x.d, c = x.c, oos = x.oos, m = x.m, up = x.up, fg = x.fg or figNow[x.id] }) end
    end
    return c
  end
  for _, sn in ipairs(S.snaps or {}) do table.insert(out.snaps, cleanSnap(sn)) end
  local live = cleanSnap(publicSnapshot())
  live.live = true
  table.insert(out.snaps, live)
  -- random events, special rules, dice and running totals
  pcall(chronicleBackfill)
  out.events = S.eventLog or {}
  out.rules = {}
  for _, sd in ipairs(SIDES) do
    for _, r in ipairs(rulesFor(sd)) do
      table.insert(out.rules, { s = sd, k = r.key, name = r.name, text = r.text, declare = r.declare, cd = r.cooldown, auto = r.auto,
                                used = S.ruleUsed and S.ruleUsed[sd .. ":" .. r.key] or nil })
    end
  end
  out.ruleLog = S.ruleLog or {}
  out.dice = {}
  for i = math.max(1, #(S.diceLog or {}) - 299), #(S.diceLog or {}) do table.insert(out.dice, S.diceLog[i]) end
  out.totals = (not hidden) and (S.totals or {}) or {}
  out.missive = S.missive
  if not hidden then
    for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
      table.insert(out.armies, { name = a.name, side = a.side, loc = a.loc, size = a.size, leader = a.leader, damage = a.damage or 0,
                                 status = a.status, points = a.roster and a.roster.points or nil })
    end
  end
  return "WCWEB2:" .. b64encode(JSON.encode(out))
end

-- Campaigns from before these were kept: rebuild events, special-rule uses and priority dice from the log, once.
function chronicleBackfill()
  if S.chronBackfilled then return end
  S.chronBackfilled = true
  local ev, rl, dice = {}, {}, {}
  for _, e in ipairs(S.log or {}) do
    local m = e.m or ""
    local roller, roll = string.match(m, "^Random events: (%a+) rolled (%d)")
    if roller then table.insert(ev, { t = e.t, roller = roller, roll = tonumber(roll) }) end
    local side, name, detail = string.match(m, "^(%a+) event %- (.-): (.*)$")
    if side and ev[#ev] and ev[#ev].t == e.t then ev[#ev][side] = { name = name, detail = detail } end
    if string.find(m, "Palant", 1, true) and string.find(m, "wins priority", 1, true) then table.insert(rl, { t = e.t, s = "Good", k = "palantir" }) end
    if string.find(m, "declared the Iron Fist of Angmar", 1, true) then table.insert(rl, { t = e.t, s = "Evil", k = "ironfist" }) end
    if string.find(m, "turned the Eye of Sauron", 1, true) then table.insert(rl, { t = e.t, s = "Evil", k = "eye" }) end
    local pg, pe = string.match(m, "^Priority: Good (%d), Evil (%d)")
    if pg then table.insert(dice, { t = e.t, s = "Good", w = "Priority", n = 6, v = tonumber(pg) }); table.insert(dice, { t = e.t, s = "Evil", w = "Priority", n = 6, v = tonumber(pe) }) end
  end
  -- rebuilt history fills the gaps before the update
  local function merge(old, new, same)
    local out = {}
    for _, x in ipairs(old or {}) do table.insert(out, x) end          -- what was recorded properly wins
    for _, x in ipairs(new) do
      local dup = false
      for _, y in ipairs(old or {}) do if same(x, y) then dup = true end end
      if not dup then table.insert(out, x) end
    end
    table.sort(out, function(a, b) return (a.t or 0) < (b.t or 0) end)
    return out
  end
  S.eventLog = merge(S.eventLog, ev, function(x, y) return x.t == y.t end)
  S.ruleLog = merge(S.ruleLog, rl, function(x, y) return x.t == y.t and x.k == y.k end)
  if not S.diceLog or #S.diceLog == 0 then S.diceLog = dice end
end

-- Base64 keeps the code to plain letters and digits, so nothing in the game's text box can alter it.
local B64 = "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/"
function b64encode(s)
  local out = {}
  local len = #s
  for i = 1, len, 3 do
    local a, b, c = string.byte(s, i, i + 2)
    local n = a * 65536 + (b or 0) * 256 + (c or 0)
    local c1 = math.floor(n / 262144) % 64
    local c2 = math.floor(n / 4096) % 64
    local c3 = math.floor(n / 64) % 64
    local c4 = n % 64
    out[#out + 1] = string.sub(B64, c1 + 1, c1 + 1) .. string.sub(B64, c2 + 1, c2 + 1) ..
                    (b and string.sub(B64, c3 + 1, c3 + 1) or "=") .. (c and string.sub(B64, c4 + 1, c4 + 1) or "=")
  end
  return table.concat(out)
end

local function reportById(id)
  for i, r in ipairs(S.reports or {}) do if tostring(r.id) == tostring(id) then return r, i end end
end

local function battleOpts()
  local opts = { "(no linked battle)" }
  for i = #S.battles, 1, -1 do
    local b = S.battles[i]
    table.insert(opts, "Turn " .. b.t .. " · " .. (NAMES[b.n] or "?") .. (b.win and (" · " .. b.win .. " won") or "") .. "  #" .. i)
  end
  return opts
end

local function multiInput(pk, fld, placeholder, h)
  return el("InputField", { id = inputId(pk, fld), onValueChanged = GP .. "uiPaste", text = uiState(pk).f[fld] or "", placeholder = placeholder,
                            fontSize = 13, preferredHeight = h or 90, flexibleWidth = 1, lineType = "MultiLineNewline",
                            colors = "#F3E7C9|#FFF5DC|#FFF5DC|#B8AC90", textColor = "#2A1C10" })
end

local function reportView(pk, r)
  local wc = (r.winner == "Good" and C.goodInk) or (r.winner == "Evil" and C.evilInk) or C.dim2
  local k = {
    row({ btn(pk, "All reports", { w = 140, h = 30 }, "repback"), spacer(), btn(pk, "Edit", { w = 90, h = 30 }, "repedit", r.id) }, 30),
    txt(r.title ~= "" and r.title or "Untitled battle", { size = 26, style = "Bold", color = C.gold, h = 36 }),
    txt("Turn " .. (r.t or "?") .. "  ·  " .. (r.n and NAMES[r.n] or "?") .. ((r.scenario and r.scenario ~= "") and ("  ·  " .. r.scenario) or ""), { size = 13, color = C.dim, h = 20 }),
    row({ card({ cap("Forces of Good", C.goodInk), txt(r.good or "", { size = 14, wrap = true, ww = (MAIN_W - 60) / 2 }) }, { bg = C.goodBg2, border = "#36593F", spacing = 4 }),
          card({ cap("Forces of Evil", C.evilInk), txt(r.evil or "", { size = 14, wrap = true, ww = (MAIN_W - 60) / 2 }) }, { bg = C.evilBg, border = "#6B3631", spacing = 4 }) }, nil, 14),
    strip(((r.winner == "Draw") and "A draw" or ((r.winner or "?") .. " victory")) .. ((r.result and r.result ~= "") and (" - " .. r.result) or ""), (r.winner == "Draw") and "dim" or "ok", { h = 36 }),
  }
  if r.hero and r.hero ~= "" then table.insert(k, txt("Hero of the battle: " .. r.hero, { size = 14, color = C.goldHi, h = 22 })) end
  if r.cas and r.cas ~= "" then table.insert(k, txt("Casualties: " .. r.cas, { size = 13, color = C.dim2, wrap = true })) end
  if r.story and r.story ~= "" then table.insert(k, cap("The battle", C.gold)); table.insert(k, txt(r.story, { size = 14, wrap = true })) end
  if r.moments and r.moments ~= "" then table.insert(k, cap("Key moments", C.gold)); table.insert(k, txt(r.moments, { size = 14, wrap = true })) end
  table.insert(k, txt("Written by " .. (r.author or "?") .. ". To make an image, copy the web page code (Reports tab) into the campaign page and use Save as image.",
                      { size = 12, color = C.dim, wrap = true }))
  return { card(k, { spacing = 10 }) }
end

local function reportEdit(pk, r)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local all = {}
  for n = 1, #NAMES do table.insert(all, terrOpt(n)) end
  local k = {
    row({ heading(r and "Edit battle report" or "New battle report", { size = 20, h = 30 }), spacer(),
          btn(pk, "Cancel", { w = 90, h = 30 }, "repback") }, 30),
    field("Based on", row({ dropdown(pk, "rpBattle", battleOpts()), btn(pk, "Fill from battle", { w = 140, h = 32, tip = "Copies the turn, territory, sides and result of a recorded battle" }, "repfill") }, 32, 8)),
