    field("Title", input(pk, "rpTitle", "e.g. The Fall of Fornost")),
    field("Territory", dropdown(pk, "rpTerr", all)),
    field("Scenario", input(pk, "rpScenario", "e.g. Domination, Siege")),
    field("Forces of Good", input(pk, "rpGood", "army, leader, points")),
    field("Forces of Evil", input(pk, "rpEvil", "army, leader, points")),
    field("Winner", choice(pk, "rpWinner", { "Good", "Evil", "Draw" }, { default = "Good", w = 90 })),
    field("Result", input(pk, "rpResult", "e.g. Evil army Broken, 8 VP to 3")),
    field("Hero of the battle", input(pk, "rpHero", "who stood out")),
    field("Casualties", input(pk, "rpCas", "e.g. Good lost 9 models, Evil 17")),
    cap("The battle"), multiInput(pk, "rpStory", "Tell the story of the battle...", 120),
    cap("Key moments"), multiInput(pk, "rpMoments", "Heroic actions, duels, turning points...", 70),
    row({ spacer(), btn(pk, "Save report", { w = 200, h = 40, colors = BTN_GOLD }, "repsave") }, 40),
  }
  return { card(k, { spacing = 10 }) }
end

local function tabReports(pk)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local mode = u.rep and u.rep.mode
  if mode == "view" then
    local r = reportById(u.rep.id)
    if r then return reportView(pk, r) end
  elseif mode == "edit" then
    return reportEdit(pk, u.rep.id and reportById(u.rep.id) or nil)
  end
  local out = {}
  table.insert(out, row({ heading("Battle reports", { size = 20, h = 30 }), spacer(),
                          btn(pk, "Web page code", { w = 150, h = 34, colors = u.showWeb and BTN_SEL or BTN, tip = "Code to paste into the campaign web page" }, "webcode"),
                          btn(pk, "+ New report", { w = 150, h = 34, colors = BTN_GOLD }, "repnew") }, 34, 8))
  if u.showWeb then
    local code = webExport()
    table.insert(out, card({
      heading("Campaign web page code", { size = 15 }),
      txt("Click the box, press Ctrl+A then Ctrl+C, and paste it into the campaign page's Update box. " ..
          (S.settings.hidden and "Hidden information is on, so only public information is included." or "Hidden information is off, so armies and stockpiles are included."),
          { size = 12, color = C.dim, wrap = true }),
      el("InputField", { id = inputId(pk, "webCode"), text = code, readOnly = "true", fontSize = 11, preferredHeight = 80, flexibleWidth = 1,
                         lineType = "MultiLineNewline", colors = "#F3E7C9|#FFF5DC|#FFF5DC|#B8AC90", textColor = "#2A1C10" }),
      txt(#code .. " characters", { size = 11, color = C.dim, h = 16 }),
    }, { spacing = 8 }))
  end
  local reps = S.reports or {}
  if #reps == 0 then
    table.insert(out, card({ txt("No reports yet. After a battle, press New report and write it up.", { color = C.dim2 }) }))
  end
  for i = #reps, 1, -1 do
    local r = reps[i]
    local confirm = u.confirm == ("repdel:" .. r.id)
    table.insert(out, row({
      swatch((r.winner == "Good" and C.good) or (r.winner == "Evil" and C.evil) or C.neutral, 4, 56),
      btn(pk, "  " .. (r.title ~= "" and r.title or "Untitled battle") .. "\n  Turn " .. (r.t or "?") .. " · " .. (r.n and NAMES[r.n] or "?") ..
              " · " .. ((r.winner == "Draw") and "Draw" or ((r.winner or "?") .. " won")),
          { h = 56, align = "MiddleLeft", colors = BTN_GHOST, size = 14 }, "repview", r.id),
      btn(pk, "Edit", { w = 70, h = 56 }, "repedit", r.id),
      btn(pk, confirm and "Sure?" or "Delete", { w = 80, h = 56, colors = BTN_DANGER }, "repdel", r.id),
    }, 56, 0))
  end
  return out
end

local TAB_FN = { Turn = tabTurn, History = tabHistory, Reports = tabReports, Territories = tabTerritories, Armies = tabArmies, Agents = tabAgents, Wagons = tabWagons,
                 Battles = tabBattles, Supplies = tabSupplies, Log = tabLog, Settings = tabSettings }

-- ===================================================================
-- panel assembly
-- ===================================================================
-- The chooser list for choice boxes: always in the panel (hidden until needed) with a fixed number of rows, so
-- opening it, or opening it for another box, only changes text and which rows show.
PICK_SLOTS = 64
function pickerModal(pk)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local field = u.picker
  if not field and liveHas and liveHas("wc_pick_" .. pk) then
    return { tag = "Panel", attributes = { id = "wc_pick_" .. pk, active = "false" }, children = {}, _keep = true }
  end
  if field then u.pickLast = field end
  local shown = field or u.pickLast   -- closed: keep the last list (hidden) so closing changes nothing else
  local opts = (shown and u.opts and u.opts[shown]) or {}
  local cur = shown and u.f[shown]
  local kids = {}
  for i = 1, math.max(PICK_SLOTS, #opts) do
    local o = opts[i]
    local b = btn(pk, o and ("  " .. o) or "", { h = 30, size = 14, align = "MiddleLeft", colors = (o ~= nil and o == cur) and BTN_SEL or BTN }, "pick", shown or "-", i)
    if not o then b.attributes.active = "false" end
    table.insert(kids, b)
  end
  local listH = math.max(#opts, 1) * 33
  local inner = el("VerticalLayout", { height = listH, spacing = 3, padding = "0 12 0 0", childForceExpandHeight = "false",
                                       childForceExpandWidth = "true", childAlignment = "UpperLeft" }, kids)
  local sv = el("VerticalScrollView", { preferredHeight = 440, flexibleWidth = 1, color = "#00000000", scrollSensitivity = 40,
                                        verticalScrollbarVisibility = "AutoHideAndExpandViewport", horizontalScrollbarVisibility = "Hidden",
                                        scrollbarColors = "#3A2E21|#6E5530|#8A6A2E|#3A2E21", scrollbarBackgroundColor = "#17110C" }, { inner })
  local box = el("Panel", { rectAlignment = "MiddleCenter", width = 520, height = 560, color = C.bg, outline = "#C9A24F", outlineSize = "2 2" }, {
    el("VerticalLayout", { padding = "18 18 16 14", spacing = 10, childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true" }, {
      txt("Choose one", { size = 16, style = "Bold", color = C.gold, h = 24 }),
      sv,
      row({ spacer(), btn(pk, "Cancel", { w = 120, h = 32 }, "pickclose") }, 32),
    }),
  })
  return el("Panel", { id = "wc_pick_" .. pk, rectAlignment = "MiddleCenter", width = PW, height = PH, color = "#000000B0",
                       active = field and "true" or "false" }, { box })
end

-- Built tab pages, kept per panel while the campaign hasn't changed (STATE_VER counts every change). Going back to
-- a tab then reuses the very same page, which the screen recognises at once (see "_from" in the reconciler), so
-- nothing has to be built or compared again.
STATE_VER, PAGE_CACHE = 0, {}
;(function()
  local function sigOf(v, out, depth)
    if type(v) == "table" then
      if depth > 8 then out[#out + 1] = "~"; return end
      local ks = {}
      for k in pairs(v) do if k ~= "opts" then ks[#ks + 1] = k end end
      table.sort(ks, function(a, b) return tostring(a) < tostring(b) end)
      out[#out + 1] = "{"
      for _, k in ipairs(ks) do out[#out + 1] = tostring(k); out[#out + 1] = "="; sigOf(v[k], out, depth + 1); out[#out + 1] = ";" end
      out[#out + 1] = "}"
    else out[#out + 1] = type(v) == "string" and ("'" .. v) or tostring(v) end
  end
  function pageSig(u) local out = {}; sigOf(u, out, 0); return table.concat(out) end
  function pageCacheGet(pk, sig)
    local c = PAGE_CACHE[pk]
    if not c or c.ver ~= STATE_VER then return nil end
    local e = c.e[sig]
    if not e then return nil end
    local u = uiState(pk)
    u.opts = u.opts or {}
    for k, v in pairs(e.opts) do u.opts[k] = v end
    if PERF and PERF.on then PERF.count("page reused") end
    return e.page
  end
  function pageCachePut(pk, sig, page, opts)
    local c = PAGE_CACHE[pk]
    if not c or c.ver ~= STATE_VER then c = { ver = STATE_VER, e = {}, n = 0 }; PAGE_CACHE[pk] = c end
    if c.n >= 8 then c.e, c.n = {}, 0 end
    if not c.e[sig] then c.n = c.n + 1 end
    c.e[sig] = { page = page, opts = opts or {} }
  end
end)()

-- The panel's current tab as a page: a tab shown before, with nothing changed since, is reused as it was.
function makePage(pk)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local sig = pageSig(u)
  local page = pageCacheGet(pk, sig)
  if page then return page end
  OPT_REC = {}
  local ok, content, noScroll = pcall(TAB_FN[u.tab] or tabTurn, pk)
  if not ok then
    print("War Council " .. tostring(u.tab) .. " tab error: " .. tostring(content))
    content, noScroll = { strip("Display error (" .. tostring(u.tab) .. "): " .. tostring(content), "err", { h = 80 }) }, false
  end
  local bodyKids = noScroll and content or { scroll(content, BODY_H, nil, 14) }
  -- the tab's content is one page; the screen keeps pages it has shown, hidden, so switching back needs no redraw
  page = el("VerticalLayout", { spacing = 14, flexibleHeight = 1, childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true", childAlignment = "UpperLeft" }, bodyKids)
  page._key = tostring(u.tab)
  if ok then
    pageCachePut(pk, sig, page, OPT_REC)
    local after = pageSig(u)   -- building may fill in a default (a choice box's first option): file it under that too
    if after ~= sig then pageCachePut(pk, after, page, OPT_REC) end
  end
  OPT_REC = nil
  return page
end

local function buildPanel(pk)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  -- a closed panel isn't drawn again: the screen keeps it as it was, hidden, until it opens
  if not u.open then return { tag = "Panel", attributes = { id = "wc_panel_" .. pk, active = "false" }, children = {}, _stub = true } end
  applySize()
  -- header
  local sub = "NO CAMPAIGN"
  if S.started then
    sub = "TURN " .. S.turn .. " · " .. string.upper(stageName()) .. " STAGE"
    if S.settings.readyCheck then sub = sub .. "   ·   GOOD " .. (S.ready.Good and "READY" or "WAITING") .. " · EVIL " .. (S.ready.Evil and "READY" or "WAITING") end
  end
  local head = {
    el("VerticalLayout", { preferredWidth = 520, flexibleWidth = 0, preferredHeight = 46, spacing = 2, childForceExpandHeight = "false",
                           childForceExpandWidth = "false", childAlignment = "MiddleLeft" }, {
      hasAsset("wc_wordmark") and el("Image", { image = "wc_wordmark", preferredWidth = 156, preferredHeight = 26, flexibleWidth = 0, preserveAspect = "true" })
        or txt("WAR COUNCIL", { size = 22, style = "Bold", color = C.gold, h = 26 }),
      txt(sub, { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 }),
    }),
    spacer(),
  }
  if pk == "All" or not S.settings.hidden then
    table.insert(head, el("HorizontalLayout", { color = C.card, outline = C.line, outlineSize = "1 -1", padding = "8 4 4 4", spacing = 4, preferredWidth = 250,
                                                flexibleWidth = 0, preferredHeight = 40, childForceExpandWidth = "false", childForceExpandHeight = "true",
                                                childAlignment = "MiddleLeft" }, {
      txt("Acting as", { w = 70, size = 12, color = C.dim }),
      btn(pk, "Good", { w = 80, h = 32, colors = (u.acting == "Good") and BTN_GOOD or BTN_GHOST, textColor = (u.acting ~= "Good") and C.goodInk or nil,
                        style = (u.acting == "Good") and "Bold" or "Normal" }, "acting", "Good"),
      btn(pk, "Evil", { w = 80, h = 32, colors = (u.acting == "Evil") and BTN_EVIL or BTN_GHOST, textColor = (u.acting ~= "Evil") and C.evilInk or nil,
                        style = (u.acting == "Evil") and "Bold" or "Normal" }, "acting", "Evil"),
    }))
  else
    table.insert(head, chip("Playing " .. pk, sideInk(pk), sideBg(pk), { w = 160, h = 36, size = 14 }))
  end
  local un = undoTop(pk)
  table.insert(head, btn(pk, " Undo", { w = 92, h = 36, size = 13, colors = BTN_GHOST, textColor = un and C.dim2 or "#6B5E48", enabled = un ~= nil,
                                       icon = "wc_ic_undo", iconWidth = 16,
                                       tip = un and ("Undo: " .. un.label .. " (Turn " .. un.t .. " " .. (STAGES[un.s] or "") .. ")") or "Nothing to undo" }, "undo"))
  table.insert(head, btn(pk, "Refresh", { w = 92, h = 36, size = 13, colors = BTN_GHOST, textColor = C.dim2,
                                         tip = "Redraw the War Council and recheck battles, map markers and where the models stand" }, "refresh"))
  table.insert(head, btn(pk, "X", { w = 40, h = 36, size = 15, colors = BTN_GHOST, textColor = C.dim2 }, "close"))
  local header = el("HorizontalLayout", { color = C.head, padding = "20 16 9 9", spacing = 14, preferredHeight = HEAD_H, childForceExpandWidth = "false",
                                          childForceExpandHeight = "false", childAlignment = "MiddleLeft" }, head)
  -- nav
  local nav = {}
  for _, t in ipairs(TABS) do
    local on = u.tab == t
    table.insert(nav, row({ swatch(on and "#C9A24F" or C.nav, 3, 40),
                            btn(pk, "   " .. t, { h = 40, size = 15, align = "MiddleLeft", colors = on and BTN_SEL or BTN_NAV,
                                                  textColor = on and C.goldHi or C.dim2, style = on and "Bold" or "Normal",
                                                  icon = "wc_ic_" .. string.lower(t), iconWidth = 18 }, "tab", t) }, 40, 0))
  end
  table.insert(nav, gap(10))
  table.insert(nav, txt(S.started and ("Theatre: " .. (S.settings.theatre or "Eriador")) or "", { size = 11, color = C.dim, h = 16 }))
  table.insert(nav, filler())
  local navCol = el("VerticalLayout", { color = C.nav, padding = "10 10 14 14", spacing = 4, preferredWidth = NAV_W, flexibleWidth = 0,
                                        childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true", childAlignment = "UpperLeft" }, nav)
  local page = makePage(pk)
  -- an empty message keeps the same shape as a message, so a message coming or going only changes text and colour
  local msgRow = strip(u.msg or "", u.msgOk and "ok" or "err", { h = math.max(26, wrapH(u.msg or "", 13, MAIN_W - 40) + 12) })
  if not u.msg then
    msgRow.attributes.color = "#00000000"
    msgRow.children[1].attributes.color = "#00000000"
  end
  local waiting = diceWaiting and diceWaiting() or {}
  if #waiting > 0 then
    local parts, need = {}, {}
    for _, d in ipairs(waiting) do
      table.insert(parts, d.side .. " D" .. d.sides .. " (" .. d.why .. ")")
      if not d.thrown and not d.auto then need[d.side] = true end
    end
    local kids = { strip("Rolling: " .. table.concat(parts, ", ") .. ((next(need) and "  ·  waiting for a throw") or "  ·  landing..."), "warn", { h = 26 }) }
    for _, sd in ipairs(SIDES) do
      if need[sd] and canSee(pk, sd) then table.insert(kids, btn(pk, "Throw for " .. sd, { w = 140, h = 26, size = 12, colors = sideBtn(sd) }, "dthrow", sd)) end
    end
    msgRow = row(kids, 26, 8)
  end
  local pagesEl = el("VerticalLayout", { id = "wc_pages_" .. pk, preferredHeight = BODY_H, spacing = 0, childForceExpandHeight = "true", childForceExpandWidth = "true" }, { page })
  pagesEl._pages = true
  local main = el("VerticalLayout", { padding = "24 24 12 12", spacing = 8, flexibleWidth = 1, childForceExpandHeight = "false",
                                      childForceExpandWidth = "true", childAlignment = "UpperLeft" }, {
    msgRow,
    pagesEl,
  })
  local bodyRow = el("HorizontalLayout", { preferredHeight = PH - HEAD_H, spacing = 0, childForceExpandWidth = "false", childForceExpandHeight = "true" }, { navCol, main })
  local p = el("Panel", { id = "wc_panel_" .. pk, rectAlignment = "MiddleRight", offsetXY = "-10 0", width = PW, height = PH,
                       color = C.bg, outline = C.frame, outlineSize = "2 2", active = u.open and "true" or "false",
                       allowDragging = "true", returnToOriginalPositionWhenReleased = "false" }, {
    el("VerticalLayout", { padding = "0 0 0 0", spacing = 0, childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true" }, { header, bodyRow }),
  })
  table.insert(p.children, pickerModal(pk))
  if visFor(pk) then p.attributes.visibility = visFor(pk) end
  return p
end

-- ===================================================================
-- dispatches pop-up (one per seated player)
-- ===================================================================
local NEWS_CATS = {
  { "war", "VICTORY", C.gold, C.raised },
  { "turn", "TURN SUMMARY", C.gold, C.raised },
  { "captures", "CAPTURES & RAIDS", "#F0A59B", C.evilBg },
  { "territories", "TERRITORIES", C.warn, "#2A2117" },
  { "construction", "CONSTRUCTION FINISHED", "#9FD4AE", C.goodBg2 },
  { "armies", "ARMIES", C.dim2, "#2A2117" },
  { "agents", "AGENTS", C.dim2, "#2A2117" },
  { "routes", "SUPPLY ROUTES", C.dim2, "#2A2117" },
}

local function viewerKey(color)
  if color == S.settings.goodColor then return "Good" end
  if color == S.settings.evilColor then return "Evil" end
  return "All"
end
function viewerKeyOf(color) return viewerKey(color) end

local function seatedColors()
  local out = {}
  local ok, players = pcall(function() return Player.getPlayers() end)
  if ok and players then
    for _, p in ipairs(players) do
      if p.color and p.color ~= "Grey" then table.insert(out, p.color) end
    end
  end
  return out
end

local function buildNews(color)
  if not S.settings.popups or not S.news then return nil end
  if S.settings.newsTiming == "turn" and S.stage ~= 1 then return nil end
  local seen = (S.newsSeen and S.newsSeen[color]) or 0
  local pk = viewerKey(color)
  local items = {}
  for _, e in ipairs(S.news) do
    if e.id > seen and (e.v == nil or canSee(pk, e.v)) then table.insert(items, e) end
  end
  if #items == 0 then return nil end
  local first, last = items[1], items[#items]
  local when = "Turn " .. first.t .. " " .. (STAGES[first.s] or "")
  if first.t ~= last.t or first.s ~= last.s then when = when .. " to Turn " .. last.t .. " " .. (STAGES[last.s] or "") end
  local W = 680
  local lines = {}
  for _, cat in ipairs(NEWS_CATS) do
    local group = {}
    for _, e in ipairs(items) do if e.c == cat[1] then table.insert(group, e) end end
    if #group > 0 then
      table.insert(lines, cap(cat[2] .. "  (" .. #group .. ")", cat[3], { h = 20, size = 12 }))
      for _, e in ipairs(group) do
        local h = wrapH(e.m, 14, W - 90) + 16
        local r = row({ swatch(e.v and sideColor(e.v) or cat[3], 4, h - 16), txt(e.m, { size = 14, h = h - 16 }) }, h, 10)
        r.attributes.color = cat[4]
        r.attributes.padding = "12 12 8 8"
        table.insert(lines, r)
      end
      table.insert(lines, gap(4))
    end
  end
  local listH = math.min(420, stackH(lines, 6, 8))
  local H = listH + 64 + 64 + 20
  local panel = el("Panel", { id = "wc_news_" .. color, rectAlignment = "UpperCenter", offsetXY = "0 -70", width = W, height = H,
                              color = C.bg, outline = "#C9A24F", outlineSize = "2 2", allowDragging = "true",
                              returnToOriginalPositionWhenReleased = "false", visibility = color }, {
    el("VerticalLayout", { padding = "0 0 0 0", spacing = 0, childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true" }, {
      el("HorizontalLayout", { color = C.head, padding = "18 14 10 10", spacing = 10, preferredHeight = 64, childForceExpandWidth = "false", childForceExpandHeight = "false",
                               childAlignment = "MiddleLeft" }, {
        el("VerticalLayout", { flexibleWidth = 1, preferredHeight = 44, spacing = 0, childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true" }, {
          txt("DISPATCHES", { size = 20, style = "Bold", color = C.gold, h = 26 }),
          txt(when .. " · " .. #items .. " update" .. (#items == 1 and "" or "s"), { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 }),
        }),
        btn("All", "X", { w = 36, h = 34, colors = BTN_GHOST, textColor = C.dim2 }, "newsok", color),
      }),
      el("VerticalLayout", { padding = "18 18 14 6", preferredHeight = listH + 20, childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true" }, {
        scroll(lines, listH, nil, 6),
      }),
      el("HorizontalLayout", { color = C.nav, padding = "18 18 12 12", spacing = 10, preferredHeight = 64, childForceExpandWidth = "false",
                               childForceExpandHeight = "true" }, {
        spacer(),
        btn("All", "Open log", { w = 120, h = 40, colors = BTN_GHOST, textColor = C.dim2 }, "newslog", color),
        btn("All", "Dismiss", { w = 140, h = 40, colors = BTN_GOLD }, "newsok", color),
      }),
    }),
  })
  return panel
end

local function buildBattlePrompt(color)
  if not S.started or STAGES[S.stage] ~= "Battle" or S.bpTurn ~= S.turn then return nil end
  if S.bpSeen and S.bpSeen[color] then return nil end
  local list = battleTerritories()
  if #list == 0 then return nil end
  local pk = viewerKey(color)
  local W = 640
  local items = {}
  for _, n in ipairs(list) do
    local here = armiesIn(n)
    local names = {}
    for _, a in ipairs(here) do table.insert(names, armyLabel(pk, a) .. " (" .. a.side .. ")") end
    local siege = S.terr[n].b.fort and S.terr[n].owner ~= "Neutral"
    if siege and #names < 2 then table.insert(names, "the " .. S.terr[n].owner .. " garrison") end
    local k = { txt(terrDot(n) .. (siege and "  ·  siege" or ""), { size = 16, style = "Bold", color = C.gold, h = 24 }),
                txt(table.concat(names, "  vs  "), { size = 14, wrap = true, ww = W - 80 }) }
    local lines = battleModifiers(n, here[1], here[2])
    for i, l in ipairs(lines) do
      if i > 3 then break end
      table.insert(k, txt("·  " .. maskNames(pk, l, here), { size = 12, color = C.dim2, wrap = true, ww = W - 80 }))
    end
    table.insert(items, card(k, { spacing = 4, bg = "#2A2117", border = C.line }))
  end
  local listH = math.min(420, stackH(items, 8, 4))
  local H = listH + 64 + 64 + 24
  return el("Panel", { id = "wc_bp_" .. color, rectAlignment = "MiddleCenter", offsetXY = "0 40", width = W, height = H,
                       color = C.bg, outline = "#C9A24F", outlineSize = "2 2", allowDragging = "true",
                       returnToOriginalPositionWhenReleased = "false", visibility = color }, {
    el("VerticalLayout", { padding = "0 0 0 0", spacing = 0, childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true" }, {
      el("HorizontalLayout", { color = C.head, padding = "18 14 10 10", spacing = 10, preferredHeight = 64, childForceExpandWidth = "false",
                               childForceExpandHeight = "false", childAlignment = "MiddleLeft" }, {
        el("VerticalLayout", { flexibleWidth = 1, preferredHeight = 44, spacing = 0, childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true" }, {
          txt("BATTLES THIS TURN", { size = 20, style = "Bold", color = C.gold, h = 26 }),
          txt("Turn " .. S.turn .. " · " .. #list .. " battle" .. ((#list == 1) and "" or "s") .. " to fight · roll battle priority in the Battles tab", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 }),
        }),
        btn("All", "X", { w = 36, h = 34, colors = BTN_GHOST, textColor = C.dim2 }, "bpok", color),
      }),
      el("VerticalLayout", { padding = "18 18 14 6", preferredHeight = listH + 24, childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true" }, {
        scroll(items, listH, nil, 8),
      }),
      el("HorizontalLayout", { color = C.nav, padding = "18 18 12 12", spacing = 10, preferredHeight = 64, childForceExpandWidth = "false",
                               childForceExpandHeight = "true" }, {
        spacer(),
        btn("All", "Dismiss", { w = 120, h = 40, colors = BTN_GHOST, textColor = C.dim2 }, "bpok", color),
        btn("All", "Open Battles tab", { w = 180, h = 40, colors = BTN_GOLD }, "bpopen", color),
      }),
    }),
  })
end

local function buildToggle(pk)
  local b = el("Button", { id = "wc_toggle_" .. pk, rectAlignment = "LowerRight", offsetXY = "-12 150", width = 160, height = 40,
                        onClick = act(pk, "open"), fontSize = 15, fontStyle = "Bold", colors = BTN_GOLD, textColor = "#1C150F",
                        outline = "#E9C877", outlineSize = "1 -1" }, nil, "War Council")
  if visFor(pk) then b.attributes.visibility = visFor(pk) end
  return b
end


-- ===================================================================
-- big stage banner (everyone sees it for a few seconds when the stage changes)
-- ===================================================================
STAGE_BANNER = nil
function showStageBanner()
  if S.settings.banners == false or not S.started then return end
  local b
  if S.stage == 1 then b = { kicker = "A NEW TURN BEGINS", title = "TURN " .. S.turn }
  else b = { kicker = "TURN " .. S.turn, title = string.upper(stageName() or "") .. " STAGE" } end
  b.fresh = true
  STAGE_BANNER = b
  pcall(function()
    Wait.time(function()
      if STAGE_BANNER ~= b then return end
      pcall(function() UI.hide("wc_banner") end)
      Wait.time(function() if STAGE_BANNER == b then STAGE_BANNER = nil end end, 0.6)
    end, 3.4)
  end)
end

local function buildBanner()
  local b = STAGE_BANNER
  if not b then return nil end
  local attrs = { id = "wc_banner", rectAlignment = "MiddleCenter", offsetXY = "0 230", width = 820, height = 132,
                  color = "#140F0AE6", outline = "#C9A24F", outlineSize = "2 2", showAnimation = "Grow", hideAnimation = "FadeOut", animationDuration = "0.5" }
  b.fresh = false
  return el("Panel", attrs, {
    el("VerticalLayout", { padding = "20 20 16 14", spacing = 2, childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true", childAlignment = "MiddleCenter" }, {
      el("Panel", { color = "#C9A24F", preferredHeight = 2, flexibleWidth = 1 }),
      txt(b.kicker, { size = 16, style = "Bold", color = "#B7A687", h = 24, align = "MiddleCenter" }),
      txt(b.title, { size = 46, style = "Bold", color = C.goldHi, h = 56, align = "MiddleCenter" }),
      el("Panel", { color = "#C9A24F", preferredHeight = 2, flexibleWidth = 1 }),
    }),
  })
end

-- ===================================================================
-- hover info: what's in the territory under each player's pointer (only they see it)
-- ===================================================================
HOVER = {}
local function hoverLines(color, n)
  local pk = viewerKey(color)
  local t = S.terr[n]
  local lines = {}
  local isCap = (n == S.settings.capGood) or (n == S.settings.capEvil)
  table.insert(lines, t.owner .. (isCap and " capital" or "") .. (canSee(pk, t.owner) and t.owner ~= "Neutral" and ("  ·  " .. (t.stock or 0) .. " RP stockpiled") or ""))
  local bs = {}
  for key, has in pairs(t.b or {}) do if has and BUILDING_BY_KEY[key] then table.insert(bs, BUILDING_BY_KEY[key].name) end end
  table.sort(bs)
  if #bs > 0 then table.insert(lines, "Buildings: " .. table.concat(bs, ", ")) end
  for _, q in ipairs(t.q or {}) do
    if canSee(pk, q.side or t.owner) and BUILDING_BY_KEY[q.key] then table.insert(lines, "Building: " .. BUILDING_BY_KEY[q.key].name .. " (" .. q.left .. " turn" .. ((q.left == 1) and "" or "s") .. ")") end
  end
  if t.conq then
    table.insert(lines, t.conq.held and (t.conq.by .. " held back by a fortress") or (t.conq.by .. " conquering " .. t.conq.prog .. "/" .. t.conq.need))
  end
  for _, l in ipairs(actionLines(n)) do table.insert(lines, l[2] .. (l[1] and (" (" .. l[1] .. ")") or "")) end
  for _, a in ipairs(armiesIn(n)) do
    table.insert(lines, "Army: " .. armyLabel(pk, a) .. " (" .. a.side .. ")" .. ((canSee(pk, a.side) and (a.damage or 0) > 0) and ("  -" .. a.damage .. "%") or ""))
  end
  for _, g in ipairs(S.agents or {}) do
    if agentLoc(g) == n and canSee(pk, g.side) then table.insert(lines, (isWagon and isWagon(g) and "Wagon" or "Agent") .. ": " .. (g.kind or g.name or "?") .. " (" .. g.side .. ")") end
  end
  return lines
end

-- The panel is always in the layout (hidden when the pointer isn't over a territory) and is updated in place,
-- so moving the pointer never rebuilds the whole interface (which would interrupt anyone typing).
local function hoverContent(color)
  local n = HOVER[color]
  if S.settings.hoverInfo == false or not n or not S.terr[n] then return nil end
  local lines = hoverLines(color, n)
  return n .. " · " .. (NAMES[n] or tname(n)), table.concat(lines, "\n"), 44 + #lines * 21 + 14, #lines * 21
end

local function buildHover(color)
  local title, body, h, bh = hoverContent(color)
  local t = txt(title or "", { size = 17, style = "Bold", color = C.gold, h = 24 })
  t.attributes.id = "wc_hover_" .. color .. "_t"
  local b = txt(body or "", { size = 14, color = C.ink, h = bh or 21 })
  b.attributes.id = "wc_hover_" .. color .. "_b"
  return el("Panel", { id = "wc_hover_" .. color, visibility = color, rectAlignment = "UpperLeft", offsetXY = "16 -96", width = 380, height = h or 80,
                       color = "#17110CE6", outline = "#6E5530", outlineSize = "1 1", raycastTarget = "false", active = title and "true" or "false" }, {
    el("VerticalLayout", { padding = "14 14 10 10", spacing = 4, childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true" }, { t, b }),
  })
end

local function updateHover(color)
  local id = "wc_hover_" .. color
  local title, body, h, bh = hoverContent(color)
  pcall(function()
    if not title then UI.setAttribute(id, "active", "false"); return end
    UI.setValue(id .. "_t", title)
    UI.setValue(id .. "_b", body)
    UI.setAttribute(id .. "_b", "preferredHeight", tostring(bh))
    UI.setAttribute(id, "height", tostring(h))
    UI.setAttribute(id, "active", "true")
  end)
end

local hoverBusy = false
HOVER_AT = {}
function hoverTick()
  if hoverBusy or not S or not S.started then return end
  hoverBusy = true
  local ok, err = pcall(function()
    local b = getBoard()
    local on = S.settings.hoverInfo ~= false
    for _, color in ipairs(seatedColors()) do
      local n = nil
      if on and b then
        local okP, pos = pcall(function() return Player[color].getPointerPosition() end)
        if okP and pos then
          local last = HOVER_AT[color]
          if last and (pos.x - last.x) ^ 2 + (pos.z - last.z) ^ 2 < 0.04 then
            n = HOVER[color]   -- the pointer hasn't really moved: same territory
          else
            local okT, r = pcall(function() return b.call("territoryAt", { position = pos }) end)
            if okT then n = r; HOVER_AT[color] = { x = pos.x, z = pos.z } end
          end
        end
      end
      if HOVER[color] ~= n then HOVER[color] = n; updateHover(color) end
    end
  end)
  hoverBusy = false
  if not ok then print("War Council hover info: " .. tostring(err)) end
end

-- ===================================================================
-- campaign summary (shown to everyone when the war is won; can be opened any time)
-- ===================================================================
END_SEEN, SHOW_SUMMARY = {}, {}
function campaignStats()
  local st = { owned = { Good = 0, Evil = 0, Neutral = 0 }, rp = { Good = 0, Evil = 0 }, armies = { Good = 0, Evil = 0 },
               wins = { Good = 0, Evil = 0 }, draws = 0, battles = #(S.battles or {}), peak = { Good = 0, Evil = 0 } }
  for _, t in ipairs(S.terr) do
    st.owned[t.owner] = (st.owned[t.owner] or 0) + 1
    if st.rp[t.owner] then st.rp[t.owner] = st.rp[t.owner] + (t.stock or 0) end
  end
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do st.armies[a.side] = (st.armies[a.side] or 0) + 1 end
  local where = {}
  for _, b in ipairs(S.battles or {}) do
    if st.wins[b.win] then st.wins[b.win] = st.wins[b.win] + 1 else st.draws = st.draws + 1 end
    if b.n then where[b.n] = (where[b.n] or 0) + 1 end
  end
  local topN, topC = nil, 0
  for n, c in pairs(where) do if c > topC then topN, topC = n, c end end
  st.contested = topN and (tname(topN) .. " (" .. topC .. " battle" .. ((topC == 1) and "" or "s") .. ")") or "-"
  for _, row in ipairs(S.hist or {}) do
    for _, side in ipairs({ "Good", "Evil" }) do
      if row[side] and row[side].terr and row[side].terr > st.peak[side] then st.peak[side] = row[side].terr end
    end
  end
  local heroes = {}
  for _, r in ipairs(S.reports or {}) do
    local h = r.hero and string.match(r.hero, "%S") and r.hero or nil
    if h then heroes[h] = (heroes[h] or 0) + 1 end
  end
  local hn, hc = nil, 0
  for h, c in pairs(heroes) do if c > hc then hn, hc = h, c end end
  st.hero = hn and (hn .. ((hc > 1) and (" (" .. hc .. " battles)") or "")) or "-"
  return st
end

local function buildSummary(color)
  local show = SHOW_SUMMARY[color] or (S.winner and not END_SEEN[color])
  if not show or not S.started then return nil end
  local st = campaignStats()
  local W, H = 860, 600
  local won = S.winner
  local head = won and (string.upper(won) .. " WINS THE CAMPAIGN") or ("THE WAR SO FAR")
  local sub = won and ("After " .. S.turn .. " turn" .. ((S.turn == 1) and "" or "s") .. ", " .. OTHER[won] .. " has no territory left to hold.") or ("Turn " .. S.turn .. "  ·  " .. (stageName() or "") .. " stage")
  if won and S.winnerNote then sub = S.winnerNote end
  local function sideCol(side, label)
    local c = (side == "Good") and C.good or C.evil
    return el("VerticalLayout", { color = (side == "Good") and C.goodBg or C.evilBg, padding = "16 16 12 12", spacing = 4, flexibleWidth = 1,
                                  childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true" }, {
      txt(label, { size = 20, style = "Bold", color = c, h = 28 }),
      txt(st.owned[side] .. " territories held  ·  peak " .. math.max(st.peak[side], st.owned[side]), { size = 14, h = 20 }),
      txt(st.wins[side] .. " battle" .. ((st.wins[side] == 1) and "" or "s") .. " won", { size = 14, h = 20 }),
      txt(st.armies[side] .. " arm" .. ((st.armies[side] == 1) and "y" or "ies") .. " in the field  ·  " .. st.rp[side] .. " RP stockpiled", { size = 14, h = 20 }),
    })
  end
  local cols = row({ sideCol("Good", "Forces of Good"), sideCol("Evil", "Forces of Evil") }, 128, 14)
  local facts = row({
    tile(S.turn, "Turns fought", { size = 24 }),
    tile(st.battles, "Battles", { size = 24, sub = (st.draws > 0) and (st.draws .. " without a victor") or nil }),
    tile(st.owned.Neutral, "Still neutral", { size = 24 }),
  }, nil, 10)
  local facts2 = row({ tile(st.contested, "Most fought over", { size = 16 }), tile(st.hero, "Hero of the war", { size = 16 }) }, nil, 10)
  local headColor = won and ((won == "Good") and C.good or C.evil) or C.goldHi
  -- it only animates in the first time; later redraws leave it where it is
  SUMMARY_SHOWN = SUMMARY_SHOWN or {}
  local anim = (not SUMMARY_SHOWN[color]) and (won and "Grow" or "FadeIn") or "None"
  SUMMARY_SHOWN[color] = true
  return el("Panel", { id = "wc_summary_" .. color, visibility = color, rectAlignment = "MiddleCenter", width = W, height = H, color = C.bg,
                       outline = "#C9A24F", outlineSize = "2 2", showAnimation = anim, allowDragging = "true",
                       returnToOriginalPositionWhenReleased = "false" }, {
    el("VerticalLayout", { padding = "28 28 22 18", spacing = 12, childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true" }, {
      txt(won and "THE WAR IS OVER" or "CAMPAIGN SUMMARY", { size = 15, style = "Bold", color = C.dim, h = 20, align = "MiddleCenter" }),
      txt(head, { size = 38, style = "Bold", color = headColor, h = 50, align = "MiddleCenter" }),
      txt(sub, { size = 15, color = C.dim2, h = 22, align = "MiddleCenter" }),
      el("Panel", { color = "#6E5530", preferredHeight = 1, flexibleWidth = 1 }),
      cols, facts, facts2,
      filler(),
      row({ spacer(), btn("All", "Open History", { w = 150, h = 40, colors = BTN_GHOST, textColor = C.dim2 }, "sumhist", color),
            btn("All", "Close", { w = 150, h = 40, colors = BTN_GOLD }, "sumok", color) }, 40, 10),
    }),
  })
end

-- ===================================================================
-- Secret orders: a small panel only the seated player of each side can see
-- ===================================================================
SECRET_UI = {}
do
local function seatSideOf(color)
  if color == S.settings.goodColor then return "Good" end
  if color == S.settings.evilColor then return "Evil" end
end
local SBTN = "#3A2C4A|#4A3A5E|#2C2238|#241D2A"
local function sbtn(color, label, w, enabled, tip, ...)
  local parts = { color }
  for _, v in ipairs({ ... }) do table.insert(parts, tostring(v)) end
  return el("Button", { onClick = GP .. "secretUi(" .. table.concat(parts, "~") .. ")", fontSize = 12, textColor = enabled and C.ink or "#7D6F57",
                        colors = SBTN, preferredHeight = 28, preferredWidth = w or -1, flexibleWidth = w and 0 or 1,
                        interactable = enabled and "true" or "false", tooltip = tip or "" }, nil, label)
end

function buildSecret(color)
  local side = seatSideOf(color)
  if not side or not S.started then return nil end
  local st = SECRET_UI[color] or {}
  SECRET_UI[color] = st
  local mine = 0
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do if a.side == side and a.order and a.order.secret then mine = mine + 1 end end
  mine = mine + #secretLines(side)
  local out = {}
  table.insert(out, el("Button", { id = "wc_secret_btn_" .. color, visibility = color, rectAlignment = "LowerRight", offsetXY = "-12 196", width = 160, height = 34,
                                   onClick = GP .. "secretUi(" .. color .. "~toggle)", fontSize = 13, fontStyle = "Bold", colors = SBTN, textColor = "#E6DAF5" },
                       nil, "Secret orders" .. ((mine > 0) and (" (" .. mine .. ")") or "")))
  if not st.open then return out end
  local W = 470
  local k = {}
  table.insert(k, row({ txt("SECRET ORDERS", { size = 15, style = "Bold", color = "#CDB8F0", h = 22 }), txt("only you can see this", { size = 11, color = C.dim, h = 22, align = "MiddleRight" }),
                        sbtn(color, "X", 30, true, "Close", "toggle") }, 24, 6))
  if st.msg then table.insert(k, txt(st.msg, { size = 12, color = st.ok and C.ok or C.warn, wrap = true, ww = W - 30 })) end
  local inLog = STAGES[S.stage] == "Logistics" or S.settings.override
  if not inLog then table.insert(k, txt("Orders can be given in the Logistics stage.", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 })) end
  local any = false
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
    if a.side == side and a.status ~= "building" then
      any = true
      table.insert(k, txt(a.name .. "  ·  " .. NAMES[a.loc], { size = 13, style = "Bold", color = C.gold, h = 20 }))
      local o = a.order
      if o and o.secret then
        table.insert(k, row({ txt(ACTIONS[o.k].name .. " ordered: completes at the end of the turn" .. (o.revealed and " (discovered)" or ""), { size = 12, color = C.ink, h = 28 }),
                              sbtn(color, "Cancel", 70, true, "Call it off (gives back its move if it hadn't moved)", "cancel", a.id) }, 28, 6))
      elseif o then
        table.insert(k, txt("Busy: " .. ACTIONS[o.k].verb .. " (public order)", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 }))
      else
        local kids = {}
        for _, key in ipairs(SECRET_ACTIONS) do
          local okA = actionAllowed(a, key)
          table.insert(kids, sbtn(color, ACTIONS[key].name, nil, okA == true, ACTIONS[key].rules .. ((okA ~= true) and ("\n" .. okA) or ""), key, a.id))
        end
        table.insert(k, row(kids, 28, 6))
      end
    end
  end
  if not any then table.insert(k, txt("No armies ready to act.", { size = 12, color = C.dim, h = 18 })) end
  local lines = secretLines(side)
  if #lines > 0 then
    table.insert(k, txt("IN PLACE", { size = 11, style = "Bold", color = C.dim, h = 16 }))
    for _, l in ipairs(lines) do table.insert(k, txt(l, { size = 12, h = 18 })) end
  end
  local log = (S.secretLog or {})[side] or {}
  if #log > 0 then
    table.insert(k, txt("RECENT", { size = 11, style = "Bold", color = C.dim, h = 16 }))
    for i = math.max(1, #log - 3), #log do table.insert(k, txt("T" .. log[i].t .. ": " .. log[i].m, { size = 11, color = C.dim2, wrap = true, ww = W - 30 })) end
  end
  table.insert(k, txt("Discovered by an enemy army entering, an enemy Scout there, or an enemy Scout Tower next to it.", { size = 11, color = C.dim, wrap = true, ww = W - 30 }))
  local h = 24
  for _, c in ipairs(k) do h = h + hOf(c) + 6 end
  table.insert(out, el("Panel", { id = "wc_secret_" .. color, visibility = color, rectAlignment = "LowerRight", offsetXY = "-12 240", width = W, height = h,
                                  color = "#15101CF0", outline = "#5E4A7A", outlineSize = "1 1" }, {
    el("VerticalLayout", { padding = "12 12 10 10", spacing = 6, childForceExpandHeight = "false", childForceExpandWidth = "true" }, k) }))
  return out
end

function secretUi(player, arg)
  local parts = {}
  for p in string.gmatch(arg or "", "[^~]+") do table.insert(parts, p) end
  local color, a, id = parts[1], parts[2], tonumber(parts[3])
  if not color or (player and player.color ~= color) then return end
  local side = seatSideOf(color)
  if not side then return end
  local st = SECRET_UI[color] or {}
  SECRET_UI[color] = st
  st.msg = nil
  if a == "toggle" then st.open = not st.open
  elseif a == "cancel" then
    local ar = armyById(id)
    if ar and ar.side == side and ar.order and ar.order.secret then
      cancelOrder(ar, "called off", true); st.msg, st.ok = "Order cancelled.", true
    end
  elseif ACTIONS[a] and ACTIONS[a].secret then
    local ar = armyById(id)
    if ar and ar.side == side then
      local r = orderAction(ar, a)
      if r == true then st.msg, st.ok = ACTIONS[a].name .. " ordered for " .. ar.name .. ".", true else st.msg, st.ok = r, false end
    end
  end
  rebuild()
end
end

-- onlyPk: just that panel changed (switching tab, picking something): the rest of the screen is left alone.
-- Otherwise the campaign changed: the panel of whoever acted (ACT_PK) is redrawn at once, the other panels one per
-- frame after it, then the pop-ups - so the work is spread out instead of landing in one frame.
BUILT_VER, ACT_PK = {}, nil
;(function()
  local queued = false
  -- which panels someone at the table can see right now (if that can't be told, all of them)
  function panelsWatched()
    local out, any = {}, false
    local ok = pcall(function()
      for _, p in ipairs(Player.getPlayers()) do
        if p.color then out[viewerKey(p.color)] = true; any = true end
      end
    end)
    if not ok or not any then return { Good = true, Evil = true, All = true } end
    return out
  end
  local function base()
    local xml = {}
    for _, e in ipairs(baseXml) do table.insert(xml, e) end
    return xml
  end
  local function panelsNow(list, noFull)
    local xml = base()
    for _, pk in ipairs(list) do
      table.insert(xml, buildToggle(pk))
      table.insert(xml, buildPanel(pk))
      BUILT_VER[pk] = STATE_VER
    end
    if applyXml(xml, #baseXml, true, noFull) == "full" then
      for _, pk in ipairs(list) do BUILT_VER[pk] = nil end
      return false
    end
    return true
  end
  local function popups()
    local xml = base()
    if S.started then
      for _, color in ipairs(seatedColors()) do
        local ok, p = pcall(buildNews, color)
        if ok and p then table.insert(xml, p) end
        local ok2, bp = pcall(buildBattlePrompt, color)
        if ok2 and bp then table.insert(xml, bp) end
        if S.settings.hoverInfo ~= false then
          local ok3, hv = pcall(buildHover, color)
          if ok3 and hv then table.insert(xml, hv) end
        end
        local ok4, sm = pcall(buildSummary, color)
        if ok4 and sm then table.insert(xml, sm) end
        local ok5, sc = pcall(buildSecret, color)
        if ok5 and sc then for _, e in ipairs(sc) do table.insert(xml, e) end
        elseif not ok5 then print("War Council secret orders: " .. tostring(sc)) end
      end
    end
    local okB, bn = pcall(buildBanner)
    if okB and bn then table.insert(xml, bn) end
    return xml
  end
  -- everything at once (first draw, or after the screen was reset)
  local function allNow()
    local xml = base()
    for _, pk in ipairs(panelKeys()) do
      table.insert(xml, buildToggle(pk))
      table.insert(xml, buildPanel(pk))
      BUILT_VER[pk] = STATE_VER
    end
    local px = popups()
    for i = #baseXml + 1, #px do table.insert(xml, px[i]) end
    applyXml(xml, #baseXml)
    pcall(lookAll)
  end
  local rest
  rest = function()
    local done = true
    local ok, err = pcall(function()
      local stale, closed = nil, {}
      local watched = panelsWatched()
      for _, pk in ipairs(panelKeys()) do
        if BUILT_VER[pk] ~= STATE_VER and watched[pk] then   -- a panel nobody can see waits until someone sits there
          if uiState(pk).open then stale = stale or pk else table.insert(closed, pk) end
        end
      end
      if stale then table.insert(closed, stale) end
      -- a panel whose layout changed needs a full redraw: then everything is redrawn at once, in one go
      if #closed > 0 and not panelsNow(closed, true) then allNow(); return end
      if stale then done = false; return end
      local xml = popups()
      if applyXml(xml, #baseXml, "popups", true) == "full" then allNow(); return end
      pcall(lookAll)
    end)
    if not ok then print("War Council redraw: " .. tostring(err)); done = true end
    if done then queued = false else Wait.frames(rest, 1) end
  end
  function rebuild(onlyPk)
    loadAssets()
    if onlyPk then
      panelsNow({ onlyPk })
      return
    end
    STATE_VER = STATE_VER + 1
    if liveTree() == nil then queued = false; allNow(); return end
    if ACT_PK and not panelsNow({ ACT_PK }, true) then allNow(); return end
    if not queued then queued = true; Wait.frames(rest, 1) end
  end
end)()

-- Tabs prepared in the background (see mergePages): after a change, once nobody has clicked for a moment and a
-- half, one tab every half second is built and checked; tabs that changed shape are sent in one redraw at the end.
PREP = { ver = -1, queue = {}, merge = {}, idle = 0, n = 0 }
function prewarmNote() PREP.idle = 0 end
function prewarmTick()
  if not S or not S.started or DICE.busy or QUIET or S.settings.prewarm == false then return end
  PREP.idle = PREP.idle + 1
  if PREP.idle < 6 or not liveTree() then return end
  PREP.n = PREP.n + 1
  if PREP.n % 2 == 1 then return end
  -- only after the stage (or turn) moved on: that's when most tabs change shape, and the stage banner covers the
  -- moment; a redraw after every single action would cost more than it saves
  local stageKey = S.turn .. ":" .. S.stage
  if PREP.ver ~= STATE_VER and (PREP.stageKey ~= stageKey or #PREP.queue > 0 or #PREP.merge > 0) then
    PREP.stageKey = stageKey
    PREP.ver, PREP.queue, PREP.merge = STATE_VER, {}, {}
    local watched = panelsWatched()
    for _, pk in ipairs(panelKeys()) do
      local u = uiState(pk)
      if watched[pk] and u.open then
        for _, key in ipairs(livePageKeys(pk)) do
          if key ~= u.tab and TAB_FN[key] then table.insert(PREP.queue, { pk = pk, tab = key }) end
        end
      end
    end
  end
  local item = table.remove(PREP.queue, 1)
  if item then
    local u = uiState(item.pk)
    if not u.open or u.tab == item.tab then return end
    local was = u.tab
    u.tab = item.tab
    local ok, page = pcall(makePage, item.pk)
    u.tab = was
    if ok and page then
      local okF, fits = pcall(pageFits, item.pk, page)
      if okF and not fits then table.insert(PREP.merge, { pk = item.pk, page = page }) end
    end
    return
  end
  -- one combined redraw only pays off when it saves at least two later ones
  if #PREP.merge >= 2 then
    local list = PREP.merge
    PREP.merge = {}
    pcall(mergePages, list)
  else
    PREP.merge = {}
  end
end

-- Rebuilding the whole interface makes every open panel flicker for everyone, closes dropdowns, resets scrolling and
-- snaps dragged panels back. So the War Council keeps a copy of what is on screen and only sends what changed:
--  * text, colours, buttons and such are updated in place;
--  * lists keep a few hidden spare rows, so they can grow or shrink by showing and hiding rows;
--  * each panel keeps the tabs it has shown as hidden pages, so switching tab just swaps which page is visible;
--  * pop-ups that close stay in the layout, hidden, ready to come back.
-- Only a layout the screen has never had (a new tab, a list that grew past its spares) needs a full redraw, and
-- after that it is remembered.
;(function()   -- its own function, so its helpers don't count against the main chunk's 200 locals
local LIVE = nil
local sendFull
local MAX_PAGES = 8
local SPARES = 2
local pageSeq, clock, retry = 0, 0, false

local function baseId(id) return (string.gsub(id or "", "~.*$", "")) end
local function shape(n)
  if n._shape then return n._shape end
  local parts = { n.tag or "?" }
  for _, c in ipairs(n.children or {}) do table.insert(parts, "(" .. shape(c) .. ")") end
  n._shape = table.concat(parts)
  return n._shape
end
local function copy(n)
  local a = {}
  for k, v in pairs(n.attributes or {}) do a[k] = v end
  local kids = {}
  for _, c in ipairs(n.children or {}) do table.insert(kids, copy(c)) end
  local out = { tag = n.tag, attributes = a, children = kids, value = n.value }
  out._pages, out._key, out._xid, out._spare, out._used, out._prep = n._pages, n._key, n._xid, n._spare, n._used, n._prep
  return out
end
local function plain(n)
  local a = {}
  for k, v in pairs(n.attributes or {}) do a[k] = v end
  local kids = {}
  for _, c in ipairs(n.children or {}) do table.insert(kids, plain(c)) end
  return { tag = n.tag, attributes = a, children = kids, value = n.value }
end
local function on(n) return (n.attributes.active or "true") ~= "false" end

-- Give a fresh subtree ids (by position, or its own id plus the page it lives on) and spare rows for its lists.
local prepare
local function preparePages(n)
  local pg = n.children[1]
  n.children = {}
  if pg then
    pageSeq = pageSeq + 1
    prepare(pg, n.attributes.id .. "_p" .. pageSeq, "~p" .. pageSeq)
    pg._used = clock
    table.insert(n.children, pg)
  end
end
prepare = function(n, path, suffix)
  n._prep = true
  n.attributes = n.attributes or {}
  for k, v in pairs(n.attributes) do n.attributes[k] = tostring(v) end
  if n.attributes.id then
    n._xid = n._xid or baseId(n.attributes.id)
    if suffix and not string.find(n.attributes.id, "~p%d+$") then n.attributes.id = n.attributes.id .. suffix end
  else
    n.attributes.id = path
  end
  if n._pages then preparePages(n); return end
  local kids = n.children or {}
  n.children = kids
  local spares = 0
  for k = #kids, 1, -1 do if kids[k]._spare then spares = spares + 1 else break end end
  local lastReal = #kids - spares
  if spares < SPARES and lastReal >= 2 and shape(kids[lastReal]) == shape(kids[lastReal - 1]) then
    for _ = spares + 1, SPARES do
      local c = copy(kids[lastReal])
      local function noIds(x) x.attributes.id = x._xid; x._prep = nil; for _, y in ipairs(x.children) do noIds(y) end end
      noIds(c)
      c.attributes.active = "false"
      c._spare = true
      table.insert(kids, c)
    end
  end
  for i, c in ipairs(kids) do prepare(c, n.attributes.id .. "_" .. i, suffix) end
end

-- Can the live node show the new one with attribute changes only? Collects those changes into ops.
local matchPages
local match
local function matchInner(live, new, ops)
  if new._keep then
    -- "leave it as it is", just shown or hidden
    if live.tag ~= new.tag or baseId(live.attributes.id) ~= baseId(new.attributes.id) then return false end
    local want = (new.attributes.active == "false") and "false" or "true"
    if (live.attributes.active or "true") ~= want then table.insert(ops, { node = live, attrs = { active = want } }) end
    return true
  end
  if live._from == new then
    -- the very same layout this node last showed (a reused page): nothing inside can differ, only whether it's shown
    local want = (tostring((new.attributes or {}).active) == "false") and "false" or "true"
    if (live.attributes.active or "true") ~= want then table.insert(ops, { node = live, attrs = { active = want } }) end
    return true
  end
  if live.tag ~= new.tag then return false end
  local la, na = live.attributes, new.attributes or {}
  if na.id and baseId(na.id) ~= baseId(la.id) then return false end
  -- the same set of attributes both ways (a node never gains one it can't later lose): every new one exists on the
  -- live node, and both have as many
  local ch, nn = nil, 0
  for k, v in pairs(na) do
    if k ~= "id" and k ~= "active" then
      local lv = la[k]
      if lv == nil then return false end
      nn = nn + 1
      if type(v) ~= "string" then v = tostring(v) end
      if lv ~= v then ch = ch or {}; ch[k] = v end
    end
  end
  local ln = live._nk
  if not ln then
    ln = 0
    for k in pairs(la) do if k ~= "id" and k ~= "active" then ln = ln + 1 end end
    live._nk = ln   -- attribute names never change on a live node, only values
  end
  if ln ~= nn then return false end
  local want = (tostring(na.active) == "false") and "false" or "true"
  if (la.active or "true") ~= want then ch = ch or {}; ch.active = want end
  if ch then table.insert(ops, { node = live, attrs = ch }) end
  if (live.value or "") ~= (new.value or "") then table.insert(ops, { node = live, value = new.value or "" }) end
  if live._pages then return matchPages(live, new, ops) end
  -- children: each new child finds its counterpart in order; live children passed over are hidden (so an optional
  -- row or badge that isn't wanted now simply disappears). Quick in-order pass first; if that can't place everyone,
  -- a full search for an order-keeping assignment.
  local lk, nk = live.children or {}, new.children or {}
  if #nk > #lk then return false end
  -- pairs already compared in the quick pass are remembered for the full search: comparing them again would double
  -- the work at every level of nesting
  local memoCan = {}
  do
    local quick, j, okq = {}, 1, true
    for i = 1, #nk do
      local found = false
      while j <= #lk and (#lk - j) >= (#nk - i) do
        local tmp = {}
        local okm = match(lk[j], nk[i], tmp)
        memoCan[i * 4096 + j] = okm and tmp or false
        if okm then
          quick[#quick + 1] = { list = tmp }
          j = j + 1; found = true; break
        end
        if on(lk[j]) then table.insert(quick, { node = lk[j], attrs = { active = "false" } }) end
        j = j + 1
      end
      if not found then okq = false; break end
    end
    if okq then
      ops[#ops + 1] = { list = quick }
      for k = j, #lk do if on(lk[k]) then table.insert(ops, { node = lk[k], attrs = { active = "false" } }) end end
      return true
    end
  end
  local memoF = {}
  local function can(i, j)
    local key = i * 4096 + j
    local v = memoCan[key]
    if v == nil then local t = {}; v = match(lk[j], nk[i], t) and t or false; memoCan[key] = v end
    return v
  end
  local function fits(i, j)
    if i > #nk then return true end
    if (#lk - j + 1) < (#nk - i + 1) then return false end
    local key = i * 4096 + j
    if memoF[key] == nil then memoF[key] = (can(i, j) and fits(i + 1, j + 1)) or fits(i, j + 1) or false end
    return memoF[key]
  end
  if not fits(1, 1) then
    return false
  end
  local i, j = 1, 1
  while i <= #nk do
    local c = can(i, j)
    if c and fits(i + 1, j + 1) then
      ops[#ops + 1] = { list = c }
      i = i + 1
    elseif on(lk[j]) then
      table.insert(ops, { node = lk[j], attrs = { active = "false" } })
    end
    j = j + 1
  end
  for k = j, #lk do if on(lk[k]) then table.insert(ops, { node = lk[k], attrs = { active = "false" } }) end end
  return true
end
-- a node that matched remembers which layout it now shows (applied with the other changes)
match = matchInner
matchPages = function(live, new, ops)
  local np = (new.children or {})[1]
  if not np then return false end
  local hit
  for _, lp in ipairs(live.children) do
    if lp._key == np._key then
      local tmp = {}
      if match(lp, np, tmp) then
        hit = lp
        -- the page remembers which layout it now shows (applied with the other changes)
        if lp._from ~= np then tmp[#tmp + 1] = { node = lp, from = np } end
        ops[#ops + 1] = { list = tmp }
        break
      end
    end
  end
  if not hit then FULL_WHY = "page " .. tostring(np._key) .. " in " .. tostring(live.attributes.id); return false end
  hit._used = clock
  for _, lp in ipairs(live.children) do
    if lp ~= hit and on(lp) then table.insert(ops, { node = lp, attrs = { active = "false" } }) end
  end
  return true
end

-- When something has to be redrawn anyway, the new layout is merged with the old one: anything the old one had
-- that the new one doesn't is kept, hidden. The result can show either, so going back needs no redraw.
local function freshCopy(n, hide)
  local c = copy(n)
  local function reset(x)
    -- ids handed out by position are dropped (they're handed out again); a node's own id is kept, without its page mark
    if x._prep then x.attributes.id = x._xid end
    x._prep, x._shape = nil, nil
    for _, y in ipairs(x.children) do reset(y) end
  end
  reset(c)
  if hide then c.attributes.active = "false" end
  return c
end
local function sameId(o, n)
  local nid = n.attributes and n.attributes.id
  if not nid then return o._xid == nil end
  return (o._xid or baseId(o.attributes.id)) == baseId(nid)
end
local function keySig(n)
  if n._ks then return n._ks end   -- a node's attribute names never change (only their values), so remember it
  local ks = {}
  for k in pairs(n.attributes or {}) do if k ~= "id" and k ~= "active" then table.insert(ks, k) end end
  table.sort(ks)
  n._ks = table.concat(ks, ",")
  return n._ks
end
local function mergeNode(old, new)
  if old.tag ~= new.tag or not sameId(old, new) then return nil end
  if new._keep then
    local c = freshCopy(old)
    c.attributes.active = (new.attributes.active == "false") and "false" or "true"
    return c
  end
  local out = { tag = new.tag, attributes = {}, children = {}, value = new.value, _key = new._key, _pages = new._pages }
  for k, v in pairs(new.attributes or {}) do out.attributes[k] = tostring(v) end
  if new._pages then
    if new.children[1] then out.children = { freshCopy(new.children[1]) } end
    return out
  end
  -- line the two lists of children up (longest common run of matching children), then interleave: matching
  -- pairs are merged, old-only children stay (hidden), new-only children are added, all in an order both share
  local ok, nk = {}, new.children or {}
  for _, c in ipairs(old.children or {}) do if not c._spare then table.insert(ok, c) end end
  local function same(a, b) return a.tag == b.tag and sameId(a, b) and keySig(a) == keySig(b) end
  local L = {}
  for i = #ok + 1, 1, -1 do L[i] = {} end
  for i = #ok + 1, 1, -1 do
    for j = #nk + 1, 1, -1 do
      if i > #ok or j > #nk then L[i][j] = 0
      elseif same(ok[i], nk[j]) then L[i][j] = L[i + 1][j + 1] + 1
      else L[i][j] = math.max(L[i + 1][j], L[i][j + 1]) end
    end
  end
  local i, j = 1, 1
  while i <= #ok or j <= #nk do
    if i <= #ok and j <= #nk and same(ok[i], nk[j]) and L[i][j] == L[i + 1][j + 1] + 1 then
      table.insert(out.children, mergeNode(ok[i], nk[j]) or freshCopy(nk[j])); i = i + 1; j = j + 1
    elseif j <= #nk and (i > #ok or L[i][j + 1] >= L[i + 1][j]) then
      table.insert(out.children, freshCopy(nk[j])); j = j + 1
    else
      table.insert(out.children, freshCopy(ok[i], true)); i = i + 1
    end
  end
  return out
end

-- A top-level element that has to be rebuilt keeps the pages its old self had cached.
local function findPages(n, out)
  if n._pages then out[n.attributes.id] = n end
  for _, c in ipairs(n.children or {}) do findPages(c, out) end
  return out
end
local function rebuildTop(new, old)
  local fresh = (old and mergeNode(old, new)) or freshCopy(new)
  local oldPages = old and findPages(old, {}) or {}
  -- a tab's new page is merged with the page it had before, rather than kept as a second copy
  local used = {}
  for id, np in pairs(findPages(fresh, {})) do
    local op, cur = oldPages[id], np.children[1]
    if op and cur then
      for _, lp in ipairs(op.children) do
        if lp._key == cur._key then
          local m = mergeNode(lp, cur)
          if m then np.children[1] = m; used[lp] = true end
          break
        end
      end
    end
  end
  prepare(fresh, fresh.attributes.id or "wcx", nil)
  if old then
    for id, np in pairs(findPages(fresh, {})) do
      local op = oldPages[id]
      if op then
        for _, lp in ipairs(op.children) do
          if not used[lp] then
            lp.attributes.active = "false"
            table.insert(np.children, lp)
          end
        end
        -- keep the most recently used pages
        if #np.children > MAX_PAGES then
          local keep = { np.children[1] }
          local rest = {}
          for i = 2, #np.children do table.insert(rest, np.children[i]) end
          table.sort(rest, function(a, b) return (a._used or 0) > (b._used or 0) end)
          for i = 1, MAX_PAGES - 1 do table.insert(keep, rest[i]) end
          np.children = keep
        end
      end
    end
  end
  return fresh
end

local function apply(op)
  if op.from then op.node._from = op.from; return end
  local id = op.node.attributes.id
  if PERF and PERF.on then PERF.count("screen changes") end
  if op.attrs then
    for k, v in pairs(op.attrs) do op.node.attributes[k] = v end
    local a = op.attrs
    if a.active == "true" and op.node.attributes.showAnimation and op.node.attributes.showAnimation ~= "None" then
      a.active = nil; UI.show(id)
    elseif a.active == "false" and op.node.attributes.hideAnimation and op.node.attributes.hideAnimation ~= "None" then
      a.active = nil; UI.hide(id)
    end
    -- a button switched on or off: Tabletop Simulator can leave it painted in its old (dark, disabled) colour, so the
    -- switch is sent on its own, last, and its colours again after it, which repaints it
    local inter = a.interactable
    if inter ~= nil then a.interactable = nil end
    if next(a) then UI.setAttributes(id, a) end
    if inter ~= nil then
      UI.setAttribute(id, "interactable", inter)
      local cols = op.node.attributes.colors
      if cols then UI.setAttribute(id, "colors", cols) end
    end
  else
    op.node.value = op.value
    UI.setValue(id, op.value)
  end
end

-- every change in a nested list of changes
local function eachOp(ops, fn)
  for i = 1, #ops do
    local o = ops[i]
    if o.list then eachOp(o.list, fn) else fn(o) end
  end
end
local function managed(id) return string.sub(id or "", 1, 9) == "wc_hover_" end
local function isPopup(id) return string.sub(id or "", 1, 9) ~= "wc_panel_" and string.sub(id or "", 1, 10) ~= "wc_toggle_" end

-- noFull: if this needs a full redraw, do nothing and say so ("full"), so the caller can redraw everything in one go.
function applyXml(xml, nBase, partial, noFull)
  clock = clock + 1
  FULL_WHY = nil
  local tops = {}
  for i = (nBase or 0) + 1, #xml do table.insert(tops, xml[i]) end
  local loading = false
  pcall(function() loading = UI.loading and true or false end)
  if loading and LIVE then
    -- the last full redraw is still loading: try again in a moment
    if not retry then retry = true; pcall(function() Wait.time(function() retry = false; rebuild() end, 0.2) end) end
    return
  end
  local ops, needFull, results = {}, (LIVE == nil), {}
  if LIVE == nil then FULL_WHY = "first draw or Refresh" end
  local byId = {}
  for _, l in ipairs(LIVE or {}) do byId[l.attributes.id] = l end
  local seen = {}
  for _, n in ipairs(tops) do
    local id = n.attributes and n.attributes.id or "?"
    seen[id] = true
    local l = byId[id]
    if n._stub and l then
      results[id] = "keep"
      if on(l) then table.insert(ops, { node = l, attrs = { active = "false" } }) end
    elseif not l then needFull = true; FULL_WHY = "new " .. id
    elseif managed(id) then results[id] = "keep"
    else
      local tmp = {}
      if match(l, n, tmp) then
        results[id] = tmp
        ops[#ops + 1] = { list = tmp }
      else needFull = true; FULL_WHY = FULL_WHY or ("changed " .. id) end
    end
  end
  local group = (partial == "popups")
  if not partial or group then
    for _, l in ipairs(LIVE or {}) do
      if not seen[l.attributes.id] and on(l) and not managed(l.attributes.id) and (not group or isPopup(l.attributes.id)) then
        table.insert(ops, { node = l, attrs = { active = "false" } })
      end
    end
  end
  if not needFull then
    local ok = pcall(function() eachOp(ops, apply) end)
    if ok then return end
  end
  if noFull then return "full" end
  -- full redraw: keep what matched, rebuild the rest, keep closed pop-ups hidden for next time
  local nextLive = {}
  for _, n in ipairs(tops) do
    local id = n.attributes and n.attributes.id or "?"
    local l = byId[id]
    local r = results[id]
    if l and type(r) == "table" then
      eachOp(r, function(o)
        if o.from then o.node._from = o.from
        elseif o.attrs then for k, v in pairs(o.attrs) do o.node.attributes[k] = v end else o.node.value = o.value end
      end)
      table.insert(nextLive, l)
    elseif l and n._stub then
      l.attributes.active = "false"
      table.insert(nextLive, l)
    else
      table.insert(nextLive, rebuildTop(n, l))
    end
  end
  if partial then
    -- only part of the screen was given: everything else stays exactly as it is, in its place
    local newById = {}
    for _, x in ipairs(nextLive) do newById[x.attributes.id] = x end
    local merged, done = {}, {}
    for _, l in ipairs(LIVE or {}) do
      local id = l.attributes.id
      table.insert(merged, newById[id] or l)
      done[id] = true
    end
    for _, x in ipairs(nextLive) do if not done[x.attributes.id] then table.insert(merged, x) end end
    nextLive = merged
    if group then
      for _, l in ipairs(nextLive) do
        if not seen[l.attributes.id] and isPopup(l.attributes.id) and not managed(l.attributes.id) then l.attributes.active = "false" end
      end
    end
  else
    for _, l in ipairs(LIVE or {}) do
      if not seen[l.attributes.id] and not managed(l.attributes.id) then l.attributes.active = "false"; table.insert(nextLive, l) end
    end
  end
  LIVE = nextLive
  local base = {}
  for i = 1, (nBase or 0) do table.insert(base, xml[i]) end
  sendFull(base)
end

-- Send the whole screen (LIVE) to Tabletop Simulator in one go.
sendFull = function(base)
  -- every element needs its own id (a field can now exist on several pages, or twice on one page, hidden)
  local usedIds = {}
  local function unique(list)
    for _, n in ipairs(list) do
      local id = n.attributes.id
      if id then
        if usedIds[id] then
          local k = 1
          while usedIds[id .. "~d" .. k] do k = k + 1 end
          id = id .. "~d" .. k
          n.attributes.id = id
        end
        usedIds[id] = true
      end
      unique(n.children or {})
    end
  end
  unique(LIVE)
  LIVE_IDS = usedIds
  local out = { UI_DEFAULTS }
  for _, e in ipairs(base or {}) do table.insert(out, e) end
  for _, l in ipairs(LIVE) do table.insert(out, plain(l)) end
  FULL_REDRAWS = (FULL_REDRAWS or 0) + 1
  if PERF and PERF.on then
    PERF.count("full redraws")
    PERF.why(string.gsub(tostring(FULL_WHY or "?"), "~.*$", ""))
  end
  UI.setXmlTable(out)
end

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- tabs prepared in the background
-- A tab whose layout changed needs the whole screen sent again the first time it's shown. So while nobody is
-- clicking, the War Council builds the tabs a panel has shown before and checks each against what the screen has;
-- any that changed are merged in together and sent in ONE redraw, at that quiet moment, instead of one redraw per
-- tab later on, each the moment someone clicks it.
local function shapeK(n)
  if n._shk then return n._shk end
  local parts = { n.tag or "?", ":", keySig(n), ":", (n.attributes and n.attributes.id) and baseId(n.attributes.id) or "" }
  for _, c in ipairs(n.children or {}) do parts[#parts + 1] = "(" .. shapeK(c) .. ")" end
  n._shk = table.concat(parts)
  return n._shk
end
local function pagesOf(pk)
  for _, l in ipairs(LIVE or {}) do
    if l.attributes.id == "wc_panel_" .. pk then
      for _, c in pairs(findPages(l, {})) do return c end
    end
  end
end
-- the pages the screen keeps for this panel
function livePageKeys(pk)
  local c = pagesOf(pk)
  local out = {}
  if c then for _, lp in ipairs(c.children) do if lp._key then table.insert(out, lp._key) end end end
  return out
end
-- would this freshly built page show without a full redraw?
function pageFits(pk, np)
  local c = pagesOf(pk)
  if not c then return true end
  for _, lp in ipairs(c.children) do
    if lp._key == np._key then
      local sk = shapeK(np)
      lp._fit = lp._fit or {}
      if lp._fit[sk] then return true end   -- the same layout fitted before: it still does
      local ok = match(lp, np, {})
      if ok then lp._fit[sk] = true end
      return ok
    end
  end
  return true   -- a tab the screen doesn't keep: nothing to prepare
end
-- merge these built pages in (hidden) and send the screen once
function mergePages(list)
  if not LIVE then return false end
  local loading = false
  pcall(function() loading = UI.loading and true or false end)
  if loading then return false end
  local any = false
  for _, item in ipairs(list) do
    local c = pagesOf(item.pk)
    local np = item.page
    if c then
      local idx
      for i, lp in ipairs(c.children) do if lp._key == np._key then idx = i end end
      if not (idx and on(c.children[idx])) then   -- never the page someone is looking at
        local m = (idx and mergeNode(c.children[idx], np)) or freshCopy(np)
        m._key = np._key
        pageSeq = pageSeq + 1
        prepare(m, c.attributes.id .. "_p" .. pageSeq, "~p" .. pageSeq)
        m.attributes.active = "false"
        m._used = clock
        m._from = np                           -- it shows exactly this page when it's opened
        m._fit = { [shapeK(np)] = true }
        if idx then c.children[idx] = m else table.insert(c.children, m) end
        any = true
      end
    end
  end
  if not any then return false end
  FULL_WHY = "tabs prepared in the background"
  sendFull(baseXml)
  return true
end

-- The player typed into a field: what's on screen already says this, so the next update needn't send it again.
function lastSet(id, attr, value)
  STATE_VER = (STATE_VER or 0) + 1   -- typed text may be campaign data (notes): built pages are out of date
  local function walk(list)
    for _, e in ipairs(list or {}) do
      if e.attributes and e.attributes.id == id then e.attributes[attr] = tostring(value or ""); e._from = nil; e._nk = nil; e._ks = nil; return true end
      if walk(e.children) then e._from = nil; return true end
    end
  end
  pcall(walk, LIVE)
end
function liveTree() return LIVE end
function resetScreen() LIVE = nil; LIVE_IDS = nil end
function liveHas(id) return LIVE_IDS ~= nil and LIVE_IDS[id] == true end
RECON = { match = match, merge = mergeNode, prepare = prepare, fresh = freshCopy }
end)()


-- ===================================================================
-- ===================================================================
-- callbacks
-- ===================================================================
local function say(pk, ok, msg)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  if ok == true then u.msg, u.msgOk = msg, true
  elseif type(ok) == "string" then u.msg, u.msgOk = ok, false
  else u.msg, u.msgOk = msg, true end
end

function uiInput(player, value, id)
  prewarmNote()
  local rawId = id
  id = (string.gsub(id or "", "~.*$", ""))
  local pk, field = string.match(id or "", "^wc_(%a+)_(.+)$")
  if not pk then return end
  uiState(pk).f[field] = value
  lastSet(rawId, "text", value)
  if field == "imgFolder" then S.settings.imgFolder = value; return end
  if string.sub(field, 1, 4) == "snd_" then
    S.settings.sounds = S.settings.sounds or {}
    -- trim by hand: Tabletop Simulator's Lua gives up on "(.-)%s*$" for long links
    local v = value or ""
    local i = string.find(v, "%S")
    if i then
      local j = #v
      while j > i and string.find(string.sub(v, j, j), "%s") do j = j - 1 end
      v = string.sub(v, i, j)
    else v = "" end
    S.settings.sounds[string.sub(field, 5)] = (v ~= "") and v or nil
    return
  end
  if string.sub(field, 1, 2) == "r_" then
    if field == "r_setGood" then S.settings.goodColor = value; enforceSeats(); rebuild(); return end
    if field == "r_setEvil" then S.settings.evilColor = value; enforceSeats(); rebuild(); return end
    if field == "r_logQ" then uiState(pk).f.logPage = nil end
    if field == "r_bTerr" then uiState(pk).f.r_bAtk = nil; uiState(pk).f.r_bDef = nil end
    if field == "r_bAtk" then uiState(pk).f.r_bDef = nil end
    if field == "r_bLostA" or field == "r_bLostD" then
      local u2 = uiState(pk)
      local a2 = armyById(idOf((field == "r_bLostA") and u2.f.r_bAtk or u2.f.r_bDef))
      local pct = lostInfo(a2, value)
      if pct then u2.f[(field == "r_bLostA") and "bCasA" or "bCasD"] = tostring(pct) end
    end
    if field == "r_paste" or field == "r_pasteA" then
      local u2 = uiState(pk)
      u2.paste = u2.paste or {}
      u2.pasteErr = u2.pasteErr or {}
      if value == nil or value == "" then u2.paste[field], u2.pasteErr[field] = nil, nil
      else
        local rr, err = parseRoster(value)
        u2.paste[field] = rr
        u2.pasteErr[field] = (not rr) and err or nil
      end
    end
    local wid, k, st = string.match(field, "^r_stop_(%d+)_(%d+)_(%d+)$")
    if wid then
      local g = agentById(wid)
      if g and g.route then
        local r = setRouteStep(g, tonumber(k), tonumber(st), numOf(value))
        if r ~= true then uiState(pk).msg, uiState(pk).msgOk = r, false end
      end
    end
    local wid2, k2, st2 = string.match(field, "^r_amt_(%d+)_(%d+)_(%d+)$")
    if wid2 then
      local g = agentById(wid2)
      if g and g.route then setStopAmount(g, tonumber(k2), tonumber(st2), tonumber(value)) end
    end
    rebuild()
  end
end

-- Territory notes are written straight into the state as they're typed.
function uiNote(player, value, id)
  lastSet(id, "text", value)
  id = (string.gsub(id or "", "~.*$", ""))
  local pk, sd = string.match(id or "", "^wc_(%a+)_note_(%a+)$")
  local n = pk and (uiState(pk).sel.terr or 1)
  if not n or not S.terr[n] then return end
  S.terr[n].notes = S.terr[n].notes or {}
  S.terr[n].notes[sd] = value
end

-- Paste box: keep the text as it changes (no rebuild, so typing/pasting isn't interrupted).
function uiPaste(player, value, id)
  local rawId = id
  id = (string.gsub(id or "", "~.*$", ""))
  local pk, field = string.match(id or "", "^wc_(%a+)_(.+)$")
  if not pk then return end
  uiState(pk).f[field] = value
  lastSet(rawId, "text", value)
end

local function writeNotebook(body)
  local tabs = Notes.getNotebookTabs()
  for _, t in ipairs(tabs) do
    if t.title == "War Council Save" then
      Notes.editNotebookTab({ index = t.index, title = t.title, body = body, color = "Grey" })
      return
    end
  end
  Notes.addNotebookTab({ title = "War Council Save", body = body, color = "Grey" })
end

local function readNotebook()
  for _, t in ipairs(Notes.getNotebookTabs()) do
    if t.title == "War Council Save" then return t.body end
  end
end


-- ===================================================================
-- undo, backups, prompts, map hints, rulebook
-- ===================================================================
UNDO = UNDO or {}
-- clicks that only change what one panel shows (nothing in the campaign changes)
PANEL_ONLY = { tab = true, open = true, close = true, acting = true, set = true, ftog = true, noop = true, selT = true, selA = true,
               selG = true, selW = true, rsel = true, gtgt = true, sep = true, bterr = true, pickopen = true, pickclose = true,
               bopen = true, bback = true, bswap = true }
-- Pick the battles up again as if the Battle stage had just begun: reveal the armies and show everyone the pop-up.
function promptBattles()
  if STAGES[S.stage] ~= "Battle" then return "Battles are announced in the Battle stage." end
  pcall(revealBattles)
  S.bpTurn, S.bpSeen = S.turn, {}
  if #battleTerritories() == 0 then return "No battles due: no territory has armies of both sides (or an army at an enemy fortress)." end
  pcall(soundEvent, "battle")
  return true
end

-- Refresh: redraw everything from scratch and recheck what the War Council might have missed (after an update or a reload).
function refreshCouncil(pk)
  pcall(fixState)
  pcall(syncAllOwners)
  pcall(resetScreen)
  MARKERS_SCANNED = nil
  pcall(reconcileMarkers, true)
  pcall(reconcileRoads, true)
  pcall(reconcileBuildings)
  pcall(piecesRefresh)
  if STAGES[S.stage] == "Battle" then pcall(promptBattles) end
  local notes = {}
  local b = getBoard()
  for _, a in ipairs(S.armies) do
    if a.status ~= "building" and a.tok and b then
      local o = tokenObject("army", a)
      if o then
        local okP, n = pcall(function() return b.call("territoryAt", { position = o.getPosition() }) end)
        if okP and n and n ~= a.loc then
          table.insert(notes, armyLabel(pk, a) .. "'s model stands in " .. NAMES[n] .. ", but the War Council has it in " .. NAMES[a.loc] .. ".")
        end
      end
    end
  end
  local bt = battleTerritories()
  local names = {}
  for _, n in ipairs(bt) do table.insert(names, NAMES[n]) end
  local msg = "Refreshed. " .. ((#bt > 0) and ("Battle" .. ((#bt == 1) and "" or "s") .. " due at " .. table.concat(names, ", ") .. ".")
                                          or "No battles due: no territory has armies of both sides (or an army at an enemy fortress).")
  if #notes > 0 then msg = msg .. " " .. table.concat(notes, " ") .. " Drop the model again (or move the army from its panel) to fix that." end
  return msg
end

local NO_UNDO = { refresh = true, bprompt = true, pickopen = true, pickclose = true, pick = true, tab = true, open = true, close = true, acting = true, set = true, ftog = true, noop = true, selT = true, selA = true,
                  selG = true, selW = true, rsel = true, gtgt = true, sep = true, qamt = true, bterr = true, newsok = true, newslog = true,
                  bopen = true, bback = true, bswap = true,
                  cal = true, export = true, undo = true, rule = true, pasteuse = true, pasteclr = true, slotedit = true, slotcancel = true,
                  psize = true, maphints = true, newstiming = true, markerflip = true, rebuildpieces = true, brestore = true,
                  bpok = true, bpopen = true, webcode = true, repnew = true, repback = true, repview = true, repedit = true, repfill = true,
                  cards = true, cardbig = true, deal = true, cardsclr = true,
                  sndtest = true, mapupdate = true, sumok = true, sumshow = true, sumhist = true, perftog = true, perfrep = true, prewarmtog = true }
local UNDO_LABEL = { apick = "Pick up supplies", afill = "Fill up supplies", adrop = "Drop off supplies", arename = "Rename army", prio = "Priority roll", first = "Choose who acts first", adv = "Advance stage", ready = "Ready", force = "Force advance",
  own = "Change owner", stock = "Stockpile correction", build = "Start construction", raise = "Raise army", move = "Move army",
  rep = "Replenish", up = "Upgrade army", edit = "Edit army", disband = "Disband army", retreat = "Retreat", destroy = "Destroy army",
  recruit = "Recruit agent", gmove = "Move agent", gact = "Agent action", gdis = "Dismiss agent", bprio = "Battle priority roll",
  bapply = "Battle result", xfer = "Supply transfer", hidden = "Hidden information", override = "Rule override", start = "Start new campaign",
  import = "Import", tokspawn = "Spawn token", toklink = "Link token", tokplace = "Bring token", tokunlink = "Unlink token",
  canmarch = "Heroic March setting", wrecruit = "Recruit wagon", wload = "Load wagon", wunload = "Unload wagon", wmove = "Move wagon",
