  wauto = "Auto-unload", rule = "Special rule", evmerc = "Hire mercenaries", evtroll = "Raid the hoard", evno = "Decline event", rsupply = "Supply run", act = "Territory action", actcancel = "Cancel action", rnew = "Create route", rtoggle = "Route on/off", sact = "Route stop action", addstop = "Add route stop",
  rmstop = "Remove route stop", radd = "Add route turn", rdel = "Remove route turn", rclear = "Delete route", rsave = "Save route",
  rapply = "Apply route", rlibdel = "Delete saved route", rall = "Pause/resume routes", pasteedit = "Replace roster",
  slotsave = "Save slots", slotreset = "Reset slots", theatre = "Theatre", backups = "Backup setting",
  repsave = "Save battle report", repdel = "Delete battle report" }

function pushUndo(snap, action, pk)
  table.insert(UNDO, { snap = snap, label = UNDO_LABEL[action] or action, pk = pk or "All", t = S.turn, s = S.stage })
  while #UNDO > 20 do table.remove(UNDO, 1) end
end

function undoTop(pk)
  for i = #UNDO, 1, -1 do
    local e = UNDO[i]
    if pk == "All" or e.pk == pk then return e end
  end
end

local function entLocation(kind, e) if kind == "army" then return e.loc end return agentLoc(e) end

-- After the state jumps back (undo / restore), put tokens where the state says and tidy orphans.
function restoreTokens(prev)
  local function find(kind, id)
    for _, e in ipairs((kind == "army") and S.armies or S.agents) do if tostring(e.id) == tostring(id) then return e end end
  end
  for _, spec in ipairs({ { "army", prev and prev.armies or {} }, { "agent", prev and prev.agents or {} } }) do
    for _, e in ipairs(spec[2]) do
      if e.tok and not find(spec[1], e.id) then
        local o = e.tok.guid and getObjectFromGUID(e.tok.guid)
        if o then if e.tok.spawned then o.destruct() else o.setGMNotes("") end end
      end
    end
  end
  local b = getBoard()
  for _, spec in ipairs({ { "army", S.armies }, { "agent", S.agents } }) do
    for _, e in ipairs(spec[2]) do
      if e.tok then
        e.tok.animating = nil
        local o = tokenObject(spec[1], e)
        if o then
          local p = o.getPosition()
          local n = b and b.call("territoryAt", { position = p }) or nil
          if n ~= entLocation(spec[1], e) then pcall(placeToken, spec[1], e)
          else e.tok.pos = { p.x, p.z }; e.tok.terr = n; e.tok.mismatch = nil end
        end
      end
    end
  end
end

function afterRestore(prev)
  pcall(syncAllOwners)
  pcall(reconcileBuildings)
  pcall(restoreTokens, prev)
end

function doUndo(pk)
  local e = undoTop(pk)
  if not e then return "Nothing to undo." end
  for i = #UNDO, 1, -1 do if UNDO[i] == e then table.remove(UNDO, i); break end end
  local prev = S
  local ok, data = pcall(stateRestore, e.snap)
  if not ok or type(data) ~= "table" or not data.terr then return "Couldn't undo that." end
  S = data
  fixState()
  afterRestore(prev)
  clog("Undid: " .. e.label .. ".", (e.pk ~= "All") and e.pk or nil)
  return true, "Undid: " .. e.label .. "."
end

local BK = "WC Backup - "
function backupList()
  local out = {}
  local ok, tabs = pcall(function() return Notes.getNotebookTabs() end)
  if ok and tabs then
    for _, t in ipairs(tabs) do
      if string.sub(t.title or "", 1, #BK) == BK then table.insert(out, 1, string.sub(t.title, #BK + 1)) end
    end
  end
  return out
end

function readBackup(label)
  for _, t in ipairs(Notes.getNotebookTabs()) do if t.title == BK .. (label or "") then return t.body end end
end

function writeBackup(label)
  local mine = {}
  for _, t in ipairs(Notes.getNotebookTabs()) do
    if string.sub(t.title or "", 1, #BK) == BK then table.insert(mine, t) end
  end
  local extra = #mine - 4
  if extra > 0 then
    local idx = {}
    for i = 1, extra do table.insert(idx, mine[i].index) end
    table.sort(idx, function(x, y) return x > y end)
    for _, ix in ipairs(idx) do Notes.removeNotebookTab(ix) end
  end
  Notes.addNotebookTab({ title = BK .. label, body = exportState(), color = "Grey" })
end

function onNewTurn()
  if S.settings.backups == false then return end
  local stamp = ""
  pcall(function() stamp = "  (" .. os.date("%d %b %H:%M") .. ")" end)
  pcall(writeBackup, "Turn " .. S.turn .. stamp)
end

function announceStage()
  pcall(showStageBanner)
  pcall(function()
    broadcastToAll("Turn " .. S.turn .. "  ·  " .. stageName() .. " stage", { 0.91, 0.78, 0.47 })
  end)
end

local function seatColor(side) return (side == "Good") and S.settings.goodColor or S.settings.evilColor end

function openRule(player, key)
  local page = RULE_PAGES[key]
  if not page then return "Unknown rule section." end
  local book = getObjectFromGUID("b00c01")
  if not book then
    for _, o in ipairs(getAllObjects()) do
      if string.find(string.lower(o.getName() or ""), "campaign rules", 1, true) then book = o end
    end
  end
  if not book then return "Couldn't find the campaign rulebook on the table." end
  local ok = pcall(function() book.Book.setPage(page - 1) end)
  if not ok then return "Couldn't turn the rulebook's page - open it to page " .. page .. "." end
  if player then pcall(function() player.pingTable(book.getPosition()) end) end
  return true
end

function updateHints(pk)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  if not u.open then return clearHints() end
  if u.tab == "Armies" and u.sel.army then
    local a = armyById(u.sel.army)
    if a and canSee(pk, a.side) then
      local budget = armyMove(a) - (a.slow or 0)
      return showRange(a.side, a.loc, reachable(a.loc, budget, false, nil, a.side), "a" .. a.id .. ":" .. a.loc .. ":" .. budget)
    end
  elseif u.tab == "Agents" and u.sel.agent then
    local g = agentById(u.sel.agent)
    if g and canSee(pk, g.side) and not g.army and g.loc then
      return showRange(g.side, g.loc, reachable(g.loc, agentMoveLeft(g), g.type == "Supply Wagon", agentRoadFree(g), g.side), "g" .. g.id .. ":" .. g.loc)
    end
  elseif u.tab == "Wagons" and u.sel.wagon then
    local g = agentById(u.sel.wagon)
    if g and canSee(pk, g.side) and g.loc then
      if g.route and isArmyRoute(g.route) then
        -- outline where it's heading next: the army, or the depot when empty
        local a = armyById(g.route.army)
        local tgt = ((g.carried or 0) > 0 and a) and a.loc or g.route.depot
        local path = supplyPath(g.loc, tgt, g.side) or {}
        table.insert(path, 1, g.loc)
        return showRange(g.side, g.loc, path, "ws" .. g.id .. ":" .. g.loc .. ":" .. tostring(tgt))
      end
      if g.route then return showRoute(g, "w" .. g.id .. ":" .. JSON.encode(g.route.legs) .. ":" .. tostring(g.route.start)) end
      return showRange(g.side, g.loc, reachable(g.loc, AGENTS["Supply Wagon"].move, true, nil, g.side), "wr" .. g.id .. ":" .. g.loc)
    end
  end
  clearHints()
end

function afterAction(pk)
  LAST_HINT_PK = pk
  pcall(revealSweep)
  pcall(soundCheck, SND_SILENT); SND_SILENT = nil
  pcall(reconcileMarkers)
  pcall(reconcileRoads)
  local ok, err = pcall(updateHints, pk)
  if not ok then print("War Council map hints: " .. tostring(err)) end
end

-- Right-click on the map (board script) or the hotkey: show that territory in the player's panel.
function openTerritoryFor(p)
  local n = tonumber(p and p.n)
  if not n or not NAMES[n] then return end
  local pk = viewerKey(p.color)
  local u = uiState(pk)
  u.open = true
  u.tab = "Territories"
  u.sel.terr = n
  u.f.tFilter = "All"
  ACT_PK = pk
  rebuild()
  ACT_PK = nil
end

-- Actions that roll dice go through the dice tray first.
DICE_ACTIONS = { prio = true, rule = true, gmove = true, gact = true, retreat = true, bprio = true, bapply = true, evtroll = true }
function ui(player, arg)
  prewarmNote()
  local a = string.match(arg or "", "^[^~]+~([^~]+)")
  if a == "dthrow" then
    local pk, side = string.match(arg, "^([^~]+)~[^~]+~([^~]+)")
    throwForMe(side); return
  end
  if DICE.busy and a and not NO_UNDO[a] then
    local pk = string.match(arg, "^([^~]+)")
    if pk then uiState(pk).msg, uiState(pk).msgOk = "The dice are still rolling - wait for them to land.", false; rebuild() end
    return
  end
  if a and DICE_ACTIONS[a] and physicalDice() and not DICE.inside then return withDice(function() uiRun(player, arg) end) end
  return uiRun(player, arg)
end

-- Actions that change the battle reports or the saved routes: their Undo copies those too (others leave them out).
FULL_SNAP = { repsave = true, repdel = true, rsave = true, rlibdel = true, import = true, start = true, brestore = true, reset = true }
function uiRun(player, arg)
  local parts = {}
  for p in string.gmatch(arg or "", "[^~]+") do table.insert(parts, p) end
  local pk, a, p1, p2 = parts[1], parts[2], parts[3], parts[4]
  if not pk then return end
  local u = uiState(pk)
  local f = u.f
  local side = actingSide(pk)
  local keepConfirm = false
  u.msg = nil
  local r
  local before = (not NO_UNDO[a]) and stateSnap(FULL_SNAP[a]) or nil
  ACT_PK = pk
  if a == "undo" or a == "brestore" or a == "start" or a == "import" then SND_SILENT = true end
  local turnBefore, stageBefore = S.turn, S.stage

  if a == "tab" then u.tab = p1
  elseif a == "open" then u.open = not u.open
  elseif a == "close" then u.open = false
  elseif a == "acting" then u.acting = p1; u.sel = {}
  elseif a == "pickopen" then u.picker = p1
  elseif a == "pickclose" then u.picker = nil
  elseif a == "pick" then
    local opt = u.opts and u.opts[p1] and u.opts[p1][tonumber(p2)]
    u.picker = nil
    if opt then uiInput(player, opt, "wc_" .. pk .. "_" .. p1) end
  elseif a == "set" then f[p1] = p2
  elseif a == "ftog" then f[p1] = (f[p1] == "True") and "False" or "True"
  elseif a == "noop" then
  elseif a == "bterr" then f.r_bTerr = terrOpt(tonumber(p1)); f.r_bAtk = nil; f.r_bDef = nil
  elseif a == "bopen" then
    local n = (p1 == "any") and numOf(f.r_bAny) or tonumber(p1)
    if n and NAMES[n] then
      u.bopen = n; f.r_bTerr = terrOpt(n); f.r_bAtk = nil; f.r_bDef = nil; f.bSwap = nil
      for _, k in ipairs({ "bWin", "bOut", "bCasA", "bCasD", "bDuel", "r_bLostA", "r_bLostD", "bRetreat" }) do f[k] = nil end
    end
  elseif a == "bback" then u.bopen = nil
  elseif a == "bswap" then f.bSwap = (f.bSwap == "True") and "False" or "True"; f.r_bAtk = nil; f.r_bDef = nil
  elseif a == "gtgt" then f.gTargetKey = p1
  elseif a == "sep" then
    if p1 == "from" then f.sFrom = "x [" .. p2 .. "]"; if string.match(f.sTo or "", "%[(.-)%]$") == p2 then f.sTo = nil end
    else f.sTo = "x [" .. p2 .. "]"; if string.match(f.sFrom or "", "%[(.-)%]$") == p2 then f.sFrom = nil end end
  elseif a == "qamt" then f.sAmt = p1
  elseif a == "undo" then local ok3, m = doUndo(pk); say(pk, ok3, m)
  elseif a == "rule" and p1 ~= "Good" and p1 ~= "Evil" then r = openRule(player, p1); say(pk, r, "The campaign rulebook on the table is open at page " .. (RULE_PAGES[p1] or "?") .. ".")
  elseif a == "pasteuse" then
    local rr = u.paste and u.paste.r_paste
    if not rr then say(pk, "Paste a list first.")
    else
      if rr.list then f.aList = rr.list end
      if rr.general then f.aLeader = rr.general end
      if rr.size then f.aSize = tostring(rr.size) end
      f.aUnits = string.sub(rr.summary or "", 1, 600)
      if (not f.aName or f.aName == "") and rr.name then f.aName = rr.name end
      if rr.list and rosterFit(rr, side) == "allied" and not alliedArmy(side) then f.aAllied = "True"; f.aMerc = nil end
      u.pasteRoster = rosterForArmy(rr)
      say(pk, true, "Form filled from the pasted list - check it, then press Raise army.")
    end
  elseif a == "bpok" then S.bpSeen = S.bpSeen or {}; S.bpSeen[p1] = true
  elseif a == "bpopen" then
    S.bpSeen = S.bpSeen or {}; S.bpSeen[p1] = true
    local vk = viewerKey(p1)
    uiState(vk).open = true
    uiState(vk).tab = "Battles"
    local btl = battleTerritories()
    uiState(vk).bopen = (#btl == 1) and btl[1] or nil
  elseif a == "webcode" then u.showWeb = not u.showWeb
  elseif a == "repnew" then
    u.rep = { mode = "edit" }
    for _, k in ipairs({ "rpTitle", "rpScenario", "rpGood", "rpEvil", "rpResult", "rpHero", "rpCas", "rpStory", "rpMoments", "rpBattle", "rpTerr" }) do f[k] = nil end
    f.rpWinner = "Good"
  elseif a == "repback" then u.rep = nil
  elseif a == "repview" then u.rep = { mode = "view", id = p1 }
  elseif a == "repedit" then
    local rr = reportById(p1)
    if rr then
      u.rep = { mode = "edit", id = rr.id }
      f.rpTitle, f.rpScenario, f.rpGood, f.rpEvil, f.rpResult = rr.title, rr.scenario, rr.good, rr.evil, rr.result
      f.rpHero, f.rpCas, f.rpStory, f.rpMoments, f.rpWinner = rr.hero, rr.cas, rr.story, rr.moments, rr.winner
      f.rpTerr = rr.n and terrOpt(rr.n) or nil
    end
  elseif a == "repfill" then
    local idx = tonumber(string.match(f.rpBattle or "", "#(%d+)$"))
    local b = idx and S.battles[idx]
    if not b then say(pk, "Choose a recorded battle first.")
    else
      local view = (pk ~= "All" and S.settings.hidden) and pk or nil
      local nm = (b.names and (b.names[view or "All"] or b.names.All)) or {}
      f.rpTerr = terrOpt(b.n)
      local good, evil = nil, nil
      if b.atkSide == "Good" then good, evil = nm[1], nm[2] else good, evil = nm[2], nm[1] end
      f.rpGood = f.rpGood or good
      f.rpEvil = f.rpEvil or evil
      if b.win then f.rpWinner = b.win end
      if b.outcome then f.rpResult = f.rpResult or ((b.win and (OTHER[b.win] .. " army ") or "Loser ") .. b.outcome) end
      if not f.rpTitle or f.rpTitle == "" then f.rpTitle = "The Battle of " .. NAMES[b.n] end
      u.rep.battle = idx
      u.rep.t = b.t
      say(pk, true, "Filled from the battle at " .. NAMES[b.n] .. ".")
    end
  elseif a == "repsave" then
    S.reports = S.reports or {}
    local rr = u.rep and u.rep.id and reportById(u.rep.id)
    if not rr then
      S.repId = (S.repId or 0) + 1
      rr = { id = S.repId, t = (u.rep and u.rep.t) or S.turn, author = side }
      table.insert(S.reports, rr)
    end
    rr.title, rr.scenario, rr.good, rr.evil = f.rpTitle or "", f.rpScenario or "", f.rpGood or "", f.rpEvil or ""
    rr.winner, rr.result, rr.hero, rr.cas = f.rpWinner or "Good", f.rpResult or "", f.rpHero or "", f.rpCas or ""
    rr.story, rr.moments, rr.n = f.rpStory or "", f.rpMoments or "", numOf(f.rpTerr)
    if u.rep and u.rep.battle then rr.battle = u.rep.battle end
    u.rep = { mode = "view", id = rr.id }
    say(pk, true, "Report saved.")
  elseif a == "repdel" then
    if u.confirm == ("repdel:" .. p1) then
      local _, i = reportById(p1)
      if i then table.remove(S.reports, i) end
      say(pk, true, "Report deleted.")
    else u.confirm = "repdel:" .. p1; keepConfirm = true end
  elseif a == "pasteread" then
    u.paste = u.paste or {}
    u.pasteErr = u.pasteErr or {}
    local rr, err = parseRoster(f[p1] or "")
    u.paste[p1] = rr
    u.pasteErr[p1] = (not rr) and err or nil
    if rr then say(pk, true, "List read: " .. rosterLine(rr) .. ".") else say(pk, err) end
  elseif a == "sumok" then SHOW_SUMMARY[p1] = nil; if SUMMARY_SHOWN then SUMMARY_SHOWN[p1] = nil end; END_SEEN[p1] = true
  elseif a == "sumshow" then SHOW_SUMMARY[player and player.color or "White"] = true
  elseif a == "sumhist" then SHOW_SUMMARY[p1] = nil; if SUMMARY_SHOWN then SUMMARY_SHOWN[p1] = nil end; END_SEEN[p1] = true; local vk = viewerKey(p1); uiState(vk).open = true; uiState(vk).tab = "History"
  elseif a == "bannertog" then S.settings.banners = (S.settings.banners == false)
  elseif a == "roadtog" then S.settings.roadLines = (S.settings.roadLines == false)
  elseif a == "hovertog" then S.settings.hoverInfo = (S.settings.hoverInfo == false); HOVER = {}
  elseif a == "endwar" then
    if u.confirm == ("endwar:" .. p1) then
      S.winner = p1; S.winnerNote = p1 .. " is declared the victor after " .. S.turn .. " turn" .. ((S.turn == 1) and "" or "s") .. "."
      END_SEEN = {}
      clog("*** The campaign is declared over: " .. p1 .. " wins. ***", nil, "war")
      broadcastToAll("War Council: " .. p1 .. " wins the campaign!", { 1, 0.85, 0.4 })
    else u.confirm = "endwar:" .. p1; keepConfirm = true end
  elseif a == "unendwar" then S.winner = nil; S.winnerNote = nil
  elseif a == "mapupdate" then
    local ok3, m = migrateMap()
    if ok3 then say(pk, true, "Moving the campaign onto the new map...") else say(pk, m or "Couldn't update the map.") end
  elseif a == "sndtest" then
    if soundUrl(p1) then playSound(p1); say(pk, true, "Playing " .. p1 .. " through the Music Player.")
    else say(pk, "Paste a link for that sound first (press Enter or click away from the box to save it).") end
  elseif a == "sndtog" then S.settings.sound = (S.settings.sound == false)
  elseif a == "refresh" then r = refreshCouncil(pk); say(pk, true, r)
  elseif a == "bprompt" then r = promptBattles(); say(pk, r, "Battles announced again: everyone sees the battle pop-up.")
  elseif a == "apick" or a == "afill" or a == "adrop" then
    local ar = armyById(p1)
    local amt = (a ~= "afill") and tonumber(f.aRP) or nil
    local had = ar and (ar.carried or 0) or 0
    r = armySupplies(ar, (a == "adrop") and "drop" or "pick", amt)
    if r == true then f.aRP = nil end
    say(pk, r, ar and (((a == "adrop") and "Dropped off " or "Picked up ") .. math.abs((ar.carried or 0) - had) .. " RP. " .. ar.name .. " now carries " .. (ar.carried or 0) .. " RP.") or nil)
  elseif a == "arename" then
    local ar = armyById(p1)
    r = renameArmy(ar, f.aRename)
    if r == true then f.aRename = nil end
    say(pk, r, ar and ("Renamed to " .. ar.name .. ".") or nil)
  elseif a == "musictog" then S.settings.musicResume = (S.settings.musicResume == false)
  elseif a == "prewarmtog" then S.settings.prewarm = (S.settings.prewarm == false)
  elseif a == "perftog" then
    if PERF.on then PERF.on = false; say(pk, true, "Timing switched off.")
    else PERF.start(); say(pk, true, "Timing switched on. Play normally for a minute or two, then press Print report.") end
  elseif a == "perfrep" then
    local lines = PERF.report()
    local color = player and player.color
    for _, l in ipairs(lines) do
      if color and color ~= "Grey" then pcall(function() printToColor(l, color, { 0.85, 0.8, 0.65 }) end) else print(l) end
    end
    say(pk, true, "Report printed in the chat.")
  elseif a == "minortog" then S.settings.minorSounds = (S.settings.minorSounds == false)
  elseif a == "sndgap" then S.settings.soundGap = tonumber(p1) or 4
  elseif a == "cards" then
    local ar = armyById(tonumber(p1))
    if u.cardsFor == tonumber(p1) then u.cardsFor = nil
    elseif ar then u.cardsFor = ar.id; u.cardBig = nil; ensureCardAssets(armyCards(ar)) end
  elseif a == "cardbig" then
    u.cardBig = (tonumber(p1) or 0) > 0 and tonumber(p1) or nil
  elseif a == "deal" then
    local ar = armyById(tonumber(p1))
    if ar and canSee(pk, ar.side) then local ok2, m = dealCards(player and player.color, ar); if ok2 then say(pk, true, m) else say(pk, m) end end
  elseif a == "cardsclr" then
    local n = clearCards(tonumber(p1))
    say(pk, true, (n > 0) and ("Removed " .. n .. " profile card" .. ((n == 1) and "" or "s") .. " from the table.") or "No profile cards of this army on the table.")
  elseif a == "pasteclr" then
    f[p1] = nil
    if u.paste then u.paste[p1] = nil end
    if u.pasteErr then u.pasteErr[p1] = nil end
    if p1 == "r_paste" then u.pasteRoster = nil end
  elseif a == "pasteedit" then
    local arm = armyById(p1)
    local rr = u.paste and u.paste.r_pasteA
    if not arm or not rr then say(pk, "Paste a list first.")
    else
      r = editArmy(arm, { leader = rr.general, list = rr.list, units = string.sub(rr.summary or "", 1, 600), roster = rosterForArmy(rr) })
      say(pk, r, "Roster replaced.")
      if r == true then f.r_pasteA = nil; u.paste.r_pasteA = nil; f.eLeader, f.eList, f.eUnits = arm.leader, arm.list, arm.units end
    end
  elseif a == "psize" then S.settings.panelSize = p1
  elseif a == "maphints" then S.settings.mapHints = p1
  elseif a == "newstiming" then S.settings.newsTiming = p1
  elseif a == "markerflip" then S.settings.markerFlip = not S.settings.markerFlip
  elseif a == "backups" then S.settings.backups = (S.settings.backups == false)
  elseif a == "brestore" then
    if u.confirm == "brestore" then
      local body = readBackup(f.backupSel)
      if not body then say(pk, "That backup wasn't found in the Notebook.")
      else
        local snap, prev = JSON.encode(S), S
        r = importState(body)
        if r == true then pushUndo(snap, "Restore backup", pk); afterRestore(prev) end
        say(pk, r, "Backup restored. Undo brings back what you had before.")
      end
    else u.confirm = "brestore"; keepConfirm = true end
  elseif a == "prio" then r = rollPriority(); say(pk, r)
  elseif a == "rule" then r = declareRule(p1, p2, numOf(f.evEyeT)); say(pk, r, "Special rule used.")
  elseif a == "evmerc" then
    r = hireMercs(p1, numOf(f.evMercT), { name = f.evMercName, leader = f.evMercLeader, list = f.evMercList, units = f.evMercUnits,
                                          named = f.evMercNamed == "True", cavalry = f.evMercCav == "True", canMarch = f.evMercMarch == "True", roster = u.pasteRoster })
    if r == true then u.pasteRoster = nil; if u.paste then u.paste.r_paste = nil end end
    say(pk, r, "Mercenaries hired.")
  elseif a == "evtroll" then r = raidTrollHoard(p1); say(pk, r, "The hoard was raided.")
  elseif a == "evno" then r = declineEvent(p1); say(pk, r, "Done.")
  elseif a == "sail" then r = sailArmy(armyById(numOf(p1)), numOf(p2)); say(pk, r, "Set sail.")
  elseif a == "evpick" then r = eventChoose(p1, numOf(f.evPayT), idOf(f.evArmy) or numOf(f.evArmy)); say(pk, r, "Done.")
  elseif a == "evtog" then S.settings.randomEvents = (S.settings.randomEvents == false)
  elseif a == "dicetog" then S.settings.dice = (S.settings.dice == "digital") and "physical" or "digital"
  elseif a == "diceby" then S.settings.diceBy = S.settings.diceBy or {}; S.settings.diceBy[p1] = p2
  elseif a == "first" then r = chooseFirst(p1); say(pk, r)
  elseif a == "adv" then r = advanceStage(); say(pk, r, "Now in the " .. stageName() .. " stage.")
  elseif a == "ready" then
    local sd = p1
    local seat = player and player.color
    if seat == S.settings.goodColor and sd ~= "Good" or seat == S.settings.evilColor and sd ~= "Evil" then
      say(pk, "You can only ready your own side.")
    else
      local ok2, how = setReady(sd, not S.ready[sd])
      if ok2 == true and how == "advanced" then say(pk, true, "Both sides ready. Now in the " .. stageName() .. " stage.")
      else
        say(pk, ok2, S.ready[sd] and (sd .. " is ready. Waiting for " .. OTHER[sd] .. ".") or (sd .. " is no longer ready."))
        if ok2 == true and S.ready[sd] then
          pcall(function() broadcastToColor(sd .. " is ready to leave the " .. stageName() .. " stage - waiting on you.", seatColor(OTHER[sd]), { 0.95, 0.8, 0.4 }) end)
        end
      end
    end
  elseif a == "force" then
    if u.confirm == "force" then r = advanceStage(); say(pk, r, "Forced. Now in the " .. stageName() .. " stage.")
    else u.confirm = "force"; keepConfirm = true end
  elseif a == "readycheck" then S.settings.readyCheck = not S.settings.readyCheck; S.ready = { Good = false, Evil = false }
  elseif a == "selT" then u.sel.terr = tonumber(p1)
  elseif a == "own" then setOwner(tonumber(p1), p2, "set by " .. (player and player.steam_name or "player")); say(pk, true, tname(tonumber(p1)) .. " is now " .. p2 .. ".")
  elseif a == "stock" then
    local amt = tonumber(f.stockAdj or "")
    if not amt then say(pk, "Enter an amount.") else
      local tn = tonumber(p1); local delta = (p2 == "set") and (math.max(0, amt) - (S.terr[tn].stock or 0)) or amt * tonumber(p2)
      r = adjustStock(tn, delta, player and player.steam_name or "player"); say(pk, r, "Stockpile updated.") end
  elseif a == "build" then r = construct(side, tonumber(p1), p2); say(pk, r, "Construction started.")
  elseif a == "selA" then u.sel.army = (p1 ~= "new") and tonumber(p1) or nil
    local arm = u.sel.army and armyById(u.sel.army)
    if arm then f.eLeader, f.eList, f.eUnits = arm.leader, arm.list, arm.units end
  elseif a == "raise" then
    local ok2, arm = createArmy(side, { name = f.aName, size = f.aSize or "400", leader = f.aLeader or "", named = f.aNamed == "True",
      list = f.aList or "", merc = f.aMerc == "True" and f.aAllied ~= "True", allied = f.aAllied == "True", cavalry = f.aCav == "True", canMarch = f.aMarchOK == "True", units = f.aUnits or "", terr = numOf(f.aTerr),
      roster = u.pasteRoster })
    say(pk, ok2, "Army is being raised.")
    if ok2 == true then
      u.sel.army = arm.id; f.aName, f.aLeader, f.aUnits = nil, nil, nil
      u.pasteRoster = nil; f.r_paste = nil; if u.paste then u.paste.r_paste = nil end
    else r = ok2 end
  elseif a == "move" then r = moveArmy(armyById(p1), numOf(f.mDest), f.mMarch == "True"); say(pk, r, "Army moved."); if r == true then f.mMarch = nil end
  elseif a == "rep" then r = replenish(armyById(p1)); say(pk, r, "Replenishing.")
  elseif a == "up" then r = upgradeArmy(armyById(p1)); say(pk, r, "Upgrade started.")
  elseif a == "uppaste" then   -- upgrade with the new, bigger list pasted (or, while upgrading, set that list)
    local arm, rr = armyById(p1), u.paste and u.paste.r_pasteU
    if not arm then say(pk, "Choose an army.")
    elseif not rr then say(pk, "Paste the army's new list first.")
    else
      if arm.upLeft then r = setUpgradeList(arm, rr); say(pk, r, "New list set: it takes over when the upgrade finishes.")
      else r = upgradeArmy(arm, rr); say(pk, r, "Upgrade started: the new list takes over when it finishes.") end
      if r == true then u.paste.r_pasteU = nil end
    end
  elseif a == "edit" then r = editArmy(armyById(p1), { leader = f.eLeader, list = f.eList, units = f.eUnits }); say(pk, r, "Army edited.")
  elseif a == "acancel" then
    if u.confirm == ("acancel:" .. p1) then r = cancelRaise(armyById(p1)); say(pk, r, "Recruitment cancelled and refunded."); if r == true then u.sel.army = nil end
    else u.confirm = "acancel:" .. p1; keepConfirm = true end
  elseif a == "disband" then
    if u.confirm == ("disband:" .. p1) then r = disbandArmy(armyById(p1)); say(pk, r, "Army disbanded."); if r == true then u.sel.army = nil end
    else u.confirm = "disband:" .. p1; keepConfirm = true end
  elseif a == "retreat" then r = retreatArmy(armyById(p1), numOf(f.rDest)); say(pk, r, "Army retreated.")
  elseif a == "destroy" then r = destroyArmy(armyById(p1), "no retreat"); say(pk, r, "Army removed."); u.sel.army = nil
  elseif a == "selG" then u.sel.agent = (p1 ~= "new") and tonumber(p1) or nil
  elseif a == "recruit" then
    local ok2, g = recruitAgent(side, f.gType or "Scout", numOf(f.gTerr), idOf(f.gArmy), f.gName)
    if ok2 == true then f.gName = nil end
    say(pk, ok2, "Agent recruited."); if ok2 == true then u.sel.agent = g.id end
  elseif a == "gmove" then r = moveAgent(agentById(p1), numOf(f.gDest)); say(pk, r, "Agent moved.")
  elseif a == "gact" then
    local tgt = f.gTargetKey or string.match(f.gTarget or "", "%[(.-)%]$")
    r = agentAction(agentById(p1), tgt); say(pk, r, "Done - see the Log for the result.")
    if not agentById(p1) then u.sel.agent = nil end
  elseif a == "gdis" then
    if u.confirm == ("dismiss:" .. p1) then local g = agentById(p1); if g then removeAgent(g); clog(g.side .. " dismissed a " .. g.type .. ".", g.side) end; u.sel.agent = nil; say(pk, true, "Agent dismissed.")
    else u.confirm = "dismiss:" .. p1; keepConfirm = true end
  elseif a == "bprio" then
    local n = numOf(f.r_bTerr)
    if n then r = rollBattlePriority(n, armyById(idOf(f.r_bAtk)), armyById(idOf(f.r_bDef))); say(pk, r, "Battle priority rolled - see the Log.") end
  elseif a == "bapply" then
    r = applyBattle({ terr = numOf(f.r_bTerr), atk = idOf(f.r_bAtk),
                      def = (string.find(f.r_bDef or "", "Garrison", 1, true) and "garrison") or idOf(f.r_bDef),
                      winner = f.bWin, outcome = f.bOut or "Broken", casA = f.bCasA, casD = f.bCasD, duel = f.bDuel or "none",
                      retreat = f.bRetreat == "True", atkAll = f.bAtkAll, defAll = f.bDefAll, cas = f.bCas, outs = f.bOuts, rets = f.bRets, leaders = f.bLeaders })
    say(pk, r, "Battle recorded. Losers retreat in the Retreat stage.")
    if r == true then f.bCasA, f.bCasD, f.bWin, f.bDuel, f.r_bLostA, f.r_bLostD, f.bRetreat = nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil, nil; u.bopen = nil end
  elseif a == "xfer" then
    local fe = string.match(f.sFrom or "", "%[(.-)%]$")
    local te = string.match(f.sTo or "", "%[(.-)%]$")
    r = transfer(side, fe, te, f.sAmt); say(pk, r, "Resources moved.")
  elseif a == "hidden" then S.settings.hidden = not S.settings.hidden
  elseif a == "override" then S.settings.override = not S.settings.override
  elseif a == "start" then
    if u.confirm == "start" then
      if f.theatre and f.theatre ~= "" then local okT = setTheatre(f.theatre, true); if okT ~= true then say(pk, okT); return end end
      S.settings.capGood = numOf(f.capGood) or S.settings.capGood
      S.settings.capEvil = numOf(f.capEvil) or S.settings.capEvil
      S.settings.startRP = tonumber(f.startRP or "") or S.settings.startRP
      startCampaign()
      for _, k in ipairs({ "All", "Good", "Evil" }) do if U[k] then U[k].sel = {}; U[k].tab = "Turn" end end
      say(pk, true, "A new campaign has begun.")
    else u.confirm = "start"; keepConfirm = true end
  elseif a == "export" then writeNotebook(exportState()); say(pk, true, "Saved to the Notebook tab 'War Council Save'.")
  elseif a == "import" then
    local body = readNotebook()
    if not body then say(pk, "No Notebook tab called 'War Council Save' found.") else r = importState(body); say(pk, r, "Campaign imported.") end
  elseif a == "tokspawn" or a == "toklink" or a == "tokplace" or a == "tokunlink" then
    local ent = (p1 == "army") and armyById(p2) or agentById(p2)
    if not ent then say(pk, "Not found.")
    elseif a == "tokspawn" then r = spawnToken(p1, ent); say(pk, r, "Token spawned.")
    elseif a == "tokplace" then placeToken(p1, ent); say(pk, true, "Token moved to its territory.")
    elseif a == "tokunlink" then unlinkToken(p1, ent); say(pk, true, "Token unlinked.")
    else
      local sel = player and player.getSelectedObjects and player.getSelectedObjects() or {}
      local o = sel[1]
      if not o then say(pk, "Select the object on the table first (click it so it highlights), then press Link.")
      elseif #sel > 1 then say(pk, "Select just one object.")
      else linkToken(p1, ent, o); say(pk, true, "Linked to '" .. (o.getName() ~= "" and o.getName() or "object") .. "'.") end
    end
  elseif a == "canmarch" then local arm = armyById(p1); if arm then arm.canMarch = not arm.canMarch end
  elseif a == "dropmove" then S.settings.dropMove = not S.settings.dropMove
  elseif a == "drivespeed" then S.settings.driveSpeed = p1
  elseif a == "theatre" then r = setTheatre(p1); say(pk, r, "Theatre: " .. tostring(p1) .. ".")
  elseif a == "wagonface" then S.settings.wagonFace = not (S.settings.wagonFace ~= false)
  elseif a == "rebuildpieces" then
    local ok3, err = pcall(reconcileBuildings); say(pk, ok3 or tostring(err), "Building pieces rebuilt.")
  elseif a == "slotedit" then r = editSlots(numOf(f.slotTerr)); say(pk, r, "Markers placed. Drag them, then Save.")
  elseif a == "slotsave" then r = saveSlotEdit(); say(pk, r, "Slots saved.")
  elseif a == "slotreset" then r = resetSlotEdit(S.editing); say(pk, r, "Slots reset to default.")
  elseif a == "slotcancel" then cancelSlotEdit(); say(pk, true, "Slot editing cancelled.")
  elseif a == "selW" then u.sel.wagon = (p1 ~= "new") and tonumber(p1) or nil; u.sel.leg = 1
  elseif a == "wrecruit" then
    local ok2, g = recruitAgent(side, "Supply Wagon", numOf(f.wTerr))
    say(pk, ok2, ok2 == true and (g.name .. " recruited.") or nil); if ok2 == true then u.sel.wagon = g.id end
  elseif a == "wload" then r = wagonLoadAll(agentById(p1), numOf(f.wAmt)); say(pk, r, "Loaded.")
  elseif a == "wunload" then r = wagonUnloadAll(agentById(p1), numOf(f.wAmt)); say(pk, r, "Unloaded.")
  elseif a == "wauto" then local g = agentById(p1); if g then g.autoUnload = not g.autoUnload end
  elseif a == "wmove" then r = moveAgent(agentById(p1), numOf(f.wDest)); say(pk, r, "Wagon moved.")
  elseif a == "act" then
    local ar = armyById(p2)
    if ar then
      if p1 == "scorch" and u.confirm ~= ("act:scorch:" .. ar.id) then
        u.confirm = "act:scorch:" .. ar.id; keepConfirm = true
      else
        local target
        if p1 == "sabotage" then
          local t = S.terr[ar.loc]
          local i = 1
          for k2, item in ipairs(t.q or {}) do if (BUILDING_BY_KEY[item.key].name .. " (" .. item.left .. "t left)") == f.actSab then i = k2 end end
          target = t.q[i] and t.q[i].key
        end
        r = orderAction(ar, p1, target); say(pk, r, ACTIONS[p1].name .. " ordered.")
      end
    end
  elseif a == "actcancel" then local ar = armyById(p1); if ar then r = cancelOrder(ar, "called off", true); say(pk, r, "Order cancelled.") end
  elseif a == "rnew" then local g = agentById(p1); if g then newRoute(g) end
  elseif a == "rsupply" then
    local g = agentById(p1)
    if g then
      local had = g.route and isArmyRoute(g.route)
      r = newArmyRoute(g, idOf(f.sArmy), numOf(f.sDepot))
      if r == true and not had then r = toggleRoute(g) end
      say(pk, r, had and "Supply run updated." or "Supply run started.")
    end
  elseif a == "rtoggle" then r = toggleRoute(agentById(p1)); say(pk, r, "Route updated.")
  elseif a == "rsel" then u.sel.leg = tonumber(p1)
  elseif a == "sact" then
    local g = agentById(p1)
    if g and g.route then
      local leg = g.route.legs[tonumber(p2)]
      local cur = leg and ((tonumber(parts[5]) == 0) and leg.depart or leg.acts[tonumber(parts[5])])
      setStopAct(g, tonumber(p2), tonumber(parts[5]), parts[6], cur and cur.a or nil)
    end
  elseif a == "addstop" then
    local g = agentById(p1)
    if g then r = addRouteStop(g, tonumber(p2), numOf(f.addStop)); say(pk, r, "Stop added."); f.addStop = nil end
  elseif a == "rmstop" then
    local g = agentById(p1)
    if g then r = setRouteStep(g, tonumber(p2), tonumber(parts[5]), nil); say(pk, r, "Stop removed.") end
  elseif a == "radd" then local g = agentById(p1); if g then addRouteLeg(g); u.sel.leg = #g.route.legs end
  elseif a == "rdel" then r = removeRouteLeg(agentById(p1)); say(pk, r, "Turn removed.")
  elseif a == "rclear" then
    if u.confirm == ("rclear:" .. p1) then local g = agentById(p1); if g then g.route = nil end; say(pk, true, "Route deleted.")
    else u.confirm = "rclear:" .. p1; keepConfirm = true end
  elseif a == "rsave" then r = saveRoute(agentById(p1), f.routeName); say(pk, r, "Route saved to the library."); if r == true then f.routeName = nil end
  elseif a == "rapply" then
    local idx = numOf(f.libSel)
    local g = u.sel.wagon and agentById(u.sel.wagon)
    if g then local ok2, m = applyRoute(g, idx); say(pk, ok2, m) end
  elseif a == "rlibdel" then r = deleteRoute(numOf(f.libSel)); say(pk, r, "Saved route deleted."); f.libSel = nil
  elseif a == "rall" then local ok2, n = setAllRoutes(side, p1 == "1"); say(pk, ok2, n .. " route(s) " .. (p1 == "1" and "resumed." or "paused."))
  elseif a == "newsok" then
    S.newsSeen = S.newsSeen or {}
    S.newsSeen[p1] = S.newsId or 0
  elseif a == "newslog" then
    S.newsSeen = S.newsSeen or {}
    S.newsSeen[p1] = S.newsId or 0
    local vk = viewerKey(p1)
    uiState(vk).open = true
    uiState(vk).tab = "Log"
  elseif a == "popups" then S.settings.popups = not S.settings.popups
  elseif a == "lockseats" then S.settings.lockSeats = not S.settings.lockSeats; enforceSeats()
  elseif a == "cal" then
    local b = getBoard()
    if b then b.call("calibrate", { what = p1 }); say(pk, true, "Board overlays updated.") else say(pk, "Couldn't find the map board.") end
  end
  if not keepConfirm then u.confirm = nil end
  if QUIET then return end   -- only counting the dice this action needs
  if before and (r == nil or r == true) then pushUndo(before, a, pk) end
  if S.started and (S.turn ~= turnBefore or S.stage ~= stageBefore) then announceStage() end
  local panelOnly = PANEL_ONLY[a] and not (S.started and (S.turn ~= turnBefore or S.stage ~= stageBefore))
  if panelOnly then
    -- only this panel's view changed: nothing to reveal, sound or mark on the map, just the map highlight
    LAST_HINT_PK = pk
    local okH, errH = pcall(updateHints, pk)
    if not okH then print("War Council map hints: " .. tostring(errH)) end
    rebuild(pk)
  else
    afterAction(pk)
    rebuild()
  end
  ACT_PK = nil
end

-- ===================================================================
-- lifecycle
-- ===================================================================
function onLoad(saved)
  -- pasted onto the campaign map by mistake? Then don't take over the map: say what to do instead.
  if self.getGUID() == BOARD_GUID then
    WRONG_OBJECT = true
    WRONG_SAVED = saved   -- kept exactly as it was, so nothing is lost
    -- a campaign was being kept here: copy it into the Notebook so the real War Council can import it
    local kept = false
    if saved and saved ~= "" and string.find(saved, '"terr"', 1, true) then kept = pcall(writeNotebook, saved) end
    Wait.time(function()
      broadcastToAll("The War Council script has been pasted into the campaign MAP. Right-click the map > Scripting, paste EriadorBoard.lua there instead, " ..
                     "then Save & Play. WarCouncil.lua belongs in the War Council object." ..
                     (kept and " Your campaign has been copied to the Notebook tab 'War Council Save': use Settings > Import from Notebook in the War Council." or ""),
                     { 1, 0.45, 0.35 })
    end, 2)
    return
  end
  math.randomseed(os.time())
  GP = self.getGUID() .. "/"
  useTheatreMap("Eriador")
  S = newState()
  if saved and saved ~= "" then
    local ok, data = pcall(JSON.decode, saved)
    if ok and type(data) == "table" and data.terr then S = data; fixState() end
  end
  self.setName("War Council")
  self.setDescription("Campaign manager. Press the War Council button at the bottom right of the screen.")
  local existing = UI.getXmlTable() or {}
  baseXml = {}
  for _, e in ipairs(existing) do
    local id = e.attributes and e.attributes.id or ""
    if string.sub(id, 1, 3) ~= "wc_" and e.tag ~= "Defaults" then table.insert(baseXml, e) end
  end
  pcall(soundCheck, true)
  addHotkey("War Council: open / close", function(color)
    local pk = "All"
    if color == S.settings.goodColor then pk = "Good" elseif color == S.settings.evilColor then pk = "Evil" end
    uiState(pk).open = not uiState(pk).open
    afterAction(pk)
    rebuild()
  end)
  addHotkey("War Council: open territory under cursor", function(color, obj, pointer)
    local b = getBoard()
    local n = b and pointer and b.call("territoryAt", { position = pointer }) or nil
    if n then openTerritoryFor({ color = color, n = n })
    else pcall(function() broadcastToColor("No territory under the cursor.", color, { 0.9, 0.6, 0.5 }) end) end
  end)
  Wait.frames(function()
    if S.started then syncAllOwners() end
    rebuild()
    enforceSeats()
  end, 20)
  startPieces()
  Wait.time(function() pcall(hoverTick) end, 0.25, -1)
  Wait.time(function() pcall(prewarmTick) end, 0.25, -1)
  -- campaigns saved on the old (uncropped) map move over by themselves
  Wait.time(function()
    local ok, r, m = pcall(migrateMap)
    if not ok then print("War Council map update: " .. tostring(r)) elseif r == false and m then broadcastToAll("War Council: " .. m, { 0.95, 0.75, 0.45 }) end
  end, 4)
  Wait.frames(function() pcall(reconcileMarkers, true); pcall(reconcileRoads, true) end, 90)
end

-- Tabletop Simulator saves often (autosave, rewind points). Encoding a long campaign takes a moment, so:
--  * the last copy is reused while nothing has changed (for up to half a minute);
--  * the long lists that only ever grow at the end (log, turn snapshots, news, dice) keep their encoded text, and
--    only new entries are encoded and added to it. Every 20th save encodes them afresh, to be safe.
SAVE_CACHE = nil
SAVE_LISTS = { log = true, snaps = true, news = true, diceLog = true }
SAVE_PARTS = {}
function saveEncodeList(k, l)
  local c = SAVE_PARTS[k]
  local n = #l
  if c and c.saves < 20 and n >= c.n and (c.n == 0 or l[c.n] == c.last) then
    if n > c.n then
      local new = {}
      for i = c.n + 1, n do new[#new + 1] = l[i] end
      local inner = string.sub(JSON.encode(new), 2, -2)
      c.body = (c.body == "") and inner or (c.body .. "," .. inner)
      c.n, c.last = n, l[n]
    end
    c.saves = c.saves + 1
  else
    local js = (n > 0) and JSON.encode(l) or "[]"
    c = { n = n, last = l[n], body = string.sub(js, 2, -2), saves = 0 }
    SAVE_PARTS[k] = c
  end
  return "[" .. c.body .. "]"
end
function saveEncode()
  local parts = {}
  for k, v in pairs(S) do
    local tv, js = type(v), nil
    if tv == "table" then
      if SAVE_LISTS[k] and (#v > 0 or next(v) == nil) then js = saveEncodeList(k, v) else js = JSON.encode(v) end
    elseif tv == "number" then
      js = (v == math.floor(v) and math.abs(v) < 1e15) and string.format("%d", v) or tostring(v)
    elseif tv == "boolean" then js = v and "true" or "false"
    elseif tv == "string" then js = string.sub(JSON.encode({ v }), 2, -2)
    end
    if js then table.insert(parts, '"' .. tostring(k) .. '":' .. js) end
  end
  return "{" .. table.concat(parts, ",") .. "}"
end
function onSave()
  if WRONG_OBJECT then return WRONG_SAVED or "" end
  if not S then return "" end
  local now = os.time()
  local c = SAVE_CACHE
  if c and c.ver == STATE_VER and c.undo == #UNDO and (now - c.t) < 30 then return c.js end
  local ok, js = pcall(saveEncode)
  if not ok or type(js) ~= "string" then js = JSON.encode(S) end
  SAVE_CACHE = { ver = STATE_VER, undo = #UNDO, t = now, js = js }
  return js
end

-- Seats: only the Good and Evil colours (plus Grey spectators) are allowed while seats are locked.
local function seatAllowed(color)
  return color == "Grey" or color == S.settings.goodColor or color == S.settings.evilColor
end

function enforceSeats()
  if not S or not S.settings.lockSeats then return end
  local ok, players = pcall(function() return Player.getPlayers() end)
  if not ok or not players then return end
  for _, p in ipairs(players) do
    if p.color and not seatAllowed(p.color) then
      local from = p.color
      p.changeColor("Grey")
      broadcastToAll((p.steam_name or "A player") .. " moved to spectator: only " .. S.settings.goodColor .. " (Good) and " ..
                     S.settings.evilColor .. " (Evil) can be used.", { 0.95, 0.75, 0.45 })
    end
  end
end

function onPlayerChangeColor(color)
  if not S then return end
  if S.settings.lockSeats and color and not seatAllowed(color) then
    Wait.frames(enforceSeats, 1)
    return
  end
  rebuild()
end


-- A player pressed R on a die in the tray.
function onObjectRandomize(obj, color) if diceRandomized then pcall(diceRandomized, obj, color) end end


-- ===================================================================
-- performance report: how long each part of the War Council takes, measured in the real game
-- ===================================================================
PERF = { on = false }
;(function()
  local T, C, F, started = {}, {}, nil, 0
  local clock = os.clock
  local NAMES_P = {
    uiRun = "Handling a click (everything below included)", rebuild = "Redrawing panels (incl. updating the screen)",
    applyXml = "Updating the screen", stateSnap = "Undo copy", stateRestore = "Undo restore", afterAction = "Map updates after a click",
    reconcileMarkers = "Map markers", reconcileRoads = "Roads", updateHints = "Map highlights", revealSweep = "Reveal checks",
    soundCheck = "Sounds", pieceTick = "Background check (4 a second)", hoverTick = "Hover info (4 a second)",
    pollTokens = "Full token check", lookAll = "Army model names", fillMissingBuildings = "Building pieces",
    onSave = "Saving the campaign", withDice = "Dice handling", prewarmTick = "Preparing tabs in the background",
  }
  local WHY = {}
  function PERF.count(k) C[k] = (C[k] or 0) + 1 end
  function PERF.why(w) w = string.gsub(w, "^page (%a+) in wc_pages_(%a+)$", "%2 panel, %1 tab changed shape"); WHY[w] = (WHY[w] or 0) + 1 end
  local function frameTick()
    if not PERF.on then return end
    local dt = 0
    pcall(function() dt = Time.delta_time * 1000 end)
    if F then
      F.n = F.n + 1
      if dt > 50 then F.slow = F.slow + 1 end
      if dt > 100 then F.vslow = F.vslow + 1 end
      if dt > F.worst then F.worst = dt end
    end
    Wait.frames(frameTick, 1)
  end
  function PERF.start()
    T, C, WHY = {}, {}, {}
    F = { n = 0, slow = 0, vslow = 0, worst = 0 }
    started = os.time()
    PERF.on = true
    pcall(function() Wait.frames(frameTick, 1) end)
  end
  local function wrap(name)
    local f = _G[name]
    if type(f) ~= "function" then return end
    _G[name] = function(...)
      if not PERF.on or not clock then return f(...) end
      local t0 = clock()
      local r = table.pack(f(...))
      local dt = (clock() - t0) * 1000
      local e = T[name]
      if not e then e = { n = 0, tot = 0, max = 0 }; T[name] = e end
      e.n = e.n + 1; e.tot = e.tot + dt
      if dt > e.max then e.max = dt end
      return table.unpack(r, 1, r.n)
    end
  end
  for name in pairs(NAMES_P) do wrap(name) end
  local function ms(x) return string.format("%.1f", x) end
  function PERF.report()
    local out = {}
    if not clock then return { "War Council: this copy of Tabletop Simulator has no timer, so no report can be made." } end
    local secs = math.max(1, os.time() - started)
    table.insert(out, "[b]War Council performance[/b] - last " .. secs .. " s")
    if F and F.n > 0 then
      table.insert(out, "Frames: " .. F.n .. " (" .. string.format("%.0f", F.n / secs) .. " a second). Slow frames: " .. F.slow ..
                        " over 50 ms, " .. F.vslow .. " over 100 ms. Worst: " .. ms(F.worst) .. " ms.")
    end
    local list = {}
    for k, e in pairs(T) do table.insert(list, { k = k, e = e }) end
    table.sort(list, function(a, b) return a.e.tot > b.e.tot end)
    if #list == 0 then table.insert(out, "Nothing timed yet.") end
    for i, x in ipairs(list) do
      if i > 16 then break end
      local e = x.e
      table.insert(out, (NAMES_P[x.k] or x.k) .. ": " .. e.n .. "x, average " .. ms(e.tot / e.n) .. " ms, longest " .. ms(e.max) ..
                        " ms (" .. ms(e.tot) .. " ms in all)")
    end
    local cs = {}
    for k, v in pairs(C) do table.insert(cs, k .. " " .. v) end
    table.sort(cs)
    if #cs > 0 then table.insert(out, "Counts: " .. table.concat(cs, ", ") .. ".") end
    local ws = {}
    for k, v in pairs(WHY) do table.insert(ws, { k = k, v = v }) end
    table.sort(ws, function(a, b) return a.v > b.v end)
    if #ws > 0 then
      local parts = {}
      for i, w in ipairs(ws) do if i <= 8 then table.insert(parts, w.k .. " (" .. w.v .. ")") end end
      table.insert(out, "Full redraws because: " .. table.concat(parts, "; ") .. ".")
    end
    local objs = 0
    pcall(function() objs = #getAllObjects() end)
    table.insert(out, "Objects on the table: " .. objs .. ". Armies: " .. #(S and S.armies or {}) .. ", agents: " .. #(S and S.agents or {}) .. ".")
    return out
  end
end)()
